# Community rejection cases (2022–2026)

Real rejection stories collected from Apple Developer Forums, Google Play Developer Community, Stack Overflow (via the Stack Exchange API), GitHub issues/PRs, Hacker News, Kakao DevTalk and developer blogs in English, Korean, Japanese and Chinese. Compiled 2026-08-28 in three passes (English → Korean/Japanese/Chinese → thin areas + Russian/Spanish/Portuguese/Vietnamese/Indonesian/Turkish/German/French); **1270 case entries** (some entries bundle several rounds of one app). Every case links to its source; **high** = the page was read in full, **medium** = search snippet / unresolved thread where the "fix" is expert advice rather than a confirmed approval.

How the skill uses this file:
- **Mode B (rejection intake)** — match the reviewer's wording against the *Reviewer phrasings* index at the bottom to jump to the checklist IDs and to similar cases.
- **Mode A (pre-check)** — when an item is FAIL/WARN, cite the closest case here ("this exact thing got someone rejected — this is what fixed it").
- Contributions: add a bullet in the matching section with the same fields; map it to checklist IDs (`→`).

First pass did not cover Reddit, Stack Overflow, CSDN (crawler-blocked); the 2nd pass (sections below the Google Play block) adds Korean/Japanese/Chinese/Stack-Exchange-sourced cases. Fields: date · app · guideline/policy · reviewer quote · cause · fix · rounds · source (confidence, language) · → checklist IDs.

---

## Apple App Store

### 1.2 User-Generated Content
- **2025-11 · messaging/events app (Bitchat-like)** — 1.2. Round 1 listed the five standard items; round 2 added "age rating must reflect 18+", "mechanism for users to immediately remove posts from feed", "contact information in app for reporting". Fix: EULA on first launch, content filter in settings, press-and-hold report on messages, report button on events, block via profile, admin backend credentials + testing guide. 2 rejections. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/807358) (high, en) → UGC-01~05, META-03, ACC-03
- **2022-05 · RN + Next.js webview community app (Korea)** — 1.2 (+5.1.2, 4.8 button naming, 2.1). "reporting function missing". Fix: post **and user** reporting; ATT popup after login; Apple button renamed to "Apple로 로그인"; personal Naver account with international login enabled for reviewers; camera/photo purpose strings expanded. 3 iOS + 3 Android rejections over ~3 months. [source](https://velog.io/@rkd028/iOSAndroid-%EC%BB%A4%EB%AE%A4%EB%8B%88%ED%8B%B0-%EC%95%B1-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9DReject-%EC%82%AC%EC%9C%A0-%EC%A0%95%EB%A6%AC-%EB%B0%8F-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC-%ED%9B%84%EA%B8%B0) (high, ko) → UGC-03, PRIV-03, IOS-LOGIN-01, ACC-03, PERM-01
- **2022-02 · Flutter ramen map/review app (Japan)** — 1.2 "Apps with user-generated content must take specific steps to moderate content and prevent abusive behavior". Timeline feature had no report/block. Fix: report + block UI. 1 of 5 rejections before release. [source](https://zenn.dev/wakanao/articles/88d406f313e129) (high, ja) → UGC-03, UGC-04

### 2.1 App Completeness / Information Needed (demo account, crashes, hidden gates)
- **2026-03 · RN/Expo fitness app, Supabase email login** — 2.1. Reviewer screenshot "Invalid login credentials" with correct email. Cause unconfirmed (network path from review devices suspected). Proposed fix: "Demo Mode" button bypassing auth for review. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/817939) (high, en) → ACC-03
- **2025-08 · phone + OTP login app** — 2.1 "Cannot login" then "No Internet Connection". Server saw no attempts; reviewers likely offline. Fix: demo account, documented internet requirement, offline error handling. 4+ rounds. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/797152) (high, en) → ACC-03, CONTENT-01
- **2025-11 · Swift app with forced-update gate** — 2.1 "we are unable to access content without updating the app". The mandatory-update check blocked the review build because its version was newer than the store version. Fix: compare versions segment-by-segment; only alert when local < store. [source](https://qiita.com/TanakaMikihisa/items/d5c84b03c7088521d25e) (high, ja) → CONTENT-11
- **2024-09 · collaborative todo app (Firebase)** — 2.1 Information Needed; 1.5; 2.1; 5.1.2. "We were unable to create a new group" on iPad Air 5 / iOS 18. Support URL was a Twitter link; UI froze on a slow Firestore call; privacy label declared crash data as tracking without ATT. Fix: anonymous auth + explanation; Google Form as support URL; network handling; ATT. 4 rounds. [source](https://qiita.com/Q64570316/items/465f611238c2e2d8a1c7) (high, ja) → CONTENT-05, CONTENT-01, PRIV-03, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2024-07 · location-based territory game (Korea)** — 2.1 "The app displayed a splash screen indefinitely and was not responsive" (network + GPS off at splash); then 5.1.1 for a mandatory gender field. Fix: GPS load moved after splash; gender optional. 2 rejections, live 3 days later. [source](https://velog.io/@koomin1227/%EB%B0%B1%EC%97%94%EB%93%9C-%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%EC%9E%90%EC%9D%98-%EC%A2%8C%EC%B6%A9%EC%9A%B0%EB%8F%8C-%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC%EA%B8%B0) (high, ko) → CONTENT-01, PERM-02, ACC-01
- **2024-12 · indie apps roundup (Japan)** — 2.1 ×2: "In-app purchase products submitted but functionality not present in binary" (unfinished IAP left in ASC → deleted); "Unable to locate ATT permission request" (ATT shown only deep in the app → moved earlier). [source](https://zenn.dev/mixi/articles/8f331315d75b11) (high, ja) → PAY-04, PRIV-03
- **2024-07 · ad-supported app deferring ATT by 24 h** — 2.1 Information Needed "unable to locate the App Tracking Transparency permission request". Fix: repro steps (advance device date) + video; recurs every submission. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/758583) (high, en) → PRIV-03, META-05
- **2023-02 · game with data-transfer accounts** — 2.3.4, 1.5, 2.1: preview video with black bars; dead support URL; expired demo credentials. Fix: removed previews, fixed URL, fresh account. Approved on 3rd. [source](https://qiita.com/Nov-F/items/97dcc5c4f2f03dca8808) (high, ja) → META-04, ACC-03, IOS-META-01
- **2023 · NFC app** — 2.1 "We need a demo video that shows all the NFC functionality". Fix: physical-device demo video. [source](https://note.com/_ring/n/n412aeda8e988) (high, ja) → META-05
- **2022-02 · Flutter app (Japan)** — 2.1 ×2: infinite spinner when location denied; Sign in with Apple failed on iPad (bundle ID mismatch with Firebase). Fix: fallback coordinates; re-linked Firebase. [source](https://zenn.dev/wakanao/articles/88d406f313e129) (high, ja) → PERM-02, ACC-04
- **2024-07→2025-02 · Flutter apps stuck on splash on iPad Air 5 / iOS 17.5.1** — 2.1, unreproducible for three developers; 4 rounds. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/759572) (high, en) → IOS-IPAD-01, CONTENT-01
- **2022-03 · SwiftUI weather app (OpenWeather + reverseGeocode)** — 2.1 repeated; removed networking pieces until approved; IPv6 suspected. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/701619) (high, en) → CONTENT-09
- **2023-09 / 2024-04 · Bubble native apps** — 2.5.4 / 5.1.1 / 2.1: "Provide a username and password in the App Review Information section"; purpose strings insufficient; audio background mode removed. [source](https://forum.zeroqode.com/t/app-rejected-app-store/10980) (high, en) → ACC-03, PERM-01, IOS-BG-01

### 2.3 Accurate Metadata (screenshots, other platforms, What's New, age rating)
- **2025-09 · iOS app v2.0 after redesign (Korea)** — 2.3.3 "6.7-inch iPhone screenshots do not show the current version". Fix: re-shot screenshots; also added account deletion. [source](https://velog.io/@junnkyuu/%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC%EB%A5%BC-%ED%86%B5%EA%B3%BC%ED%95%98%EC%A7%80-%EB%AA%BB%ED%95%A8-%EC%B2%AB-App-Store-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%B0%98%EB%A0%A4-%EB%B0%8F-%EB%8C%80%EC%9D%91-%ED%9B%84%EA%B8%B0) (high, ko) → META-01, ACC-02
- **2024-12 · cross-platform app (Japan)** — 2.3.10 "third-party platform info not relevant to App Store users": IAP text said purchases don't transfer between iOS/Android. Fix: show the sentence only on Android. Same roundup: 2.5.10 "screenshots include test advertisements" → hid ads in screenshots. [source](https://zenn.dev/mixi/articles/8f331315d75b11) (high, ja) → CONTENT-04, META-01
- **2024-03 · unspecified** — 2.3.10 "Revise the app's home page to remove Google Play references" (linked website!). [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/748862) (high, en) → CONTENT-04
- **2025-06 · open-source uploader** — 2.3.10 + 3.1.1 because the linked GitHub page had Play Store and donation links. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/786489) (high, en) → CONTENT-04, PAY-01
- **2026-08 · astronomy app (HN)** — reviewers misidentified a "live tarot reading feature"; commenters report "Android" in a changelog and in a privacy policy each triggered rejections. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49214863) (high/medium, en) → CONTENT-04
- **2022-10 · schedule app (Korea)** — 2.3.12 What's New "Happy Halloween~!" → replied with the real bug-fix text in Resolution Center; approved within an hour without resubmitting. [source](https://velog.io/@luciancah/%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EB%B9%A0%EA%BE%B8-%EB%8C%80%EC%B2%98%EA%B8%B0-1) (high, ko) → META-02
- **2023 · indie app (Japan)** — 2.3.4 "Your previews include device images and/or device frames". Fix: removed bezels from preview videos. [source](https://note.com/_ring/n/n412aeda8e988) (high, ja) → IOS-META-01
- **2025-02 · iPhone app** — "The 13-inch iPad screenshots show an iPhone image that has been modified or stretched". Fix: native iPad screenshots or iPhone-only. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/774488) (high, en) → IOS-META-01, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2023-06 · Flutter token calculator (Japan)** — 4.1 "your app's name includes references to ChatGPT". Fix: replaced every ChatGPT/GPT/OpenAI in title, subtitle, keywords, icon. ~1 month, 3 appeals denied. [source](https://zenn.dev/flutternyumon/articles/04b5f6c3c08fd0) (high, ja) → META-02
- **2024-03 · gambling app** — 2.3.6 "you must select 'Yes' for Gambling"; gambling apps must be organization accounts. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/748902) (high, en) → META-03, IOS-META-06
- **2025-12 · women's health Q&A app** — 2.3.6 "remove 'Age Assurance' unless the feature can be located" — reviewers couldn't find the DOB gate. Fix: attach DOB-screen screenshots on every submission. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/810077) (high, en) → IOS-META-06, META-05

### 2.5.4 Background modes (silent audio, alarms)
- **2024-05 · Flutter alarm app (gdelataillade/alarm)** — 2.5.4 R1 "Your app plays an alarm but does not display the notification"; R2 background audio "inappropriate because it can be replicated with Local Notifications". Fix: technical rebuttal (local notifications can't play long audio, bypass DND, vibrate adequately) → approved. Later commenters approved after explaining exactly which audio plays. [source](https://github.com/gdelataillade/alarm/discussions/87) (high, en) → IOS-BG-01, META-05
- **2022-11 · Flutter audio_service user** — 2.5.4 "declares support for audio in the UIBackgroundModes key… but we were unable to play any audible content when the app was running in the background". Fix: play real audio or remove the key. [source](https://github.com/ryanheise/audio_service/issues/975) (high, en) → IOS-BG-01, IOS-PLIST-02
- **2023-09 · Bubble native app** — 2.5.4 "unable to locate any features that require persistent audio" → rebuilt without the audio mode. [source](https://forum.zeroqode.com/t/app-rejected-app-store/10980) (high, en) → IOS-BG-01
- **2025-03 · audio recording app** — 2.5.4 "the audio background mode is only supposed to be used for audio playback" (used to keep recording alive). [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/776949) (high, en) → IOS-BG-01

### 2.5.1 / 2.3.1 Software requirements, leftover capabilities, hidden features
- **2024-11 · any Flutter 3.24.3/3.24.4 app** — 2.5.1 "non-public or deprecated APIs… _kCTFontPaletteAttribute" from Skia; fixed by Flutter hotfix. [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/158423) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2026-04 · app that removed FamilyControls** — 2.5.1 "still includes ScreenTime API without ScreenTime features" — capability still enabled on the App ID in the developer portal. Fix: remove Family Controls at developer.apple.com identifiers. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/822078) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04, IOS-ENT-02
- **2024-08 · app with no HealthKit code** — 2.5.1 "binary includes references to HealthKit components" — capability accidentally enabled. Fix: disable in Signing & Capabilities; check dependencies. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/762128) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04
- **2025-09 · step-count app** — 2.5.1 HealthKit "does not clearly identify the HealthKit… functionality in the app's user interface"; unresolved after onboarding text, indicator. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/802626) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04
- **2024-02 · app that removed tracking** — 2.5.1 "binary contains references to App Tracking Transparency, but you have indicated you do not intend to ask users for permission to track". Fix: remove NSUserTrackingUsageDescription and every ATTrackingManager reference incl. 3rd-party libs. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/747231) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04, PRIV-03
- **2024-04 · parental-control app** — 2.5.1 "still uses Screentime API to hide apps from user" (blockedApplications); appeal declined. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/749466) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-02
- **2024-06 · corporate VPN client** — 2.5.1 "using NEVPNManager APIs without using VPN APIs". [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/757698) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2024-03 · RN 0.70 + react-native-code-push** — 2.3.1 "The app contains hidden features." Fix: removed CodePush; approved. [source](https://github.com/microsoft/code-push/issues/841) (high, en) → CONTENT-10
- **2025-09 · app embedding web games** — 2.3.1 + 3.1.1 "code which causes it to behave differently during the review process regarding web game presentation and payment". Fix: removed web-game code and JS-bridge web payments. 3+ rounds. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/802224) (high, en) → CONTENT-10, PAY-01
- **guide · RN/Flutter 2.3.1 triggers** — undeclared OTA updates, DEBUG banners/dev overlays, remote feature flags, unreachable OTP-gated screens. [source](https://www.codewithkarani.com/blog/app-store-guideline-2-3-1-rejection-react-native-flutter) (high, en) → CONTENT-10, CONTENT-02

### 3.1.1 In-App Purchase
- **2025-09 · quiz-onboarding + Stripe link app** — 3.1.1 R1 "uses promo codes to unlock digital content"; R2 "Lifetime Access and Monthly Access can be purchased… using payment mechanisms other than in-app purchase". Fix required: IAP products; App Store offer codes instead of own promo codes. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/799798) (high, en) → PAY-01
- **2025-07 · hardware tracker with data plans sold outside** — 3.1.1 "accesses digital content purchased outside the app… but that content isn't available to purchase using in-app purchase"; 4+ rejections then silently resolved. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/794034) (high, en) → PAY-01
- **2021-10 · Flutter + RevenueCat (canonical restore case)** — 3.1.1 "offers in-app purchases that can be restored but does not include a 'Restore Purchases' feature" — restore was implemented but had no visible button. [source](https://community.revenuecat.com/sdks-51/we-get-rejected-from-apple-store-review-about-restore-purchases-693) (high, en) → PAY-03
- **2021-03 · Chinese course app** — 3.1.1 "Promo codes bypass App Store payment system"; also ¥ symbol removed and QQ/WeChat login buttons hidden when apps not installed (4.2.3). [source](https://www.cnblogs.com/goloving/p/14557050.html) (high, zh) → PAY-01, CONTENT-04

### 3.1.2 Subscriptions
- **2026-01 · language app "LingoBoard"** — 3.1.2 "did not include all the required information for apps offering auto-renewable subscriptions" — no tappable Privacy Policy / Terms links inside the running app. Fix: links in footer and paywall; Apple standard EULA. Passed on resubmit. [source](https://www.angularcorp.com/insights/apple-guideline-3-1-2-subscription-rejection-missing-links/) (high, en) → PAY-02
- **2026-01 · subscription app** — 3.1.2 links present in app but **missing from the App Store description**. Fix: add both URLs to metadata. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/813493) (high, en) → PAY-02
- **2025-11 · subscription app** — 3.1.2 "metadata is missing… A functional link to the Terms of Use (EULA)" and "The billed amount… is not clearly and conspicuously displayed" (trial price more prominent). [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/807082) (high, en) → PAY-02
- **2025-06→08 · card app with cloud sync** — 3.1.2 "Auto-renewing subscriptions are only appropriate for services that provide dynamic, ongoing value". Fix: converted to non-renewing subscriptions → approved. [source](https://community.revenuecat.com/general-questions-7/app-review-rejection-guideline-3-1-2-ongoing-value-6617) (high, en) → PAY-02
- **2026-08 · AI app (Japan, Firebase)** — 3.1.2(c) subscription info missing on the purchase screen (terms only in settings); also 4 Design (FirebaseUI contrast), 1.1 (keywords "匿名/Anonymous"), 5.1.1(v) (deletion existed but not found → demo video). 4 rounds. [source](https://qiita.com/i_aki0110/items/879c2a556782a5998acb) (high, ja) → PAY-02, IOS-UI-01, META-02, ACC-02, META-05

### 4.0 Design / Sign in with Apple UX
- **2026-02 · iPhone-only app reviewed on iPad Air 11" M3** — 2.1 "'Create PIN' button was not responsive" in iPad compatibility mode. Advice: adapt UI for iPad or restrict properly. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/815079) (high, en) → IOS-IPAD-01
- **2024-04 · Flutter macOS app + google_sign_in** — 4.0 "taken to the default web browser to sign in… poor user experience". [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/147246) (high, en) → IOS-UI-01
- **2024-12 / 2022-02 · Japanese apps** — 4.0 permission dialogs not localized. Fix: localize all NS*UsageDescription. [source](https://zenn.dev/mixi/articles/8f331315d75b11) (high, ja) → IOS-PLIST-01
- **2025-03 / 2024-05 / 2023-05 · three apps** — 4.0 "Your app requires users to provide their name and/or email address after using Sign in with Apple. This information is already provided by the Authentication Services framework." Causes: extra form; blocking relay emails; Firebase returning null displayName. Fix: drop the post-SIWA prompt; accept private-relay emails. [1](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/776815) [2](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/751115) [3](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/730223) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-04
- **2021-08 · hybrid app (Korea)** — 5.1.1 "users must furnish name and email after Sign in with Apple" → prefilled/removed the form → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@boms2/iOS-%EC%95%A0%ED%94%8C-%EB%A1%9C%EA%B7%B8%EC%9D%B8-%EC%A0%95%EC%B1%85-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%EC%82%AC%EC%9C%A0-%EC%A0%95%EB%A6%AC) (high, ko) → IOS-LOGIN-04

### 4.2 Minimum functionality
- **2022-07 · Flutter karate news app** — 4.2.2 "only includes links, images, or content aggregated from the Internet". Fix: no code change — detailed rebuttal listing native features; approved minutes after reply. [source](https://dev.to/xurxodev/how-i-got-a-rejected-app-approved-in-the-ios-app-store-gg0) (high, en) → CONTENT-03
- **2025-02 · roulette app for projectors** — 4.2. Fix: PDF explaining purpose + first-launch tutorial; approved on 3rd cycle. [source](https://note.com/yuyayamaki/n/ne0243a2e9cc5) (high, ja) → CONTENT-03, META-05
- **2024 · invite-only social revival; school bell app (HN)** — 4.2 "intended for a small, or niche, set of users". Fix: Unlisted App Distribution. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47916438) (high, en) → CONTENT-03

### 4.3 Spam / duplicates / templates
- **2025-01 · indie app** — 4.3 "We cannot provide individual feedback". Features + screenshots + description changes failed; **changing the icon significantly** → approved. 3 rounds, ~1 week. [source](https://tomolog.reafo.io/en/article/guidline4-3-design-spam-push-into-sleeplessweek) (high, en) → CONTENT-07
- **2024-07 · developer with several similar apps (Japan)** — 4.3(a). Fix: new icons + button layout **and** a new image-sharing feature; color-only changes had failed. [source](https://qiita.com/razuma/items/a959cb6fb78149615649) (high, ja) → CONTENT-07
- **2024-07 · Chinese indie app** — 4.3(a) "apps previously submitted by a terminated Apple Developer Program account". Fix: real screenshots, edited description/code, polite appeal; App Review Board found the original feedback inaccurate. [source](https://fangjunyu.com/2024/10/31/app-store-connect%E5%AE%A1%E6%A0%B8%E6%8B%92%E7%BB%9D%E5%8E%9F%E5%9B%A0%EF%BC%9Aguideline-4-3a-design-spam/) (high, zh) → CONTENT-07
- **2025-02 · in-house app** — 4.3(a) "terminated account" → appeal + phone call from App Review → approved. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/774789) (high, en) → CONTENT-07
- **2025-02 · Flutter live-score app** — 4.3(a) "Sharing similar binaries, metadata" (common pub.dev packages suspected); unresolved. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/775597) (high, en) → CONTENT-07
- **2024-08→2025-01 · unspecified** — 4.3(a) appeal approved, later update re-rejected; advice: put a reviewer note on every submission explaining the prior appeal outcome. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/773200) (high, en) → CONTENT-07, META-05
- **2026-03 / 2025-09 · live app v3.2; trading journal** — 4.1(c)/4.1 "misrepresenting itself as another popular app"; Apple pointed to "Preventing Copycat and Impersonation Rejections". [1](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/821107) [2](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/800311) (high, en) → META-02

### 4.8 Sign in with Apple
- **2023-07 · Flutter social-audio map app, Google-only login (Japan)** — 4.8 "Apps employing external authentication services must offer Sign in with Apple as an equivalent option". Fix: `sign_in_with_apple`, capability, Firebase Apple provider, null-safe name/email. 1 round. [source](https://zenn.dev/n_thy/articles/scade_apple_sign_in_d06b2e9551891b) (high, ja) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2025-03 · webview apps with Kakao/Google/Facebook/Naver login (Korea)** — 4.8 "uses third-party login but doesn't offer Apple Sign-in" — described as a "100%" rejection. Fix: Apple login on the website auth (or hide social logins during review). [source](https://www.swing2app.co.kr/blog/808919/) (high, ko) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2024-10 · e-learning app (email + Google + Facebook)** — 4.8 "does not appear to offer an equivalent login option with Sign in with Apple". [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/765145) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2024-07→2025-05 · multiple apps** — 4.8 three-bullet quote; email/password alternative still rejected; identical rejections ×3. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/760302) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2022-08 · client app, Google-only + web signup** — 4.8 "suggesting we should include Apple sign in option"; repeated by others in 2024. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/711724) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-01

### 5.1.1(v) Account deletion
- **2022-11→2024-11 · third-party Trakt clients** — 5.1.1(v); linking to a generic settings page wasn't accepted. Fix: link **directly** to the provider's deletion confirmation page + explain third-party client. [source](https://github.com/trakt/api-help/discussions/327) (high, en) → ACC-02
- **2023 · indie app (Japan)** — 5.1.1(v) "directs users to a form… but the form is not specifically for account deletion". Fix: dedicated deletion flow. [source](https://note.com/_ring/n/n412aeda8e988) (high, ja) → ACC-02
- **2024-10 · e-learning app** — 5.1.1(v) "supports account creation but does not include an option to initiate account deletion". [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/765160) (high, en) → ACC-02
- **2023-09 · first-time Korean submitter** — build removed with 5 reasons incl. no account deletion and "free" claims. [source](https://www.clien.net/service/board/cm_app/18288937) (high, ko) → ACC-02, META-02

### 5.1.1(ii) Purpose strings
- **2025-03 · MusicKit demo app** — 5.1.1 "purpose strings… do not sufficiently explain" — the real cause was the message shown **after "Don't Allow"**. Fix: detailed denial message → approved. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/776682) (high, en) → PERM-01
- **2025-06 · policy app** — 5.1.1 "Update the camera and photo library purpose string to explain how the app will use the requested information and provide a specific example" ("In order to take a picture or video, this app requires permission" was insufficient). [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/790111) (high, en) → PERM-01, IOS-PLIST-01
- **2023-09 · messaging app** — 5.1.1 camera/photos "not sufficient" twice; strings naming profile picture + in-app messaging. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/737757) (high, en) → PERM-01
- **2022-04 · Japanese app** — 5.1.1 camera/photos. Fix: concrete strings with usage examples. [source](https://zenn.dev/mukkun69n/articles/a79d42572bb60a) (high, ja) → PERM-01
- **2024-09 / 2025-05 · FlutterFlow app; RN + react-native-share** — ITMS-90683 "Missing purpose string" for NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription even when not using photos (library references). Fix: add the key anyway. [1](https://github.com/FlutterFlow/flutterflow-issues/issues/3820) [2](https://github.com/react-native-share/react-native-share/issues/1669) (high, en) → IOS-PLIST-01

### 5.1.1(iv) Login wall / data minimization
- **2025-09 · content app** — 5.1.1 "requires users to register or log in to access features that are not account-based". Fix: "Continue as Guest" (all but comments/profile/favorites). 2 rounds. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/801888) (high, en) → ACC-01
- **2025-01 · social-style app** — 5.1.1 "requires users to register before accessing any content"; appeal rejected. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/772174) (high, en) → ACC-01
- **roundup (Korea)** — mandatory birthdate/gender/address at signup → optional; referral rewards disabled on iOS. [source](https://brunch.co.kr/@mobiinside/2033) (medium, ko) → ACC-01, PRIV-04

### 5.1.2 Data use / ATT
- **2022-02 · app with owned web content** — 5.1.2 "collect data to track after the user selects 'Ask App Not to Track'… collects cookies for tracking". Fix: stop cookie tracking on denial; correct labels. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/701508) (high, en) → PRIV-03, PRIV-02
- **2025-02 · TikTok-like app with engagement rewards** — 5.1.2 interaction data without ATT; rewards questioned. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/773679) (high, en) → PRIV-03
- **2024-04 · paid-tasks app** — 5.1.2(i) "primary purpose… encourage users to perform digital tasks in exchange for compensation… not appropriate". [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/750319) (high, en) → PRIV-03

### Privacy manifest (ITMS-91053 / 91061)
- **2024-03 · Flutter + datadog_flutter_plugin** — ITMS-91053 "Missing API declaration… FileTimestamp" (+DiskSpace, SystemBootTime, UserDefaults). Fix: SDK update with NSPrivacyAccessedAPITypes. [source](https://github.com/DataDog/dd-sdk-flutter/issues/587) (high, en) → IOS-PRIV-03
- **2024-12 · Flutter + connectivity_plus 6.1.x** — ITMS-91061 "Missing privacy manifest" for the plugin framework. Fix: plugin PR; clean build. [source](https://github.com/fluttercommunity/plus_plugins/issues/3397) (high, en) → IOS-PRIV-03
- **2025-03 · .NET MAUI + Firebase** — ITMS-91061 naming FirebaseCore, GoogleDataTransport, nanopb… (transitive frameworks without manifests; app-level manifest insufficient). Fix: updated SDK versions. [1](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/28262) [2](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/780724) (high, en) → IOS-PRIV-03

### Kids (1.3), IP (5.2.1), regulated entities (5.1.1(ix)), business model (3.2.2), location (5.1.5), objectionable (1.1)
- **2026-01 · kids sounds app** — 1.3 + 4.0 "may include links, commerce, or ad interactions without parental permission"; "users are required to view advertisements prior to using the app". 2+ weeks. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/813791) (high, en) → UGC-07, ADS-02
- **2026-02 · education app live since 2020** — 1.3: add parental gates → "does not appear to be designed for kids aged 11 and under" → "previously approved for the Kids category" → new App ID. 4 rejections in 7 days. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/816277) (high, en) → UGC-07, IOS-META-06
- **2024-10 · RN kids app** — 1.3 "implements the App Tracking Transparency framework" (ATT references in a Kids app). [source](https://github.com/facebook/react-native/issues/47200) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04
- **2024-04 · garage-door app** — 5.2.1 "content or features from <hardware brand>… without the necessary authorization". Suggested: authorization letter in review notes. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/749400) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-01 · government-council app** — 5.1.1(ix) "must be published under a seller name and company name that reflects the <organization> name"; permission letters not sufficient. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/813228) (high, en) → META-02
- **2024-12 · baccarat simulator, individual account (Japan)** — 5.1.1(ix) simulated gambling not allowed from individual accounts. [source](https://zenn.dev/mixi/articles/8f331315d75b11) (high, ja) → META-03
- **2025-10 · loan app** — 3.2.2(ix) "the loan terms are not included in the app". [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/803869) (high, en) → META-02
- **2024-03 · console-game catalogue** — 3.2.2 "displays apps other than your own for purchase or promotion in a manner similar to the App Store". [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/748929) (high, en) → CONTENT-03
- **2022-04 · location-based discovery app** — 5.1.5 "not appropriate for your app to require the user to provide access to their location". Fix: fallback content when denied, WhenInUse, pre-permission explanation. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/704032) (high, en) → PERM-02
- **2025-12 · fitness tracking app** — 5.1.5 + 5.1.1 "the app is not functional when Location Services are disabled" and "encourages or directs" users to Settings. [source](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/810096) (high, en) → PERM-02
- **2026 · social/discovery app** — 1.1 rejected twice for keywords "random", "anonymous", "strangers" and competitor names → metadata scrub. [source](https://medium.com/@vincent_88233/we-got-rejected-6-times-by-apple-in-45-days-heres-how-we-fixed-it-2b6616977619) (medium, en) → META-02, UGC-07

### 1.5 Developer information (frequent co-rejection)
- Support URL without contact details (2024-12, zenn/mixi); support URL = Twitter (2024-09, Qiita); dead support URL (2023-02, Qiita). Fix each time: a real page/form with contact info. → CONTENT-05, META-04

---

## Google Play

### Photo and Video Permissions (READ_MEDIA_IMAGES / VIDEO)
- **2025-11 · Flutter field-documentation app** — "Photo and Video Permissions policy". Cause: `image_picker`, `file_picker`, `permission_handler` manifests merged `READ_MEDIA_*` in. Fix: `<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.READ_MEDIA_IMAGES" tools:node="remove"/>` (+VIDEO); image_picker ≥1.0 already uses the Photo Picker. Approved within 48 h. [source](https://medium.com/@punitsolanki157/how-to-fix-google-plays-photo-video-permissions-policy-rejection-in-flutter-f1a1b1d3378e) (high, en) → AND-PERM-08, AND-PERM-13
- **2025-06 · Flutter app with file_picker + PDF viewer** — rejection listing `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES`, `READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` "but I never added those". Fix: `tools:node="remove"`, verify in the merged manifest. [source](https://dev.to/alaminkarno/bro-my-app-got-rejected-but-i-didnt-even-add-those-permissions-48o7) (high, en) → AND-PERM-13
- **2025-08 · Flutter app** — "Permission use is not directly related to your app's core purpose… Remove the use of READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/READ_MEDIA_VIDEO permission from all version codes within the submission… consider using the Android photo picker." Flutter closed as not-a-bug. [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/173536) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-08, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2024-11 · Flutter app** — same text; deadline 2025-01-22 / 2025-05-28. [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/158315) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-08
- **2026-04 · established app whose older sibling was approved** — identical text; enforcement tightened, old approvals don't carry over. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/428635251) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-08
- **2026-01 · app rejected after removing the permissions** — rejection cited **old version code 129** while submitting 141: older artifact still active in a track. Lesson: deactivate/replace every older build in every track. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/398424477) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2025-03 · Expo + expo-image-picker** — "requests READ_MEDIA_IMAGES and READ_MEDIA_VIDEO… only allowed for apps that need persistent access". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/330810742) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13
- **2026-02 · Expo blank template** — expo-image-picker adds `READ_MEDIA_*` at prebuild even if unused (fixed in v15.1.0). [source](https://github.com/expo/expo/issues/42819) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13
- **2024-11 · Korean Android app** — declaration demanded. Fix: Android 13+ → PhotoPicker (no permission), ≤12 → legacy READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE; passed first resubmission. [source](https://velog.io/@jeep_chief_14/Play-Store-%EC%82%AC%EC%A7%84-%EB%B0%8F-%EB%8F%99%EC%98%81%EC%83%81-%EA%B6%8C%ED%95%9C-%EC%A0%95%EC%B1%85-%EB%8C%80%EC%9D%91) (high, ko) → AND-PERM-08
- **2024-12 · Japanese chat app (proactive)** — switched to `PickVisualMedia`; redirected file-picker video selection to the Photo Picker. [source](https://zenn.dev/sun_asterisk/articles/d0ca4a297a9c1f) (medium, ja) → AND-PERM-08
- **2025-08 · App Inventor app** — "Action required: Your app is not compliant" for READ_MEDIA_*; since 2025-05-28 no declaration without review. Fix: SAF / Photo Picker. [source](https://community.appinventor.mit.edu/t/action-required-your-app-is-not-compliant-with-google-play-policies/154022) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-08

### Foreground service declarations
- **2024-03 · Japanese utility app** — "権限の使用が申告されていないか、間違って申告されています": declared `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MEDIA_PLAYBACK` but only started a location FGS. Fix: removed the unused type, corrected the Console declaration. Approved 2 days later. [source](https://www.jisei-firm.com/google_play3/) (high, ja) → AND-PERM-05
- **2023-11 · 5G status app (Japan)** — approved first time: step-by-step description of when the FGS starts + unlisted YouTube recording install → FGS start → stop. [source](https://takusan.negitoro.dev/posts/android_foreground_service_permission_play_console/) (high, ja) → AND-PERM-05, AND-CONSOLE-06
- **2024-04 · app using android-upload-service** — "You must let us know whether your app uses any Foreground Service permissions": library adds `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_DATA_SYNC` (manifest merger). [source](https://github.com/gotev/android-upload-service/issues/660) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13
- **2025-04 · RN + Agora SDK via CI/CD** — "Your app contains permissions that require a declaration but were not declared in Play Console" / API error "You must let us know…": Agora upgrade added `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MEDIA_PROJECTION`; Fastlane upload bypasses the Console declaration prompt. Fix: upload the AAB once manually in the Console UI to surface the form. [source](https://dulanwirajith.medium.com/google-play-permissions-declaration-not-showing-heres-how-i-fixed-it-after-a-ci-cd-upload-7f3443224918) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-06, AND-PERM-13
- **2025-07 · Flutter flutter_foreground_task** — "Apps targeting Android 15 or later cannot use BOOT_COMPLETED broadcast receivers to launch certain foreground service types." [source](https://github.com/Dev-hwang/flutter_foreground_task/issues/356) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-05
- **2024-05 · AIR game** — "Play Console declaration and/or app description… does not sufficiently demonstrate the use of permission": generic gameplay video. Lesson: the video must show the FGS feature itself. [source](https://github.com/airsdk/Adobe-Runtime-Support/issues/3273) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-06
- **2024-02 · RN + background-geolocation** — "undeclared foreground service permissions… FOREGROUND_SERVICE_HEALTH FOREGROUND_SERVICE_LOCATION" (plugin adds HEALTH). [source](https://github.com/transistorsoft/react-native-background-geolocation/issues/1953) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13
- **2026-01 · RN background downloader** — declaration: type Data sync, task "Network processing", justification "background file downloads with user notification". [source](https://github.com/kesha-antonov/react-native-background-downloader/issues/134) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-05
- **2025-01 · Jitsi Meet Android** — rejected: `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MEDIA_PROJECTION` permission without `foregroundServiceType` on the service. [source](https://github.com/jitsi/jitsi-meet/issues/15449) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-05
- **2025-09 · Expo expo-video** — adds `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MEDIA_PLAYBACK` even with background playback off → declaration demanded. [source](https://github.com/expo/expo/issues/39988) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13
- **2026-06 · background-geolocation users** — Google: "Effective October 28, 2026, geofencing will no longer be a permitted use case for foreground service location". [source](https://github.com/transistorsoft/react-native-background-geolocation/issues/2621) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-05

### QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES, accessibility, background location, all-files, full-screen intent, exact alarm
- **2024-01 · Flutter app using QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES to open URLs** — "you certainly wont get permission to use that just to open a browser" → url_launcher. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/254251612) (high, en) → AND-PERM-02
- **2023-04 · app rejected after removing QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES** — older builds in internal/closed/open tracks still had it; deactivate tracks and still submit the declaration. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/209706631) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2022-07 · flutter permission_handler 9.0.2** — "If your app requires the QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES permission, you need to submit the declaration form" — transitive from the plugin. [source](https://github.com/Baseflow/flutter-permission-handler/issues/873) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13
- **2024-01 · Flutter cricket-scoring app (overlay + accessibility)** — "Accessibility API Policy: Missing prominent disclosure… we could not locate prominent disclosure of the AccessibilityService API in your app" (disclosure only in the store description). Fix: in-app disclosure + consent before enabling. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/250427714) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-06, PRIV-04
- **2023-11 · app that removed its accessibility service** — still rejected: Console App content still listed the Accessibility declaration. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/244726551) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-06, AND-CONSOLE-06
- **2022-08 · Wikimedia Commons** — "Your declared feature does not meet the requirements for background location access" (transitive permission). [source](https://github.com/commons-app/apps-android-commons/issues/5031) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-04, AND-PERM-13
- **2023-10 · 5G status app background location (Japan)** — rejected twice: privacy policy insufficient; "プライバシーポリシーへのリンクが無効です" because the policy was a Markdown file in a GitHub repo. Fix: host on own domain with app name, data items, purposes, sharing. Approved 3rd time. [source](https://takusan.negitoro.dev/posts/android_background_location_play_console/) (high, ja) → AND-PERM-04, PRIV-01
- **2024-09→2025-05 · Nextcloud Android (MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE)** — permission revoked, "copy-and-paste" appeals; restored after public pressure; ~8 months. [source](https://nextcloud.com/blog/nextcloud-android-file-upload-issue-google/) (high, en) → AND-PERM-03
- **2024 · Syncthing-Android** — MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE rejections; Play requires SAF; app discontinued on Play. [source](https://forum.syncthing.net/t/discontinuing-syncthing-android/23002) (high, en) → AND-PERM-03
- **2024-10 · Flutter app with USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT from a dependency** — "From May 31, 2024, some apps may no longer be eligible to have this permission pre-granted". Fix: `tools:node="remove"`. [source](https://dev.to/junian/removing-full-screen-intent-permission-from-flutter-android-12a3) (high, en) → AND-PERM-10, AND-PERM-13
- **2024-01 · .NET MAUI + Plugin.LocalNotification** — "Your app schedules exact alarms without checking whether the SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM permission has been granted." Fix: check/request before scheduling. [source](https://github.com/thudugala/Plugin.LocalNotification/issues/465) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-10

### SMS / Call Log
- **2024-05 · replacement dialer** — "Default Phone Handler" use case not accepted; 3+ identical rejections. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/279935655) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-01
- **2024-08 · Indian banking app (SIM binding)** — SEND_SMS + call log rejected 5 times: "Read call logs is not allowed under the financial transactions exception". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/288449665) (high, en) → AND-PERM-01

### User Data: privacy policy, Data safety, prominent disclosure
- **2024-03 · app uploading contacts + images** — "APK REQUIRES PROMINENT DISCLOSURE… uploading users' Contact List, Image information without a prominent disclosure" (disclosure only in the policy text). Fix: in-app dialog before the permission naming both data types + consent + matching Data safety. 8–10 builds rejected. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/262599589) (high, en) → PRIV-04
- **2024-04 · Wikimedia Commons** — "Invalid Data safety form. We detected user data transmitted off device that you have not disclosed… Approximate Location". Fix: declare (incl. SDK collection) or remove. [source](https://github.com/commons-app/apps-android-commons/issues/5708) (high, en) → PRIV-02, AND-CONSOLE-02
- **2023-12 · preschool app** — "Invalid privacy policy": Blogspot hosting, missing elements, JS-required page. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/249431313) (medium, en) → PRIV-01
- **2023-04 · app with invalid privacy URL** — "you must link to a privacy policy on your app's store listing page and within your app"; URL must be active, public, non-geofenced, non-PDF, non-editable. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/211125896) (medium, en) → PRIV-01
- **2023–2024 · Japanese indie apps** — repeated privacy-policy rejections until: policy text, hosted on a fixed page (an editable WordPress *post* was flagged as "変更可能"), URL in Console, in-app access. [1](https://zenn.dev/zmsoft/articles/799a6b0946480e) [2](https://dev.to/zmsoft/must-read-for-new-developers-5-minute-playstore-rejections-3ghd) (medium, ja/en) → PRIV-01
- **2023 · Korean government-funded app** — inadequate privacy policy + no account deletion + copyrighted images; approved on 3rd. [source](https://geekdive-corp.com/column/playstore-reject-resaon) (medium, ko) → PRIV-01, ACC-02, META-02

### Account deletion
- **2024-04 · app with deletion URL on own server** — "Invalid account / data deletion link on your Data safety form": the URL's TLS/server config failed for non-browser clients (Google's checker). Lesson: plain HTTPS reachable by any client. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/269694457) (medium, en) → ACC-02, AND-CONSOLE-08
- **2023-09 · Kakao-login apps (Korea)** — Play requires a web deletion link; developers couldn't delete Kakao users on external requests. Kakao: call the Unlink API when deleting. [source](https://devtalk.kakao.com/t/topic/131608) (medium, ko) → ACC-02

### UGC
- **2022-09 · app embedding a Discourse community** — UGC: apps "MUST have… an in-app function that allows users to report/flag other users…; report/flag potential violating content…; remove/block abusive users". Fixes: user-report entry point, post flagging at trust level 0, mute/ignore documented with video, mandatory ToS acceptance at signup, guidelines defining objectionable content. 3 submissions + 2 appeals, ~18 days. [source](https://meta.discourse.org/t/discourse-google-play-user-generated-content-policy-violation/238395) (high, en) → UGC-01, UGC-03, UGC-04
- **2022-05 · Korean community app (SNS-only login)** — 3 Android rejections: test account needed (Kakao/Naver/Apple login only) → personal Naver account with overseas login; "사용자에 대한 신고도 추가" (only post reporting existed) → user reporting; terms must define offensive content → expanded ToS. [source](https://velog.io/@rkd028/iOSAndroid-%EC%BB%A4%EB%AE%A4%EB%8B%88%ED%8B%B0-%EC%95%B1-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9DReject-%EC%82%AC%EC%9C%A0-%EC%A0%95%EB%A6%AC-%EB%B0%8F-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC-%ED%9B%84%EA%B8%B0) (high, ko) → ACC-03, UGC-03, UGC-01
- **2024-07 · app warned under UGC policy** — shipped report content, report user, block/hide; "no need to appeal – just uploading a new corrected app bundle will resolve this". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/286502099) (medium, en) → UGC-03, UGC-04

### Sign-in details / Broken functionality
- **2025-01 · app rejected repeatedly for credentials, then suspended** — "provided login credentials did not allow access to in-app content" / "Repeated app rejections". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/thread/316454907) (medium, en) → ACC-03, AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2024-05 · app with a 0.5 s coin loading screen** — Broken Functionality "App installs, but doesn't load" → appeal → suspension. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/276810360) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-07 · existing app declined for a crash in a previous release** — workaround: roll out to 1% production then test track, then 100%. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/285252426) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2024-04 · Flutter book-card app** — "contains a button that is not responsive or leads to a blank page… notify users that the button/feature may return no results" (empty state read as broken). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/266937860) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-10 · game** — "We don't allow apps that crash, force close, freeze… your app installs, but doesn't load." [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/304500301) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01

### Metadata / misleading claims
- **2022-01 · Dominion card game (HN)** — "The app title or description does not accurately describe the app's functionality": the line "▪ Tutorial & Rules". Fix: "▪ In app Tutorial & Rules". 10+ releases. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=33361311) (high, en) → META-02
- **2022-01 · Catima loyalty cards** — Spanish title containing "libre" treated as the banned word "free" + title length; ~11 months. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=29967404) (high, en) → META-02, AND-META-01
- **2022-09 · Chinese OEM multi-language app** — "应用名称或说明未准确反映应用的功能": wrong Spanish-locale name. [source](https://www.tuyaos.com/viewtopic.php?p=444) (high, zh) → META-02
- **2024-05 · karate-club app** — Metadata: description about the organisation, not the app's features. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/276173808) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2024 · Japanese "BearRepellent" app** — "誤解を招く記載" (name promised an unverifiable safety outcome) → renamed "BearBell". [source](https://zenn.dev/zmsoft/articles/799a6b0946480e) (high, ja) → META-02
- **2025-10 · portfolio of AdMob utility apps** — "Metadata Policy Violation" then account termination. [source](https://community.appinventor.mit.edu/t/google-play-store-app-rejection-metadata-policy-violation-issue/157424) (medium, en) → META-02, CONTENT-07

### Ads, subscriptions, payments
- **2023-11 · app with interstitials + click-triggered ads** — "Code that implements ads on button clicks, which interferes with the normal use of the application"; removing interstitials wasn't enough. ≥4 submissions. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/243265770) (high, en) → ADS-02
- **2024-11 · subscription app (solved)** — "Details about the subscription offer are missing… when a free trial will convert… how much… that a user can cancel". Fix: simplified paywall keeping price, cycle, conversion date, cancel info. ~5 weeks. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/307673785) (high, en) → PAY-02
- **2024-04 · three-tier app with no trial** — same trial-terms text although no trial existed. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/269654938) (medium, en) → PAY-02
- **2022-07 · KakaoTalk (Korea)** — Play updates halted over an in-app link to external web payment; Kakao withdrew the link. [source](https://techcrunch.com/2022/07/14/kakao-confirms-withdrawal-external-payment-option-after-standoff-with-google) (medium, en) → PAY-01, AND-PAY-01

### Health Connect / Financial features / VPN declarations
- **2025-12→2026-02 · fitness-challenge app** — "Excessive data access for declared feature"; 6 rejections. Fix: description listing each data type and use; Health declaration updated; policy URLs made public. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/398224811) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, META-04
- **2025-09 · research app** — "Health Connect by Android Permissions policy: Inappropriate Health Connect Access Requested… only request access to permissions that are critical". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/374207829) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2025-08 · health apps re-rejected with no code change** — "Insufficient justification for requested permission(s)"; fix: rewrite the **full** store description to explain Health Connect usage. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/363615739) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2024-03 · licensed microfinance app** — "The Financial features declaration… is inaccurate"; ~4 weeks. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/264875599) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2025-09 · loan/SIP calculator** — "Violation of Financial Services policy… declaration… is inaccurate" (declared none). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/370548306) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **JA · BNPL feature** — treated as a personal loan; interest rate, term, total cost in description + declaration form → approved. [source](https://pentagon.tokyo/app/1285/) (medium, ja) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2023-09 / 2026-04 / 2024-03 · VpnService apps** — "We are unable to confirm your app's declared use of VpnService as a permitted use case" / "should be removed from the app manifest across all release tracks". [1](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/235925715) [2](https://www.qubetics.com/blog/android-dvpn-build-status-why-google-play-keeps-rejecting-it/) [3](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/265472632) (high/medium, en) → AND-PERM-14

### Closed testing, malware false positives, verification, target API, 16 KB
- **2024-11 · Japanese indie developer** — "クローズドテスト中にテスターがあなたのアプリに関与しなかった": 12 acquaintances opted in but never used the app. [source](https://coconala.com/blogs/5010983/501293) (high, ja) → AND-TEST-01
- **2025-02 · Android closed-test community (Japan)** — approvals came with ≥3-line answers per question, crash-free statement, concrete feedback, free exploration by testers. [source](https://zenn.dev/android_tester/articles/510e99718de271) (medium, ja) → AND-TEST-01
- **2024-07 · hybrid-casual game** — "MALWARE, BEHAVIOR TRANSPARENCY AND MOBILE UNWANTED SOFTWARE": Firebase Analytics endpoint IP listed as malicious by a threat feed; account terminated; restored after ~1.5 months. [source](https://medium.com/@blackboxdigitalkz/google-terminated-our-old-google-play-developer-account-due-to-a-false-positive-reason-42191a404b74) (high, en) → AND-QUALITY-02
- **2024-03 · betting-tips app** — "trojan code that appears to be benign" (play-services-ads-lite suspected); reinstated after 2nd appeal. [source](https://medium.com/@jnma.droidydev/google-play-malware-policy-violation-51ed05e98fa0) (high, en) → AND-QUALITY-02
- **2024-08 · idle RPG (HN)** — "Malware or Deceptive Behavior / changing device settings without user consent": `PowerManager.WakeLock` keeping the screen on; re-upload → permanent ban. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=41332537) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02
- **2023-10 · 7-year Unity account** — terminated (Unity asset plugin suspected); restored after 16 days. [source](https://medium.com/@raffaelet/my-seven-years-old-google-play-developer-account-has-been-terminated-ed321754cd51) (high, en) → AND-QUALITY-02
- **2024-02 · Flutter + GA, AD_ID declaration mismatch** — publish blocked despite AD_ID in manifests; unblocked via an empty internal-testing release choosing "release without permission". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/256980426) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-04
- **2024-07 · target API warning after updating to 34** — older builds in test tracks kept the warning. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/285527282) (medium, en) → AND-SDK-01, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2025-10 · Flutter 16 KB** — "now requires a 16 KB page size": Flutter 3.10 plugins with 4 KB .so. Fix: Flutter 3.35.5, plugin upgrades, minSdk 24. [source](https://www.junian.dev/tech/flutter-android-16kb-page-size/) (high, en) → AND-SDK-02
- **2025-12 · D-U-N-S / identity verification (vendor guide)** — "organization name on the uploaded document does not match… DUNS Profile" / "Google Couldn't Verify Your Identity". Fix: align D&B profile, payments profile. [source](https://support.subsplash.com/en/articles/10228303-troubleshooting-rejected-google-play-console-verification) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-01
- **2023-03 · kids education app** — Families: SDKs transmitting device identifiers flagged though the app collects nothing (embedded YouTube suspected). [source](https://community.appinventor.mit.edu/t/mit-appinventor-solely-targeted-for-children-approved-by-google-family-policy/79855) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07

---

## Cases from Korean-language sources (2nd pass, 2026-08-28)

Sources: velog, tistory, Naver blogs, Kakao DevTalk, vendor roundups (Swing2App — marked medium because they aggregate client cases). Two developer "fixes" below are anti-patterns and are flagged as such (`NSAllowsArbitraryLoads=YES`, `usesCleartextTraffic="true"` — use HTTPS instead).

### Apple — 1.2 UGC
- **2026-05 · Flutter travel-companion community, Naver-only login** — 4.8 → 1.2 → 1.2 → 2.1, four rounds: SIWA required alongside Naver; block missing (only report existed); no EULA before login; "empty data" made features unverifiable. Fix: SIWA + optional profile step for gender/age; block hides profile/posts; pre-login EULA with community guidelines; seeded sample posts/chats. [source](https://velog.io/@imkara/%EB%AA%A8%ED%96%89-8.-iOS-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC) (high, ko) → IOS-LOGIN-01, IOS-LOGIN-04, UGC-04, UGC-01, UGC-06, ACC-03
- **2026-01 · identity-journal platform (video/image/text posts, AI-generated code)** — 1.2 + 5.1.1(v) "does not have all the required precautions" / "does not include an option to initiate account deletion". Fix: report button under each post → report or block user. [source](https://blog.naver.com/flyhams/224166176718) (medium, ko) → UGC-03, UGC-04, UGC-01, ACC-02
- **2024-05 · chat/meeting app** — 2.1 then 1.2 twice. R1 login button did nothing — backend EC2 had no IPv6 → assigned IPv6. R2 user/meeting reporting + 24 h monitoring → still rejected; R3 separate **block** feature (blocked user's content hidden permanently) → approved. 3 rounds / 7 days. [source](https://velog.io/@swoolee97/%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC-%EA%B1%B0%EC%A0%88-%EB%8C%80%EC%9D%91%EA%B8%B0) (high, ko) → CONTENT-09, UGC-03, UGC-04, UGC-05
- **2024-05 · university community app** — 1.2 + 1.5 + 2.3.8 + 5.1.2. "사용자가 약관(EULA)에 동의해야 하며 이러한 약관에서는 불쾌한 콘텐츠나 악의적인 사용자에 대한 허용이 없음을 분명히 해야 합니다"; 2.3.8 "앱 마켓플레이스에 표시되는 앱 이름과 기기에 표시되는 앱 이름이 충분히 일치하지 않아" (long store name vs short device name). Fix: report API, auto-ban after 3 reports, block, 24 h commitment; support Notion page with contacts. [source](https://velog.io/@dlrjswns135/iOS-%EC%95%B1-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%EC%82%AC%EC%9C%A0-%ED%95%B4%EA%B2%B0%EB%AA%A9%EB%A1%9D) (high, ko) → UGC-01, UGC-03, UGC-04, UGC-05, CONTENT-05, META-06
- **2024-07 · app with blocking but no content reporting** — 1.2 "A mechanism for users to flag objectionable content". Fix: per-item report with multiple reasons → approved. [source](https://sseymorr.github.io/ios/solutions/app-store-rejected-safety/) (high, ko) → UGC-03
- **2023-06 · app with posting** — 1.2 twice. Report alert promised a **5-day** response, no filter explanation, no contact → "Still does not have all the required precautions." Fix: 24 h SLA text, "3+ reports → cannot post", Contact Us in settings, composer placeholder warning about sanctions, video of the report flow → approved. [source](https://hyggeinx.tistory.com/102) (high, ko) → UGC-05, UGC-02, UGC-06, CONTENT-05
- **2022-08 · app with posts** — 1.2 → report/block. [source](https://jineyne.github.io/blog/user-generated-content/) (medium, ko) → UGC-03, UGC-04

### Apple — 2.1 completeness / demo account / IPv6 / iPad
- **2026-06 · study timer with friend cheering (Flutter, first release)** — 2.1(a) error on launch on the review device: fresh install + Release config differed from the dev phone (initial auth setup). Fix: fixed Release config, re-tested delete → install → sign-up → login → core → relaunch; 4-line reply. Approved. [source](https://blog.naver.com/rose10816/224331752861) (high, ko) → CONTENT-12, CONTENT-01
- **2026-05 · Flutter Pomodoro timer** — 2.1 Information Needed: reviewer couldn't tell how to use the app (first screen is settings); App Review Information was blank. Fix: 3–5 step Notes + simulator recording → approved 4 h after resubmit. [source](https://blog.naver.com/ssamssae/224279616939) (high, ko) → META-05, IOS-META-02
- **2026-01 · team app on a free DuckDNS domain** — 2.1 ×5: rounds 3–5 the reviewers couldn't reach the server — free dynamic-DNS domain unreachable from Apple's IPv6-only network. Fix: ₩500 paid domain with IPv6 → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@ayeon0/ipv6-%EC%A3%BC%EC%86%8C%EA%B0%80-%EB%B6%88%EB%9F%AC%EC%9D%BC%EC%9C%BC%EC%BC%B0%EB%8D%98-ios-%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-5%EC%97%B0%EC%86%8D-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%EC%82%AC%EA%B1%B4) (high, ko) → CONTENT-09
- **2025-12 · hardware-paired app** — 2.1 "We need a demo video that shows a physical Apple device and the designated hardware pairing together" + 5.1.1 "The app encourages or directs users to allow the app to access the camera" (custom pre-prompt with exit button + Settings redirect). Fix: demo video; removed exit buttons/redirects. [source](https://velog.io/@nuegnoseel/%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-2.1-5.1.1-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%ED%95%B4%EA%B2%B0-%EA%B3%BC%EC%A0%95) (medium, ko) → META-05, PERM-02
- **2025-10 · Flutter iPhone-only app with PASS/SMS carrier authentication** — 2.1 ×3 + 4.0, all on iPad Air: "Unable to proceed with PASS authentication after selecting a carrier option" (iPad has no SMS); error on launch; "UI crowded". `UIDeviceFamily=1`, removing iPad screenshots/orientations didn't stop iPad review; only `UIRequiredDeviceCapabilities: telephony` made the app uninstallable on iPad → approved. 4 rounds. [source](https://velog.io/@rhdrnsckdgh0921/iPad-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-4%EC%97%B0%ED%83%80-%ED%9B%84-%ED%86%B5%EA%B3%BC%ED%95%98%EB%8B%A4..-iPhone-%EC%A0%84%EC%9A%A9-%EC%95%B1-%EC%84%A4%EC%A0%95-%EC%82%BD%EC%A7%88%EA%B8%B0) (high, ko) → IOS-IPAD-02, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2025-09 · facility-inspection app reviewed on iPad Air 11" M2 (5-post series)** — (a) 2.1 error on login: ATS -1022 "requires the use of secure connection" (HTTP API) — developer set `NSAllowsArbitraryLoads=YES` (anti-pattern; use HTTPS) [1](https://velog.io/@money/%EB%A1%9C%EA%B7%B8%EC%9D%B8-%EC%98%A4%EB%A5%98); (b) "the majority of the buttons were unresponsive when tapped" → unreproducible → video → approved [2](https://velog.io/@money/%EB%B2%84%ED%8A%BC-%ED%81%B4%EB%A6%AD%EC%9D%B4-%EC%95%88%EB%90%A8); (c) 2.1 "it appears your app may access or include paid digital content or services" → answered Apple's IAP questions [3](https://velog.io/@money/%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D%EC%97%90-%EB%8B%B5%EB%B3%80); (d) "unable to locate the App Tracking Transparency permission request when reviewed on iPadOS 18.5" → implemented ATT, removed unused permission keys [4](https://velog.io/@money/%EC%95%B1-%EA%B6%8C%ED%95%9C); (e) 5.1.1(v) account deletion [5](https://velog.io/@money/%ED%9A%8C%EC%9B%90%ED%83%88%ED%87%B4-524s5b0x) (high, ko) → IOS-PLIST-04, IOS-IPAD-01, META-05, PAY-01, PRIV-03, IOS-ENT-04, ACC-02
- **2025-08 · location-based app** — 5.1.1 ×3 + 2.1 ×2: mandatory birthday → optional; location purpose unclear → plist string + explanation; forced Settings navigation when denied → usable without location; "Show the app running on a physical iOS device, not on a simulator"; phone-auth credentials → fixed test number + code. [source](https://velog.io/@chansolshin/%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%EC%82%AC%EC%9C%A0-%ED%9A%8C%EA%B3%A0) (high, ko) → ACC-01, PERM-01, PERM-02, META-05, ACC-03
- **2025-07 · SwiftUI app with GitHub + Apple login** — 2.1 "The demo account provided could not be logged into" (GitHub 2FA; new-device challenge also on the Google recovery account). Fix: requested a phone call with App Review (held in Korean) → approved. ~1.5 months. [source](https://velog.io/@minzzzun/%EC%95%A0%ED%94%8C%EA%B3%BC-%ED%86%B5%ED%99%94%ED%95%9C-%EC%8D%B0%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%ED%95%B4%EA%B2%B0) (high, ko) → ACC-03
- **2025-02 · friend-alarm app (FCM, phone verification, group codes)** — 4 rounds: "앱이 기업 및 비즈니스 내에서 내부적으로 사용하는 앱인 경우 다른 경로로 배포해주세요. 가입은 되지만, 탈퇴 기능이 없습니다." (deletion existed but hidden → pointed to it); reused test account's stale FCM token + single-use phone verification blocked re-login → fresh account, token at sign-up; push-permission placement. [source](https://velog.io/@hisehoonan/%EC%9C%97%EB%AA%A8%EB%8B%9D-%EC%B5%9C%EC%B4%88-%EB%B2%84%EC%A0%84-%EC%9A%B0%EB%8B%B9%ED%83%95%ED%83%95-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D%EC%9D%98-%EB%B0%98%EB%B3%B5%EA%B3%BC-%EC%B6%9C%EC%8B%9C) (high, ko) → ACC-02, ACC-03, PUSH-01, CONTENT-03
- **2024-11 · Flutter/Firebase app** — after account deletion + re-sign-up with Apple, login stuck: redirect ran before the Firestore user doc was written. Fix: save user → refresh provider → redirect. [source](https://blog.naver.com/kangsudal-dev/223666806071) (high, ko) → ACC-02, CONTENT-01
- **2024-10 · Bluetooth-accessory app** — 2.1 can't test without hardware → unlisted YouTube demo → resolved. [source](https://velog.io/@yuiop1029/iOS-Guideline-2.1-Information-Needed) (high, ko) → META-05
- **2024-09 · app with own backend** — 3 rounds / 4 days: support URL invalid → Google Form; "data not loading" → Release config lacked host/port (only in Debug). [source](https://velog.io/@kangciu/%ED%97%98%EB%82%9C%ED%96%88%EB%8D%98-%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-4%EC%9D%BC%EA%B0%84%EC%9D%98-%EC%97%AC%EC%A0%95) (high, ko) → CONTENT-05, CONTENT-12
- **2024-09 · RN WebView app** — 4 rejections over months: dev server unreachable from overseas; broken scrolling; no account deletion; dummy privacy-policy text at sign-up. [source](https://velog.io/@selvan/%EC%95%A0%ED%94%8C-%EC%95%B1%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%B0%98%EB%A0%A4%EC%8B%9C-%EB%8C%80%EC%9D%91-%EB%B0%A9%EB%B2%95) (high, ko) → CONTENT-01, ACC-02, PRIV-01
- **2024-09 · Flutter app, first release** — 5 submissions / ~1 month: 1.5 support URL = GitHub → Notion with email; 2.3.3 screenshots with test ads / Android frames; 2.5.10 "your app or its screenshots include test advertisements"; 5.1.1(ii) tracking rejected **three times** → `app_tracking_transparency` with a 1 s delay; 4.8 → Sign in with Apple. [source](https://blog.naver.com/distance_from/223586150652) (high, ko) → CONTENT-05, META-01, ADS-03, PRIV-03, IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2024-03 · RN hardware-companion app** — 2.1 "we need access to a video that shows… the initial pairing process; the entire app workflow with the designated hardware" → complete pairing video. [source](https://velog.io/@kwb020312/%EC%95%8C%EA%B2%8C%EB%90%9C-%EA%B2%83-%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC-Guideline-2.1-Information-Needed) (high, ko) → META-05
- **2023-10 · Flutter lottery QR app** — 2.1 reviewers asked for sample QR codes → notes + sample files. [source](https://aprilslab.github.io/posts/logs/appstore/rejected/000/) (medium, ko) → META-05
- **2023-08 · Storyboard app** — 2.1 crash on launch in the US-region review: Korean not declared in Info > Localizations. [source](https://velog.io/@rayoung/ios-%EC%95%B1-reject-%EC%82%AC%EC%9C%A0-App-completeness) (high, ko) → CONTENT-13
- **2023-05 · toilet-finder app (three posts)** — 2.1 crash on tapping current-location without permission → permission check [1](https://velog.io/@gnunee/%EA%B8%89%EB%98%A5-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-Guideline-2.1-Performance-App-Completeness); 5.1.1 "doesn't sufficiently explain the use of the location in the purpose string" [2](https://velog.io/@gnunee/%EA%B8%89%EB%98%A5-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-Guideline-5.1.1-Legal-Privacy-Data-Collection-and-Storage); 1.5 support URL with unencoded Korean characters → Notion [3](https://velog.io/@gnunee/Guideline-1.5-Safety-Developer-Information) (high, ko) → PERM-02, PERM-01, CONTENT-05
- **2023-04 · RN app** — 2.1 "crashed during review… tapped 'video'": missing `NSMicrophoneUsageDescription` for video capture. [source](https://velog.io/@2da1/ios-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%EC%95%B1-%EC%B6%A9%EB%8F%8C-%EB%B9%84%EB%94%94%EC%98%A4) (high, ko) → IOS-PLIST-01
- **2022-12 · Flutter app** — 2.1 "crashed on launch": `CFBundleDevelopmentRegion = $(DEVELOPMENT_LANGUAGE)` → exception on non-Korean-region devices. Fix: `ko_KR`. ~2 weeks. [source](https://velog.io/@pplm1042/iOS-%EC%95%B1%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%ED%95%B4%EA%B2%B0-Guideline-2.1-Performance-App-Completeness) (high, ko); same fix for an RN app 2024-05 [source](https://velog.io/@rlawnsduq311/%EC%95%B1-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC-4-) → CONTENT-13
- **2022-12 · RN phone-bill analysis app** — 8 issues: 2.1 "Does your app upload the contacts to your server?" → purpose string says so; 2.2 "appears to be a pre-release" → removed "서비스 준비중" modals; 4.5.4 push consent must be optional → decline button; purpose strings with examples; 5.1.2 contacts precautions; 2.3.3 iPad screenshots in iPhone frames; 2.3.10 Android references; demo video. [source](https://velog.io/@acwell94/React-Native-IOS-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EA%B1%B0%EC%A0%88-%ED%95%B4%EA%B2%B0-%EA%B3%BC%EC%A0%95) (high, ko) → PERM-01, PRIV-04, CONTENT-01, PUSH-01, IOS-META-01, CONTENT-04, META-05
- **2022-09 · kids mental-math app (Firebase)** — 2.1 "Does your app contain third-party analytics/advertising? Is data shared?" [1](https://velog.io/@bargoloft/%EB%91%90%EC%88%98%EC%95%9E-4.-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-Guideline-2.1-Information-Needed); 2.3.7 screenshot text "모든 기능은 무료입니다" = price mention → removed [2](https://velog.io/@bargoloft/%EB%91%90%EC%88%98%EC%95%9E-5.-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D2-Guideline-2.3.7-Performance-Accurate-Metadata) (high, ko) → PRIV-02, META-02
- **2022-06 · ranking app ported from web** — 4.2.2 + 2.1 "How do we login in your app?" / error alert questioned → clarified no login, replaced alert with an input page; rival-challenge feature + onboarding → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@rlawns1235/App-Store-Reject-%EB%8C%80%EC%9D%91%EA%B8%B0) (high, ko) → CONTENT-03, CONTENT-01
- **2022-02 · AI camera app with AdMob** — 2.1.0 demo video on a physical device; AdMob test ID → production ID; screenshot of live ads → approved in 2 days. [source](https://velog.io/@heyksw/iOS-%EC%95%B1-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%ED%95%B4%EA%B2%B0-2.1.0) (high, ko) → META-05, ADS-03

### Apple — 2.3 metadata / age rating / screenshots / support URL
- **2025-01→02 · reading/detox app** — ATT "사용자를 추적하는 데 사용할 수 있는 데이터가 수집되기 전의 요청을 표시해야" (prompt after sign-up → at launch); 2.3.3 "스크린샷은 … 실제 사용 중인 앱을 보여주지 않습니다" (localization mismatch after switching country); 4.0 "앱은 기기 화면 크기나 해상도를 지원하도록 최적화되지 않았습니다" → iPad layout. [1](https://velog.io/@strongbunny/%EC%9E%90%EC%9E%91%EC%95%B1-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC%EC%9D%BC%EC%A7%801-%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D) [2](https://velog.io/@strongbunny/%EC%9E%90%EC%9E%91%EC%95%B1-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC%EC%9D%BC%EC%A7%802-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC%ED%86%B5%EA%B3%BC) (high, ko) → PRIV-03, META-01, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2025-01 · content app embedding YouTube in a WebView** — age rating forced to 12+ (unrestricted web). Fix: open YouTube externally → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@cheshire0105/%EB%82%98%EC%9D%98-%EC%95%A0%ED%94%8C-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%EC%97%B0%EB%A0%B9%EB%B3%84) (high, ko) → META-03, IOS-META-06
- **2022-11 · TOEIC vocabulary app** — 1.5 support URL was a default Apache page; 2.3.7 subtitle "광고 없는 무료 토익 단어장" = price reference → renamed. [source](https://velog.io/@soo2283/iOS-App-Store-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-1) (high, ko) → CONTENT-05, META-02
- **2023-05 · RN WebView news aggregator** — 11 submissions: 2.1 no back button; 2.3.10 "Your screenshots includes non-iOS device images."; 4.2.2 ×9 until "Please add functionality such as: saving, favorites and sharing functionality." → bookmarks → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@nan8420/Apple-App-Store-%EC%B6%9C%EC%8B%9C) (high, ko) → CONTENT-01, CONTENT-04, CONTENT-03
- **2026-06 · RN baseball community app** — copycat/IP flag on team logos in screenshots → redesign; leftover data-collection permission config; crash on iPad (app.json iPhone-only not enforced by prebuild) [1](https://velog.io/@khaneul1/BETA-%EC%95%A0%ED%94%8C-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%EB%8C%80%EC%9D%91); sign-up crash from state-persist hydration racing navigation reset [2](https://velog.io/@khaneul1/Reject-Fix-1-%ED%9A%8C%EC%9B%90%EA%B0%80%EC%9E%85-%ED%81%AC%EB%9E%98%EC%8B%9C-%EB%B0%8F-%EB%AC%B4%ED%95%9C-reset) (high, ko) → META-02, IOS-ENT-04, IOS-IPAD-01, CONTENT-01
- **2025-09 · Flutter app with Kakao/Google/Apple login** — login wall before core features → guest; mandatory age/region/gender → optional; iPhone screenshots used for iPad → real iPad captures. [source](https://velog.io/@keepdev0919/iOS-%EC%95%B1-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%EB%8D%95%EB%B6%84%EC%97%90-%EB%B8%8C%EB%9E%9C%EC%B9%98-%EC%83%88%EB%A1%9C%ED%8C%8C%EC%84%9C-%EC%9E%91%EC%97%85%ED%95%B4%EB%B4%84) (medium, ko) → ACC-01, PRIV-04, IOS-META-01
- **2023-04 · app** — 1.5 "No support contact was found." [source](https://velog.io/@emprore/ComeOn-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC%EA%B8%B0) (medium, ko) → CONTENT-05
- **2023-06 · app with a Notion support URL** — 1.5 rejected although the link worked → video proof + email on the page. [source](https://hyggeinx.tistory.com/103) (high, ko) → CONTENT-05

### Apple — 2.5 background / SDK binaries
- **2025-09 · SwiftUI timer app** — legacy `audio` background mode without an audio session → removed; iPad support left on with iPhone-only screenshots → disabled iPad → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@data_sy/AI%EB%A5%BC-%ED%99%9C%EC%9A%A9%ED%95%9C-%ED%83%80%EC%9D%B4%EB%A8%B8-%EC%95%B1-%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%EA%B8%B0-%EC%A3%BC%EA%B0%84-%ED%9A%8C%EA%B3%A0-9) (high, ko) → IOS-BG-01, IOS-PLIST-02, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-03 · app using Kakao Navi KNSDK-UI 1.12.16 (Xcode 26.2)** — ITMS-90512 "Invalid SDK value" (bundled Realm built with SDK 26.4) + ITMS-91065 "Missing signature… RealmSwift". Re-signing destroys the signature. Fix: KNSDK-UI 1.12.12. [source](https://devtalk.kakao.com/t/topic/148802) (high, ko) → IOS-PRIV-03, IOS-META-04
- **2023-08 · ARKit/TrueDepth app** — rejected over TrueDepth usage → removed face tracking. [source](https://velog.io/@mystic/ARKit%EA%B4%80%EB%A0%A8-%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D%EC%82%AC%EC%9C%A0) (medium, ko) → IOS-ENT-04

### Apple — 3.1 payments / subscriptions
- **2026-08 · subscription app built with an AI coding tool (sole proprietor)** — IAP rejected 3–4 times while the developer kept "fixing" subscription groups. Real cause: **Paid Apps Agreement unsigned, tax/bank info missing** (사업자등록번호 required since 2024-12); missing subscription review screenshot. [1](https://blog.naver.com/purmil7/224373734912) [2](https://blog.naver.com/purmil7/224373980599) (high, ko) → PAY-04, IOS-IAP-01, KR-02
- **2023-08 · subscription app** — 3.1.2 EULA link added in-app only → "the EULA link is missing in metadata(in app description)" ×3 → link in the description **and a new build** (Resolution Center replies don't update metadata) → approved. 5 rounds / ~24 h. [source](https://velog.io/@chaentopia/Apple-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EC%9D%B8%EC%95%B1%EA%B2%B0%EC%A0%9C-%EA%B5%AC%EB%8F%85-EULA-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D) (high, ko) → PAY-02
- **2023-04 · auto-renewable subscription app** — Schedule 2 §3.8(b) missing Terms of Use link → description link or custom EULA in ASC. [source](https://velog.io/@ssw0220/%EC%95%A0%ED%94%8C-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-specified-in-Schedule-2-section-3.8b-of-the-Paid-Applications-agreement) (medium, ko) → PAY-02

### Apple — 4.0 / 4.2 / 4.3
- **2023-06 · UIKit app opening Safari for sign-up/deletion** — 4.0 "the user is taken to the default web browser to sign in or register for an account, which provides a poor user experience." → `SFSafariViewController`. [source](https://hyggeinx.tistory.com/105) (high, ko) → IOS-UI-01, ACC-02
- **2026-01 · RN number-baseball mini game** — 4.3(a) concept too similar → per-game statistics, history/analytics, explanatory letter → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@seongnam-si/App-Store-4.3a-Design-Spam-%EB%8C%80%EC%9D%91%EA%B8%B0) (high, ko) → CONTENT-07
- **2025-09 · fortune-telling lottery-number app** — 4.3(b) duplicate/saturated; appeal with differentiation video denied; removing fortune features still rejected; 4th detailed resubmission approved. ~1 week. [source](https://velog.io/@gomhyeok/AppStore-%EA%B1%B0%EC%A0%88-4.3b-Design-Spam) (high, ko) → CONTENT-07, META-05
- **2023-11 · dating app with AI caricatures** — 4.3.0 "Your app primarily features dating features… duplicates the content and functionality of many other similar apps" / appeal: "we simply have enough of these types of apps". Fix: repositioned around AI/caricature, new icon, intro, UI → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@oasis444/4.3.0-Design-Spam-%EB%8C%80%EC%B2%98-%EB%B0%A9%EB%B2%95) (high, ko) → CONTENT-07
- **2026-02 · astrology/fortune WebView apps (vendor case)** — 4.3(b) "Your app primarily features astrology, horoscopes, palm reading, fortune telling or zodiac reports that duplicate the content and functionality of similar apps in a saturated category. There are already enough of these apps on the App Store." → "Reconsider your app concept and submit a new app that provides a unique experience." [source](https://blog.naver.com/swing2app/224187879458) (medium, ko) → CONTENT-07

### Apple — 4.8 Sign in with Apple / Kakao login during review
- **2026-04 · Flutter + Supabase, Kakao-only login** — 4.8 "이 앱은 타사 로그인 서비스를 사용하지만, 다음과 같은 모든 기능을 갖춘 다른 로그인 옵션을 제공하지 않는 것으로 보입니다." Fix: SIWA with nonce, official button, Supabase Apple provider → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@honey_mandarin/App-Store-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%ED%9B%84%EA%B8%B0-%EA%B0%80%EC%9D%B4%EB%93%9C%EB%9D%BC%EC%9D%B8-4.8-%EB%A1%9C%EA%B7%B8%EC%9D%B8) (high, ko) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2025-11 · Kakao-login app (DevTalk)** — 4.8 "does not appear to offer as an equivalent login option another login service with all of the following features"; Kakao: add Apple Sign-In. [source](https://devtalk.kakao.com/t/topic/147345) (high, ko) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2024-05 · RN app** — support URL broken; "계정 삭제 옵션이 제공되지 않았음"; "개인 정보 수집에 대한 설명이 부족함"; "제3자 로그인 서비스가 요구 사항을 충족시키지 못함" → Apple Sign-In via Firebase [1](https://velog.io/@rlawnsduq311/%EC%95%B1-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%EC%82%AC%EC%9C%A0); then 5.1.1 consent/purpose → detailed plist strings [2](https://velog.io/@rlawnsduq311/%EC%95%B1-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D%EC%82%AC%EC%9C%A0-3-) (high, ko) → CONTENT-05, ACC-02, PRIV-01, IOS-LOGIN-01, PERM-01
- **2024-03 · WebView apps with Kakao/Google login (vendor case)** — 4.8 "Your app uses a third-party login service, but does not offer Sign in with Apple…" Fix: Apple login on the website, or hide social logins during review (re-hide on every update). [source](https://blog.naver.com/swing2app/223376305161) (medium, ko) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2025-08 · DevTalk question** — claims the Korea storefront follows the EU 4.8 wording since 2025-04 (login service limited to name/email instead of mandating SIWA); unverified — rejections continued through 2026-04. [source](https://devtalk.kakao.com/t/topic/145953) (medium, ko) → IOS-LOGIN-01, KR-04
- **2024-03→04 · iOS apps with Kakao SDK** — rejected with a screenshot of Kakao's "install KakaoTalk" page: universal-link flow when the reviewer cancels "카카오톡으로 열겠습니까?". Kakao staff: explain to the reviewer, ask them to delete KakaoTalk or clear Safari website data → approved after the reply. [source](https://devtalk.kakao.com/t/topic/135723) (high, ko) → ACC-05, IOS-PLIST-03
- **2024-09 · app with KakaoTalk + Apple login** — reviewer's device hits Kakao 2-step verification even with 2FA/overseas-block off (account active on another phone). Kakao: hand over an email inbox for the 8-digit code, or create a Kakao account not linked to KakaoTalk. [source](https://devtalk.kakao.com/t/topic/139785) (high, ko) → ACC-05, ACC-03

### Apple — 5.1.1 account deletion / purpose strings / ATT
- **2024-10 · Flutter WebView app** — 5.1.1(v) → 회원탈퇴; post also lists Korea rules (SIWA with social login; IAP for digital goods; KR external-purchase PSPs). [source](https://blog.naver.com/raon0112/223606195150) (high, ko) → ACC-02, IOS-LOGIN-01, IOS-IAP-02
- **2024-10 · course app** — 5.1.1(v) "회원 탈퇴 기능이 없다" although it existed → screenshots of the button location → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@yuiop1029/iOSGuideline-5.1.1v-Data-Collection-and-Storage) (high, ko) → ACC-02, META-05
