- **2022-06 · Spring backend + Apple login** — 5.1.1(v) "use the Sign in with Apple REST API to revoke user tokens" → authorization_code → client_secret → /auth/token → /auth/revoke on deletion. [source](https://velog.io/@givepro91/jjo2cyus) (high, ko) → ACC-02, IOS-LOGIN-02
- **2023-01 · app** — second rejection for missing deletion. [source](https://velog.io/@windylung/1.20-%EB%91%90%EB%B2%88%EC%A7%B8-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D%EA%B3%BC-%EB%B9%A0%EB%A5%B8-%EC%95%B1-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EC%9A%94%EC%B2%AD) (medium, ko) → ACC-02
- **2025-02 · photo social app** — purpose string too generic ("앱에서 사진을 선택하기 위해") → feature-specific ("행복 작성에 사진을 첨부하려면") → approved ~8 h. [source](https://velog.io/@maxminseok/%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D) (high, ko) → PERM-01
- **2023-06 · app** — 5.1.1 "doesn't sufficiently explain the use of the photos in the purpose string" → named the features (profile photo, post photo) → approved. [source](https://hyggeinx.tistory.com/104) (high, ko) → PERM-01
- **2022-05 · app** — 5.1.1 camera/photos purpose → rewritten with examples. [source](https://velog.io/@dksalsgus/5.1.1-Legal-Privacy-Data-Collection-and-Storage-%EC%95%B1%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC%EA%B1%B0%EC%A0%88) (medium, ko) → PERM-01
- **2024-11 · RN app with ATT** — "Remove the custom prompts, and request permission using the AppTrackingTransparency framework before collecting data used to track the user." / "Indicate in the Review Notes where the permission request is located." ATT was the 4th prompt in a chain → ATT first → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@hnmpot/App-Tracking-Transparency) (high, ko) → PRIV-03, META-05
- **2023-10 · Flutter app with Geolocator + ads** — 5.1.2 ATT + localized string; 5.1.5 app must work when location denied. [source](https://velog.io/@jeong_woo/Flutter-violation-when-deploy-iOS) (medium, ko) → PRIV-03, PERM-02

### Google Play — photo/video permissions, full-screen intent, old tracks
- **2025-11 · app that captures its logo to an image** — "사진 및 동영상 권한 정책: 권한 사용이 앱의 핵심 목적과 직접적인 관련이 없습니다". Removed permission (VC 6) → rejected again "제출한 모든 버전 코드에서 … 삭제하세요" because the **open-testing track still carried VC 3** → new open-testing release from VC 6 → approved within hours. [source](https://blog.naver.com/login2you/224093553821) (high, ko) → AND-PERM-08, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2024-10 · cross-platform app** — Health-declaration notice, then READ_MEDIA_*: "앱에서 사진 및 동영상에 일회성으로 또는 빈번하지 않게 액세스해야 하는 경우 Android 사진 선택 도구로 이전하세요." Fix: written justification of frequent media use → approved in a week. [source](https://velog.io/@gorokeya/%EC%95%88%EB%93%9C%EB%A1%9C%EC%9D%B4%EB%93%9C-%EC%95%B1%EC%97%90%EC%84%9C-READMEDIAIMAGES-%EA%B6%8C%ED%95%9C%EC%9D%84-%EB%B0%9B%EA%B3%A0-%EC%9E%88%EB%8B%A4%EB%A9%B4) (high, ko) → AND-PERM-08, AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2025-11 · WebView apps (vendor roundup)** — READ_MEDIA_* "핵심 목적과 직접적인 관련이 없습니다 … 프로덕션 및 테스트 트랙이 모두 포함됩니다" — WebView file chooser + manifest permission → delete in all tracks. [source](https://blog.naver.com/swing2app/224089817842) (medium, ko) → AND-PERM-08, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2025-11 · WebView apps (vendor roundup)** — "앱이 USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT 권한의 사용 방식을 준수하지 않는 것으로 확인되었습니다" (auto-added by builders) → remove in every track. [source](https://blog.naver.com/swing2app/224091122773) (medium, ko) → AND-PERM-10, AND-PERM-13, AND-CONSOLE-11

### Google Play — sign-in details / broken functionality / build
- **2025-02 · QR-gated enterprise app** — "앱에 액세스하는 데 필요한 QR 코드 또는 바코드가 제공되지 않았습니다" → "활성 데모/게스트 계정 … 제공되지 않았습니다" → "제공하신 로그인 사용자 인증 정보로는 인앱 콘텐츠에 액세스할 수 없었습니다… 앱의 동영상 데모를 제출하실 수 없습니다." (one-week QR expired during the appeal; videos not accepted). Fix: fresh QR via Drive link in a new publishing update → approved. 4 rounds + 3 appeals, 47 days (iOS passed in 3 days with a video). [1](https://blog.naver.com/ewrim88/223765949594) [2](https://blog.naver.com/ewrim88/223767635159) (high, ko) → ACC-03, AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2024-03 · Kakao-login app** — Play rejected with a screenshot of Kakao's overseas 2-step SMS verification (same account passed App Store). Kakao: provide an email inbox + steps for the 8-digit code in Sign-in details. [source](https://devtalk.kakao.com/t/topic/135699) (high, ko) → ACC-05, AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2025-09 · vendor roundup (20k apps)** — "제공된 사용자 아이디 또는 비밀번호가 잘못됨 / 앱을 검토하려면 로그인하여 모든 항목을 확인할 수 있어야 합니다." Rules: non-admin account, ASCII-only ID ("아이디는 한국어를 사용할 수 없습니다"), account stays valid after launch (random checks), OTP-only login needs a master code, Kakao/Naver/Google-only login not accepted → add ID/PW login. [source](https://blog.naver.com/swing2app/224002651520) (medium, ko) → ACC-03, AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2024-12 · app with 10k+ DAU** — 손상된 기능: "앱에 응답하지 않거나 콘텐츠를 반환하지 않는 아이콘 또는 버튼이 포함되어 있습니다" — blank error screen when offline; notice landed during 추석. Fix: error copy + refresh button. [source](https://velog.io/@qqqqlss/%EA%B5%AC%EA%B8%80-%ED%94%8C%EB%A0%88%EC%9D%B4%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EC%A0%95%EC%B1%85%EC%9C%84%EB%B0%98-%EB%8C%80%EC%B2%98) (high, ko) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-04 · RN app** — rejected with `ERR_CLEARTEXT_NOT_PERMITTED` screenshots (OAuth over HTTP; passed as APK, failed as AAB). Developer set `usesCleartextTraffic="true"` (anti-pattern; use HTTPS). [source](https://velog.io/@ljy9505/Android-%EC%95%B1-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%EA%B1%B0%EC%A0%88-ERRCLEARTEXTNOTPERMITTED) (medium, ko) → CONTENT-02, AND-BUILD-02, CONTENT-12
- **2026-02 · Flutter parking app first update** — "앱 업데이트가 거부되었습니다": `1.0.1+0` after `1.0.0+7` (versionCode went backwards) → `1.0.1+8`. [source](https://blog.naver.com/hjinha2/224197165606) (high, ko) → AND-BUILD-01
- **2022-12 · team project** — re-review error: AAB uploaded from a teammate's machine → upload-key mismatch. [source](https://velog.io/@sksksk951/%EC%95%88%EB%93%9C%EB%A1%9C%EC%9D%B4%EB%93%9C-%EC%8A%A4%ED%8A%9C%EB%94%94%EC%98%A4-%EB%B0%B0%ED%8F%AC-%EB%A6%AC%EC%A0%9D-%ED%9B%84-%EC%9E%AC%EA%B2%80%ED%86%A0-%EC%9A%94%EC%B2%AD-%EC%98%A4%EB%A5%98) (medium, ko) → AND-BUILD-01
- **2024-11 · Android app** — repeated target-SDK emails; bundles in test tracks can't be deleted — Google support: overlay every track with a compliant release. [source](https://velog.io/@mraz3068/Bundles-uploaded-to-play-console-testing-cannot-be-deleted) (high, ko) → AND-SDK-01, AND-CONSOLE-11

### Google Play — UGC / metadata / data safety
- **2022-05 · community apps** — UGC: no report/block of abusive users → "해당 유저 신고" with immediate content hiding → approved. [source](https://velog.io/@dddooo9/Android-UGC-%EC%A0%95%EC%B1%85-%EB%95%8C%EB%A0%A4%EC%9E%A1%EA%B8%B0) (high, ko) → UGC-03, UGC-04
- **2024-06 · youth-policy app (new personal account)** — identity verification (statement lacked address → 주민등록등본); closed test "준비가 되지 않았다" → 86 testers, passed 2nd attempt; production rejected **4×** under Metadata ("앱 소개가 문제") → concrete feature-list description; suspended after the 3rd → appeal → approved. ~36 days. [source](https://velog.io/@rhkrwngud445/%ED%94%8C%EB%A0%88%EC%9D%B4%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EB%B9%84%EA%B3%B5%EA%B0%9C-%ED%85%8C%EC%8A%A4%ED%8A%B8-%ED%9B%84%EA%B8%B0) (high, ko) → AND-ACCOUNT-01, AND-TEST-01, META-02
- **2026-01 · government-information WebView app (vendor case)** — "제공된 출처가 불충분함" (Misleading Claims): sources missing/broken, implied government affiliation. Fix: official source URLs, disclaimer "본 앱은 정부 기관을 대표하거나 정부와 제휴한 서비스가 아닙니다", remove "공식/정부 제공/국가 인증". [source](https://blog.naver.com/swing2app/224139077853) (medium, ko) → AND-META-04
- **2025-09 · vendor roundup** — "앱에 메타데이터 정책을 준수하지 않는 콘텐츠가 포함되어 있습니다" (예: '최고', '1위', '인기', '신규', '할인', '세일', '무료', '백만 다운로드'), also in screenshots ("NO.1", "TOP 랭킹"). [source](https://blog.naver.com/swing2app/224004007876) (medium, ko) → META-02, AND-META-01
- **2026-04 · vendor roundup** — "데이터 보안 양식의 계정 삭제 링크가 잘못됨": page lacked app/developer name and steps; "contact support", login-gated and generic privacy pages fail. [source](https://blog.naver.com/swing2app/224250998047) (medium, ko) → ACC-02, AND-CONSOLE-08

### Google Play — closed testing / verification / termination / 16 KB
- **2026-07 · indie app** — production rejected after 14 days: testers didn't engage, no updates, 12-tester count not maintained to the end (품앗이 testers dropped). [source](https://blog.naver.com/calvinsmith/224361088852) (high, ko) → AND-TEST-01
- **2026-01 · Flutter parking app** — rejected despite 14 days / 20 testers: opt-outs, install-only testers, no in-test updates, thin questionnaire; launched ~2 months later. [source](https://blog.naver.com/hjinha2/224161970289) (high, ko) → AND-TEST-01
- **2024-09 · Flutter app** — production access answered with macro emails for ~2 months; approved the day after updating the closed track (accessibility warnings suspected). [source](https://velog.io/@valborgs/%EB%93%9C%EB%94%94%EC%96%B4-%ED%94%8C%EB%A0%88%EC%9D%B4%EC%BD%98%EC%86%94-%ED%94%84%EB%A1%9C%EB%8D%95%EC%85%98-%EC%8B%A0%EC%B2%AD%EC%9D%B4-%EC%8A%B9%EC%9D%B8%EB%90%98%EC%97%88%EB%8B%A4) (high, ko) → AND-TEST-01, AND-QUALITY-01
- **2024-02 · Android app** — closed testing rejected 4× over 3 weeks. [source](https://velog.io/@choco5732/%EB%93%9C%EB%94%94%EC%96%B4..-%EB%B9%84%EA%B3%B5%EA%B0%9C-%ED%85%8C%EC%8A%A4%ED%8A%B8-%EC%8B%AC%EC%82%AC-%ED%86%B5%EA%B3%BC) (medium, ko) → AND-TEST-01
- **2026-07 · lunch-diary app** — production rejected for low usage. [source](https://velog.io/@dangdong/%EC%A0%90%EC%8B%AC-%EB%8B%A4%EC%9D%B4%EC%96%B4%EB%A6%AC-12-%ED%94%84%EB%A1%9C%EB%8D%95%EC%85%98-%EB%B0%98%EB%A0%A4%EB%B6%80%ED%84%B0-%EA%B0%88%EC%95%84%EC%97%8E%EA%B8%B0%EA%B9%8C%EC%A7%80-lw6np5ls) (medium, ko) → AND-TEST-01
- **2024-08 · student developer** — identity verification failed 5×; approved with 주민등록등본 once the payments-profile address matched exactly (지번 vs 도로명). ~1 month. [source](https://velog.io/@kk21/%EA%B5%AC%EA%B8%80%ED%94%8C%EB%A0%88%EC%9D%B4-%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%EC%9E%90-%EA%B3%84%EC%A0%95-%EB%B3%B8%EC%9D%B8-%ED%99%95%EC%9D%B8) (high, ko) → AND-ACCOUNT-01
- **2025-08 · vendor roundup** — "Status: Account Terminated / Issue found: High Risk Behavior": repeat violations, duplicate apps, interstitial at launch, external PG for digital goods, unnecessary permissions in WebView apps. [source](https://blog.naver.com/swing2app/223985074740) (medium, ko) → AND-QUALITY-02, CONTENT-07
- **2026-04 · Flutter app with native deps** — 16 KB warning; 4 KB-aligned .so from old OpenCV/ML Kit deps. Fix: `android.experimental.16kb-enabled=true` (AGP 8.3+, compileSdk 35), readelf check. [source](https://velog.io/@androidcapgoing/Android-16KB-%EB%A9%94%EB%AA%A8%EB%A6%AC-%ED%8E%98%EC%9D%B4%EC%A7%80-%ED%81%AC%EA%B8%B0-%EC%A7%80%EC%9B%90-%EB%8C%80%EC%9D%91%EA%B8%B0-Flutter) (high, ko) → AND-SDK-02
- **2025-09 · SDK vendor (OpenCV, TFLite)** — 16 KB: OpenCV 4.12.0, NDK r28, `check_elf_alignment.sh`. [source](https://velog.io/@gudrmsglgl/Android-15-Support-Memory-Page-16KB-%EB%8C%80%EC%9D%91) (medium, ko) → AND-SDK-02

---

## Google Play — additional cases (2nd pass, 2026-08-28: Stack Exchange API, Play community, GitHub issues, HN)

188 cases (39 high / 149 medium). Medium = thread read via a reader proxy or an unresolved thread where the "fix" is the expert's advice. Sources not covered here: Reddit (blocked in every form).

### Photo and Video Permissions — additional cases (library-injected, Cordova/Capacitor/Expo)
- **2025-07 · Flutter app with open_filex + file_picker** — "Photo and Video Permissions Policy: Use of permissions is not directly related to the core purpose of your app. Your app only requires one-time or infrequent access to media files on the device." Cause: `open_filex` merges `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/VIDEO/AUDIO` although the app manifest declares none. Fix: `tools:node="remove"` for all three READ_MEDIA_* entries (accepted answer). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79704631/google-play-app-rejected-for-read-media-images-read-media-video-usage-but-not) (high, en) → AND-PERM-08, AND-PERM-13
- **2025-12 · voice/video-call app using the Mesibo SDK** — same policy; the Mesibo core manifest adds `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/VIDEO` for its media-sharing feature. Fix (vendor-confirmed): `tools:node="remove"` for both; calls do not need them. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79834456/mesibo-sdk-adds-read-media-images-read-media-video-causing-google-play-rejecti) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13
- **2025-10 · Cordova app with an in-app photo viewer (pinch-zoom + download)** — "Your app only requires one-time or infrequent access to media files on the device. Only apps with a core use case that require persistent access to photo and video files located in shared storage on devices are allowed to use photo and video permissions… Remove the use of READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/READ_MEDIA_VIDEO permission from all version codes within the submission. This includes both production and testing tracks." Cause: the `photoviewer` Cordova plugin declares the permissions; first rejection after years of updates. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79795823/cordova-in-app-picture-viewer-and-android-read-media-images-permissions-issue) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-08, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2025-07 · Cordova app using cordova-plugin-camera** — told to "switch to Android Photo Picker"; camera plugin 8.0.0 dropped READ_MEDIA_* but still opened the legacy picker. Community fix: find the offending plugin with `grep -R "READ_MEDIA" plugins`, use `<input type="file" accept="image/*">` with `READ_MEDIA_VISUAL_USER_SELECTED`. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79717906/how-do-i-use-android-photo-picker-from-cordova) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-08, AND-PERM-13
- **2025-02 · publisher of 10 learning apps (same code base, HN)** — one app rejected for "not filling out the reasoning for camera / photo permissions (READ_MEDIA_IMAGES, READ_MEDIA_VIDEO)" that the app does not request; resubmitting with "we don't use it" was rejected "immediately again (likely an automated test)"; after force-removing a related permission "the permission reappeared". No human reachable. Lesson: audit the merged manifest of the exact AAB and every track. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=42973924) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2026-06 · Capacitor CCTV client (zmNinja)** — "Permission use is not directly related to your app's core purpose. Remove the use of READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/READ_MEDIA_VIDEO permission from all version codes." Cause: manifest still declared `READ_MEDIA_*` + `READ/WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` although `@capacitor-community/media` only *saves* snapshots (no permission needed in non-gallery mode). Fix: delete all four. [source](https://github.com/ZoneMinder/zmNinjaNg/issues/190) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-08, PERM-03
- **2026-06 · Capacitor moderation app (Freegle ModTools)** — same text; cause: three vestigial permissions from an old Cordova camera plugin in a native project shared by two apps; `@capacitor/camera` v7 already uses `PickVisualMedia`. Fix: removed `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES`, `READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE`, `WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` from both apps. [source](https://github.com/Freegle/Iznik/pull/760) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13
- **2026-07 · Expo fitness app (POWR)** — rejected under the Photo and Video Permissions policy; cause: `expo-media-library`'s config plugin injected `READ_MEDIA_*` through a dead import. Fix: drop the package + `android.blockedPermissions` for READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/VIDEO/AUDIO/VISUAL_USER_SELECTED; verified with `expo prebuild --clean` + `./gradlew :app:processReleaseMainManifest`. [source](https://github.com/POWR-LIFE/POWR/pull/157) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13
- **2026-06 · Capacitor app (Capgo)** — rejected "for declaring photo/video media permissions even though Capgo only needs one-time or infrequent media access"; cause: `@capgo/capacitor-photo-library` manifest. Fix: remove the plugin, keep Camera/Photo Picker; merged debug manifest checked. [source](https://github.com/Cap-go/capgo.app/pull/2448) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13

### Foreground services, exact alarms, full-screen intent, background location — additional cases
- **2024-03 · app embedding the Zoom Meeting SDK (custom UI)** — "Permissions for Foreground Services: Functionality is not initiated by or perceptible to the user… Use of FGS is not perceptible to the user when performing Media Playback… Media Projection…". Cause: the Zoom SDK declares five FGS types; removing the permissions crashed with `SecurityException: Starting FGS with type mediaPlayback`. Fix: Zoom SDK ≥ 6.0.2 honours `tools:replace="android:foregroundServiceType"` on `ScreenShareServiceForSDK`; `tools:node="remove"` the unused `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_*` permissions. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78165800/app-using-3rd-party-sdk-zoom-meeting-sdk-has-been-rejected-due-to-foreground-s) (high, en) → AND-PERM-05, AND-PERM-13
- **2025-01 · Organic Maps** — "Play Console declaration and/or app description in Google Play Store does not sufficiently demonstrate the use of permission. We are unable to approve your app's use of FOREGROUND_SERVICE permission because… The video link you provided… and/or The app description". After adding videos: "Service interruption or deferment of functionality does not have material user impact… Your app is using foreground service to Special Use - Other when it is not required to do so." Fix: videos for navigation, track recorder and Android Auto, then dropped `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_SPECIAL_USE` in favour of typed services; approved after ~3 days. [source](https://github.com/organicmaps/organicmaps/issues/10140) (high, en) → AND-PERM-05, AND-CONSOLE-06
- **2024-12 · Capacitor app with background-geolocation** — "Your app is using foreground service to Health - Other when it is not required to do so"; "the user is not made aware of functionality requiring permission when active"; "Your in-app experience or video does not match the Health - Other in your declaration." Cause: an older plugin version merged `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_HEALTH`/`ACTIVITY_RECOGNITION`; the geofencing box was ticked although unused. Fix: `tools:node="remove"` both permissions, untick geofencing, rewrite the Health declaration; two appeals had been rejected first. [source](https://github.com/transistorsoft/capacitor-background-geolocation/issues/299) (high, en) → AND-PERM-05, AND-CONSOLE-06, AND-PERM-13
- **2026-05 · research data-collection app (RADAR-base pRMT)** — rejected for a service declared with `specialUse` alongside health/location/microphone/connectedDevice. Fix: start as a bound service and promote to FGS later; `specialUse` removed. [source](https://github.com/RADAR-base/radar-prmt-android/pull/163) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-05
- **2026-05 · timer app (Random Timer)** — internal-track upload blocked: "You must let us know whether your app uses any Foreground Service permissions." Cause: `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_SPECIAL_USE` in the release manifest triggers the declaration gate. Fix: `mediaPlayback` type only. [source](https://github.com/IgorGanapolsky/Random-Timer/pull/1299) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-05, AND-CONSOLE-06
- **2024-06 · React Native app using @notifee/react-native** — "Remove Full-screen intent permission or submit declaration"; the declaration blocks every upload and has no "I don't use it" option. Cause: Notifee < 9.0.0 adds `USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT`. Fix: `tools:node="remove"` (or notifee ≥ 9), then delete the pending release / archive every bundle that still carries the permission (App bundle explorer). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78569030/playstore-is-rejecting-my-app-due-to-full-screen-intent-permission) (high, en) → AND-PERM-10, AND-PERM-13, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2024-07 · video-conferencing app** — USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT rejected as "not related to our app's core purpose" despite incoming-call notifications; "Remove the use of USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT permission from all version codes within the submission. This includes both production and testing tracks." Appeals gave no answer; an alarm-app developer in the thread passed after auto-dismissing the full-screen intent after a few minutes. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78785047/android-full-screen-intent-compliance) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-10
- **2026-03 · journey-tracking app (not alarm/call)** — told to request the FSI permission at runtime instead; catch-22: with `USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT` in the manifest Play rejects, without it the Settings toggle is greyed out. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79902327/how-do-i-get-fullscreenintent-accepted-on-playstore-for-an-app-that-does-not-inv) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-10
- **2023-11 · app with no alarm feature** — Console warning "Your use of exact alarms is causing your app to crash for some Android users. Your app schedules exact alarms without checking whether the SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM permission has been granted." from a library calling `AlarmManager.setExact`. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77491890/warning-about-needs-of-schedule-exact-alarm-despite-app-doesnt-schedule-any-ala) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-10, AND-PERM-13
- **2024-11 · Flutter app** — same exact-alarm warning plus "Authentication via WebView: Your app performs authentication via WebViews, which leads to security and usability issues" (an Apple-login WebView dialog). Fix: check `canScheduleExactAlarms()` on 13+; move OAuth to Custom Tabs. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79214556/getting-google-play-store-deployment-permission-warning) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-10, ACC-04
- **2026-05 · Flutter location plugin consumers (libre-location)** — upload API error "Invalid request - You must let us know whether your app uses any exact alarm permissions"; plugin removed `SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM`/`USE_EXACT_ALARM` and replaced AlarmManager heartbeats with a Handler loop. [source](https://github.com/Rezivure/libre-location/pull/11) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-10, AND-PERM-13
- **2025-11 · fitness-equipment app (QZ)** — rejected: `USE_EXACT_ALARM`/`SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM` "not directly related to core functionality"; cause: ML Kit → `com.google.android.datatransport` telemetry using AlarmManager. Fix: exclude datatransport/firebase from the ML Kit dependency + a CI check that fails the build if EXACT_ALARM appears. [source](https://github.com/cagnulein/qdomyos-zwift/pull/3857) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-10, AND-PERM-13
- **2025-07 · Flutter app using the `alarm` package** — rejected for `USE_EXACT_ALARM` + `USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT`; approved after removing both; the maintainer declined the PR ("explain with detail how these permissions are important to your app"). [source](https://github.com/gdelataillade/alarm/pull/377) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-10
- **2024-10 · Flutter food-delivery app (driver live tracking)** — "Background location access not declared: We detected that your app contains at least one feature that requests access to location in the background, however your permission declaration form did not reflect this." Fix: resubmit the location declaration with background = yes, or drop `ACCESS_BACKGROUND_LOCATION` (a location FGS started by the user does not need it). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79087290/background-location-enabled-for-my-app-play-store-rejected-my-app-update-how-t) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-04, AND-CONSOLE-06

### QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES, All files access, REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES, SMS — additional cases
- **2022-08 · app sharing to WhatsApp/Gmail** — 3 rejections: "Less broad app-visibility method should be used. We are unable to approve your app's use of QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES permission because the declared task can be done with a less broad app-visibility method." Cause: the Macrobenchmark module added at project creation declares the permission. Fix: remove the module → approved. Google support's route: App bundle explorer → note non-compliant versions → replace them in every track. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73224026/how-do-i-remove-the-query-all-packages-permission-from-my-android-app) (high, en) → AND-PERM-02, AND-PERM-13, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2022-08 · screen-time / app-blocking app** — same wording; suggested replacement: `<queries><intent><action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN"/><category android:name="android.intent.category.LAUNCHER"/></intent></queries>`. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73465187/less-broad-app-visibility-method-should-be-used) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-02
- **2022-07 · Chinese app listing installed non-system apps** — QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES rejection; replaced the permission with a `<queries><intent><action MAIN/></intent></queries>` block (list differs by ~10 system apps); the console warning persisted about a day after approval. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7137864762045497358) (high, zh) → AND-PERM-02
- **2025-08 · Flutter app** — Console "Incomplete broad package visibility declaration" for QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES not present in the manifest, plus a contradictory AD_ID error; cause: old builds in closed/open testing. Fix: deactivate/stop those tracks and release again without a new upload. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79740721/google-play-console-permission-declaration-errors-despite-correct-manifest-que) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2022-12 · Ecuador government e-signature app (FirmaEC)** — "Permission use is not directly related to your app's core purpose. We found that your app is not compliant with how REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES permission is allowed to be used… Please remove the use of REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES permission from your app." (SPLIT_BUNDLE 15). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/74774448/how-to-solve-split-bundle-15) (medium, en) → PERM-03
- **2022-11 · Flutter app using `open_file`** — rejected for `REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES` injected by the plugin; `tools:node="remove"` was tried but unnecessary — replacing `open_file` with `open_filex` removed the permission and the resubmission passed. [source](https://zenn.dev/flutternyumon/articles/85aa7a2e3f7616) (high, ja) → PERM-03, AND-PERM-13
- **2023-08 · Flutter PDF reader listing local PDFs** — "Not a core feature: The app feature that is dependent on this permission does not appear to be critical to the core functionality of your app" for MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE; advice: SAF / media permissions instead. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76983934/google-play-store-error-not-a-core-feature-due-to-manage-external-storage-permi) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-03
- **2022-08 · app with All files access** — "Not a core feature… The core functionality, as well as any core features that comprise this core functionality, must all be prominently documented and promoted in the app's description"; Console still showed "In review", blocking a resubmission. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73261390/i-got-an-email-that-is-my-app-is-rejected-but-in-my-developer-console-it-shows-i) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-03
- **2023-07 · scheduled-SMS sender** — SEND_SMS rejected 5 times; none of the declaration use cases (default handler, OTP, financial…) covers "send an SMS at a chosen time". [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76662911/google-play-console-app-content-sms-permission-app-rejecting) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-01
- **2024-09 · app auto-reading OTP SMS** — "Requested permissions do not match core functionality of the app… Your store listing information does not match the core functionality for your declared use case. Call based OTP verification"; there is no "SMS OTP autofill" use case — use the SMS Retriever / User Consent API. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/297270179) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-01
- **2024-03 · SMS-automation app** — same "Requested permissions do not match…" for the "Device Automation" use case; experts: Device Automation means Tasker-like apps, not automated SMS sending. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/266114920) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-01
- **2024-10 · missed-call auto-reply app (previously approved under Device Automation)** — "Unable to verify core functionality of app… Your in-app experience does not match the core functionality for your declared use case… The video you submitted does not demonstrate the functionality necessary to verify and approve your use case declaration". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/299842788) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-01, AND-CONSOLE-06
- **2023-07 · Unity kids game** — removed: "The target age groups you've selected are inaccurate. Make sure that you only select the appropriate age groups under 13"; then the Console refused to drop 13-15: "You must remove the request for location permissions from your app if your target audience only includes children under 13" although the manifest had none. Google support found `ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION` in an old Open Testing bundle (v20); replacing it fixed it. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76654139/unity3d-remove-the-request-for-location-permissions-from-your-app-if-your-targe) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07, AND-CONSOLE-11

### User Data: Data safety, privacy policy, prominent disclosure, SDK security flags — additional cases
- **2023-02 · app that added play-services-wearable** — rejected "invalid data safety (Phone Number)" with no phone permissions; the culprit was an outdated `play-services-ads` (21.5.0 → 21.2.0 fixed; updating dependencies via Build > Edit Libraries and Dependencies > Suggestion also worked). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75343721/policy-declaration-data-safety-section-personal-info-data-type-phone-number) (medium, en) → PRIV-02, AND-CONSOLE-02
- **2022-09 · Unity game with AdMob + GameAnalytics + Unity Ads** — "Invalid Data safety form… did not disclose that my app collects and shares 'Apps On Device'"; the analytics plugin was suspected; disclosing "installed apps" clears it. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73567542/data-safety-form-how-can-as-a-developer-know-which-data-third-party-sdk-collec) (medium, en) → PRIV-02
- **2023-03 · React Native app** — "We detected user data transmitted off device that you have not disclosed… SPLIT_BUNDLE 21: Policy Declaration - Data Safety Section: Device Or Other IDs"; on request Google named "segment_analytics SDK or an SDK that one of your libraries depends on" though the app never imported Segment. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75783450/google-play-rejected-my-react-native-app-because-it-includes-an-sdk-that-is-not) (medium, en) → PRIV-02
- **2022-06 · Expo apps (classic builds + expo-analytics-segment)** — from 10 June 2022 submissions were rejected for `com.segment.analytics.android:analytics` 4.9.4; Expo shipped `expo-analytics-segment` 11.2.1 and rebuilt classic builds for SDK 43–45; developers had to bump the version and resubmit. [source](https://github.com/expo/expo/issues/17845) (high, en) → PRIV-02, AND-PERM-13
- **2023-12 · Organic Maps** — "Invalid Data safety form… We detected user data transmitted off device… Personal info Data Type - Email address" for the OpenStreetMap login; Google demanded declaring email collection or removing the login by 10 Jan 2024. [source](https://github.com/organicmaps/organicmaps/issues/7000) (high, en) → PRIV-02
- **2024-11 · Flutter kids app that added Yandex Ads** — "Invalid Data safety form… We detected user data transmitted off device that you have not disclosed" after declaring "no data collected"; Yandex: children's apps must strip AD_ID with `tools:node="remove"`; Designed-for-Families support "not yet" available. [source](https://github.com/yandexmobile/yandex-ads-flutter-plugin/issues/54) (medium, en) → PRIV-02, AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2024-09 · Flutter app with AppLovin MAX mediation 12.6.1** — "Invalid Data safety form… Device Or Other IDs"; AppLovin: declare Device or other IDs as collected. [source](https://github.com/AppLovin/AppLovin-MAX-SDK-Android/issues/707) (medium, en) → PRIV-02
- **2024-02 · apps with Braintree 3.x (PayPal data-collector)** — "User Data policy: Violation of User Data, Permissions and APIs that Access Sensitive Information Policies… your app is using an SDK that is designed to collect device location by default… You are hereby requested to provide evidence of your compliance with the Prominent Disclosure and Consent requirements. Your app submissions will be rejected pending your action." Fix that worked: Braintree 3.21.1 / 4.41.x on **every** track (incl. private/unpublished), rollout to 100 %, then one more test-track upload to clear the stale warning; several only cleared on the 4.x line. [source](https://github.com/braintree/braintree_android/issues/931) (high, en) → PRIV-04, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2023-07 · app uploading images** — appeal reply: "your app violates the Malware and User Data policy. We detected that your app currently contains spyware code that transmits personal data off the device without adequate notice or consent… Your app is uploading users' Image information but the privacy policy does not meet the policy requirements… deactivate the non-compliant app bundle version 32 and increment the version code". [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76748552/action-required-your-app-is-not-compliant-with-google-play-policies-what-is-t) (medium, en) → PRIV-01, PRIV-04, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2022-09 · blood-donation app** — 3+ rejections: "APK HAS A PRIVACY POLICY BUT IT IS NOT ADEQUATE… Your app is uploading users' Contact list and Media information… without posting a privacy policy in Play Distributed App"; the policy listed the data but the app lacked in-app consent screens for contacts/media. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73720604/how-to-resolve-issue-with-google-user-data-policy) (medium, en) → PRIV-01, PRIV-04
- **2020-06 → 2022-10 · Pushbullet** — seven "Action Required: Your app is not compliant with Google Play Policies" privacy rejections over two years: policy link missing in onboarding, missing on the SMS-sync settings screen, disclosure not prominent enough (moved the link to the top + confirmation dialog), link text too small, Play-listing policy lacking the SMS-sync details present in-app, no statement that the Google account e-mail is sent to their servers. [source](https://blog.pushbullet.com/2022/10/27/how-we-became-the-worlds-foremost-expert-on-google-play-store-policy-violations/) (high, en) → PRIV-01, PRIV-04
- **2023-06 · Indian farmers' app (targets children)** — "Eligibility Issue: Privacy policy… You must link to a privacy policy on your app's store listing page and within your app, regardless of your app's access to sensitive permissions or data" with Google's checklist: linked on listing and in-app, titled "privacy policy", readable without plugins/PDF, active URL, non-editable, single governing policy, includes the app/entity name. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/220500452) (medium, en) → PRIV-01, AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2023-02 · Korean app with its privacy policy on Notion** — "개인정보처리방침이 플레이스토어 정책에 부합하지 않는다" (privacy policy does not meet Play policy); fix: export the Notion page to HTML and host it on the developer's own domain → approved on the next round. [source](https://velog.io/@gale4739/%ED%94%8C%EB%A0%88%EC%9D%B4%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EC%A0%95%EC%B1%85-%EB%AC%B8%EC%A0%9C-%ED%99%95%EC%9D%B8-%EB%B0%8F-%EC%A0%9C%EC%B6%9C-%ED%9B%84%EA%B8%B0) (medium, ko) → PRIV-01
- **2022-01 · app with S3 keys in BuildConfig** — "Leaked AWS credentials: Your app contains Exposed Amazon Web Services Credentials" with a fix-by deadline; fix: server-issued pre-signed URLs. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/70796001/leaked-aws-credentials-amazon-web-services-credentials-may-be-exposed-google) (medium, en) → AND-SEC-01
- **2025-04 · WebView app** — internal-testing uploads rejected for "intent scheme hijacking" in `shouldOverrideUrlLoading`; Google's suggested `addCategory(BROWSABLE)`/`component = null`/`selector = null` did not clear it; replacing every `Intent.parseUri` use did. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79547855/app-rejected-due-to-intent-scheme-vulnerability-which-part-of-my-code-is-probl) (high, en) → AND-SEC-01
- **2022-04 · WebView app loading URLs from push/mail intents** — "Your app(s) are using a WebView that is vulnerable to cross-app scripting"; fix: validate with `Patterns.WEB_URL` before `loadUrl` → approved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/71946437/your-apps-are-using-a-webview-that-is-vulnerable-to-cross-app-scripting) (high, en) → AND-SEC-01
- **2023-11 · app flagged for an "Xupdate SDK" it never imported** — rejected 3 times; advice: search the dex in APK Analyzer for transitive references. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77515365/google-play-rejected-my-app-new-version-they-said-that-xupdate-sdk-exists-in-th) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02

### Advertising ID declaration — additional cases
- **2022-07 · app with Firebase Analytics and no ads** — "This release includes the com.google.android.gms.permission.AD_ID permission but your declaration on Play Console says your app doesn't use advertising ID." Google support: answer **Yes** with the Analytics use case instead of stripping the permission (386 votes); alternative: `google_analytics_adid_collection_enabled=false` + `tools:node="remove"` + "Turn off release errors". [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73129574/you-cant-submit-updates-as-some-information-about-your-app-is-incomplete-googl) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-04
- **2023-06 · Unity app** — contradictory errors both ways ("manifest does not include AD_ID" vs "remove it"); fixes: `tools:node="remove"` in Unity's custom manifest, and deactivate old active bundles that lacked the permission. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76548139/error-updating-advertising-id-declaration-in-google-play-console-for-a-unity-app) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-04, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2023-03 · Expo SDK 48 app** — EAS Submit: "Google Api Error: Invalid request - Your app targets Android 13 (API 33) or above. You must declare the use of advertising ID in Play Console." [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75643979/expo-sdk48-google-play-console-advertising-id) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-04
- **2023-12 · Cordova app with an AdMob plugin** — rejected until the Advertising ID + Data safety forms matched what AdMob collects; a second consent plugin pulled a conflicting UMP SDK version. [source](https://github.com/EMI-INDO/emi-indo-cordova-plugin-admob/issues/21) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-04, PRIV-02
- **2024-03 · medical-device app listed "Contains ads"** — could not remove the label; fix under App content > Actioned; note from the expert: "linking to another app from your app, even if it belongs to the same company is considered as advertising". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/263523990) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-04

### Families / Designed for Families
- **2023-09 · Flutter kids app with flutter_stripe** — "Violation of Families Policy Requirements: We have detected that your app includes non-certified ad SDKs or SDKs that are not approved for use in child-directed services." Removing the Stripe SDK → approved; Stripe support (2026): the SDK is not in the Families Self-Certified program and its ToS require the age of majority. [source](https://github.com/stripe/stripe-android/issues/7361) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2022-02 · Flutter app that switched to native_admob_flutter** — same "non-certified ad SDKs" text. [source](https://github.com/bdlukaa/native_admob_flutter/issues/132) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2022-09 · Unity game with the Adjust SDK** — "your app or an SDK in your app transmits device identifier(s) that do not comply with our Families Policy… Android Advertising ID"; approved a week earlier; removing Adjust → approved. [source](https://github.com/adjust/unity_sdk/issues/253) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2023-03 · Godot kids game with AdMob** — "Apps that solely target children must not transmit the following: Android Advertising ID (AAID) SIM Serial Build Serial BSSID MAC SSID IMEI and/or IMSI". Fix: tag the AdMob plugin for child-directed treatment. [source](https://github.com/poingstudios/godot-admob-android/issues/164) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2023-03 · Unity kids app with Amplitude** — AAID sent to api2.amplitude.com; removing `useAdvertisingIdForDeviceId` was not enough. [source](https://github.com/amplitude/unity-plugin/issues/121) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2024-01 · Flutter mixed-audience app** — "Issue found: Families Data Practices… transmits 'Advertising ID' for children or users of unknown age". Fix: `tagForChildDirectedTreatment: true` in the Mobile Ads `RequestConfiguration` → accepted. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77833824/my-live-on-play-store-flutter-app-transmits-advertising-id-for-children-or-u) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2023-11 · React Native app for children and adults** — "Families Device Identifiers" persisted after removing fbsdk-next and Firebase Analytics; experts: no AD_ID may leave the device before a neutral age screen. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/244649810) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2024-07 · Beelinguapp (mixed audience, Adjust SDK)** — rejected again a year after its age screen was approved; expert diagnosis: the screen asked "age" with buttons — a neutral age screen must let users "freely enter their month, day, and year of birth" and nothing may be sent before it. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/288089174) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2024-01 · Unity 3+ game adding Firebase Crashlytics/Analytics** — "Families ad format requirements… Ads or offers for in-app purchases that aren't clearly distinguishable from your app content…" then "Neutral age screen (new)"; expert: both plugins use the advertising ID → `AD_ID tools:node="remove"`; three age buttons (3-12/13-17/18+) are not neutral. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/252490686) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2024-01 · .NET MAUI app with AdMob banners + interstitials** — same "Families ad format requirements" text; must use a Families Self-Certified Ads SDK version. [source](https://github.com/marcojak/MauiMTAdmob/issues/56) (medium, en) → ADS-02, AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2026-02 · Flutter kids colouring app** — "Unclosable ads: Ads interfere with app use and can't be closed after 5 seconds." for opt-in rewarded videos (non-skippable by design); Google closed the plugin issue as not a bug. [source](https://github.com/googleads/googleads-mobile-flutter/issues/1403) (medium, en) → ADS-02, AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2023-08 · game switching target audience** — "Ads that interfere with normal app use that aren't closeable after 5 seconds." on rewarded "extra life" ads; then flagged for looking like it targets kids after moving to 13+. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/231873499) (medium, en) → ADS-02, AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2025-04 · Unity game (Google Mobile Ads Unity 10.0.0)** — Families rejection because the interstitial/rewarded close button sat behind the navigation bar on Pixel devices; fixed by GMA Unity 10.1.0 (immersive mode during full-screen ads) + clean build. [source](https://github.com/googleads/googleads-mobile-unity/issues/3764) (high, en) → ADS-02
- **2024-01 · kids app** — removed after a screenshot update: "The ad content in your app is not consistent with the app's content rating… retake the content rating questionnaire… ensure you've excluded ads from sensitive categories"; must deactivate bundle 3 and increment. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77854014/how-to-fix-ad-content-in-is-not-consistent-with-the-apps-content-rating-play-co) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-05, ADS-02
- **2024-01 · app with AdMob set to "G"** — repeated "The ad content in your app is not consistent with the app's content rating."; experts: appeal or escalate to AdMob. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/256395498) (medium, en) → ADS-02, AND-CONSOLE-05
- **2026 · radio-streaming app with no ad SDK** — "Ad Content – The ad content in your app is not consistent with the app's content rating." although "No ads" was declared; WebView pages from the station site suspected. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/414721802) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-04, AND-CONSOLE-05
- **2022-07 · e-commerce app that ticked children as an audience** — "Eligibility Issue… App stability: During testing we experienced stability issues with your app and were unable to successfully evaluate it for policy compliance" + Families; answers: wrong target audience, and Play Asset Delivery downloads fail in the review environment. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73045802/your-app-has-been-rejected-and-wasnt-published-due-to-this-policy-issue-googl) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01, AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2022-08 → 2023-11 · kids games with on-demand asset packs (Unity PAD)** — the review environment returns `AppNotOwned` / `AppUnavailable` / `InternalError` for asset-pack downloads → "App stability" rejections under Families; workaround: make first-session packs install-time (or download everything at install). [source](https://github.com/google/play-unity-plugins/issues/190) (high, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2026-06 · Defold 1.13.0-beta game** — "Families Policy Requirements: App stability The app crashed during testing and couldn't be evaluated for policy compliance." Cause: Vulkan renderer on a Galaxy A07; OpenGL-only manifest fixed it (engine fix followed). [source](https://github.com/defold/defold/issues/12576) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-08 · Organic Maps** — removed overnight "due to not meeting the requirements for the Family Program" with no details; the team suspected the OpenStreetMap password login and disabled it; restored the next day after an appeal. [source](https://organicmaps.app/news/2024-08-17/last-night-organic-maps-was-removed-from-the-play-store-without-any-warnings-or-additional-details-due-to-not-meeting-the-requirements-for-the-family-program/) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2023-09 · parenting app** — removed after repeated Families rejections; opted out by setting the target audience to 18+, but "if Google decide that your app is targeting children (IE the content of the app looks like it targets children) then they may reject that". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/233493888) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2023-07 · Unity game for children and adults** — Play Games Services plugin v11 auto sign-in fired before the neutral age screen; only v10's manual sign-in avoided it. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76592904/how-to-disable-automatic-sign-in-google-play-games-services-android-unity) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07

### Deceptive Behavior / Misleading Claims / Impersonation / Metadata
- **2024-05 · app suspended over its icons** — "Violation of Deceptive Behavior policy… Launch / On Device Icon… Hi-res icon… Make sure that the app's title and description do not misrepresent the function of the app… Apps must not attempt to mimic functionality or warnings from the operating system or other apps." [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78516018/google-play-console-app-suspended-due-to-deceptive-behavior-policy-violation-on) (medium, en) → AND-META-01
- **2024-04 · video-downloader app** — same wording on "In-app experience" + "Hi-res icon"; cause: the icon contained the YouTube Shorts, TikTok and Instagram logos; replaced with a neutral icon → solved. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/268988793) (high, en) → AND-META-01
- **2024-05 · "AI Image Describe" (GPT-4 vision wrapper), first app of a new corporate account** — suspended outright: "Hi-res icon… Title (en-US): 'AI Image Describe'… In-app experience… We do not provide guidance on bringing your app(s) into policy compliance." Appeal reply narrowed it to Misleading Claims: "your app icon and title in the store listing information is currently different from what is actually provided by the app." [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/274440583) (high, en) → AND-META-01, META-02
- **2023-08 · ride-booking customer app (Tripbd)** — Deceptive Behavior citing the full description reused verbatim from the sibling driver app plus an in-app screenshot; the driver app with the same text stayed live. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/231189177) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2023-08 · speed-camera app** — previous app suspended (the word "measure"); the rewritten successor ("calculates the approximate average speed of the vehicle using the built-in camera") rejected again under Deceptive Behavior on short + full description. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/217884354) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2025 · "All Video Downloader" (4-year-old app, package org.tiktok.*)** — suspended: "We have identified strong indications of deceptive behaviour, such as: Account irregularities and/or inconsistencies in common with characteristics of malicious actors; Deceptive marketing, practices, features, or claims; Misrepresentation or inaccurate descriptions of functionality." Developer traced it to the "private browser… download anonymously" claim. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/377033791) (medium, en) → META-02, AND-META-01
- **2025-08 → 09 · EMI/loan calculator** — warnings for Misleading Claims (description promising SIP goals / sharing) and Device and Network Abuse (in-app screenshot), fixed and approved on 14 Aug; an appeal was filed anyway on 16 Aug; on 22 Aug the app was suspended under Deceptive Behavior with the same "strong indications" boilerplate. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/375020086) (medium, en) → META-02, AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2025-11 · grocery-delivery app** — approved, then suspended days later with the same three-bullet "strong indications of deceptive behaviour" boilerplate; the identical app passed Apple; no asset named. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/388301310) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2025-10 · matrimony app** — suspended again for Deceptive Behavior two months after an unexplained account termination was reversed; reinstated after an appeal following the community's appeal guide (~1 month). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/377401385) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2025 · SMS-parsing finance app (1.8 M downloads)** — suspended with the boilerplate; appeal denial added "functionally impossible", metadata, mimicking OS warnings and device settings — still no specific asset. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/372027903) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2024-09 · Chinese-developed app** — "Your application has violated the policies of 'malware' and 'deceptive behavior'… the application functions may not be clear enough and may include hidden, dormant, or unspecified features… Applications use some means to evade application review". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/299068141) (medium, en) → CONTENT-10, AND-QUALITY-02
- **2023-11 · game still in closed testing** — developer account terminated for Deceptive Behavior → "Malware, Mobile Unwanted Software & Behavior Transparency"; suspected cause: the guest-login feature saved a blank image named with the account ID into shared media; no prior warning; appeal "in-process" for weeks. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/244882486) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02, PRIV-04
- **2023-09 · document app** — suspended under Deceptive Behavior after adding an unencrypted document-password field, a third-party PNG as icon and an OTP SMS provider. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/237132807) (medium, en) → META-02, AND-META-01
- **2025 · palm-reading app** — first "Violation of Metadata policy: Do not use language that is not related to the function or purpose of your app, including language that reflects or suggests Google Play performance, ranking, accolades or awards, user testimonials, or price and promotional information. For example, 'Best', '#1', 'Top', 'New', 'Discount', 'Sale', 'Free', or 'Million Downloads'." for "Join Thousands… top-rated palm reading app"; then repeated Misleading Claims rejections. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/343307498) (medium, en) → AND-META-01, META-02
- **2024-10 · French police-reference app (GPro)** — "Your app doesn't provide a clear source of government information or its description lacks an easy-to-see disclaimer stating that the app doesn't represent a government entity." rejected repeatedly despite disclaimer + data source; experts: written authorization from the government is what passes. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/299947351) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, META-02
- **2025-02 → 03 · US veterans non-profit app (Mission Roll Call)** — repeated Misleading Claims (government affiliation) despite a DISCLAIMER listing every .gov domain; passed after adding an in-app "about" page with the disclaimer and making every URL in the description fully formed (reviewers copy them verbatim). ~1 month. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/324747482) (high, en) → META-02, META-04
- **2023-10 · government-info aggregator** — appeal rejected with "Identify a clear source of government information." highlighted; expert: either declare it as a government-linked app with proof or make it not look governmental. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/238772495) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, META-02
- **2023-09 · Bangladeshi app embedding government websites** — Misleading Claims / government disclaimer; expert: embedding someone else's website requires proof of permission. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/234125372) (medium, en) → CONTENT-03, META-02
- **2023-08 · Bangladeshi government-job-circular app** — same rejection after adding an independence disclaimer to the full description. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/229109777) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2024-11 · e-commerce app** — "The functionality or feature(s) outlined in the app description are not present in the app experience" ("Make payments directly through UPI, QR code and Net Banking"); third rejection → account suspended for repeated rejections. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/305874693) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2024-11 · QCrash (two years live)** — Misleading Claims after a bug-fix release; expert example: "maps for the stadiums" when a few had none → "maps of most stadiums"; features under development must not be in the description. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/305466314) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2024-05 · e-learning app (LMS)** — Misleading Claims naming only two store screenshots; expert: state what is free vs. subscription-only in the description. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/276383586) (medium, en) → META-02, PAY-02
- **2024-07 · football-club app** — "the app's icon or title when installed on the device differs from what is displayed in the Play Store" + sections without content; expert: hide empty sections or show "coming soon", and remove absent features from the description. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/283303054) (medium, en) → AND-META-01, CONTENT-01
- **2024 · app promoted from an old closed track** — same icon-differs text; the screenshot showed the icon of a paused closed-testing bundle from years earlier. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/285392161) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-11, AND-META-01
- **2024-08 · app** — icon mismatch fixed by changing only the store asset; no new bundle needed unless the app was removed; review took 15 days. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/288803492) (medium, en) → AND-META-01
- **2024-05 · offers app with dynamic backend data** — Misleading Claims on screenshot vs. in-app; expert: mark screenshots as representative ("Current available offers"). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/272199799) (medium, en) → META-01
- **2024-11 · level-based platformer** — screenshots of locked levels flagged as misleading; expert: caption "Level 12" outside the device frame or give reviewers access. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/308904442) (medium, en) → META-01
- **2024-10 · white-label sports app** — "Your app is improperly categorized…" citing the hi-res icon and an in-app launcher icon rendering white; cause: adaptive/monochrome icon resources not scaling; regenerating icons in Android Studio without monochrome layers → approved. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/303497212) (high, en) → AND-META-01
- **2024-09 · new app** — store screenshots showed screens that do not exist in the app ("Take Control" with a dancing figure, graphs). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/299480935) (medium, en) → META-01
- **2025-03 · educational app** — Misleading Claims alert quoting an old description with a removal deadline; cause: an internal test track from 2022 still active; after cleaning it the app was reinstated within 30 minutes. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/327956071) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2024-04 · deals/coupons app** — misleading: ChatGPT, Copilot and CodeWhisperer icons/names shown in-app as if they were the app's features (ads not labelled as ads). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/272019156) (medium, en) → META-02, ADS-01
- **2024-01 · WebView app for a trust** — "The app title or description does not accurately describe the app's functionality": the description described the organisation, not the app. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/253666034) (medium, en) → META-02, CONTENT-03
- **2023-11 · class-scheduling app** — rejection upheld after appeal: "Developers must provide a clear and well-written description of their app which describes their in-app each functionality"; another developer in-thread: "Metadata Policy: Identical title and description". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/246344262) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2024-05 · warranty-management app** — same title/description text; expert warnings: multiple rejections can trigger suspension; the word "battery" may read as a phone-battery-booster claim. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/274647998) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2025-02 · "Wingezz" app** — Impersonation: "Your app is currently impersonating the asset 'Wingezz' without proper authorization… You may also provide an authorization letter on the entity's official letterhead with verifiable information and signature"; a CEO letter was not accepted. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79406738/how-to-resolve-app-rejection-from-the-play-store-due-to-the-impersonation-policy) (medium, en) → AND-META-01
- **2022-10 · app with 1 M downloads** — removed, then suspended a day later for Impersonation (logo/title "copied"); appeal rejected pending written permission that cannot exist because no such brand is registered. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73916840/how-to-get-written-permission-or-letter-for-googles-impersonation-policy) (medium, en) → AND-META-01
- **2022-07 · app suspended for Impersonation** — all Console controls disabled, no way to upload a fix; appeals answered with copy-paste. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73159652/app-suspended-from-google-play-but-cannot-update-the-app-to-rectify-it) (medium, en) → AND-META-01
- **2026-08 · Brazilian checkout app (On Checkout)** — Metadata policy on a pt-BR screenshot; fix plan: clearer title/description/images, remove vague or generic images. [source](https://github.com/ControleOnline/app-community/issues/284) (medium, pt) → META-01
- **2022-03 · Japanese free/paid app pair** — notice under the "user ratings, reviews, and installs" policy: "price or promotional text in app titles, icons, or developer names"; the title contained 「無料」(free) to distinguish the free edition; removing the word restored the app. [source](https://zenn.dev/shyne/articles/9f95cf7feb2e1a) (medium, ja) → AND-META-01
- **2022-08 · Wear OS internal-testing build** — "App doesn't mention Wear OS in store listing and also All wear SS are transparent"; fix: add "this app is for Wear OS" to the description and export JPG screenshots. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73226949/wear-os-build-internal-testing-got-rejected-from-google-play-store) (high, en) → AND-META-02
- **2022-06 · Trivially (40-language app)** — removed twice by automated enforcement without warning: an Amharic title violating the title guidelines (7 days offline), then missing reviewer credentials. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=31781613) (high, en) → AND-META-01, AND-CONSOLE-03

### Ads
- **2023-08 · app** — "Violation of Deceptive Ads policy… Our review found that your app doesn't appear to comply with this policy because it contains: Ads that aren't clearly labeled." [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76853720/how-to-fix-it-issue-found-violation-of-deceptive-ads-policy) (medium, en) → ADS-01
- **2025-07 · app removed** — "Deceptive Ads policy… Ads that impersonate any app feature, such as notifications or warning elements." — an interstitial shown when the user tapped Settings; removed after being live for a while; fix: no ad on that tap; an appeal "won't matter" once fixed. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/356072096) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2022-02 · app** — malformed e-mail "Property muws_disruptive_ads_minor.POLICY_NAME not found" = Disruptive Ads (minor). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/71038950/how-to-solve-property-muws-disruptive-ads-minor-policy-name-not-found) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2025-08 · file-manager app** — failed the "better ads experiences" review for an interstitial when tapping Download; expert: users must expect the ad — relabel the button "Download after watching an ad". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/364294927) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2023-12 · app** — Disruptive Ads for interstitials between activities; expert: "you can't show interstitials on the start of activities". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/249548953) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2023-10 · fitness app** — Disruptive Ads with no class/layout named; expert: an interstitial that appears when a button should navigate is "unexpected"; show the ad on the interstitial-close callback only when the user expects it. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/239066224) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2023-11 · app** — Disruptive Ads persisted after removing every ad request; mediation networks suspected. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/245247144) (medium, en) → ADS-02, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2025-02 · audio app with an App Open ad on every foreground** — rejected; removing all ads still rejected → an older bundle (v48) was still active. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/325873255) (medium, en) → ADS-02, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2024-10 · app** — "Disruptive Ads" twice although the app has no ads; the fix was deactivating older bundles so only one compliant bundle stayed active. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/300034529) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2024-01 · app** — appeal reply: "Your app contains ads triggered by the back button or other features explicitly designed to exit the app, which violates our ads policy." — still rejected after removing back-button ads. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/252800535) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2023-06 · VPN/tunnel app after the UMP SDK update** — appeal: "Ads that are triggered by the back button or other features explicitly designed for exiting the app." — the interstitial sat on the DISCONNECT button. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/222120841) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2023-11 · strategy-builder app with rewarded interstitials (identical to the live version)** — rejected for "ads on button clicks"; the real cause turned up in the AdMob dashboard (ad-serving restriction notice), fixed there and approved. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/242005904) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2024-12 · app** — appeal reply: "The way the ads are being shown - Out of context activities with ads may appear if the app is sent to the background." although all ads were removed. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/310096401) (medium, en) → ADS-02, AND-CONSOLE-11

### Broken functionality / Sign-in details
- **2024-08 · app with a clean pre-launch report** — rejected "App installs, but doesn't load" / "App loads, but crashes"; cause found only after installing the Play-signed build from the internal testing track: a third-party library crashed accessing storage. Lesson: the pre-launch report runs on the upload-signed build, the review on the Play-signed one. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/291010390) (high, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2025-03 · Flutter apps upgraded past 3.24.5** — rejected as "crashing or not loading" (ANR) with no logs; some shipped 3.24.5 instead; Flutter closed it (others' 3.29 apps pass). [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/165052) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-01 · app** — "Your app contains content that isn't compliant with the Broken Functionality policy. App loads, but crashes" with no stack trace, device or screenshot; appeal repeated "For example, your app loads, but crashes." [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77912051/google-play-store-rejects-updates-for-android-apps-due-to-broken-functionality-p) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-03 · app** — same; R8/ProGuard stripping the AWS HTTP client ("Unrecognised suffix for the AWS http client class") suspected. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78146900/google-rejection-because-of-violation-of-broken-functionality-policy) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01, AND-BUILD-01
- **2024-05 · app** — "App contains icon(s) or button(s) that are not responsive or do not return content." with an IN_APP_EXPERIENCE screenshot. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78528845/my-recent-app-update-has-been-rejected-for-not-adhering-to-the-google-play-devel) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2023-10 · Flutter app** — same text for a decorative logo in the app bar (looks tappable, does nothing). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77236340/app-contains-icon-or-button-that-are-not-responsive-or-do-not-return-content-in) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-03 · TV + mobile app** — same text, no screenshot, no pre-launch report for the version; support ticket opened. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/263467151) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-04 · app** — "App doesn't install"; experts: run the internal-testing pre-launch report, add loaders for slow network calls. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/266950974) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-07 · app rejected twice, no screenshots** — pre-launch report: "Double splash screen" + 157 accessibility warnings; expert: request the notification permission after the home screen loads, not on the splash; "Google sometimes suspend apps after 3 consecutive rejections". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/288394910) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01, AND-PERM-11
- **2025-04 · app rejected three times** — "App is not responding: Your app is freezing or becoming unresponsive for users, or users cannot scroll" then "Problems installing: Your app does not install for users"; nothing in Android vitals. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/335168223) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-09 · Korean app with 10 k DAU** — "앱에 응답하지 않거나 콘텐츠를 반환하지 않는 아이콘 또는 버튼이 포함되어 있습니다" (icons/buttons that do not respond or return content): the reviewer tested with networking off and hit bare error screens. Fix: error message + refresh button on those screens; several appeal rounds (replying to the appeal-decision e-mail reaches the policy team). [source](https://velog.io/@qqqqlss/%EA%B5%AC%EA%B8%80-%ED%94%8C%EB%A0%88%EC%9D%B4%EC%8A%A4%ED%86%A0%EC%96%B4-%EC%A0%95%EC%B1%85%EC%9C%84%EB%B0%98-%EB%8C%80%EC%B2%98) (medium, ko) → CONTENT-01, PERM-02
- **2024-10 · church app with a Live section** — rejected because the Live player has nothing to play when no broadcast is running; expert: submit while streaming, and expect spot checks later. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/302836749) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-04 · safety-procedures app** — suspended "solely due to multiple violations of the broken functionality policy"; support silent for 6 days. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78389619/seeking-solutions-for-app-suspension-in-google-play-console-due-to-broken-functi) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-11 · long-lived app requiring phone number + code** — first rejected for no reviewer account, then "installs and loads, but is unresponsive"; expert: after self-testing from the internal track, wait 24 h and resubmit. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/306580940) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, CONTENT-01
- **2024-12 · Google's own checklist post** — Broken Functionality: install, load, run without crash/freeze, responsive icons/buttons, features "function properly"; "does not have any empty or blank pages that lack appropriate disclaimers or error messages"; "multiple rejections may lead to your app getting suspended". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/309771939) (high, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2022-09 · second-screen game (4-digit code from a desktop game)** — warned of removal without credentials; fix: a permanent demo code in the password field, step-by-step notes and a short video of reaching the code. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73877210/second-screen-app-in-violation-with-google-play-developer-policies) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2023-03 · subscription-gated app** — rejected for credentials despite a licence-tester Google account; the second notice was an unfilled template ("{If a dev provided testing credential…}"); use App access instructions + "Any other instructions". [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75612560/android-app-rejected-on-play-store-due-to-login-credentials-how-to-give-review) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, PAY-04
- **2022-11 · Flutter app with Google sign-in only** — "Invalid or incomplete login credentials"; the reviewer's Google test account kept hitting "Verify it's you"; resolved by supplying a demo video of the functionality. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/74630711/invalid-login-credentials-while-google-play-reviews-flutter-app) (medium, en) → ACC-03
- **2022-02 · app with Google-login only** — "Need login credentials for app review" twice although the dedicated account had all security checks off. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/71113634/google-play-reviewer-constantly-fails-to-login) (medium, en) → ACC-03
- **2024-10 · app** — "Families Policy Requirements: Login Credentials… Your login credentials must be accessible at all times, reusable, and valid regardless of user location… must be provided in English… We could not access the in-app content with the login credentials that you have provided." Cause: older releases carried old credentials; cleared old releases, submitted App content first, then the build. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/304629805) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2024-04 · Web3 wallet app (seed phrase, no username)** — rejected for credentials; advice: describe the wallet-creation steps and a sample password in "Any other instructions". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/271534727) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2026-07 · business app with OTP login** — reviewers could not pass two-step verification: the 15-character reviewer bypass code was untypeable in a 6-digit numeric OTP field; rotated to a 6-digit code and rewrote Sign-in details. [source](https://github.com/Mingla-LLC/mingla-main/pull/824) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2024-10 · Chinese publishing guide** — seven causes of "Login Credentials" rejections despite a test account: expired; non-ASCII; third-party login only; purchase/subscription or card required; region-locked; biometric gate; extra profile data required (e.g. vehicle details). [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7431261409020903474) (high, zh) → AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2022-02 · StreetComplete** — rejected for in-app links to Patreon/Liberapay/GitHub Sponsors (Payments policy) and for not supplying OpenStreetMap login credentials. [source](https://github.com/streetcomplete/StreetComplete/issues/3768) (high, en) → PAY-01, AND-CONSOLE-03

### Payments / subscriptions
- **2025-10 · ntfy** — Payments policy with a 22 Oct deadline: hiding the Donate button (rejected — wrong version reviewed), removing it in all tracks (rejected — docs link), then removing every link to ntfy.sh/GitHub and the "copy topic address" feature → accepted 14 Oct; the external-offers programme is EEA-only. [source](https://github.com/binwiederhier/ntfy/issues/1463) (high, en) → PAY-01, AND-PAY-01, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2022-05 · e-learning app selling courses through PayTM** — "Your app uses a non-Google Play's billing system to accept payment for access to in-app features or services, including any app functionality, digital content or goods". [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/72168657/our-application-used-paytm-for-payment-transaction) (medium, en) → PAY-01
- **2022-06 · subscription-only app** — "Your app does not comply with the Subscriptions policy: The dismiss button in your app is not clearly visible and users may not understand that they can access functionality without signing up for the free trial"; appeal explaining that nothing exists outside the paywall was accepted in 24 h, update approved the next day. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/72518243/google-play-compliance-violation-dismiss-button-not-clearly-visible) (high, en) → PAY-02
- **2023-02 · app on Billing v5** — rejected under the Subscriptions guidelines because the reviewer's paywall showed "Free": for new users the first pricing phase is the trial. Fix: display the first phase with `priceAmountMicros > 0`. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75351643/in-app-billing-v5-wrong-pricing-in-android) (high, en) → PAY-02

### Health / financial / organization-account declarations
- **2025-04 · app in Health & Fitness** — upload API: "Google Api Error: Invalid request - You must complete the health declaration." (form under Monitor and improve > Policy and programs > App content). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79580041/where-do-i-go-to-fill-out-the-google-app-health-declaration-form) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2023-11 · Flutter health app** — upload blocked: "You need approval to use the following Health Connect permissions in your app" (READ_STEPS, READ_HEART_RATE…) until the Health Connect developer declaration is approved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77450970/how-can-i-resolve-the-health-connect-permissions-approval-required-in-play-cons) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2025-12 · home-healthcare app** — "Your app is not compliant with the Play Console Requirements policy. Some types of apps can only be distributed by organizations." even after converting to an organization with D-U-N-S; only a support ticket cleared it. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79857424/google-play-console-app-rejected-for-organization-account-required-even-after) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02
- **2025 · "earn money"/rewards app, 4 rejections** — each time told it needs an organization account; the developer removed rewards, then coins, then renamed to XP and recategorized as a game — still rejected. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/357725359) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02, AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2026-08 · Expo festival app** — rejected under the health-features policy: `expo-sensors` injects `ACTIVITY_RECOGNITION` (Pedometer module), so Play classified the app as health/fitness against its "no health features" declaration. Fix: `blockedPermissions`. [source](https://github.com/rlerikse/bigfamfestival/pull/212) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, AND-PERM-13
- **2026-08 · fitness app (MotiveLife)** — "Insufficient Information to Determine App Functionality for Health Connect"; fix: strip unused data types (cadence, writes), show Health-Connect-derived tiles, add a HEALTH section to the description, complete the Health apps declaration, then ship a new AAB. [source](https://github.com/mazensamhat/motivelife/pull/403) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09

### Build: 16 KB, target API, signing
- **2025-09 · Android app** — Play still reported no 16 KB support after AGP 8.5.1/Gradle 8.7; cause: Android Studio Narwhal 2025.1.1 mis-checks AAB alignment (APKs fine). Fix: AS 2025.1.3, NDK r28, Gradle 8.14.2. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79754556/my-app-bundle-in-android-studio-2025-1-2-supports-a-16-kb-page-size-but-google) (high, en) → AND-SDK-02
- **2025-11 · Flutter app** — hard block "Your app does not support 16KB memory page sizes" despite AGP 8.12/NDK 29; cause: a plugin's 4 KB-aligned `.so`; find it with Analyze APK and upgrade/replace the plugin. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79820066/flutter-android-16kb-page-size-support-issue-google-play-console-hard-rejectio) (medium, en) → AND-SDK-02
- **2025-09 · Unity + Vuforia AR app** — `libUnityDriver.so` and `libVuforiaUnityPlayer.so` still 4 KB-aligned after Unity 6000.2.2 / Vuforia 11.3.4. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79756424/google-play-rejecting-unity-android-aab-not-supporting-16-kb-page-size) (medium, en) → AND-SDK-02
- **2024-08 · Codename One app** — "Your app currently targets API level 33 and must target at least API level 34… App updates with these issues will be rejected… Fix by Aug 31" — the framework had no 34 build yet. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78865629/does-cn1-support-android-14) (medium, en) → AND-SDK-01
- **2023-09 · app with key rotation** — release blocked: "There is a previous APK signed with key rotation, but this release introduces an upgrade…" (proof-of-rotation); Google Engineering confirmed a known issue and whitelisted the app. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77156457/unable-to-release-app-to-play-updates-from-google-play-console) (medium, en) → AND-BUILD-01
