### Closed testing, verification, suspensions and terminations
- **2024-05 · new personal account** — after applying: "your app is not ready for production yet. Again run your closed test for 14 days more"; advice: upload new releases during the test and reply to every tester's feedback. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78541674/i-applied-for-production-of-android-app-but-app-isnt-ready-for-production-yet-w) (medium, en) → AND-TEST-01
- **2024-07 · developers verifying accounts (+7, +49, +94…)** — "We can't verify your phone number right now. Try again later." Workarounds that worked: add the number as recovery/2-step phone first, incognito session, voice call instead of SMS, a number from another country; a dedicated verification-issue form exists. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78779711/we-cant-verify-your-phone-number-right-now-try-again-later-google-play-consol) (high, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-01
- **2024-08 · Spanish-speaking developer** — account restricted after verification: "You must accurately provide your developer account information, including… Legal name and address, Contact email address and phone number…"; Play Console, payments profile and ID documents must match exactly. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78822694/how-to-handle-google-play-developer-account-restriction-due-to-identity-verifica) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-01
- **2024-01 · Indian trust account** — "Google couldn't verify your identity" after PAN/Aadhaar, then licence + bill; document name/address must match character-for-character; upload an image, not a PDF. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77910825/play-console-verification-issue) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-01
- **2025-02 · company on an individual account since 2016** — all apps removed pending personal-ID verification; only a new organization account + app transfer remained. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79470402/issue-with-google-play-console-individual-account-instead-of-organization-app) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02
- **2024-04 · first submission ever** — "Violation of Enforcement Process policy… Suspension can occur as the result of egregious or multiple policy violations, as well as repeated app rejections or removals… you can submit an updated, policy compliant app using a new package name and a new app name." [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78343440/app-suspension-in-google-play-store-without-a-specific-reason) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2025-04 · app** — whole account suspended for a "prior violation of associated, previously terminated account" after inviting a Gmail user tied to a terminated account; appeal reinstated the account, but the already-approved app stayed removed with no listed issue. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79585794/google-play-app-removed-after-account-reinstatement-how-to-identify-and-fix-p) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2025-04 · food-ordering app** — removed for Deceptive Behavior, no fix allowed; experts: republish under a new package name after fixing. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/341387692) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2025-11 · Japanese solo developer** — account suspended a week after launch: 「危険性の高いパターンまたは不正使用のパターンが検出された」(high-risk or abusive patterns detected). Suspected cause: after rejections the app had been resubmitted under a package name differing by one digit plus a changed app name, read as review evasion. Reinstated ~5 weeks after an appeal explaining the history. [source](https://zenn.dev/daaaichifu/articles/291f0517a3c215) (high, ja) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2022-01 · Simple Keyboard (10-year-old account)** — terminated "as outlined in previous emails" although no violation e-mails existed; reinstated ~10 days later after press coverage, with no reason given. [source](https://github.com/rkkr/simple-keyboard/issues/333) (high, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2022-03 · Riders app (1 M users)** — payments profile suspended for two months; support: "this profile must stay suspended due to violations of our Terms of Service. We're unable to discuss the specific circumstances"; developer entity in Russia. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=30861147) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-01
- **2026-02 · UpScrolled (social app)** — suspended; the company expanded moderation and reporting tooling while "working with Google Play"; not yet reinstated at posting. [source](https://newsroom.upscrolled.com/blog-posts/view/5919/update-upscrolled-suspended-from-google-play) (medium, en) → UGC-02, UGC-03
- **2022-07 · Flutter app with JPush (极光推送)** — rejected because of the JPush SDK (reason shown only as a screenshot); fix: swap in JPush's `google_play` SDK jars (jcore/jpush *-google_play.jar) inside `jpush_flutter/android/libs` and its build.gradle. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7115298654214324255) (medium, zh) → AND-PERM-13, AND-QUALITY-02

### Android TV / form-factor eligibility
- **2022-05 · Unity game opting into Android TV** — "Your app exhibits installation issues. Apps should run successfully when the user selects the application banner from the home screen… If your app is only intended for specific devices please include a disclaimer in your store listing"; appeal returned the same text. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/72311306/google-play-android-tv-release-type-installation-issues-rejection-notice) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2022-07 · WebView app for TV** — TV eligibility rejected for "vertical letterboxing"; fixes: landscape-only, Leanback/NoTitleBar theme, 10 % overscan margins. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73178835/android-tv-layout-eligibility-issue-on-play-store) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01

---

## Cases from Japanese and Chinese sources (2nd pass, 2026-08-28)

Sources: Qiita, Zenn, note.com, hatena, DevelopersIO (JA); juejin, cnblogs, zhihu, Tencent Cloud, DCloud, V2EX (ZH). CSDN was unreadable (anti-bot) and is not cited. Medium = partial/undated/guide-style.

### Apple — 1.2 UGC
- **2023-12 · 5ch forum reader (JA)** — 1.2 (+2.3.10, 2.1): "ユーザーが即座にフィードから投稿を削除できる仕組み" demanded although there are no accounts. Fix: user keyword filter hiding threads/posts; "hide" pushes title/poster ID into the filter; in-app docs mentioning Google Play → external browser; unfinished "⋯" button fixed. [source](https://zenn.dev/taki/articles/bf236e1aed8804) (high, ja) → UGC-02, UGC-04, CONTENT-04, CONTENT-01
- **2022-07 · local-life services app (ZH)** — 1.2 + 5.1.1(v): "拉黑操作只简单了hud提示，用户动态还是存在的，结果被拒了" — block was only a toast; posts stayed. Fix: real block removing content + account deletion. [source](https://cloud.tencent.com/developer/article/2109301) (high, zh) → UGC-04, ACC-02
- **2024-05 · new app flagged as 马甲包 (ZH)** — 4.3(b) → 4.3(a) → 1.2; "最后一次审核被拒是 1.2，添加了屏蔽和举报就过了。" [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7365766277918212105) (medium, zh) → CONTENT-07, UGC-03, UGC-04
- **2023-04 · Expo app (JA)** — 1.2/5.1.1 bundle: account deletion, block, report, 24-hour response, InfoPlist not localized, no sign-in info in notes. [source](https://zenn.dev/watty/articles/7089a29ad2f164) (medium, ja) → UGC-03, UGC-04, UGC-05, ACC-02, IOS-PLIST-01, ACC-03

### Apple — 2.1 completeness / IAP wiring / demo accounts / iPad
- **2026-07 · Flutter kids routine app + RevenueCat (JA)** — 2.1(b) ×3 "Family Pack（アプリ内課金）が見つかりません", 2.1(a) settings "unresponsive", 1.5, 2.3.10, then 3.1.1. Causes: settings behind a 3-s long-press with no feedback; RevenueCat **App Store Connect API key (.p8) never uploaded** → empty offerings; restore button unreachable after purchase. Fix: progress ring on tap, upload key (no new build), permanent "購入を復元する" in settings. 4 rejections, build 2→5, 5 days. [source](https://qiita.com/tkn_works/items/c3457aab17e3be4f7a2a) (high, ja) → PAY-05, PAY-03, CONTENT-01, CONTENT-05
- **2026-06 · Flutter meal planner + RevenueCat (JA)** — 2.1 IAP not submitted + EULA missing; "Guideline 5.2" subscription terms missing from description; iPad compatibility-mode layout broken. Fix: IAP with version, EULA in description, `UIRequiresFullScreen`; lost 4 days because "App Reviewに再提出" was never pressed after replying. [source](https://qiita.com/tkn_works/items/4d2c98ba3b6af32dbcc6) (high, ja) → PAY-05, PAY-02, IOS-IPAD-01, META-07
- **2025-11 · Flutter TCG card-search app, Supabase (JA)** — 4.8 "uses a third-party login service (Google Sign-In), but does not offer Sign in with Apple"; 2.1 "in-app purchase products have not been submitted for review" ("審査と一緒に提出" unchecked); 5.1.1 "collects user data (email address, name, and purchase history), but does not have the required privacy information in App Store Connect". 3 rejections. [source](https://qiita.com/kazu_developer/items/d0189f8f902c5adb56bb) (high, ja) → IOS-LOGIN-01, PAY-05, IOS-PRIV-01
- **2026-07 · Expo word-puzzle + AdMob + RevenueCat (JA)** — 2.1 ATT not shown on iPadOS (requested before the app was active → `undetermined`); 2.1(b) "Remove ads" errored because the **Paid Applications Agreement was not in effect** → same build approved the next day after completing bank/tax. [source](https://qiita.com/satoyoshidev/items/115617483b09882369fb) (high, ja) → PRIV-05, PAY-05
- **2026-01 · first IAP app (JA)** — 2.1 "one or more of the in-app purchase products have not been submitted for review": IAP "審査準備完了" but never attached to the version. [source](https://zenn.dev/beef_stew/articles/f63ad1a050fc4c) (high, ja) → PAY-05
- **2026-07 · subscription app (JA)** — 2.1 "課金アイテムが提出されていません": each IAP "審査に提出" created a *separate* submission draft; only the app-only draft was sent. Fix: add items to the existing draft (提出物（2）…). [source](https://note.com/appdev_study/n/n4045c85b7b7c) (high, ja) → PAY-05, META-07
- **2026-07 · point-purchase app + RevenueCat (JA)** — R1 4.8; R2 "Apple reviews In-App Purchase products in the sandbox… Confirm you have a Paid Apps Agreement in effect." Cause: RevenueCat webhook env Production-only → sandbox purchases granted nothing. Fix: "Both Production and Sandbox". [source](https://note.com/09papa/n/n4d339ee2aed5) (high, ja) → IOS-LOGIN-01, PAY-05
- **2026-08 · contact-reminder app (JA)** — 4 IAP rejections whose wording never named the cause: **product ID mismatch** between StoreKit code and ASC. [source](https://note.com/juicy_kiwi3473/n/n5f1eb71c3814) (high, ja) → PAY-05
- **2026-08 · app with IAP + external link (JA)** — IAP not registered for review; text link "タップしても無反応" (tiny tap target, opened externally without feedback); pre-permission button "許可する" read as steering → "続ける". [source](https://note.com/shimogun_8560/n/n0365aa140926) (high, ja) → PAY-05, CONTENT-01, PERM-02
- **2026-06 · Google-Calendar journal app (JA)** — 2.1(a): reviewer hit Google's "Verify it's you" from a US IP even with 2FA off. Fix (no code): enable 2FA on the demo Google account and list **backup codes** in the reply. [source](https://qiita.com/downshimau/items/dca6f53dcb5ce2df57b9) (high, ja) → ACC-03
- **2025-11 · two apps (JA)** — 2.3.3 mood screenshots; 5.2.5 "iPhone" in subtitle → "スマホ"; 2.1 crash in an edge case; 2.1 demo video for the location feature. [source](https://zenn.dev/yutaka520/articles/8c7cfdb8e435fd) (high, ja) → META-01, META-02, CONTENT-01, META-05
- **2026-08 · quote-widget app (JA)** — 2.1(a) "It displayed a blank window when tapping 画像でシェア button." on iPad Air 11-inch (M3); iPhone-only app never tested on iPad. [source](https://note.com/genial_moraea252/n/n10bd2d474cb0) (high, ja) → IOS-IPAD-01, CONTENT-01
- **2022-07 / 2023-03 · fintech app (ZH)** — 2.1 "required to verify the image indefinitely upon sign-in and unable to skip"; 2.1 "your binary includes the PassKit framework for implementing Apple Pay, but we were unable to verify any integration"; 2.5.13 third-party face recognition must use LocalAuthentication; ops "禁用了海外IP". [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7116301510887079967) (high, zh) → CONTENT-01, IOS-ENT-04, IOS-UI-02, CONTENT-09
- **2025-08 · BLE health-hardware app (ZH)** — 9 rejections: 1.4.1 (blood-pressure data needs device registration docs), 2.1 network error at login, 2.1 "Film the demo video to show how the physical Apple device and the designated hardware are paired together", 5.1.1. Advice: during a 2.1 dialogue reply in Resolution Center, don't resubmit. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7542768051970080811) (medium, zh) → META-05, CONTENT-01, META-07
- **2023-05 · "network" 2.1 rejections (ZH)** — "We discovered one or more bugs in your app when reviewed on iPad… on Wi-Fi": Apple's screenshot showed no Wi-Fi icon — reviewer had Wi-Fi off; polite reply → approved. Tip: even version numbers for review builds so backend logs isolate reviewer activity. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7230737732733091901) (medium, zh) → CONTENT-01, ACC-03
- **2023-02 · app promoting a WeChat mini-program (ZH)** — 3.2.2 for promoting the mini-program; Bluetooth feature too hidden → demo video; ops opened IPv6/foreign access for 3 days. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7201806164391280697) (high, zh) → IOS-UI-03, META-05, CONTENT-09

### Apple — ATT prompt not found (2.1 / 5.1.2)
- **2026-05 · Flutter board game + AdMob (JA)** — 2.1 "ATTのアクセス許可要求が見つかりませんでした" (AdMob links ATT); R2 "Guideline 4 - Design": permission text in English on a Japanese device. Fix: `app_tracking_transparency` before `MobileAds.initialize()`; `InfoPlist.strings` per language. ~1 week. [source](https://zenn.dev/anpiropac/articles/3d6746e2be766c) (high, ja) → PRIV-05, IOS-PLIST-01
- **2026-08 · Expo ToDo-RPG (JA)** — R1 3.1.2 (EULA link missing from **App Description**); R2 2.1 "unable to locate the App Tracking Transparency permission request": requested during splash → `undetermined`, never retried. Fix: `AppState === 'active'`, retry, settings entry, recording. [source](https://qiita.com/ninomaeDev/items/d56129c46c227d09f6cc) (high, ja) → PRIV-05, PAY-02, META-05
- **2026-08 · casual game with rewarded/interstitial ads (JA)** — 5.1.2(i) + 1.5 on iPhone 17 Pro Max: ATT requested only when an ad had loaded, but AdMob serves nothing to unpublished apps → never asked. Fix: ask at the first game-over regardless of inventory; real support page. [source](https://note.com/skazuyuki/n/ncd6f73914655) (high, ja) → PRIV-05, CONTENT-05
- **2026-08 · trash-day app with ads (JA)** — ATT not found on the review iPad (SwiftUI foreground hook never fired) + empty support page. [source](https://note.com/ykdigitalworks/n/n650cf7e65bc7) (high, ja) → PRIV-05, CONTENT-05
- **2026-05 · app with an AdMob banner slot (JA)** — ATT tied to a banner mount that never mounted. Fix: request at root after active; init ads only after ATT. [source](https://note.com/claudegptai/n/n8af00ea8817c) (high, ja) → PRIV-05
- **2026-07 · Minecraft-server monitor (JA)** — 5.1.2(i) ×2 (custom consent screen rejected; FirebaseAnalytics bundled `GoogleAppMeasurementIdentitySupport`) + 4.1(c) for "Minecraft" in the **subtitle**; ASC then: "アプリにはNSUserTrackingUsageDescriptionが含まれており…" → declared analytics as tracking. [source](https://qiita.com/jqit-yukiono/items/91755838ea8589fde90d) (high, ja) → PRIV-05, PRIV-02, META-02
- **2023-05 · Chinese app (ZH)** — 5.1.2: reviewer wanted the reason for data collection stated. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7234700513224015932) (medium, zh) → PRIV-02

### Apple — 2.5.4 background modes
- **2026-08 · text-call app (JA)** — 2.5.4 audio on iPad Pro 11 (M4) / iPhone 17 Pro Max. Apple's options: recording of the background mode working (attached to the reply **and** kept in notes for future submissions), or remove `audio`. Author's earlier app was rejected for a **leftover** audio mode. [source](https://zenn.dev/artemis_rh/articles/977455018a03e5) (high, ja) → IOS-BG-01, META-05
- **2026-08 · English-conversation app (JA)** — 2.5.4 "declares support for audio in the UIBackgroundModes key… unable to play any audible content"; reviewer never reached the listening screen. Fix: recording; audit found a paused state keeping `.playback` alive. [source](https://qiita.com/Okojyo-Soft/items/c36860071e9f6308c08c) (high, ja) → IOS-BG-01, META-05
- **2026-07 · Expo SDK 56 workout log (JA)** — 2.5.4 audio although `app.json` never mentions it: `expo-audio` plugin defaults `enableBackgroundPlayback: true` (also injects `NSMicrophoneUsageDescription`); `expo-notifications` adds `aps-environment`. Fix: plugin option false, local notifications, strip the entitlement; verify with `npx expo config --type introspect`. [source](https://qiita.com/aijidokalab/items/5e79e1976a6f2709e50f) (high, ja) → IOS-PLIST-02, IOS-ENT-04
- **2026-05 · background-location pollen app (JA)** — 2.5.4 "宣言した機能を実際に使用していない" for an extra `UIBackgroundModes` entry. Rule: one declared mode per used feature; same wording in usage string, App Privacy and policy. [source](https://qiita.com/YujiNaramoto/items/3e31965b57b1d8949d16) (high, ja) → IOS-PLIST-02, PERM-01
- **2024-05 · video app with PiP (ZH)** — 2.5.4 "If the app has a feature that requires persistent audio, reply… and let us know how to locate this feature" → video of PiP; guest mode with its own token for IAP. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7363499693119668264) (medium, zh) → IOS-BG-01, ACC-01

### Apple — privacy manifest (ITMS-91053 / 91056 / 91061 / 91064)
- **2026-08 · Unity + AdMob game (JA)** — ITMS-91064 ×4 "Invalid privacy manifest - The PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy file for UnityFramework…": merged manifests set `NSPrivacyTracking=true` with **empty** domains; source edits overwritten at build. Fix: post-archive script aligning flag/domains + re-sign. [source](https://zenn.dev/dragonikko/articles/c61bbeaa701811) (high, ja) → IOS-PRIV-04
- **2026-07 · meetup app + AdMob (JA)** — ITMS-91064 "NSPrivacyTracking must be true if NSPrivacyTrackingDomains isn't empty." Fix: flag true + `googleads.g.doubleclick.net`. [source](https://zenn.dev/hosa97/articles/8a1d5b1824decb) (high, ja) → IOS-PRIV-04
- **2024-04 · Flutter apps (ZH)** — "ITMS-91053: Missing API declaration - Your app's code in the 'Runner' file": needs Flutter ≥ 3.19; plugins with `static_framework = true` land in Runner; prefer `use_frameworks!` or merge all `*.xcprivacy`. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7349895521395884069) (high, zh) → IOS-PRIV-03
- **2024-04 · iColors (ZH)** — ITMS-91053 fixed with `NSPrivacyAccessedAPITypes` (FileTimestamp DDA9.1). [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7356186685824106507) (high, zh) → IOS-PRIV-03
- **2024-05 · iOS 17 adaptation (ZH)** — duplicate `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` → "ITMS-91056: Invalid privacy manifest". [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7365771381676949538) (medium, zh) → IOS-PRIV-03
- **2024-11 · app with SDKs (ZH)** — "ITMS-91061: Missing Privacy Manifest" naming SDKs. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7442540521435136015) (medium, zh) → IOS-PRIV-03
- **2024-03 · Four Cropper (JA)** — submitted without a manifest on purpose: warning "Although submission for App Store review was successful, you may want to correct the following issues…"; the check runs on App Review submission, not upload. [source](https://dev.classmethod.jp/articles/received-email-warning-privacy-manifest/) (high, ja) → IOS-PRIV-03

### Apple — 3.x business
- **2023-03 · Flutter大学 subscription app (Stripe web) (JA)** — 3.1.3(b): 8-year web-only login model rejected; "Reader" request refused. Fix: IAP. [source](https://zenn.dev/flutteruniv_dev/articles/8dd36bd29daf3c) (high, ja) → PAY-01
- **2023-07 · OpenAI-API app (JA)** — 3.1.1 because users could paste their own API key (paid API = payment outside IAP). [source](https://qiita.com/Shota-Abe/items/880d32f29c4d989053b0) (high, ja) → PAY-01, AI-01
- **2022-08 · CSDN app (ZH)** — 3.1.1: lottery prizes were third-party virtual goods (video/music VIP). Fix: no third-party virtual prizes during review. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7136119504131588103) (high, zh) → PAY-01
- **2023-06 · subscription app (JA)** — 3.1.2 "– A functional link to the Terms of Use (EULA) – A functional link to the privacy policy"; "If you are using the standard Apple Terms of Use (EULA), you will need to include a link to the Terms of Use in your App Description." [source](https://qiita.com/Shota-Abe/items/cf2584bd6896e97128d5) (high, ja) → PAY-02
- **2026-07 · Expo + RevenueCat (JA)** — 4.8 Google-only; 3.1.2(c) no EULA/privacy links in the purchase flow; 2.1(b) subscribe button greyed: `eas build` doesn't read `.env` → RevenueCat key absent → empty offerings. [source](https://qiita.com/niconiconainu/items/d9b2b51d210ecfd25d06) (high, ja) → IOS-LOGIN-01, PAY-02, PAY-05
- **2026-04→07 · castle-stamp app (JA)** — 2.1(b) "アプリが無料トライアルを広告しているにもかかわらず、決済時にユーザーへ提供されていない": hard-coded "7日間無料で試す" while the store product had no trial. Fix: all price/trial text from StoreKit product data. [source](https://note.com/yasuda_forceai/n/nb53187c86815) (high, ja) → PAY-06, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2025 · subscription app (ZH)** — 3.0: confirm "Weekly Pro" pricing; 3.1.2: weekly-equivalent price more prominent than the actual annual billed amount → redesign paywall. [source](https://zhuanlan.zhihu.com/p/23305569476) (medium, zh) → PAY-02
- **2023-02 · finance-tool app (ZH)** — 3.0: justify a 239.99 USD IAP → explained → approved; 4.8: WeChat login was hidden when WeChat isn't installed so iPad reviewers never saw it — an iPhone reviewer did → Sign in with Apple required. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7196915437807042617) (high, zh) → IOS-LOGIN-01, META-05
- **2024-05 · Chinese app (ZH)** — 2.3.2 IAP not disclosed in description/screenshots. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7400319811358113828) (medium, zh) → META-02
- **2024-05 · shopping-memo iPhone+watchOS app (JA)** — rejected for making Apple-Watch sync a paid option; made free → approved. [source](https://zenn.dev/kabeya/articles/c8b3b706425e92) (medium, ja) → PAY-01

### Apple — 4.8 / 4.0
- **2025-02 · Google-only auth app (JA)** — "login service design" rejection ~11× over 2 months; Google auth fails the rule because its scope returns the email; Apple wrote that Apple ID auth satisfies it. [source](https://qiita.com/su3-hokkaido/items/4ee662358d9184bba84e) (high, ja) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2026-05 · beauty personal-color app, Supabase (JA)** — R1 5.1.1 no deletion; R2 SIWA missing; R3 privacy manifest missing; Metadata Rejected: App Privacy said "Email: not collected" while Supabase stores email; demo account absent. 3 rejections, 12 days. [source](https://zenn.dev/mintototo1/articles/buildlog-03-app-store-reject) (high, ja) → ACC-02, IOS-LOGIN-01, IOS-PRIV-03, PRIV-02, ACC-03
- **2024-09 · app with email + third-party login (ZH)** — 4.8 "does not offer as an equivalent option another login service with the following features:" — email/password alone rejected; phone-OTP also reported rejected. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7329699704094736395) (high, zh) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2024-04 · consumable-IAP app, no login (ZH)** — reviewer could not restore consumables → added third-party login → 4.8 → added Apple login → rejected again because the Apple button didn't follow HIG. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7363091659104124963) (high, zh) → IOS-LOGIN-01, PAY-03
- **2025-08 · Chinese guide (ZH)** — 4.8 rejections for button style ("必须要明确体现出Apple的Logo", equal prominence); SIWA apps need a visible deletion entry. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7543143650962243611) (medium, zh) → IOS-LOGIN-01, ACC-02
- **2026-05 · Tauri v2 macOS app (JA)** — "Guideline 4 - Design: required Quit option missing in App Menu"; a rejected version number can't be reused. [source](https://qiita.com/rkpg/items/9a2a1416cb9ca3e651e2) (high, ja) → IOS-UI-01, META-07

- **2022-11 · game (ZH)** — 2.3.7 "app name includes the following trademarked term or popular app name" + 4.1; "please remove all third party trademarks and reference before resubmission." - **2026-07 · Sudoku home-screen widget (JA)** — 4.3(a) on first submission with **empty review notes**; same build resubmitted with notes + recording + reply explaining WidgetKit gameplay → approved. [source](https://zenn.dev/mizapps/articles/2be1cebad5bb6b) (high, ja) → CONTENT-07, META-05

### Apple — 5.x legal / metadata / process
- **2025-09 · YouTuber tag-search app (JA)** — 5.2.2 ×2 (third-party data; "citing similar apps" rejected) → public channel data per YouTube API ToS; 2.1 logout not found; 5.1.1 forced signup → guest; 4.0 iPad layout **even with iPad unchecked** ("Appleユーザーは全デバイスで使えることを期待"); 4.2.2; 2.3.10 "Android" text. 4 rejections, ~1 month. - **2024-12 · YouTube Data API app (JA)** — 5.2.3 → reply describing ToS-compliant use. [source](https://qiita.com/SK3620/items/ae49b5fc4c26ddc47e1d) (medium, ja) → META-02
- **2024-06 · corporate app with AI feature (JA)** — "Guideline 5 - Legal": AI features require a licence for mainland China; options: remove AI, deselect China mainland, or documentation → deselected China. [source](https://qiita.com/odendayoko/items/3a3f932f7f203a7633c1) (high, ja) → CN-01, AI-01
- **2025-02 · app (JA)** — 1.1: screenshots showed only women → added a male screenshot. [source](https://qiita.com/razuma/items/58e359f30f6104aac901) (medium, ja) → META-01
- **2025-02 · Flutter app (JA)** — 2.3.8 no app icon; 2.3.10 debug banner visible in screenshots. [source](https://qiita.com/msdsm/items/3bfdc70eaedd8c805ebb) (high, ja) → META-01, CONTENT-02
- **2025-05 · preview video (JA)** — "プレビュー動画にフレームが表示されている" for a black in-app border; approved by reply. [source](https://zenn.dev/suzuki_kawasaki/articles/d5c2fcd70060ef) (high, ja) → IOS-META-01
- **2026-08 · watch+iPhone app (JA)** — 5.2.5 Apple product name in subtitle; a Resolution Center reply did **not** restart review: "However, in order for us to proceed with the review of your app, please resubmit the app for review in App Store Connect." [source](https://zenn.dev/herodini777/articles/4cc3bff80c9e03) (high, ja) → META-02, META-07
- **2022-12 · in-house submodule apps (JA)** — rejected for an unused HealthKit package merely present in the project → optional packages. [source](https://zenn.dev/offy/articles/874b115d40cd89) (high, ja) → IOS-ENT-04
- **2026-08 · 12-app pipeline (JA)** — ITMS-90111 because archives were built on beta macOS; en-US locale name collision. [source](https://qiita.com/bokuwalily/items/45aecde70e4174aa9eac) (medium, ja) → IOS-META-04
- **2025-03 · 2.3.1 self-check (ZH)** — 2.3.1 triggered by a Umeng SDK code path; after removing hidden payment also strip "wechatpay、alipay" keywords from binary/metadata. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7485507817186279465) (medium, zh) → CONTENT-10
- **2023-05 · 2.3.1 scenarios (ZH)** — keys like "isReview","verified","isHidden","isSwitch" and metadata promising absent features raise 2.3.1. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7233293200948445243) (medium, zh) → CONTENT-10

### Google Play — metadata / launcher ≡ listing / minimum functionality
- **2026-08 · Expo app (JA)** — "問題: 誤解を与える表現に関するポリシーへの違反 / 理由: アプリ ストアの掲載情報が一致しない — インストールされたアプリのアイコンまたは名称が、ストアの掲載情報と異なる". Cause: Expo template adaptive-icon PNGs never replaced; app name differed. Fix: 4 assets, `app.json` name, `prebuild --clean`. [source](https://note.com/tonari_ai_jp/n/nc5f36d89cd8f) (high, ja) → META-06, AND-META-02
- **2026-05 · sleep-sound app + 2 cert-study apps (JA)** — three apps suspended "アプリ拒否スパム: Repeated app rejections" after a run of metadata rejections (blank promo images, ja-JP icon ≠ launcher icon, "アプリは静的であり、アプリ固有の機能が含まれていません", "購入フロー内で目立つ価格の通貨が異なる"); Publisher API `403 The app is suspended.` Lessons: close one issue per resubmission; read back Console assets; don't re-create under a new package. [source](https://note.com/claudegptai/n/n06ca8699fb1e) (high, ja) → AND-ENFORCE-01, META-06, CONTENT-03, PAY-06
- **2022-03 · long-published app (JA)** — unpublished for "「ユーザーの評価、レビュー、インストール」のポリシーに違反": 「無料」 in the title (price text). Renaming fixed it. [source](https://zenn.dev/shyne/articles/9f95cf7feb2e1a) (high, ja) → META-02, AND-META-01
- **2024-03 · Wear OS app (JA)** — rejected for transparent screenshot backgrounds, missing scrollbars, no splash, listing section title must be exactly "Wear OS", UI cut off at edges. [source](https://qiita.com/tomo1139/items/09d266a6d136cbe3391b) (high, ja) → AND-META-02, AND-QUALITY-01
- **2026-05 · two cert-study apps (JA)** — "目立つ価格の通貨が購入フローと異なる": hard-coded 「月額500円」 vs localized cart currency. Fix: `priceString` from the store as the single source; no CTA until price loads. [source](https://note.com/claudegptai/n/nc60708e9be5a) (high, ja) → PAY-06
- **2026-05 · two info apps (JA)** — "static or generic". Fix: input→process→result→save loop. [source](https://note.com/claudegptai/n/n717a42e5d0de) (high, ja) → CONTENT-03

### Google Play — broken functionality / sign-in / privacy
- **2024-04 · karaoke app (JA)** — 14 rejections alternating "反応しないボタンがある" / "クラッシュする"; privacy-policy site flagged editable (comments enabled); real bug: 1-char Kotlin error on older Android. Tip: a Toast on every button so Google reports "crash" not "unresponsive". [source](https://qiita.com/mamime/items/142ee4a9fcc8b16afe99) (high, ja) → CONTENT-01, PRIV-01
- **2024-04 · Google-Auth app (JA)** — repeated "cannot log in": only the debug SHA-1 registered in Firebase; add the Play App Signing SHA-1; Google demanded a test Google account. [source](https://zenn.dev/tatukane/articles/abcd9eb58c16aa) (high, ja) → AND-REVIEW-01, ACC-03
- **2026-08 · app (JA)** — 3 差し戻し then "アプリの削除／再申請不可": reviewer IP (Manila) blocked by WAF geo-restriction; second rejection for a network error state. Appeal format: "指摘項目 → 原因 → 対応" per item. [1](https://zenn.dev/mtatsum/articles/19b077328fd305) [2](https://zenn.dev/mtatsum/articles/5d97e671e3bb3e) (high, ja) → CONTENT-09, AND-CONSOLE-03, AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2024-10 · "Login Credentials" guide (ZH)** — expired credentials, non-ASCII passwords, third-party-only login, paid gates, geo-locked accounts, biometric gates; "谷歌审核人员不会为了访问开发者的应用而创建账号". [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7431261409020903474) (medium, zh) → AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2025-07 · company + personal accounts (ZH)** — first company account terminated "高风险" ~10 days after supplying login info; a reviewer typed 'o' as '0' in the demo password → compliance notice. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7526240014499151908) (high, zh) → AND-CONSOLE-03, AND-ACCOUNT-01
- **2026-02 · Memos client (ZH)** — production access rejected after 12×14: privacy policy didn't cover Firebase/AdMob → updated → approved within hours. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7603994823327531018) (high, zh) → PRIV-01
- **2025-09 · app with Adjust SDK (JA)** — "お客様のアプリがユーザーデータを収集、共有する方法が、データ セーフティ フォームで申告された方法と一致していません… アプリのアクティビティ - デバイス上のアプリ"; fix: explicitly remove the installed-apps permission, ship, appeal → lifted. [source](https://zenn.dev/corrupt/articles/b5acb62a1ef915) (high, ja) → PRIV-02, AND-PERM-02
- **2023-09 · Unity kids-friendly app (JA)** — "アプリのストア掲載情報ページおよびアプリ内でプライバシー ポリシーにリンクする必要があります" + Family policy. [source](https://lanvill.hatenablog.com/entry/2023/09/22/223320) (high, ja) → PRIV-01, AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2023-05 / 2023-12 · Unity Ads games (JA)** — Data safety rejected until Unity Ads' collection was declared; Dec 2023 data-deletion section notice. [1](https://yasu9780.hatenablog.com/entry/2023/05/07/065839) [2](https://yasu9780.hatenablog.com/entry/2023/12/03/144253) (medium, ja) → PRIV-02, AND-CONSOLE-08
- **2022-10 · Unity app (JA)** — removed with "プライバシーポリシーのURLが無効です" (dead link — periodic check). [source](https://kan-kikuchi.hatenablog.com/entry/Invalid_privacy_policy_URL_Android) (high, ja) → PRIV-01, META-04
- **2024-09 · app (JA)** — "ターゲット ユーザー情報が欠落している、または不正確である" + "コンテンツの繰り返し" suspension. [source](https://applog.hatenablog.com/entry/202409051215) (medium, ja) → AND-CONSOLE-07, CONTENT-07
- **2024-03 · closed-test app (JA)** — "1画面内にバナー広告が複数" (Families ad-format) → one banner per screen. [source](https://qiita.com/naganoworks/items/f09942b2e98a9f4ad681) (high, ja) → ADS-02, AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2024-12 · social/live apps (ZH)** — mass removal "Violation of Policy Coverage and Inappropriate Content policies". [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7447729959890714661) (medium, zh) → UGC-07

### Google Play — permissions / Chinese SDKs / enforcement
- **2022-08 · app (ZH)** — QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES declaration rejected; warning persisted after removal → support cleared it. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7137864762045497358) (high, zh) → AND-PERM-02, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2025-02 · app (ZH)** — QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES kept being flagged: an **old AAB in the internal-testing track**. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7472786501597331475) (high, zh) → AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2023-06 · uni-app apps (ZH)** — removed: DCloud runtime contains APK-install code (`plus.runtime.install`) even without `REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES`; Google scans code, not just permissions. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7249000144092020792) (high, zh) → AND-PERM-13, AND-PERM-15
- **2025-10 · uni-app apps (ZH)** — "「照片和视频权限」政策：对该权限的使用与您应用的核心用途没有直接关系" → `chooseSystemMedia` (photo picker). [source](https://ask.dcloud.net.cn/question/214473) (high, zh) → AND-PERM-08
- **2022-11 · app (ZH)** — REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES (strict since 2022-09-29) and MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE; install code inside third-party utils also fails. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7167291367289257991) (medium, zh) → AND-PERM-15, AND-PERM-03
- **2022-07 · app (ZH)** — READ_SMS rejection; request at point of use. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7122760052095582244) (medium, zh) → AND-PERM-01
- **2024-08 · Android 14 target (ZH)** — FGS type must be declared; even `mediaPlayback` needs a Console explanation; `specialUse` strictest. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7399273700117200946) (medium, zh) → AND-PERM-05
- **2023-01 · uni-app (ZH)** — "Change your app's target API level to at least 31". [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7185463878715703333) (high, zh) → AND-SDK-01
- **2026-01 · Unity Android (JA)** — sudden "デバイスやネットワークでの不正行為に関するポリシー違反" on a clean build: a stale closed-test release built with Unity ≤ 6000.2.6f1 (Unity security issue, blocked from 2025-12-08). Fix: replace old releases in every track. [source](https://qiita.com/kitasyunpp/items/3189e375525934d43758) (high, ja) → AND-CONSOLE-11, AND-QUALITY-02
- **2023-11→2024-02 · company account (JA)** — account terminated with all apps removed (MVP game, "same assets" suspected); restored after 1 month via appeal + Google Play Biz DM; a 2nd suspension from an *unpublished* app; subscriptions auto-cancel during suspension; delete unmaintained apps. [source](https://qiita.com/hukusuke1007/items/1e5352a9bfdd08be3ab1) (high, ja) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2024-09 · apps (ZH)** — "Issue found: Violation of Enforcement Process policy" for repeated rejections. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7414118825434087474) (medium, zh) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2024-11 · malware causes (ZH)** — reflection, JS-exposed native bridges, outdated SDKs, thin H5 apps. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7438477875361873959) (medium, zh) → AND-QUALITY-02, AND-SEC-01
- **2025-01 · 3-year log (ZH)** — wrong demo password → 下架; account association → 封号; malware from an old SDK → 封号; icon/name impersonation → 暂停. [source](https://juejin.cn/post/7459572989442801727) (medium, zh) → AND-ENFORCE-01, ACC-03, AND-META-01

### Google Play — closed testing / 16 KB
- **2026-08 · TWA game (JA)** — 3 production-access rejections: "クローズドテストにテスターが関与しなかった" / "フィードバックを収集し、アップデートで対応するというベストプラクティスを実践しなかった"; 84 days. `assetlinks.json` needed the **Play app-signing** SHA-256 and GitHub Pages `.nojekyll`. [source](https://note.com/reverstargo/n/n5ae0a265ed21) (high, ja) → AND-TEST-01, AND-BUILD-01
- **2026-08 · workout app (JA)** — 2nd/3rd rejections: opted-in 12 but installed-user graph dipped to 9; 7-day retention 1–2 devices; "直近の14日間" vs "審査日から14日間". [1](https://note.com/aoharu_dev/n/nd07080c14258) [2](https://note.com/aoharu_dev/n/nd296e28c45e9) (high, ja) → AND-TEST-01
- **2026-07 · StepLock (JA)** — same reasons; only 10/12 actually installed, feedback via LINE invisible to Google → use the Console feedback email, ship 2–3 updates. [source](https://note.com/tanaka_hagatatsu/n/n3392032c7a29) (high, ja) → AND-TEST-01
- **2024-11 · (JA)** — "製品版にアクセスするには、アプリのテストをさらに行う必要があります" loops another 14 days; emulator/multi-account tricks blocked since ~2024-10. [source](https://zenn.dev/gomorepython/articles/28bec0a5e81182) (high, ja) → AND-TEST-01
- **2026-08 · Google Groups testers (JA)** — "権限がありません": approval-only group, no Join button in the LINE browser, non-Google emails, wrong Play account. [source](https://note.com/rapid_pothos2835/n/n55b66afa6637) (high, ja) → AND-TEST-01
- **2026-05 · paid app (JA)** — testers were charged: closed-test list ≠ License testing list. [source](https://zenn.dev/ikumin/articles/a04dc8108ff625) (medium, ja) → AND-TEST-01
- **2025-11 / 2026-07 / 2025-09 · 16 KB (JA)** — NDK app upload failed until alignment; Expo SDK 55 (RN ≥ 0.77) cleared it; Unity `lib_burst_generated.so` → update Burst. [1](https://zenn.dev/rg687076/articles/eb3e4f19a44c28) [2](https://zenn.dev/peipei/articles/fac98be46c7173) [3](https://zenn.dev/toto_tantanto/articles/3a3e254a5c2009) (high, ja) → AND-SDK-02

## Apple — additional cases (2nd pass, 2026-08-28: Stack Exchange API, GitHub issues/PRs, HN, Zenn/Velog)

~188 cases (en 166 / ja 15 / ko 1 / zh 1; 4 medium). GitHub issues and PRs from 2025–2026 quote Resolution Center text verbatim (submission IDs, review devices). Apple Developer Forums and Reddit were unreachable in this pass.

### 4.5.4 Push notifications
- **2026-08 · Flutter events app (OneSignal)** — 4.5.4 Design – Apple Sites and Services. "The app does not request and obtain the user's consent before sending push notifications. To resolve this issue, please revise the app to obtain the user's consent prior to delivering notifications in the app." Cause: the permissions screen was skipped on one sign-in path and OneSignal initialized + POSTed the device token at cold start before any consent. Fix: consent gate before any push registration (`OneSignal.consentRequired`). Repeat rejection, iPad Air 11" M3. [source](https://github.com/kolabing/kolabing-app/issues/126) (high, en) → PUSH-01
- **2022-11 · Cordova travel-diary app (e-mission/OpenPATH)** — 4.5.4. "We noticed that your app requires push notifications in order to function. Push notifications must be optional and must obtain the user's consent to be used within the app." Cause: onboarding would not proceed unless notifications were enabled (silent push used for trip detection). Fix discussed: let the user continue after denying, with a warning. [source](https://github.com/e-mission/e-mission-docs/issues/827) (high, en) → PUSH-01, PERM-02
- **2022-12 · Expo app live for a year** — 2.5.1. "uses public APIs with Notification Center in a manner not prescribed by Apple… the content displayed is not consistent with the purpose of Notification Center" although the app never used Notification Center. Unresolved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/74864320) (high, en) → PUSH-01, IOS-UI-02
- **2024-01 · Flutter app with Crashlytics only** — "Missing Push Notification Entitlement" (ITMS-90078) treated as a rejection; `IS_GCM_ENABLED=false` did not help (firebase_messaging linked transitively). Unresolved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77762511) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-01

### Export compliance / binary validation
- **2023-02 · watchOS standalone app (Xcode 14.2)** — "Missing Compliance" stayed on every TestFlight build although `ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption = NO` was in the Info.plist; the key was not read for the watch target. Unresolved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75426955) (high, en) → IOS-META-03
- **2022-09 · Swift app rewritten in Expo (same bundle ID)** — ITMS-90433 "Invalid Swift Support - The file libswiftAVFoundation.dylib doesn't have the correct code signature" on upload. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73783568) (high, en) → IOS-META-04
- **2025-03 · Flutter app** — ITMS-91061 "Missing privacy manifest… OrderedSet.framework" pulled in by `flutter_inappwebview_ios`; workaround: fork the plugin / upgrade Flutter 3.24. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79505711) (high, en) → IOS-PRIV-03
- **2025-04 · Flutter app (TestFlight review)** — ITMS-91061 for `flutter_local_notifications.framework` despite an app-level manifest (plugin must ship its own). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79580538) (high, en) → IOS-PRIV-03

### 2.5.x Software requirements: private/deprecated APIs, downloaded code, emulators, recording, background modes, hidden features
- **2024-06 · Mac App Store app bundling Python 3.12** — 2.5.2. "The app installed or launched executable code. Specifically, the app uses the itms-services URL scheme to install an app." Cause: the literal string `itms-services` in `Lib/urllib/parse.py(.pyc)`; Apple named the files only after an appeal. Fix: strip the string; CPython added `--with-app-store-compliance` (3.13). [source](https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/120522) (high, en) · [LWN](https://lwn.net/Articles/979671/) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2024-06 · UTM SE PC emulator** — rejected for the App Store and for notarization for third-party stores; Apple's position "PC is not a console" (4.7 only covers retro game consoles). Approved 2024-07 after public pressure. [source](https://itsfoss.com/news/apple-blocks-utm-se/) (high, en) · [HN](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=40627155) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2024-04→07 · iDOS 3 DOS emulator** — 4.7. First "Design spam"; then "The app still provides emulator functionality but is not emulating a retro game console specifically"; two-month phone call could not say what to change; appeal denied 2024-07-16. [source](https://litchie.com/2024/04/new-hope) (high, en) → CONTENT-07
- **2024-07 · social app with HTML5 mini-games ("Sticky")** — 4.7. "offering HTML5 games appears to be the primary purpose of your app"; after appeal a phone call walked that back and cited "changed the app concept" + emulator rules instead. Unresolved. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=40991796) (high, en) → CONTENT-03
- **2026-02 · YouTube-Shorts blocker (NEVPNManager + local root cert)** — 2.5.1: using a VPN profile or root certificate to block content in third-party apps is "not appropriate"; appeal denied; released via TestFlight. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=46988635) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2025-04→2026-05 · macOS dictation app (WhisperPad)** — 2.4.5: auto-pasting into other apps through the Accessibility API. 2 rejections + appeal; shipped an App Store edition (clipboard only) and a direct version. [source](https://www.mitmllc.com/blog/apple-rejected-my-dictation-app/) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2026-07 · Slint/winit macOS app** — 2.5.1 private API `_CGSSetWindowBackgroundBlurRadius` referenced by winit though never called (no dead-strip). Fix: patched winit fork + `check-private-apis.sh` in the build; also added missing PrivacyInfo. [source](https://github.com/ByteApps/graffito/pull/21) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02, IOS-PRIV-03
- **2023-09 · legacy iOS app** — ITMS-90809 "Deprecated API Usage… UIWebView" caused by an old AppsFlyerFramework pod. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77193262) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2023-09 · .NET MAUI app** — ITMS-90338 "Non-public API usage… _ucal_*, _ucol_*" (ICU symbols) from Xamarin.Firebase.iOS.Database; fix: removed that package (FirebaseDatabase.net instead) → accepted. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77110112) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2026-08 · security-camera app "SentryBlack"** — 2.5.14 rejected for stealth "screen black" recording framing. Fix: screen dims to 25 % (never 0) with a non-disableable REC indicator; support/marketing page still said "screen blacked out" and had to be scrubbed. [source](https://github.com/mwsupplylimited/mwsupplylimited.github.io/pull/8) (high, en) → CONTENT-08, META-04
- **2023-12 · app with `external-accessory` background mode** — 2.5.4. "declares support for external-accessory in the UIBackgroundModes key… but does not declare any external accessory protocols… supported protocols must be included in the UISupportedExternalAccessoryProtocols key… and the hardware's PPID # should be provided in the Review Notes". [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77626489) (high, en) → IOS-PLIST-02, META-05
- **2023-01 · Flutter just_audio_background** — 2.5.4 audio mode "unable to play any audible content… in the background". Fix: replied with a video of background playback → approved without a new build. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75008240) (high, en) → IOS-BG-01, META-05
- **2026-08 · messaging/calling app (Japan)** — 2.5.4 same wording. Fix: screen recording showing audio continuing after pressing Home, now attached to every submission. 1 round. [source](https://zenn.dev/artemis_rh/articles/977455018a03e5) (high, ja) → IOS-BG-01, META-05
- **2025-02 · Unity game with AdMob mediation** — 2.3.1. "The app contains hidden features. Specifically, we still detected hidden payment options in the app. We have detected mintegra, maio, mobilefuse payment method found in the binary" (mediation adapters); a commenter also got "hidden sign-in code". [source](https://github.com/googleads/googleads-mobile-unity/issues/3729) (high, en) → CONTENT-10, IOS-ENT-04
- **2026-06 · Expo app with expo-updates** — Guideline 4 Design: the in-app OTA banner ("A new version… is ready / Tap to reload") read as an app-update prompt not linking to the App Store; OTA itself was not the problem. Fix: headless silent updater. [source](https://github.com/shortbird/pathweaver_2.0/pull/65) (high, en) → CONTENT-10, IOS-UI-01
- **2026-08 · self-hosted trading-journal client (Expo)** — 5.6 Developer Code of Conduct. "the app contains features that appear to have been intentionally hidden during the review process." Cause: server address hidden behind an "Advanced" gear (review notes said "tap Advanced") and a visible "Change server" button removed the day before. Fix: server address as the first sign-in field. [source](https://github.com/sinhong2011/TraderMemos/pull/225) (high, en) → CONTENT-10
- **2026-08 · university sharing app** — 5.6 hidden paths: shake-only entry point (+ Motion permission), UI-test/backend overrides compiled into release. Fix: removed; feature/safety map in Settings. [source](https://github.com/EgorChulanov/UniShare/pull/10) (high, en) → CONTENT-10
- **2026-07 · Chinese campus super-app (uni-app)** — 2.3 / 4.7: the App Store build may still fetch remote config but must not let it re-enable hot updates, downloadable module bundles/mini-games or `customJs` injection. [source](https://github.com/superdaobo/mini-hbut/issues/382) (high, zh) → CONTENT-10
- **2026-08 · call-recording app (RN, China storefront)** — Guideline 5: MIIT requires CallKit to be inactive on the China App Store and Apple flags the framework linkage itself. Fix: removed CallKit; detect calls via `AVAudioSession.interruptionNotification`. [source](https://github.com/mieweb/pulse/pull/159) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04
- **2026-08 · Japanese keyboard extension (Sumibi)** — Guideline 5: the word "OpenAI" in metadata triggered a China AI-regulation concern; fix: excluded China from availability. Also 2.1 Information Needed (7 items). Both resolved with no code change. [source](https://zenn.dev/kiyoka/articles/japanese-input-method-sumibi-22) (high, ja) → META-02, META-05

### 1.x Safety (objectionable content, physical harm, Kids, anonymous UGC)
- **2026-07 · alcohol-tracking social app** — 1.4 Physical Harm. Apple: the app "is marketed as a blood alcohol content calculator". Cause: BAC code was already gone; the store screenshot showing friends labelled "1h sober / 2Y sober" read as a sobriety calculator. Fix: label "Last session", new screenshots. [source](https://github.com/PetrCala/Kiroku/pull/1509) (high, en) → META-01, META-02
- **2026-07 · fitness app pairing Bluetooth heart-rate straps** — 1.4.1 (medical) + 2.1(b) + 2.3.2 + 3.1.2(c). Fix: "consumer fitness accessories, not medical hardware" statement, "(Pro)" inline labels, T&C/privacy links in the description, paywall copy "POTS tracking… research thresholds" instead of "testing… clinical criteria"; screen recording. [source](https://github.com/austinspaeth/autonomic/pull/41) (high, en) → META-02, PAY-02, PAY-04
- **2026-08 · Expo app reviewed on iPad despite `supportsTablet: false` (Japan)** — 4 rejections: 2.1(a) and 3.1.2(c) non-functional links, 5.1.1(iv) permission-button wording, 1.4.1 missing source citations. Fix: `expo-web-browser` instead of `Linking.openURL`, error alerts. Approved. [source](https://zenn.dev/noriyuk/articles/c5de6792520bad) (high, ja) → IOS-IPAD-01, PAY-02, PERM-01
- **2026-08 · appearance-scoring app** — 1.2 "objectifying real people" (share card paired the user's face with scores; a "Masculinity" trait) + 5.1.1(iv) camera pre-permission. Fix: no face on share card, trait renamed, "Continue" button, Open Settings path when `canAskAgain=false`. [source](https://github.com/man0l/looxmaxxing/pull/77) (high, en) → UGC-07, PERM-01
- **2026-08 · devotional journal app** — 1.2. The app "enables users to post content anonymously" (a shared-reflections feed had been switched on server-side). Fix: removed the anonymous community feature entirely, not just the flag. [source](https://github.com/rwm6857/GraceChords/pull/469) (high, en) → UGC-07, UGC-03
- **2026-07 · group location-sharing app** — 5.1.2(i): location shown to other users without adequate precautions. Required: block user, explicit declinable sharing consent (separate from the OS prompt), bounded auto-stop tracking, 18+ age rating, privacy-policy URL. [source](https://github.com/skelstar/dot-watcher/pull/82) (high, en) → UGC-04, PRIV-04, META-03
- **2026-04→05 · Micro.blog client "Inkwell"** — 1.2 (no report/block), 2.1(a) SIWA button, 2.1(b) revenue-model questions, 3.1 (no IAP → removed creation/sign-up/external links, US storefront only), 4 (SIWA still prompted for name), 5.1.1(v), 5.2.5 (Apple "Inkwell" trademark) → appeal pending after ~4 weeks. [source](https://www.manton.org/2026/05/19/why-is-inkwell-stuck-in.html) (high, en) · [HN](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48211134) (high, en) → UGC-03, UGC-04, IOS-LOGIN-04, PAY-01, ACC-02, META-02
- **2026-06 · simplified image viewer for children / special needs** — 1.3: listed in the Kids Category but "does not meet the strict designed-specifically-for-kids requirements". Fix: removed the Kids designation, Utilities instead. [source](https://github.com/iammike/simple-image-library/issues/85) (high, en) → IOS-META-06, UGC-07
- **2026-08 · piano-notes app** — 2.3.8 "the app name to be displayed on the App Store includes the term 'Kids'… this app was not submitted as a Kids category app" + 2.1(b) "we cannot locate the In-App Purchases" (premium gating flag was off). Fix: renamed in ASC; flag on. [source](https://github.com/themehrdad/musica/pull/10) (high, en) → META-02, PAY-04
- **2023-04 · Flutter kids game with AdMob** — Kids Category: third-party ads need the provider's "documented practices" (human-reviewed, kid-appropriate); sending AdMob help links was not enough. Unresolved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76041643) (high, en) → UGC-07, ADS-02

### 2.1 Information Needed, crashes, unresponsive UI, iPad, process
- **2026-05 · launcher app, first submission (Japan)** — 2.1 Information Needed, seven numbered items: (1) screen recording on a physical device, (2) devices/OS tested, (3) functions and target audience, (4) setup/access instructions, (5) external services used, (6) regional differences, (7) regulated industry / protected material. Fix: reply mirroring Apple's numbering, "none" where not applicable, recording attached in the reply and in App Review Information → approved with no new binary. [source](https://zenn.dev/artemis_rh/articles/8e3b6466e8f8ee) (high, ja) → META-05, IOS-META-02
- **2026-08 · air-quality app** — 2.1 Information Needed, same seven questions; item 7 answered with the data licence (CC-BY via Open-Meteo). [source](https://github.com/j2447-beep/USAirQMinder/pull/5) (high, en) → META-05
- **2026-08 · social app (Expo)** — 2.1 Information Needed: seven answers (3,923/4,000 chars in Notes) + physical-device recording; Apple asked to see reporting *and* blocking. [source](https://github.com/brickagcopra/libertasian/pull/393) (high, en) → META-05, UGC-03
- **2026-08 · second similar app from the same account (Japan)** — 2.1. "Would you please explain the difference between this app and [App ID]?" Fix: four structural differences in the review notes (input method, use case, min iOS, shared backend by design) → accepted, no rejection. [source](https://zenn.dev/artemis_rh/articles/4c4abaa0f7b042) (high, ja) → CONTENT-07, META-05
- **2023-05 · Flutter in_app_purchase app** — 2.1 Information Needed. "your binary includes the PassKit framework for implementing Apple Pay, but we were unable to verify any integration of Apple Pay" + 3.1.2. Fix: state "no Apple Pay" in Notes. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76209970) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04, META-05
- **2022-05 · Flutter Firebase phone-auth app** — 2.1. Reviewer stuck on reCAPTCHA: "your app still displayed error message when we tried to login" (iOS 15.5). Suggested: Firebase App Check. Unresolved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/72400682) (high, en) → ACC-03
- **2022-08 · PWA/Firebase invitation-only app** — 2.1: Sign in with Apple redirected reviewers back to login (no invitation). Answer: apps cannot require invitations; show a message and give testers another way in. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73217619) (high, en) → ACC-01, ACC-03
- **2024-05 · Xamarin.Forms app** — 2.1 "We were unable to review your app as it crashed on launch" (iPad Air 5, iOS 17.4.1, SIGABRT). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78411539) (high, en) → CONTENT-01, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2022-07 · app whose only change was version strings** — 2.1 crash on launch only on the reviewer's device. Fix: moved `ATTrackingManager.requestTrackingAuthorization` out of AppDelegate into the first view controller → approved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73049778) (high, en) → PRIV-03, CONTENT-01
- **2024-11 · app with Firebase + Facebook SDK** — crash on launch on iPad Air 5: "attempts to access privacy-sensitive data without a usage description". [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79169768) (high, en) → IOS-PLIST-01
- **2023-11 · Expo app** — crash on launch only in review; reproduced with `npx expo start --no-dev --minify` (production bundle) without symbolicating. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77458141) (high, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-02 · app rejected repeatedly for an iPad / iPhone 13 crash** — three videos on the exact devices did not help; no crash log. Unresolved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77985817) (high, en) → CONTENT-01, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-08 · Capacitor practice-log app** — 2.1(a). "The app crashed during review. Steps leading to crash: Tapping 'Take Photo'" (iPad Air M3). Cause: web file input opened the camera with no `NSCameraUsageDescription` — the browser handled permissions in testing. [source](https://github.com/ahmedqahal/movement/pull/2) (high, en) → IOS-PLIST-01
- **2026-08 · SwiftUI food scanner (iPhone-only)** — 2.1(a) on iPad: nested `UIImagePickerController` fallback to photo library crashed (popover required); "Upgrade" rows did nothing because StoreKit returned empty offerings with no error and the paywall was a `.sheet`. [source](https://github.com/armctay85/FitMunch/pull/7) (high, en) → IOS-IPAD-01, PAY-04
- **2026-08 · darts scoring app** — 2.1(a). "The 'Let's Go!' Button does not work when tapped on." Cause: silent return when the name field was empty; the iPad keyboard hid the only message. [source](https://github.com/Rdiger-36/DartScore/pull/116) (high, en) → CONTENT-01, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-07 · bookmark app** — 2.1(a) "In-app URL appear unresponsive" (Save awaited a 60 s server extraction before dismissing) + 2.3.6 age rating (unrestricted-web-access answer never uploaded). [source](https://github.com/noahdevkagan/marks/pull/83) (high, en) → CONTENT-01, IOS-META-06
- **2026-08 · SSH host manager** — 2.1(a) "we were unable to paste the Private Key": a programmatic `UIPasteboard` read raised the "Allow Paste?" prompt. Fix: SwiftUI `PasteButton`. [source](https://github.com/jerryfane/herdrup/pull/155) (high, en) → CONTENT-01, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-08 · Expo SDK 52 social app** — 2.1(a) "App was unresponsive after log in" (iPad Air 5, iPadOS 26.6); nginx logs for the reviewer IP 17.64.126.219 showed login 201 / feed 200 then nothing — RN 0.76 + iOS 26 SDK window model on iPad. Fix: `supportsTablet: false`. [source](https://github.com/brickagcopra/libertasian/pull/408) (high, en) → IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-08 · Istanbul street-cats app** — 2.1(a) screenshot of a "retry" error card: denying location blocked the map; a reviewer in California got `400 invalid area`. Fix: default to the city centre, never block a screen on missing location. [source](https://github.com/tekiristanbul/tekir/pull/268) (high, en) → PERM-02, CONTENT-01
- **2026-08 · student app (Supabase)** — 2.1(a) "we are unable to log in and an error message is displayed": the demo code was valid; the backend answered 522 (pg_cron starving PostgREST). [source](https://github.com/asrbekshokirovich-bot/hanguk-uz/pull/179) (high, en) → ACC-03
- **2026-08 · Unity app with IAP (Japan)** — 2.1 "unresponsive buttons", three separate causes: external account linking required before purchase; unapproved IAP products not returned by StoreKit; sign-in callback failure. Rule: never silently return on failure. Approved after fixes + IAP status. [source](https://zenn.dev/dragonikko/articles/847b09e86816ae) (high, ja) → PAY-04, CONTENT-01
- **2026-07 · Expo + RevenueCat (Japan)** — 2.1(b) subscribe button greyed out: the RevenueCat key was only in `.env`, which `eas build` ignores. Fix: EAS environment variable → passed. [source](https://zenn.dev/nicoinu/articles/revenuecat-expo-ios) (high, ja) → PAY-04, CONTENT-02
- **2026-08 · Expo travel app (Korea)** — 2.1(a) crash on launch (iPhone 17 Pro Max, iOS 26.5.2) + 2.3.8 store name vs on-device name "app" (`CFBundleDisplayName`). [source](https://github.com/Software-Maestro-GTA/Yeolo-FE/issues/30) (high, ko) → CONTENT-01, META-02
- **2026-08 · visionOS app** — rejected because a Safari link "didn't work": visionOS reused a Safari window outside the reviewer's field of view. Approved on appeal. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49216319) (high, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2024-11 · Flutter app** — rejected because it "keeps opening the settings app" on launch during review; unreproducible (suspect early permission request / background mode). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79192279) (high, en) → PERM-02
- **2023-06 · Flutter pharmacy app** — 2.1 "does not load any content on the OnCall, All Pharmacies or Map screen" (iPhone + iPad, iOS 16.5) although fine on TestFlight. Unresolved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76537472) (high, en) → CONTENT-09, CONTENT-01
- **2026-08 · process (Japan)** — a Resolution Center reply alone did not restart review; status stayed "Unresolved Issues" until the version was resubmitted in ASC. [source](https://zenn.dev/herodini777/articles/4cc3bff80c9e03) (high, ja) → META-05
- **2026-06 · Flutter meal planner (Japan)** — 2.1 IAP products not submitted, 3.1.2 subscription disclosures, 2.1 iPad compatibility layout → `UIRequiresFullScreen`; 11 days incl. 4 lost by forgetting the resubmit button. [source](https://zenn.dev/tkn_works/articles/02af285dcc0fab) (high, ja) → PAY-04, PAY-02, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-07 · Expo word-puzzle (Japan)** — 2.1 "AppTrackingTransparency dialog not displaying on iPad" (requested before the app was active) + 2.1(b) "Remove ads" error (Paid Apps Agreement banking/tax incomplete). [source](https://zenn.dev/satoyoshi/articles/mojisand-app-review-diary) (high, ja) → PRIV-03, PAY-04
- **2026-08 · Expo todo app (Japan)** — 3.1.2 "does not include a functional link to the Terms of Use (EULA) in the app's metadata" + 2.1 "unable to locate the App Tracking Transparency permission request" (requested before active; `undetermined` misread). Approved on 3rd submission with a recording. [source](https://zenn.dev/ninomaedev/articles/cb45f50d60f5f7) (high, ja) → PAY-02, PRIV-03

### 2.3 Accurate metadata (2.3.2 IAP promo images & paid features, 2.3.3, 2.3.6, 2.3.8, 2.3.10, DSA)
- **2026-08 · weather app** — 2.3.2: the In-App Purchase promotional image was the app icon; ASC field locked while in review. [source](https://github.com/aaronpeck77/StormPath/pull/21) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-06 · flight-time tracker** — 2.3.2. "Your promotional image is the same as the app's icon." / "You submitted duplicate or identical promotional images for different promoted In-App Purchase products." Fix: distinct 1024² art per product. [source](https://github.com/kilolimallc/legal/pull/1) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-07 · focus app** — 2.3.2 twice: the promoted-IAP image was an app screenshot with hard-to-read text and referenced the price. Fix: brand artwork, no price, bottom-left 30 % kept empty. [source](https://github.com/ZackC-1/still-app/pull/114) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-08 · meal app** — 2.3.2 subscription promotional images; products locked "In Review" so they could not be edited until the review ended. [source](https://github.com/Super60x/CleanPlate/issues/18) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-06 · AI stylist app** — 2.3.2. "the app's metadata refers to paid content or features, but they are not clearly identified as requiring additional purchase." Fix: "(Premium)" labels inline, free-trial scope up front. [source](https://github.com/STGime/live-stylist-ai/issues/57) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-06 · step-count app** — 5.1.1(iv) HealthKit pre-permission screen used a "Connect" button and a "Not now" skip → "Continue", no skip; 2.3.2 promo image = icon. [source](https://github.com/JarlLyng/walkful/pull/64) (high, en) → PERM-01, META-02
- **2026-08 · deck-builder app** — 2.3.8: shipped icon was the design-template placeholder with construction guides. [source](https://github.com/ofshvmin/mtg-deckbuilder/pull/63) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-08 · NavBharatAI** — 2.3.8 "the app icons appear to be placeholder icons." [source](https://github.com/docasheesh-png/navbharatai/pull/2027) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2026-08 · billing app** — 2.3.8 placeholder icons + 1.5 Support URL not functional + 2.1 demo account not email-confirmed + 2.3.3 screenshots. [source](https://github.com/rsalmons12/BC-Billing-Website-/pull/285) (high, en) → META-02, CONTENT-05, ACC-03, META-01
- **2026-08 · video editor (Korea/EN)** — 2.3.10: the iOS "What's New" text mentioned Android (pasted from Play). Fix: release notes in repo + test rejecting Android/Google Play/APK and Korean spellings. [source](https://github.com/Theorvane/openscene/pull/251) (high, en) → CONTENT-04
- **2026-08 · Sonos controller** — 2.3.10 Android in the description + 5.2.1 (Sonos reference) re-raised; cleared by a metadata edit and a reply pointing at the earlier cleared answer. [source](https://github.com/CasperVerswijvelt/Sonority/pull/76) (high, en) → CONTENT-04, META-02
- **2024-06 · app update** — 2.3.3: generator-cropped 5.5" screenshots cut the tab bar so they "did not match the app's functionality". [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=40822467) (high, en) → META-01
- **2025-11 · app rejected under 2.3.6** — set age restriction to "None" in ASC; unclear whether resubmission is needed (answers: a reply sometimes suffices). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79808497) (high, en) → IOS-META-06
- **2026-08 · EU distribution (Japan)** — app approved but absent in EU27 ("トレーダーステータスが提供されていません"): DSA trader status not declared. Fix: ASC > Business > Compliance Requirements → verified in 30 min; contact details become public. [source](https://zenn.dev/dragonikko/articles/341cad8b473779) (high, ja) → (new ID, see patterns)

### 3.1.x Payments (IAP, reader/enterprise exceptions, subscriptions, IAP submission state, crypto, Apple Pay, 4.10)
- **2026-04 · RN app with a Stripe payment link opened in the browser** — 3.1.1. "since the app allows users to purchase premium plan using payment mechanisms other than In-App Purchase, it isn't in compliant… add the ability to pay for them in the app, using In-App Purchase." [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47640956) (high, en) → PAY-01
- **2023-05 · Flutter + Stripe** — 3.1.1. "The subscriptions can be purchased in the app using payment mechanisms other than in-app purchase." [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76217345) (high, en); **2023-10 · Expo + Stripe** same wording [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77396219) (high, en) → PAY-01
- **2024-04 · free macOS app + client iOS scanner app** — 3.1.1 because the linked website's FAQ had a buymeacoffee link / the company site mentioned "subscription"; a week of replies got copy-paste answers. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=40132797) (high, en) → PAY-01, CONTENT-04
- **2026-07 · golf app** — 3.1.1. "The app allows users to contribute donations … with a mechanism other than In-App Purchase. Although these donations may be optional, they must use In-App Purchase since they are associated with receiving digital content or services." (Ko-fi / GitHub Sponsors links). Fix: neutral website link on iOS only. [source](https://github.com/cner-smith/opengolfapp/pull/634) (high, en) → PAY-01
- **2026-08 · campsite-alert app** — 3.1.1 rejected again on the same build: every link-out was hidden for subscribers and the demo account was a subscriber, so the reviewer could not see the fix; the notes also argued the opposite. [source](https://github.com/TylerFlores1992/campsite-finder/pull/158) (high, en) → PAY-01, ACC-03
- **2026-08 · course app, Indian storefront** — 3.1.1 for sending users to pay.<domain>; the US link-out allowance Apple quoted does not apply outside the US. Fix: claim 3.1.3(a) reader app — no prices, no purchase UI anywhere on iOS, login kept. [source](https://github.com/cyberking-coder/Rishi_app2/pull/14) (high, en) → PAY-01, IOS-UI-03
- **2022-11 · masterclass app** — 3.1.1 after removing website buttons; approved after replying that it is a Reader App (3.1.3(a)). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/74495373) (high, en) → PAY-01
- **2023-01 · Flutter reader app with External Link Account entitlement** — "does not use the External Link Account API to populate the modal sheet before every instance of linking out for account creation and management." [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75220729) (high, en) → PAY-01, IOS-IAP-02
- **2026-08 · B2B document app** — 3.1.1 + 3.1.3(c): "could be interpreted as being sold to individual consumers outside In-App Purchase". Fix: enterprise-only positioning, removed free-trial CTA and upgrade actions, registration became an access request. [source](https://github.com/hsdarestani/A-SmartDocs/pull/47) (high, en) → PAY-01
- **2023-04 · POS companion app (payments on the web)** — 3.1.1; Apple asked to remove "user registration". Unresolved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/76002092) (high, en) → PAY-01
- **2025-04 / 2023-10 · private business apps** — "Guideline 3.2 - Business… intended to be used by a specific business or organization… but you've selected public distribution on the App Store". Options: Apple Business Manager custom apps / Unlisted. [1](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79548470) [2](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77393490) (high, en) → CONTENT-03
- **2026-08 · Tauri app opening Stripe Checkout** — 2.1 Information Needed with a device recording of the IAP flow requested; StoreKit products already in ASC. [source](https://github.com/phillipchan1/nuvo/issues/44) (high, en) → PAY-01, META-05
- **2026-06 · bird-log app** — 3.1.1 "Restore Purchases" hidden inside `!isPremium`; 3.1.2(c) links; 5.1.1(v); 2.3.2; 2.1(b). [source](https://github.com/RemleyNick/boges2birds/pull/15) (high, en) → PAY-03, PAY-02, ACC-02
- **2026-05 · Arabic learning app (Svelte/Capacitor)** — 2.1(a) leftover `alert()` debug calls in Apple login reported as "an error message appeared"; 3.1.2(c) EULA/privacy links; 2.3.2 promo text too small. [source](https://github.com/selmetwa/parallel-arabic/pull/149) (high, en) → CONTENT-02, PAY-02
- **2026-03 · RN app** — 3.1.2(c): terms/privacy links moved directly under the purchase buttons; debug ErrorBoundary and RC logging removed. [source](https://github.com/jiyujin-spec/one-shot/pull/87) (high, en) → PAY-02, CONTENT-02
- **2022-11 · subscription app** — 3.1.2. "We were unable to find the following required item(s) in your app's metadata: – A functional link to the Terms of Use (EULA)". If using Apple's standard EULA, its link must be in the App Description. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/74278461) (high, en) → PAY-02
- **2022-02 · weekly/monthly/yearly plans** — 3.1.2 "different durations of your auto-renewable subscriptions were still created as separate in-app purchase products, rather than as different durations within the same subscription group"; still rejected until the old products were deleted. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/70984275) (high, en) → PAY-02, IOS-IAP-01
- **2023-03 · Flutter subscription app** — rejected for a missing "Title of publication or service" (3.1.2 required disclosures). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75844789) (high, en) → PAY-02
- **2024-09 · RevenueCat app** — 2.1 "one or more of your in-app purchase products have not been submitted for review" + 3.1.1 "your submitted in-app purchases indicate a change of business model for your app. Therefore, we need to verify the implementation". [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79002395) (high, en) → PAY-04
- **2025-02 · Flutter app** — IAP products stuck "Developer Action Needed" (Paid Apps Agreement unsigned); 8–9 rejections. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79415026) (high, en) → PAY-04
- **2025-11 · existing app adding its first IAP** — "We have returned your in-app purchase products to you as the required binary was not submitted." Fix: remove the attached build so the IAP section appears, attach the product, re-attach the build. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79819546) (high, en) → PAY-04
- **2025-02 · app with an IAP stuck "In Review"** — "in-app purchase products associated with the app version… could not be found in the submitted binary". Fix: temporarily re-add the product, resubmit, then remove from sale. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79453164) (high, en) → PAY-04
- **2022-09 · iPhone app** — 2.1 IAP "did not produce further action when we tapped on the 'Buy'" on iPad; "all apps designed for use on iPhone must still be formatted correctly and behave properly when running on iPad". Resolved when the reviewer used a different Apple ID. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73578508) (high, en) → PAY-04, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2023-02 · StoreKit 2 app** — 2.1 "an error message displayed when we attempted to purchase" (iPad, iOS 16.3.1): server-side receipt validation must accept sandbox receipts from a production-signed build. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75470489) (high, en) → PAY-04
- **2023-11 · app using `AppStore.showManageSubscriptions`** — 2.1: "Can't Connect to App Store" in the review sandbox on the manage-subscription screen. Unresolved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77444177) (high, en) → PAY-04
- **2026-08 · nutrition app (Superwall)** — 2.1(b) sandbox purchase "Unable to Complete Request" on iPad (App Store server-side); agreements/products verified, SDK bumped, resubmitted. [source](https://github.com/matheusfunabashi/nutriapp/pull/68) (high, en) → PAY-04
- **2026-08 · Flutter events app** — 5.1.2(i) privacy labels over-declared "Used to Track You" with no ATT (fix labels, do not add ATT) + 2.1(b) IAP "bug" pointing at the Paid Applications Agreement. [source](https://github.com/kolabing/kolabing-app/pull/109) (high, en) → PRIV-02, PAY-04
- **2024-04 · crypto exchange with a Lithuanian VASP licence** — 3.1.5. "The storefronts you selected in App Store Connect include locations where you or your third-party service provider(s) have not provided supporting evidence of permissions, registrations, and/or licenses"; 10 rejections even with EU-only / Lithuania-only availability. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78408300) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-07 · non-custodial wallet** — 3.1.5(iii): in-app swap classified as a cryptocurrency exchange service needing per-storefront licences. Fix: swap disabled on iOS only. [source](https://github.com/0xMiden/wallet/pull/355) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-07 · Lightning wallet** — 3.1.5(iii) licensing evidence requested because of a "Buy Bitcoin" button that only linked to third-party on-ramps. Fix: button removed from App Store builds. [source](https://github.com/breez/glow-app/pull/104) (high, en) → META-02
- **2022-06 · CFD trading white-label app** — 3.2.2(viii). "facilitates trading in contracts for difference ('CFDs')… Provide documentary evidence that you have the necessary licensing and permissions for all the regions"; a software vendor's business profile was not accepted. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/72752412) (high, en) → META-02
- **2022-12 · game "rate 5 stars to unlock level"** — 3.2.2. "prompts users to rate the app in exchange for in-app currency, points, or other incentives. Incentivizing ratings and downloads has a direct influence on the App Store user reviews or chart ranking." [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/74888263) (high, en) → CONTENT-06
- **2022-08 · "Second Phone Number" app (30-app publisher)** — "We noticed your app attempts to manipulate customers into making unwanted in-app purchases. - **2026-08 · Flutter time-since app** — 4.10. "The app charges users for access to built-in iOS capabilities. Specifically, iCloud sync." Fix: sync free for everyone; paywall line renamed "Export to a file"; landing page corrected. [source](https://github.com/MarJaysonSanAgustin/timesince_landing/pull/2) (high, en) → (new ID)

Merchant display name change did not clear it. [source](https://github.com/flutter-stripe/flutter_stripe/issues/2251) (high, en) → (new ID)
