### 4.8 Sign in with Apple
- **2024-03 · app with Google + Facebook login** — 4.8: reviewer said the third-party logins fail the three points. Actual fix: show Privacy Policy and Terms during social login → passed. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78126978) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-01, PRIV-01
- **2022-09 · app with Google + LinkedIn login (admin-configurable)** — 4.8; answer warns that a review-only configuration is "cheating the process". [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73843057) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2022-02 · 7-year-old directory app (webview)** — flagged for no SIWA because third-party login pages were reachable 3–4 clicks deep inside listings; overturned on appeal after ~2 weeks. Same dev: rejected for a COVID-quarantine apology in release notes (treated as a COVID app). [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=30367723) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-01, META-02
- **2026-05 · beauty personal-colour app (Japan)** — 5.1.1 no account deletion; no Sign in with Apple next to Google; missing PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy; 3 rejections in 12 days, approved on the 4th with demo credentials. [source](https://zenn.dev/mintototo1/articles/buildlog-03-app-store-reject) (high, ja) → ACC-02, IOS-LOGIN-01, IOS-PRIV-03

### 5.1.1(v) Account deletion
- **2026-08 · dive-log app with no accounts** — 5.1.1(v) twice because the first-launch "Create Your Profile" step looked like account creation; nothing in the notes said otherwise. Fix: standing review notes shipped with every fastlane submission. [source](https://github.com/submersion-app/submersion/pull/1277) (high, en) → ACC-02, META-05
- **2026-08 · rice-calorie app** — 5.1.1(v): a web page describing an e-mail request is not deletion ("allowed only for highly regulated industries"). Fix: Me → Account → Delete, immediate, objects before the auth row. [source](https://github.com/NelsonGan/ricecal/pull/124) (high, en) → ACC-02
- **2022-06 · Firebase app with Sign in with Apple** — 5.1.1(v) token revocation: `currentUser.delete()` does not revoke; needs a server-signed JWT (Cloud Function). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/72625646) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-02

### 5.1.1(ii)/(iv) Purpose strings, pre-permission screens, data minimisation
- **2023-10 · photo app** — 5.1.1. "requests the user's consent to access the photos, but doesn't sufficiently explain the use of the photos in the purpose string… include an example" ("Allow access to photos to upload photos from your library" was rejected). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77341890) (high, en) → PERM-01
- **2024-02 · Expo app** — 5.1.1 location purpose string "doesn't sufficiently explain the use of the location". [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77987117) (high, en) → PERM-01
- **2024-09 · Halide camera app** — 5.1.1: "The camera will be used to take photographs" judged insufficient. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=41636038) (high, en) → PERM-01
- **2026-08 · dictation app (ElevenLabs STT)** — 5.1.1(ii): "Dictate a message instead of typing it." must say the audio is sent to a third party and give an example. [source](https://github.com/BennyKok/omg.dev/pull/212) (high, en) → PERM-01, AI-01
- **2026-08 · climbing app (Expo)** — 5.1.1(ii) location string must explain the use with a specific example. [source](https://github.com/nathan-hadley/cirque/pull/80) (medium, en) → PERM-01
- **2024-01 · background-location app** — 5.1.1. "A custom message appears before the permission request, and to proceed users press a 'Accept' button. Use words like 'Continue' or 'Next' on the button instead." [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/77819007) (high, en) → PERM-01
- **2026-08 · device-pairing app (RN)** — 5.1.1(iv): pre-permission button "Grant camera access" → "Continue"; add "Open Settings" when iOS will not re-prompt. [source](https://github.com/Kangentic/mobile/pull/59) (high, en) → PERM-01, PERM-02
- **2026-07 · voice-archive app (Flutter)** — 5.1.1: microphone pre-prompt copy/CTA mimicked Apple's system dialog; 2.1(a) review access code; 2.3.8 icon alpha; 3.1.2(c). [source](https://github.com/careos-healthcare/voice-memory/pull/171) (high, en) → PERM-01, META-02, PAY-02
- **2022-01 · Flutter app** — ITMS-90683 although strings existed in InfoPlist.strings: `$(NSContactsUsageDescription)` variable references in Info.plist are not resolved — use literal strings. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/70870182) (high, en) → IOS-PLIST-01
- **2023-02 · Xamarin.Forms app with a Share Extension** — ITMS-90683 NSContactsUsageDescription; fix: Linker Behavior "Link All" on the extension. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/75508688) (high, en) → IOS-PLIST-01
- **2026-08 · expo-location `motionUsagePermission: false`** — ITMS-90683: prebuild removed `NSMotionUsageDescription` while the native module still linked CoreMotion. [source](https://github.com/expo/expo/issues/49319) (high, en) → IOS-PLIST-01
- **2024-10 · watchOS-only app** — location usage key must live in the WatchKit Extension Info.plist; upload rejected when the key is in the WatchKit App plist. Unresolved. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/79136498) (high, en) → IOS-PLIST-01

### 5.1.2 ATT, tracking labels, third-party AI disclosure, health privacy, regulated entities
- **2022-01 · Flutter app with own web content** — 5.1.2 "accesses web content you own where you collect cookies… but you do not use App Tracking Transparency". Fix: video of the ATT dialog in Resolution Center → approved (twice). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/70699164) (high, en) → PRIV-03, META-05
- **2024-07 · Flutter app with AdMob UMP consent form** — 5.1.2: show ATT first (with `app_tracking_transparency`, not `permission_handler`), then the GDPR form → accepted. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/78766006) (high, en) → PRIV-03
- **2022-09 · Unity game with Unity Ads** — 5.1.1/ATT: fix via Unity's "iOS 14 Advertising Support" package. [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/73820643) (high, en) → PRIV-03
- **2026-08 · Flutter e-shop** — 5.1.2(i): App Privacy declared User-ID tracking but no ATT prompt; cause: the bundled Meta SDK manifest (`NSPrivacyTracking=true`) + `isAdvertiserTrackingEnabled = true` template leftover. Fix: removed the SDK (Facebook login via browser OAuth). [source](https://github.com/eSimplyTravel-com/eSimply-eshop-app/pull/13) (high, en) → PRIV-02, IOS-ENT-04
- **2026-07 · Expo storefront app** — 5.1.2(i) "the app still does not use App Tracking Transparency": the request resolved `denied` in 1 ms when made before UIKit was active; fix: retry on `undetermined`. [source](https://github.com/ogabasseyy/Baci/pull/3175) (high, en) → PRIV-03
- **2026-07 · Flutter sharing app (Korea)** — 5.1.2(i) no ATT request; added `NSUserTrackingUsageDescription` + request at launch. [source](https://github.com/ejoonie/share-it/pull/108) (high, en) → PRIV-03
- **2026-08 · Japanese app that removed AdMob** — 5.1.2: `NSUserTrackingUsageDescription` still present with no ATT call while ASC blocked changing the tracking label; fix: a one-line dummy `requestTrackingAuthorization` → approved on the 3rd submission. [source](https://zenn.dev/nawa_10half/articles/ios-admob-removal-att-catch22) (high, ja) → PRIV-02, PRIV-03
- **2026-05 · AI chat app** — 5.1.1(i)/5.1.2(i). "Note that only including this information in the app's Terms of Service or Privacy Policy is not sufficient." Fix: consent checkbox at sign-in + a pre-first-message modal naming each AI provider (Anthropic, OpenAI, Google) and what is sent. [source](https://github.com/shogo-labs/shogo-ai/pull/635) (high, en) → AI-01
- **2026-07 · period/health app** — 5.1.3 repeated rejections: the Termly-generated privacy policy declared interest-based ads, an offer wall and remarketing for an app handling reproductive data. Fix: rewrote the policy; HealthKit section. [source](https://github.com/Aster-Health-Inc/aster-health-website/pull/2) (high, en) → PRIV-01
- **2026-06 · body-data health app (individual account)** — 5.1.1(ix). "The app does not meet all requirements for apps that offer highly regulated services or handle sensitive user data. Specifically: The account that submits the app must be enrolled in the Apple Developer Program as an organization, and not as an individual." Documentation cannot substitute. [source](https://github.com/stormychel/LibreBase/issues/33) (high, en) → META-03
- **2026-08 · parcel companion app (sole trader)** — 5.1.1(ix) same wording for insurance purchase / claim filing; features removed rather than incorporating. [source](https://github.com/sgf36/Easy-Post-Mobile-Companion/pull/3) (high, en) → META-03

## Google Play — 3rd pass (2026-08-28): thin areas — child safety, declarations, Health Connect, form factors, enforcement

165 cases (23 high / 136 medium), mostly Play Developer Community threads read via a reader proxy plus GitHub issues; Product Expert (PE) advice is quoted where the thread was unresolved.

Format: `- **YYYY-MM · app type** — policy as quoted. "Google quote" Cause / Fix / rounds. [source](URL) (confidence, lang) → checklist IDs`
Every URL below was checked against `rejections/community-cases.md` (773 known URLs, 153 known thread ids) and is new.

### 1. Child Safety Standards / Age-Restricted Content / Age Signals

- **2025-11 · social chat app (HiCat), version code 230** — Child Safety Standards. "The published standards are invalid" — the notice required the public standards to (a) load without errors, (b) contain child-safety/CSAE content and (c) mention the app or developer name exactly as on the Play listing, plus (for social apps) a dedicated child-safety contact, an in-app user-feedback mechanism and a CSAM-handling description. Cause: the standards page did not name the app/developer and the app had no child-safety reporting entry. Fix: rewrote the CSAE page (app + developer name, explicit CSAE prohibition), added a "Child Safety Policy" report category in the app, resubmitted the App content declaration; the violation banner still showed 4 days later, appeal SLA (7 working days) exceeded the 14-Nov removal deadline; other developers reported the same in Dec 2025. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/386705063) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-10, UGC-03, UGC-07
- **2026-03 · video player / watch-party app (first publication)** — Child Safety Standards, then suspension. Rejected "multiple times" for child safety after fixing "both in app and on website"; the app was then suspended. Expert (Diamond PE): the published policy "looks ok", appeal. Lesson: a watch-party feature makes a "video player" a social app in Google's eyes, so the CSAE declaration, published standards and in-app reporting are required from the first upload. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/417745017) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-10, UGC-03, AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-04 · blood-donation app (BloodConnect, open source)** — Child Safety Standards compliance warning with a hard deadline (29-Apr-2026). The team's checklist mirrors what Google asks for: named child-safety point of contact + working support mailbox, internal CSAE/CSAM response SOP (acknowledge, escalate, preserve evidence, report, retention), public `/child-safety` page reachable anonymously (HTTP 200, no login wall), in-app report entry, then the Play Console declaration using that live URL/contact. [source](https://github.com/CraftsmenLtd/BloodConnect/issues/550) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-10, UGC-03, UGC-05
- **2026-04 · trading-card scanner (general audience, Tools category, live ~1 year)** — "Solely Targeting Children" flag under Families ("transmits device identifiers… AAID"). Cause: the Target audience selection included 0–18 age groups, so the app was treated as child-directed and the AAID transmission became a violation. Options given by experts: raise the target-audience/age rating so no child group is included, or add a neutral age screen; appeal reply kept referring to identifier transmission. Lesson: never tick child age groups unless the app is really for kids; mixed-audience apps need the neutral age screen before any AAID leaves the device. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/427964542) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07, AND-CONSOLE-04
- **2025-11 → 2025-12 · app with a website-hosted Child Safety Standards section** — Child Safety Standards, then "Repeated app rejections". "In-app experience: 'Repeated app rejections.'" Cause: after the 11-Nov rejection the developer added the standards section to the privacy page and resubmitted; a Netlify deploy bug silently dropped that section, so the appeal was filed while the page no longer contained the standards → rejected again → app suspended under the Enforcement Process policy; the single appeal was already used and the notice came from a no-reply address. Lesson: fetch the standards URL from a clean device immediately before every resubmission/appeal; the "published standards" check is re-run on each review. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/395722561) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-10, AND-ENFORCE-01, META-04
- **2026-07 · app flagged under "Age-Restricted Content and Functionality: Violation"** — the new (2026) age-restricted policy; the developer uploaded a fixed build but the violation banner had not cleared after 7 days. Expert: reviews of these fixes take 10–14 days; do not touch the console in the meantime because "changing anything on the console will reset the review timer". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/448776654) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07, META-07
- **2024-09 · social app asking where to publish the "Child Safety Point of Contact"** — expert answer: the verified developer address/e-mail is what shows on the store; the app-specific contact must go into the app description, the privacy policy and the published child-safety standards page. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/297694305) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-10, CONTENT-05

Empty after search: no thread or issue yet describes a rejection of an *anonymous/random-chat* app under the July-2026 extension, nor a rejection for misusing Age Signals API data (threads 383183265, 415804784, 438720065 are Q&A only: "Google does not enforce it… requirements for Google Play's age related policies still apply").

### 2. News apps declaration / Government apps / app registration / organization account / Account transfer

- **2025-05 · event app (schedules, exhibitors, announcements; category Events)** — News policy. "Apps that declare themselves or are listed as 'News' in the Google Play Console must meet the News policy requirements. Specifically, it appears that your app: Contains only static content—for example, content that's more than three months old". Cause: never declared as a news app, but "announcements" content and regularly updated posts were read as news; appeal (case 7-7290000039104) returned the full News policy text (ownership/author info, contact page, no static content, no affiliate/ad primary purpose). Still rejected after a revamp; unresolved at the last post. Lesson: strip "news/announcements" wording and any dated feed older than 3 months from the store listing before an event app goes live. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/343077446) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, META-02
- **2024-12 → 2025-01 · non-news app** — News policy although "You told us that your app is not a news app". Expert: look for a conflict between the app content and the Data safety/declarations and for a screenshot in the notice; the developer shipped an app update and it was published three weeks later. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/313918927) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2024-10 → 2024-11 · Norwegian police-log news app (Politiloggen), 800 articles since June** — News policy: "Contains only static content—for example, content that's more than three months old". A false positive (the reviewer probably saw a district with no recent posts); appeal accepted ~7 weeks later with "the app has been accepted as compliant for the News policy". Expert: "I would only start to get concerned if this happens two or three times in a row." [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/300747191) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, META-06
- **2025-05 · app that mistakenly answered "Yes" to "Is this a news app?"** — rejected under News; the developer could not find where to change the answer (the News declaration lives in App content; once submitted it may be hidden until the next review). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/347369854) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2025-03 · weather app (Flowx, 12 years on Play, Editors' Choice)** — Misleading Claims: "Apps that falsely claim affiliation with a government entity or to provide or facilitate government services for which they are not properly authorized." Cause: the listing named the government weather models it uses (NOAA HRRR, DWD D2, ECMWF). Fix: a clear "not affiliated with these agencies" statement in the listing; restored 1–2 days later with no notification; recurring subscriptions were cancelled during the removal. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=43346608) (high, en) → AND-META-04, AND-CONSOLE-12
- **2026-08 · AI-assistant app (NavBharat AI) on a personal account, update rejected 2026-08-02** — "Developer Account" violation: an app declaring medical features may only be published from an organization account. Cause: nine "Medical" boxes ticked in the Health apps declaration while the account was personal. Fix chosen: hide every medical-class assistant in the Play build so the declaration can truthfully say "none"; the team notes that re-enabling them later without an organization account would be Deceptive Behavior. [source](https://github.com/docasheesh-png/navbharatai/pull/2083) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02, ACC-06, AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2026-01 · app created on a personal account, then account converted to Organization** — "Play Console Terms Policy: Violation of Play Console Terms — Some app types can only be distributed by organizations. You've selected an app category or stated that your app offers specific features that require you to submit your app using an organization account." Same rejection after the conversion. Expert: an app listed before the conversion keeps the personal-account policy state; unpublish it and create a new listing under the organization account. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/404291888) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02, ACC-06
- **2026-07 · routine-tracker app (MiHabit), organization account** — "Issue found: Violation of Play Console Requirements… Some types of apps can only be distributed by organizations… We found an issue in the following area(s): Developer Account". The developer had just converted from personal to organization; expert still advised creating a new organization account and transferring the app. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/453470238) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02
- **2026-01 · VPN app (BoltVPN, India) after converting to Organization the day before** — "Violation of Play Console Requirements – Some apps (VPN) must be published using an organization account." Cause: the organization status had not propagated / the listing predates the conversion. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/401621866) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02, AND-PERM-14
- **2025-12 · clinic contact app (navigation to the clinic, phone/WhatsApp, service info; no medical features declared)** — "Some types of apps can only be distributed by organizations…" although "Other" was selected in App features and no health data is collected. Expert: since mid-2024 Google classifies by purpose — an app whose name/description points to a clinic or medical service is health-related; publish it from the clinic's organization account. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/398183169) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02, ACC-06
- **2026-08 · loan app (Loanyfy) after converting the same developer account to Organization** — still rejected with "some types of apps can only be distributed by organizations"; the developer asks whether to delete and re-create the listing (the recurring answer in these threads). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/463072653) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02, LAW-02
- **2025 · developer told by support to create a new account, transfer the apps and close the old one (organization account with an individual payments profile)** — after the transfer the old account was terminated for "high risk", then the new one a week later: "we have identified a pattern of high risk or abuse associated with your Developer Account… pursuant to Section 8.3 or 10.3 of Google Play's Developer Distribution Agreement… we can't share the reasons". Lesson: an app transfer between your own accounts links them; terminate nothing until the transfer is complete and never leave an account with an unresolved payments-profile issue. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/384554405) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01, AND-ACCOUNT-01
- **2026-08 · UK organization account** — terminated under DDA 11.4 during a D&B address update: the automated sweep compared the Play payments profile with cached D-U-N-S data and flagged a mismatch; template appeal denial; attachments sent to the enforcement e-mail were not reviewed. Lesson: freeze D-U-N-S/Companies House edits while an account is under verification, and update the payments profile in the same step. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/462694473) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-01, AND-ACCOUNT-02
- **2026-08 · US LLC owned by a Kazakhstan resident** — personal → organization conversion failed identity verification ("Google was unable to verify my identity") and the whole account was restricted; the representative's passport country differed from the organization country. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/456089418) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-01, AND-ACCOUNT-02

Empty after search: no developer report yet of an app *removed for not registering by 2026-09-30* (only the announcement threads 421099111 / 361325854 and a protest thread 447995010), and none for the Account Transfer policy of 2026-05 beyond the April-2026 policy-update thread 424824940. No Government-apps-declaration rejection surfaced beyond the Flowx "government affiliation" case above.

### 3. Alternative billing / external offers / external content links / fee model

- **2025-05 · WebView chat app with donation links that unlock features (hobby developer, no business)** — Payments policy question answered by a Diamond PE: donations are allowed outside Play Billing only when they give no in-app benefit ("including things like a donated icon"); as soon as a benefit is attached it is a digital sale; and the EEA external-offers program requires the developer to be registered as a business — an individual account cannot enrol. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/347421295) (medium, en) → AND-PAY-01, PAY-01
- **2025-05 · EEA app enrolling in external offers** — after activating external offers at account level and switching to monthly billing, "Monetization setup" showed "This app isn't yet approved to link to external offers" for days; it flipped to "There are no countries / regions setup for external offers — Add countries / regions" by itself about a week later. Lesson: enrolment is asynchronous; budget a week before the release that adds the external link. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/342920731) (medium, en) → AND-PAY-01
- **2024-12 · developer outside the alternative-billing countries** — asked whether apps can use alternative billing without the Alternative Billing API; PE: eligibility is per developer country and per user country ("You are not in a country where you can offer alternative billing as the developer"); during the pilots earnings had to be reported manually, now via the API. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/314130514) (medium, en) → AND-PAY-01, KR-04

Empty after search: no Korean alternative-billing rejection thread, no Japan external-payments-program story, no US "external content links / 2026-01-28" or "global fee model (2026-06-30)" developer case on the community, GitHub, HN or Stack Exchange (Stack Exchange API blocked mid-run; Brave rate-limited after two queries).

### 4. Families / Teacher Approved / neutral age screen / self-certified ad SDKs

- **2026-06 · food-ordering app (FoodBag, Uzbekistan) with children in its target audience, no ad SDK** — "Families Ads Policy: contains advertisements or offers that are not clearly distinguishable from app content". Cause: restaurant promo banners, discount tiles and "Featured" lists count as ads under the Families policy once the target audience includes children. Fix (expert): remove the child age groups from Target audience, or make every promotional element visibly labelled/distinguishable. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/442069836) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07, ADS-02
- **2024-01 · game rated 3+ (6+ in Germany)** — "we determined that some elements of your store listing may appeal to children under 13. Make sure that you select the relevant age groups for children in your Target audience and content settings." Adding 6–8 and 9–12 did not clear the warning; expert workaround: add every child group including "under 5", wait for the warning to clear, then remove groups from the lowest up — each removal is re-reviewed, so the offending element is found without risking removal. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/255248020) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07, META-03
- **2023-09 · French-letters learning game for 3–13-year-olds forced to target 13+** — "Make changes to your store listing by removing any excessive elements that may appeal to children". Expert: either follow the Families policy with the correct target audience/rating, or, if targeting 13+, "make it visually less appealing to younger people" (icon, screenshots, cartoon style). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/235692157) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07, META-02
- **2023-11 · kids app on Google Mobile Ads SDK 22.3.0** — rejected for "not in compliance with the Families Self-Certified Ads SDK Program… ads containing gambling content". Cause: the SDK version was compliant but AdMob's "Blocking controls" still allowed gambling categories. Fix: disable every category inappropriate for the target age in the AdMob console → resolved the same day. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/245804589) (high, en) → ADS-02, AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2024-10 · mixed-audience app with a neutral age screen (AppsFlyer for adults, Amplitude with a UUID)** — appeal denied naming Amplitude as the AAID destination although Amplitude was not configured for AAID. Expert diagnosis: an ID leaves the device *before* the neutral age screen (analytics/crash SDKs at process start); fix: no ID transmission until after the age screen, disable AAID for everyone while the rejections are cleared, and put the child login first in Sign-in details. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/299944705) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07, AND-CONSOLE-04, AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2023-10 · paid version of a children's app (The Talking Mother Goose)** — rejected by the Teacher Approved program a week after the free version was accepted; resubmitting unchanged was rejected again; the developer received no reason. Lesson: each SKU is evaluated separately and the paid listing needs the same educational-value evidence. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/240460321) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-07

Empty after search: no thread or issue on the community/GitHub/HN uses Google's newer phrase "unintended appeal to children", none reports a Families rejection caused specifically by AppLovin leaving the self-certified program, and no new Play Games auto sign-in case beyond the one already collected (SO 76592904).

### 5. Ads

- **2023-05 · app with an App Open ad on every launch + native ads in a list** — "Disruptive Ads". Diamond PE: the App Open ad shown every time the app launches "would disrupt the users who are expecting to see the app content but instead see an ad immediately"; pointed to AdMob's app-open guidance (show only on cold start / return from background after a delay, never before the app UI is ready). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/215693113) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2023-08 · app with banners + App Open ads** — Play Protect/"fraudulent" flag after moving the banner between the BottomNavigationView and the content; experts suspect the App Open ads rather than the banner ("There have been some posts about app open ads causing issues with reviews"). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/232535688) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2024-11 · AI antique-identifier app** — Disruptive Ads persisted after the version-9 fix; the developer asked Google for a screenshot. Expert rule worth quoting: "Using remote config to turn off adverts after you receive violation warning is fine. Using remote config to turn adverts off to avoid rejection and pass review is not." [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/305447204) (medium, en) → ADS-02, CONTENT-10
- **2024-10 · app removed, version code 11** — "Violation of Deceptive Ads policy… Our review found that your app doesn't appear to comply with this policy because it contains: Ads that aren't clearly labeled." with screenshot `IN_APP_EXPERIENCE-381.png`; "Ads must not simulate or impersonate the user interface of any app feature, such as notifications or warning elements of an operating system. It must be clear to the user which app is serving each ad." [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/301166223) (medium, en) → ADS-02
- **2026-08 · apps monetised with AdMob mediation / Unity Ads** — suspended under Device and Network Abuse because a mediated ad creative redirected users to an external APK download; two of four appeals accepted (see area 9, threads 462642224, 459280152, 458872947). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/462642224) (medium, en) → ADS-02, AND-QUALITY-02

Empty after search: no lock-screen-ad, notification-ad or rewarded-ad-specific rejection thread beyond those already collected; "ads in unexpected places" / "Better Ads Experiences" return only Q&A; no AdMob-invalid-traffic → Play-removal story.

### 6. Health Connect / Health apps declaration / Financial features / form factors / 16 KB / targetSdk

### Health Connect (2025–2026)
- **2026-01 · fitness-challenge app (steps, sleep, workout distance/calories leaderboards)** — "Health Connect Minimum Scope": rejected repeatedly with the list "ActiveCaloriesBurned, Distance, CyclingPedalingCadence/ExerciseSession, StepsCadence/Steps, TotalCaloriesBurned, SleepSession… don't appear required" although cadence is never requested (Play names the whole permission group). Already done: removed BODY_SENSORS, request each permission only after joining the matching challenge, literal declarations. Expert: the *store description* must let a reviewer identify every data type without reading code. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/401465466) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-06, META-02
- **2025-12 · chronic-disease monitoring app (glucose, blood pressure, heart rate with dashboard cards, charts and write dialogs)** — Health Connect permissions rejected although every type is visible in the UI. Cause: the developer had temporarily removed the UI + permissions in one release to ship bug fixes, then re-added them; the Play Console health declaration no longer matched and the previous employee's form could not be edited ("no option to remove actioned permission" → contact support). Lesson: never ship an intermediate build that drops declared Health Connect types. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/396693822) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-06, AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2025-12 · app that removed the entire health feature after "Excessive data access for declared feature"** — still rejected with the same text. Expert: a bundle in a test track still contained the old code, and production was not rolled out to 100 % — every active track is reviewed. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/391538459) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-11, AND-CONSOLE-06
- **2025-11 · corporate-wellness app requesting steps via Health Connect** — "Inappropriate Health Connect Access Requested — StepsCadence/Steps" although the listing said step data is used for statistics, recommendations and achievements. Expert (after re-reading): the paragraph was easy to miss in a long description — put the Health Connect data types in their own clearly labelled paragraph near the top, then appeal. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/384553939) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-06, META-02
- **2025-10 · white-label gym apps (hundreds of apps, one codebase) adding Health Connect** — ~15 % rejected: "Your application does not require the following permissions: BodyFat, BloodPressure. Please ensure you are only requesting the minimum necessary scopes." (sometimes also Cadence/ExerciseSession, Distance, Vo2Max, HeartRate, RestingHeartRate). Fix: name BodyFat and BloodPressure — and the feature that uses them (BioAge) — in each app's own description; a generic "steps, distance, heart rate…" sentence was not enough. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/381235420) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-06, META-02
- **2026-04 · app previously approved for Health Connect** — rejected because a copy-pasted description said "Apple Health" instead of "Health Connect" ("mentioning Apple is obviously an unsupported use case for health features"); ~3 weeks and three review rounds (5+ days each) to recover; support even suggested removing all health features. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/430582644) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-06, CONTENT-04, META-02
- **2025-06 · virtual-race app using Health Connect for 2 years (steps, distance, exercise, calories)** — suddenly "not sufficient rationale for the permissions"; appeals answered "We can't be specific. Just change it and resubmit"; in-app rationale text alone did not clear it. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/348498215) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-06

### Health apps declaration (2025–2026)
- **2026-08 · dating app with HIV/PrEP status fields and a sexual-health-clinic finder** — "Health Content and Services policy: Inaccurate Health Apps Declaration — Health features in your app don't match the information you provided in your health declaration. Check you have properly declared the following category or categories: Others". Six rejections in two days after ticking "Disease prevention and public health", "Healthcare services and management" and "Reproductive and sexual health". Expert: those categories are for dedicated health services; untick them and describe the three features under "Others" only. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/459205932) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, ACC-06
- **2026-06 · smoking-cessation / habit tracker with 4-7-8 breathing exercises** — Inaccurate Health Apps Declaration for "Stress Management, Relaxation, Mental Acuity" and "Mental and Behavioural Health"; each rejection quoted the *old* description although both locales had been rewritten. Lesson: the reviewer sees the description of the reviewed release; resubmit the listing and the bundle together. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/442662295) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, META-06
- **2026-05 · cosmetic/skincare routine organizer (Beauty/Lifestyle, declared "not a health app")** — flagged as "Diseases and Conditions Management" even after removing the Learn/Community tabs and scrubbing "acne", "eczema", "treatment" from the listing. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/437147816) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2025-01 · medication-reminder app** — "Inaccurate Health Apps Declaration — functionalities that relate to: Healthcare Services and Management; Medication and Treatment Management". Expert: the rejection means the app *is* a health app and must follow the health policy — including an organization account. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/318911743) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, ACC-06
- **2025-01 · veterinary-clinic client app (invoices, refills, appointments, rabies certificates)** — rejected a month on the "Others (Please Specify)" category; the developer had ticked four human-health categories for pet care. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/299992441) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2025-02 · fitness/nutrition platform (Trimify)** — declaration rejected twice; suspected trigger: an in-app blog post about poor sleep while "Sleep Management" was no longer declared. Expert: content shown inside the app counts, and "a number of people have reported that health declarations are being rejected when they are sure they are right". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/324075312) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09

### Financial features / loans
- **2025-06 · housing-loan calculator (no lending)** — "The Financial features declaration form… is inaccurate" after declaring "no financial features", then "Other: loan calculation". Expert: estimating how much a user can borrow is *financial advice* — declare it as such, and only organization accounts can publish financial apps. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/350994863) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, LAW-02, ACC-06
- **2025-06 · personal expense tracker (manual entry, "accounts" are just labels)** — 7th rejection for an "inaccurate" Financial features declaration; no specific field named. Lesson: the words "account", "bank", "savings", "budget" in listing/UI trigger the finance classifier; describe them as manual labels in the declaration's free text. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/344625638) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2024-09 → 2024-10 · credit-monitoring app (free credit score, third-party loan/credit-card offers, partner-bank prepaid card; years on Play)** — "The Financial features declaration form… is inaccurate" on every resubmission. Fix that worked: delete an A/B store-listing experiment and the custom store listings, then resubmit — the reviewer was seeing a different listing than the declaration described. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/299056735) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, META-06
- **2024-11 · cash-advance app (Instant Cash Advance)** — removal notice: "Based on our review, your app doesn't meet the requirements of our Financial Services policy. The Financial features declaration within Play Console that you have provided for your app is inaccurate… Select all applicable categories, deselect all inaccurate statement(s) and provide additional proof of eligibility requirements." [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/308901847) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, LAW-02
- **2026-08 · NHB-approved Indian housing-finance company** — flagged under Financial Services; the "personal loans" option demands RBI authorization documents that an HFC (regulated by NHB, secured loans, not a digital lending app) cannot provide. Still open at posting. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/461448422) (medium, en) → LAW-02, AND-CONSOLE-09

### Form factors: Android Auto / Wear OS / XR
- **2026-08 · React-Native app with iOS CarPlay only (`@iternio/react-native-auto-play`)** — "Auto App Quality Guidelines: the content of your application is not able to load" on version code 9. Cause: the library's AndroidManifest merges a full `CarAppService` (NAVIGATION category, `gms.car.application` meta-data, CAR_* permissions), so Play reviewed the app on an Auto head unit where nothing renders. Fix: an Expo config plugin that strips the service, meta-data and car permissions with `tools:node="remove"`; verified on the merged manifest. [source](https://github.com/cw0rley/Speed-Limit/pull/1) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13, AND-BUILD-02
- **2026-08 · podcast app (Media3, Kotlin Multiplatform)** — "Auto App Quality Guidelines: App crashes when we attempted to test functionality." (Apple accepted the same release). Causes: `MediaLibrarySession` granted only `DEFAULT_SESSION_COMMANDS` so the legacy browser root was null and Auto could not connect, and `playFromSearch/playFromMediaId` delivered URI-less `MediaItem`s that NPE'd in ExoPlayer. Fix: `DEFAULT_SESSION_AND_LIBRARY_COMMANDS`, resolve external requests into real playlists, on-device tests. [source](https://github.com/bnottingham/armstrong-and-getty-podcast/pull/1) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2026-08 · lap-timer app that opted into Android Auto with the POI category** — "Issue found: Parking and Charging Functionality — The app didn't provide meaningful functionality in its category relevant to driving." Not fixable (a lap timer has no POI use); the team made Auto debug-only so phone updates are not blocked. Lesson: opting into the Auto form factor in Play Console is what triggers the category review. [source](https://github.com/Richie97/track-history/pull/137) (medium, en) → AND-BUILD-02
- **2025-09 · AI voice-assistant for drivers** — mobile build approved, every build with the Android Auto manifest rejected; the app is a general-purpose assistant, not one of the Car App Library categories (media, navigation, POI, IoT, messaging). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/372403778) (medium, en) → AND-BUILD-02
- **2026-07 · dash-cam companion app (hardware-only, Android Auto)** — repeated "Missing demo or guest account details / The username or password you provided didn't work." although a demo video and a tap-10-times "Review Mode" were described in Sign-in details; a PE escalation got the submission re-queued with the advice to reply on the existing ticket if rejected again. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/455034368) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03
- **2025-10 · hardware-controller app (Android Auto eligibility)** — "Latest app update not accepted into Android (Auto)… Issue: Invalid or incomplete login credentials… you need to provide valid login credentials (such as an active demo account) and any other resources needed to review your app (for example, a QR code, instructions, etc.) via Google Play Console App access section" although the app has no accounts; only the required hardware. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/383218015) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03

### Foreground service declaration (2026)
- **2026-04 · VoIP calling app, `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MICROPHONE`** — "We are unable to approve your app's use of FOREGROUND_SERVICE permission because we found the information provided to be insufficient in evaluating compliance with foreground service permission requirements. The video link and/or the app description did not clearly demonstrate the need for this permission." A generic demo video and a description that merely "mentions background functionality" are not enough: the video must show the call continuing while the app is backgrounded with the persistent notification, and the description must state the background feature. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/429187736) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-05, BG-02

### Financial features — additional (2024–2026)
- **2025-08 · loan app** — "Violation of Personal Loan policy… Your app requests access to one or more of the following permissions… Permissions: 'App Split Bundle [11]: READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE, WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE'" although the source manifest had neither. PE list of permissions financial apps cannot use: READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE, READ_MEDIA_IMAGES, READ_CONTACTS, ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION, READ_PHONE_NUMBERS, READ_MEDIA_VIDEO, QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES, WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE; check every track including paused test tracks and SDK-merged manifests. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/367661031) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-13, AND-CONSOLE-11, LAW-02
- **2024-09 · Indian personal-loan app** — "Violation of Personal Loan Declaration Requirements… The partner bank(s) or NBFC(s) listed in your app's description does not match with the name and/or URLs of bank(s) or NBFC(s) provided in your Financial features declaration." Fix: identical partner names/URLs in the listing and the declaration. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/297079562) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, LAW-02, META-02
- **2026-05 · offline point-of-sale / inventory app (solo developer)** — classified in the "Finance vertical" and told to submit from an organization account with D-U-N-S; appeal confirmed. Expert: invoices/costs/POS keywords and price/tax code trigger the classifier; staying personal means a new package name and removing all expense/invoice code. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/434411683) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02, ACC-06
- **2026 · gig-shift booking app with an in-app "Coins" mechanic** — flagged as financial services even after removing "wallet/balance/earn/rewards/credits" wording, changing the category to Productivity and dropping "Other financial info" from Data safety. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/433656114) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2026-02 · gift-card marketplace paid in cryptocurrency** — Payments policy violation ("must use Google Play Billing") although the FAQ exempts gift cards; appeal template-denied; PE's first question: "Is your app selling anything that can be consumed within the app? This could include memberships, discounts, extra features etc?" (any in-app benefit breaks the exemption). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/412511722) (medium, en) → AND-PAY-01, PAY-01
- **2025-08 · timber-industry classifieds with a Razorpay-processed "Pro Membership"** — "Violation of Play Console Requirements… should be submitted by an organization account due to offering financial features"; the subscription itself is also a Play Billing problem (digital feature sold through Razorpay). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/366803089) (medium, en) → AND-ACCOUNT-02, PAY-01
- **2024-12 · cloud-mining contracts app with BTC withdrawals** — Financial Services rejection after selecting "cryptocurrency wallet"; PE: "management or investment of money and cryptocurrencies" is in scope, whichever category is chosen must be explained in the description. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/311568822) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09, LAW-02

### Wear OS (2025–2026)
- **2025-12 · Wear OS app update** — "Missing ongoing activity: When a user has an ongoing activity, your app did not do one or more of the following: show the ongoing activity indicator on the watch face; update recent apps with the appropriate app launcher chip; reference the ongoing activity from the tile if the tile is present in the tile carousel." Appeal returned the same text; the developer could not reproduce. Same text in 2025-11 for an open-source Wear app (thread 385399551). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/395667997) (medium, en) → AND-BUILD-02
- **2025-07 · non-standalone Wear app** — "Wear app functionality not working as described… Your app indicates it is a non-standalone but the companion app or browser (mobile or desktop) doesn't connect with the Wear app and/or allow the user to use the Wear app as expected." Two appeals answered with the identical text; the reviewer apparently could not pair the companion. Lesson: if the phone app is required, ship both in one review with pairing steps in Sign-in details, or mark the Wear app standalone. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/358380132) (medium, en) → AND-BUILD-02, AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2026-04 · habit app's Wear build** — repeatedly rejected for "not mentioning complications in the store listing" although complications were described in three places; Wear reviews check the listing text of the *Wear* form factor (separate listing/screenshots), not the phone listing. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/425325140) (medium, en) → AND-META-02
- **2025-02 · watch-face catalog (phone app) + individual watch-face apps** — Misleading Claims: "When it's installed, your app's icon or name is different from the one shown in its store listing." because the reviewer compared the catalog icon with the watch-face icon; appeal with an explanatory screenshot still rejected. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/332152023) (medium, en) → AND-META-01, META-06
- **2024-09 · diabetes watch face (glucose/IOB complications)** — Health apps declaration rejected with "Activity and exercise monitor", then with disease management, then with "Other"; the policy snippet quoted the whole store description. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/297323167) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-09
- **2026-06 → 2026-07 · paid Wear OS watch face** — production access request "locked" for 45 days with no rejection or action item; support only said the technical team is investigating (several similar threads: 441197110, 446686094). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/452695301) (medium, en) → AND-TEST-01

### Android Auto — additional
- **2026 · Bulgaria-only navigation app (BlitzWay), Android Auto** — rejected with the "EP-1/EP-2" functionality template ("could not load a map to start navigation") because the reviewer's device was outside Bulgaria; the geo-restriction was documented in App access and two videos were supplied; a previous identical dispute had been resolved through the EU out-of-court mechanism. Lesson: geo-locked apps must offer a mock-location/"review region" switch, not just instructions. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/420471425) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, CONTENT-13
- **2025-08 · app whose Auto screen shows a "log in on your phone" button when logged out** — "Missing demo or guest account details" with a screenshot of that Auto screen; working credentials in App access did not help — the reviewer expects the Auto surface itself to be usable with the demo account (pre-login on the phone before the head-unit test is not assumed). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/368360143) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2024-12 · BLE-device control app adding Android Auto** — "We could not access the in-app content with the login credentials that you have provided." although the phone app passed; PE: for hardware-bound apps put a public page (video + guide) link in App access. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/311114524) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03
- **2025-04 · Mappls-SDK navigation app** — Auto rejected because the map did not load on the reviewer's head unit although it worked on real cars; PE: connectivity, memory or geo-blocking in the review environment — fix it, do not resubmit the same build. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/337067941) (medium, en) → AND-REVIEW-01

### 7. Data deletion / Data safety SDK mismatches / prominent disclosure

### Prominent disclosure (2025–2026, mostly location)
- **2026-07 · OpenRemote IoT console (Kotlin)** — "Your app is not compliant with the Prominent Disclosure and Consent Requirement of the User Data policy. Requests for in-app user consent and runtime permission are not immediately preceded by an in-app disclosure." with a review video. Cause: all rationale dialogs were gated behind `shouldShowRequestPermissionRationale`, so first-time requests went straight to the system dialog and the "Background location needed" dialog appeared *after* it. Fix: disclosure before every runtime prompt (fine + background location, notifications, storage, camera, Bluetooth), background wording "even when the app is closed or not in use". [source](https://github.com/openremote/console-android/issues/67) (high, en) → PRIV-04, AND-PERM-04
- **2026-06 · audio-tour app (walkspace, v1.1.7)** — "Inadequate Prominent Disclosure" under User Data; the evidence screenshot showed a build several versions old. Fix: full-screen "Location Access" disclosure on first launch before any prompt, stating background collection "including in the background while a tour is active", EN/IT/FR, tested on a physical Galaxy A03s. [source](https://github.com/gigiorda84/walkspace-app/pull/1) (medium, en) → PRIV-04, AND-PERM-04, AND-REVIEW-01
- **2026-05 · RADAR-base research app** — rejected for missing a prominent disclosure before the `ACCESS_BACKGROUND_LOCATION` runtime request; fix: a dedicated dialog layout shown immediately before the OS background-location prompt. [source](https://github.com/RADAR-base/radar-commons-android/pull/503) (medium, en) → PRIV-04, AND-PERM-04
- **2026-02 · TrashMob (.NET MAUI) route tracking** — video review rejected for a foreground location service: missing prominent disclosure and an unnecessary background-location declaration. Fix: drop `ACCESS_BACKGROUND_LOCATION` (a foreground service with `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_LOCATION` only needs fine location), request `LocationWhenInUse`, add the disclosure dialog. [source](https://github.com/TrashMob-eco/TrashMob/pull/2907) (medium, en) → PRIV-04, AND-PERM-04, AND-PERM-05
- **2025-08 → 2026-05 · app with Braintree Drop-in 6.17.0** — "Prominent Disclosure & Consent Requirement: Violation of User Data policy… Braintree Payments SDK com.braintreepayments.api:data-collector: Consider upgrading to version 4.31.0 of the SDK" plus the full "upload the modified policy compliant APK across all tracks, and deactivate the noncompliant APK(s)" checklist; removing the SDK made the release pass. Vendor: the version hint is stale — set `dropInRequest.hasUserLocationConsent` (added in Drop-in 6.16.0) so the Magnes data collector does not collect location without consent. [source](https://github.com/braintree/braintree-android-drop-in/issues/505) (high, en) → PRIV-04, AND-PERM-13, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2026-08 · app using an AccessibilityService for one callback** — "Issue found: Prominent disclosure Non compliant design — We were unable to approve your app because the prominent disclosure of your use of the AccessibilityService API does not meet one or more of the following guidelines… Must present the consent dialog clearly and unambiguously before requesting the permission; Must require affirmative user action…; Must not interpret navigation away from the disclosure… as consent; Must not use auto-dismissing or expiring messages… Note: A toggle button will not meet the threshold for explicit consent… apps with only one button (for example 'Continue', 'OK', etc.) and/or utilizing a physical button as a form of consent will not be compliant. Apps must have two buttons clearly displaying consent options for users." Repeated boilerplate rejections. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/457911309) (high, en) → AND-PERM-06, PRIV-04

### Data safety ↔ SDK mismatches (named SDKs)
- **2026-04 → 2026-05 · Swiss investment app (RealUnit) with the Sumsub KYC SDK (`flutter_idensic_mobile_sdk_plugin`)** — "Issue found: Invalid Data safety form — The way that your app collects and shares user data does not match your declaration in the data safety form. We detected user data transmitted off devices that you have not disclosed in your app's data safety form as user data collected… Precise Location". Cause: the Sumsub AAR adds location permissions and sends geo signals for anti-fraud during KYC. Fix: declare Precise location (collected, shared with Sumsub) in Data safety and the privacy policy — no code change. [source](https://github.com/RealUnitCH/app/issues/463) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-02, PRIV-02, AND-PERM-13
- **2026-01 · Flutter app with `gma_mediation_inmobi` 2.0.0** — rejected for "policy issue(s)" because the mediation plugin's manifest declares `ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION` (no longer needed by InMobi ≥ 10.8.3). Google's answer: keep it (better targeting) or `tools:node="remove"` in the app manifest. [source](https://github.com/googleads/googleads-mobile-flutter/issues/1384) (high, en) → AND-PERM-13, AND-CONSOLE-02
- **2024-10 · mixed-audience app on AppsFlyer + Amplitude** — see area 4 (thread 299944705): the rejection named Amplitude as the AAID destination while AppsFlyer was the real sender before the age screen. → AND-CONSOLE-04

Empty after search: no thread names TikTok, Sentry, Branch, Adjust or Firebase Performance as the SDK behind a Data safety mismatch in 2025–2026 (the Adjust/AppsFlyer/Facebook cases already collected remain the only ones); no new account-deletion-URL thread beyond those in the 2nd pass except 455158541 (2026-07, listed in the "Account/data-deletion URL" block below).

### 8. Sign-in details / paywalls / broken functionality / old build reviewed

- **2026-08 · veterinary pet-care app with subscription paywall (VetPet)** — "Play Console Requirements — App content is restricted by a paywall / could not review with the login credentials provided." The rejection screenshots showed *version code 11* (old pricing, no login button) while the developer had uploaded version 25 with a reviewer account holding an active Pro subscription and a top-right "Log in" button. Lesson: the review may run on an older active bundle in another track; deactivate it first. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/453491093) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, AND-CONSOLE-11, ACC-03
- **2025-05 → 2025-06 · subscription-only app with no login (years on Play)** — "Paywall restriction. The in-app experience is behind a paywall or a subscription is required. Update your app login credentials in the app access declaration." with a 14-day removal deadline; "the examiner just needs to subscribe" was no longer accepted and the single appeal was used. Fix (Diamond PE): create a fresh Gmail account, subscribe it to the app and put those credentials in Sign-in details; PE escalation restored the app ~3 weeks later. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/346992873) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03, PAY-04
- **2025-12 · five anonymous-login apps with everything behind a paywall** — accepted pattern: a reviewer landing page whose deep link grants the current anonymous UID premium status for 24 h, plus a License-testing Google account for free test purchases, both described in Sign-in details ("This app uses anonymous login; no credentials are required. To bypass the paywall for review: …"). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/395468868) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03, PAY-04
- **2026-02 · app with a valid demo account** — repeatedly "Username or password didn't work" under Play Console Requirements → App Access. PE checklist: no geo-restriction on the demo account, no OTP/expiry, then a support ticket. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/406692104) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03
- **2026-04 · app whose reviewer login was a deep-link flow** — two rejections ("reviewers were unable to log in") → suspension; after suspension the App access declaration can no longer be edited, so the fixed HTTPS reviewer login with a reusable test account (no OTP, no expiry) could only be described in the appeal, which was judged on the original rejections. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/427569202) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-02 · Emberkin, P2P parental-monitoring app (background service, accessibility)** — production-access request after the 14-day/20-tester closed test rejected for "Insufficient user engagement" — a silent background app generates no engagement events; told to run the 14 days again. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=46938101) (medium, en) → AND-TEST-01

Empty after search: no new Kakao/Naver/LINE/WeChat-only login or OTP-only rejection thread beyond those already collected; "Broken Functionality" network-off and Play-signing SHA mismatch searches returned only known cases.

### 9. Enforcement: High Risk Behavior, associated accounts, repeated rejections, malware/DNA

- **2026-03 · Dott Plus (Pakistan)** — suspended under the Enforcement Process policy after four rejections in a month (unjustified READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/VIDEO → Photo Picker; app failed to load → Firebase init fix; crash → NPE fix; invalid demo credentials → administrative mistake); appeal denied with "repeated violations". Lesson: four *different* minor rejections on one app within ~30 days is enough for suspension; test each fix on a fresh install before resubmitting. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/419880770) (high, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01, AND-PERM-08, AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2026-08 · Control Center & Wallpaper app (Skyline Techs)** — app suspended under the Malware policy, then the whole account terminated (DDA 8.3); appeal reply: "Billing Fraud — code that automatically charges the user in an intentionally deceptive way". Real cause: a "Try for Free" button charged immediately because no trial phase was configured on the SKU; two appeals with source code got the same paragraph. Lesson: a trial label without a configured free-trial phase is treated as malware/billing fraud, not as a subscriptions-policy issue. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/460254661) (high, en) → PAY-02, PAY-06, AND-QUALITY-02
- **2025-06 · Hong Kong company account, zero activity since the last release** — "High-risk behavior" termination; the notice: "The termination… is related to the developer's behavior and may also affect multiple registered accounts and associated Google services." No policy change, no ads, Play Integrity in use; the developer suspects account association. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/349999133) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2025-08 · first organization account, non-custodial blockchain-content app** — terminated for "High Risk Behavior" a month after verification. PE appeal checklist worth reusing: (1) anyone with access to the account linked to a terminated account? (2) are all assets self-made or licensed? (3) is all user data declared? (4) could the app be confused with another app? [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/366576846) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2025-09 · 2-year-old account, main app at 6 M downloads** — terminated overnight for "High Risk Behavior" with no prior warning. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/374067366) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-08 · Loryana LLC** — terminated 2025-12-30 for "High Risk Behavior… may be related to an association with another developer account that was previously terminated"; still terminated 8 months later despite offering registration documents. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/459208348) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-08 · PCI-DSS payment app (LLC, Flutterwave)** — organization account removed for an "Associated Account" violation after an external contractor accessed the console through a VPN whose shared IP was already flagged; automated appeal denial. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/458559297) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026 · Indian startup (Tyvoll)** — first account terminated for "Related Account Violation" after a third-party contractor set up the console; appeal denied without disclosing the association. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/453939309) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-07 · first-time developer, devotional-content apps** — "related account" termination 6 weeks after production access; the only plausible link: a second-hand phone whose previous owner had a terminated account. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/450410068) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-04 · Sova AI (on-device agent that operates other apps through the Accessibility API)** — rejected by Play for using accessibility for "universal automation"; the team distributes the APK directly. A Korean banking-security engineer in the thread notes such automation is indistinguishable from FakeCall-style malware. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47613614) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-06
- **2024-07 · coding-education app using VpnService for a terminal sandbox** — "Use of VpnService is not a permitted use case"; PE: no exemption for non-VPN use, "VPN Service core functionality requirement does not change by account type". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/286341962) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-14
- **2024-09 → 2025-03 · Flutter app with connectivity_plus** — "Issue found: Intent Redirection — We found that your app contains security vulnerabilities… This is a violation of Device and Network Abuse policy. Specifically, your app(s) are vulnerable to Intent Redirection… Code Analysis: 'zc.b$b.onReceive'" (an obfuscated BroadcastReceiver); discussion points to `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION` handling. [source](https://github.com/fluttercommunity/plus_plugins/issues/3255) (medium, en) → AND-SEC-01
- **2023-10 → 2025-03 · Expo/EAS app bundling Robolectric** — "We reviewed SDKs used by your app and found noncompliant version(s) of SDK(s) which contains code to download executable code (for example, dex, JAR, .so files) from a source other than Google Play." (Google misspelled it "roboelectric"); cause: Robolectric on `implementation` instead of `testImplementation` (EAS shipped `librobolectric-nativeruntime.dylib` in the AAB). [source](https://github.com/robolectric/robolectric/issues/8563) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02, AND-PERM-13
- **2025-03 · app embedding YoutubeDl-Boom (yt-dlp wrapper)** — "The app may be modifying, replacing, or updating itself using a method other than Google Play's update mechanism. It may also be downloading executable code (e.g., dex files, JARs, .so files) dynamically" — the library downloads Python/yt-dlp binaries at runtime. [source](https://github.com/farimarwat/YoutubeDl-Boom/issues/7) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02
- **2025-03 · app using Apero in-house ads + Facebook/Vungle/Pangle mediation** — suspended under Device and Network Abuse; the developer could not locate the code; PEs asked for the notice text (ad SDKs that download/execute code are the usual cause). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/332791351) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02, ADS-02

### Account/data-deletion URL and Data safety — additional (2024–2026)
- **2026-07 · app that moved its account-deletion page to a new domain** — "Invalid account deletion link on your Data safety form" kept quoting the *old* URL after a new open-testing release. Expert: the deletion URL is an app-wide App content → Data safety declaration, not part of a release — edit it there and re-submit; the old URL was returning "Internal Server Error". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/455158541) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-08, META-04
- **2024-08 → 2024-09 · app with no in-app account creation** — "You have declared that your app does not allow users to create an account in your app's Data safety form and we've detected that there is account creation available." Fix: the web deletion page must let a user request deletion *without opening the app* (uninstalled users); appeal accepted after rewriting the page. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/293361577) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-08, ACC-02
- **2024-08 · two university/workplace apps using Microsoft SSO** — same "account creation available" rejection because the Microsoft login page shows "No account? Create one"; one appeal accepted, the resubmission rejected again for the same reason. Lesson: with third-party SSO, declare "users can create an account" (and give a deletion path) rather than arguing. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/292353185) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-08, ACC-04
- **2024-08 · school app whose accounts are created by admins on another platform** — appeal accepted, then rejected again after resubmission; another developer in the thread: "release blocked because of an old bundle in open testing" while using Azure + Google SSO. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/291231626) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-08, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2024-09 · app on shared hosting (Hostinger)** — deletion link flagged invalid because the host was down during review; PE: just resend the same URL for review, no ticket needed. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/297044448) (medium, en) → META-04, AND-CONSOLE-08
- **2025-01 · game using a Notion form as the deletion page** — "The account/data deletion link in the Data Safety form is invalid." eight times; the form described retention of banned device IDs and in-app deletion; Notion pages are frequently unreadable to the checker (JS-rendered). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/315928821) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-08, META-04
- **2025-07 · Unity game with the ironSource/Unity LevelPlay SDK** — the SDK's data-deletion form URL was deprecated (now e-mail only) and the Data safety form refuses an e-mail address; PE: host your own deletion page that lists the third-party services and forwards requests. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/359863261) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-08
- **2026-03 · Unity game using the Google Play Games plugin** — rejected for transmitting "Device identifiers and other IDs" not declared; `GooglePlayGamesManifest.androidlib` injects the Nearby Connections SERVICE_ID, Games APP_ID and unityVersion meta-data; removing them did not clear the flag until *every* track used the new bundle and production was at 100 %. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/416425534) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-02, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2025-12 · Expo/React-Native app with Meta SDK + Firebase** — "Version code 3: Policy Declaration - Data Safety Section: Device Or Other IDs Data Type - Device Or Other IDs (some common examples may include Advertising ID, Android ID, IMEI, BSSID, MAC address)." although Device or other IDs was declared collected + shared with all purposes. Unresolved at posting; typical culprit is a still-active older bundle or a missing "shared" partner. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/391811655) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-02
- **2025-10 · enterprise app with the Scandit barcode SDK (upgraded 6.1.2 → 6.28.6 for 16 KB)** — "discrepancies between your disclosure in the Data safety section and app behavior… data types sent off device that were not yet disclosed: Device Or Other IDs"; Scandit's SDK-generated device ID counts even though it is not a hardware ID; appeal denied. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/381052297) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-02, PRIV-02, AND-SDK-02
- **2025-06 · school app (HTTPS only)** — "Invalid Encryption Declaration" on the Data safety form; PE: the e-mail normally names the unencrypted endpoint — usually an SDK or a WebView asset loaded over http. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/352952614) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-02

### 16 KB and target API (2025–2026)
- **2025-08 · app update** — console message "From May 31, 2026, if your app updates do not support 16 KB memory page sizes, you won't be able to release these updates. Your latest production release does not support 16 KB memory page sizes." shown as a rejection reason three months before the Nov-2025 date; PEs treated it as a warning mis-read as a rejection. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/368386720) (medium, en) → AND-SDK-02
- **2026-05 · developer asking what happens after 31-May-2026** — PE answers: the live app is not removed; non-compliant uploads are blocked after the app's deadline; compliant uploads are accepted afterwards. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/433563114) (medium, en) → AND-SDK-02
- **2026-07 · app on targetSdk 35** — "Update your target API level by August 31, 2026." is a reminder; from 31-Aug-2026 updates must target SDK 36. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/453449487) (medium, en) → AND-SDK-01
- **2025-09 · app already on API 36** — the "App must target Android 15 (API level 35) or higher" warning persisted because an older bundle in another track still targeted 34. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/374788272) (medium, en) → AND-SDK-01, AND-CONSOLE-11

Empty after search: no developer report yet of an actual *hard block* for 16 KB (2027-02) or of a blocked upload on 2026-08-31 for targetSdk 36 — only pre-deadline questions.

### 10. Framework-specific (2025–2026), beyond cases already collected

- **2025-11 · .NET MAUI 9 app using `MediaPicker`** — rejected under Photo and Video Permissions because the documented manifest entries (`READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/VIDEO`, legacy storage) are only for one-time capture/selection. Fix: delete the entries — on Android 13+ MediaPicker works without them (runtime prompts still appear); Android ≤ 12 camera capture then fails until .NET 10, which fixes it. [source](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/32866) (high, en) → AND-PERM-08, AND-PERM-13
- **2026-05 · .NET MAUI app (Krauthi/ipm)** — same rejection; fix: remove READ/WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE (maxSdk 32), READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/VIDEO/AUDIO, request `Permissions.Photos` only on iOS, use `FilePicker.Default.PickMultipleAsync` on Android. [source](https://github.com/Krauthi/ipm/pull/65) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-08
- **2026-06 · Flutter app (image_picker 1.2.1 + gal 2.3.2)** — versionCode 6 rejected: "Permission use is not directly related to your app's core purpose"; the permissions were declared directly in the manifest and unused; fix: `tools:node="remove"` pins so no future plugin re-adds them (manifest-merger blame report used as evidence). [source](https://github.com/gwhitley1969/CliquePix/pull/52) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-08, AND-PERM-13
- **2024-04 · Flutter app with agora_rtc_engine 6.2.1 (audio calls only)** — updates rejected for undeclared `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MEDIA_PROJECTION` injected by Agora's `full-screen-sharing` module. Fix: `configurations.all { exclude group:"io.agora.rtc", module:"full-screen-sharing" }` **and** delete `android/.gradle/caches` + `app/build` before rebuilding — several developers still saw the permission until the caches were cleared. [source](https://github.com/AgoraIO-Extensions/Agora-Flutter-SDK/issues/1666) (high, en) → AND-PERM-05, AND-PERM-13
- **2026-01 · Flutter `gma_mediation_inmobi` 2.0.0** — plugin manifest adds `ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION` (see area 7). [source](https://github.com/googleads/googleads-mobile-flutter/issues/1384) → AND-PERM-13
- **2026-05 · Flutter `flutter_idensic_mobile_sdk_plugin` (Sumsub KYC)** — undeclared Precise location (see area 7). [source](https://github.com/RealUnitCH/app/issues/463) → AND-CONSOLE-02, AND-PERM-13
- **2026-08 · React Native / Expo with `@iternio/react-native-auto-play` (CarPlay only)** — phantom Android Auto `CarAppService` merged from the library (see area 6). [source](https://github.com/cw0rley/Speed-Limit/pull/1) → AND-PERM-13, AND-BUILD-02
- **2023-10 → 2025-03 · Expo/EAS with Robolectric on `implementation`** — "downloads executable code" DNA rejection (see area 9). [source](https://github.com/robolectric/robolectric/issues/8563) → AND-QUALITY-02
- **2026-03 · Unity + Google Play Games plugin** — device-ID meta-data injected by `GooglePlayGamesManifest.androidlib` (see area 7). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/416425534) → AND-CONSOLE-02

Empty after search: no new Capacitor, Godot or Kotlin-Multiplatform rejection tied to an injected permission/FGS type in 2025–2026; Unity issues in this period are ad-SDK (Unity Ads → DNA) rather than manifest injection.

### Device and Network Abuse via ad SDKs, re-scans, stalkerware flag, repeated-rejection suspensions (2025–2026)
- **2026-08 · portfolio of ~40 casino/"earn money" games on Unity Ads** — several apps suspended under Device and Network Abuse for "causing users to download or install applications from unknown sources outside Google Play"; the developer saw a Unity Ads creative start an APK download, disabled Unity Ads account-wide, invalidated Game IDs and rebuilt all 40 apps on AdMob within 48 h. Two appeals accepted, two rejected, a third app suspended while its corrected build was already in review. Lesson: the app is liable for mediated creatives; suspension can land mid-review. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/458872947) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02, ADS-02, AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-08 · AdMob-mediation app** — same suspension; the offending ad was found in the AdMob Ad Review Center (landing page redirecting to a third-party APK site); blocked/reported it, disabled all mediation sources, tightened blocking controls, full audit — appeal still upheld. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/462642224) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02, ADS-02
- **2026-08 · 5-year-old subscription app** — same suspension for AdMob-served APK-download ads; removed AdMob entirely; appeal answered with the standard template asking to "re-release" the app (i.e. republish under a new listing). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/459280152) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02, ADS-02, AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-08 · app whose DNA appeal was conditionally accepted** — told to deactivate version code 25 and publish a compliant 26; while 26 was being tested the automated scanner re-scanned bundle 25 still on the production track and re-suspended the app, locking the console.
