PE: "you needed to deactivate the bundle before uploading anything else". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/458733551) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-11, AND-QUALITY-02
- **2026-04 · lock-screen app (AdMob, Firebase, Retrofit, Glide, PyTorch)** — "Device and Network Abuse" for version code 8 ("downloading executable code or APKs from unknown sources") with no such feature and no REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES; answers point at PyTorch dynamic model download / other runtime-loaded binaries and at old bundles in closed/internal tracks. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/427150012) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2024-12 · app flagged "Malware policy — ELEVATED PRIVILEGE ABUSE"** — PE: this label is normally reserved for root apps abusing root permissions; post the notice to identify the code. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/313897268) (medium, en) → AND-QUALITY-02
- **2025-05 · workplace attendance app (face clock-in + geolocation)** — flagged under "Stalkerware and Monitoring Applications" although `<meta-data android:name="android.app.isMonitoringTool" android:value="false"/>` was added — placed both inside `<application>` and (wrongly) at manifest root. Google's answer/12955211 is the reference: the flag goes inside `<application>` and monitoring apps must also show a persistent notification and unique icon. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/342455393) (medium, en) → AND-BUILD-02
- **2025-05 · app update** — rejected "the 'isMonitoringTool' flag is missing from the manifest file"; pointed to the same help page. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/347248114) (medium, en) → AND-BUILD-02
- **2026-05 · Spectoria AI Video Studio** — one screenshot (`APP_SCREENSHOT-4207.png`) with a "Best/#1/Top"-type claim → Metadata rejection; after replacing it, the resubmission was answered with a suspension under the Enforcement Process policy for "repeated app rejections"; appeal rejected. Lesson: rejections on the same listing accumulate even when each is fixed. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/437114451) (medium, en) → AND-META-01, AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026 · suspension notice text (Enforcement Process)** — "Your app was suspended for repeated non-compliance with Developer Program Policies, demonstrated by multiple previous rejections… In-app experience: 'Repeated app rejections.'… Review other apps within your developer account… Once all necessary changes have been implemented across your portfolio, you may submit an appeal… submitting an appeal does not guarantee the app will be reinstated." [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/454167198) (high, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2024-05 · Arabic game whose release name was abbreviated to "JYL"** — suspended for Deceptive Behavior because the release/app name differed from the store name; the abbreviation matched the package name `com.kingsarabia.JYL`; appeal (case 3-4132000036447) refused a year later. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/438048369) (medium, en) → AND-META-01, AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-06 · developer terminated in 2024-03 for High Risk Behavior (first app: a rewarded-ads viewer)** — used the "2026 one-time Appeal Grace Period (deadline July 27, 2026)" to request a human review; PE pointed to the community guide on prior-violation terminations and the standard appeal path. Several similar threads (449721620, 453628921, 416272805, 407486314) show the grace-period appeal being template-denied or blocked by a "180-day rule". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/444664290) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01

### Sign-in details — additional (2024–2026)
- **2025-01 · app that disabled its reviewer test account after launch** — ~15 days after release Google e-mailed that the credentials were inaccessible during a re-test. PE rule: "Your test login credentials must be accessible at all times, reusable, and valid regardless of user location… if your app typically requires a 2-Step Verification code or One-Time Password, make sure to provide reusable login credentials that can bypass these requirements." Post-launch re-reviews use the same Sign-in details. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/320923249) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03
- **2025-10 · CoachMi user app** — repeated "login credentials are invalid" despite a no-OTP demo account and a video; PE: every step must be in the App Instructions field itself (reviewers do not open external videos first). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/379117381) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03
- **2024-08 · app with social login resubmitted after a long gap** — still "login credentials issue" after refreshing the Gmail test account (2FA off). PE test recipe: download the APK from App bundle explorer, install it locally and paste the exact credentials from the console — "that is what the review team will be doing". [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/289523996) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-04, AND-REVIEW-01
- **2024-01 · OTP-login app** — hundreds of login attempts during review even with a fixed demo OTP; and 2023-09 PE guidance for phone-OTP apps: provide a (possibly fake) phone number plus an OTP that always works for it. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/254105776) / [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/233509374) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03

### Associated-account terminations — additional (2026)
- **2026-06 · Indian IT-services agency (organization account)** — terminated after two external clients were given app-level admin access; their apps (Tower Rush, Royal Reels) were suspended for Riskware/Malware within weeks and the whole organization account followed; appeal rejected. Lesson: delegated console access to a client makes their malware yours. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/439519048) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026 · account terminated for a home-network link to another terminated account** — the linked account was later reinstated, but the appeal button is gone and support refuses anything "outside the 180 day window"; no path for new evidence. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/462400222) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-08 · first-time developer who lent his laptop to a relative** — "High Risk Behavior and associated accounts" termination attributed to another person's login on the same device before the account was created; appeal rejected. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/458605579) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2026-07 · developer terminated before 2026-01-28** — could not find any form for the "one-time grace period" appeal (deadline 2026-07-27): every support path redirected to the original termination page. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/453628921) (medium, en) → AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2025-11 · manual meal-logging app** — suspended under Deceptive Behavior with no specific reason; PE: the three enumerated items and the closing paragraph of the notice are the only hints, usually metadata promising something the app cannot do. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/388566491) (medium, en) → AND-META-01, AND-QUALITY-02

### Prominent disclosure / SMS / old-bundle loops — additional (2025–2026)
- **2025-01 · Google Play PE announcement "Tips and best practices for complying with Login Credentials requirements"** — the official checklist reviewers apply: credentials accessible at all times, reusable, valid regardless of location; OTP/2-step bypass; credentials in English; QR codes/PIN set-up instructions; third-party sign-in ("sign in with Google, Facebook") with full account details; geo-gated passwords valid everywhere; and for paywalled apps without login, "additional instructions or access details that will allow us to fully and freely access and review the app behind the paywall"; keep instructions simple ("imagine that you are teaching a parent"), up to five screenshots. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/317411637) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03, PAY-04
- **2026-02 · app with background location, rejected for months** — "Inadequate Prominent Disclosure" with the *same* months-old screenshot (same version, clock, hand) on every new release; the reviewer credentials were never used. PE: an old bundle is still active — see the community guide "Deactivating a bundle" (community-guide/246555298); push 100 % on all tracks, then open a Help ticket. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/413467257) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-11, PRIV-04
- **2026-01 · location app** — "Inadequate Prominent Disclosure" citing a screen no longer in the app although unused tracks were *paused*; PE: "Pausing is not enough, you will have to either fully replace or deactivate the track. In the production track, the new build must also be fully rolled out (100%) without the old bundle/s." [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/398834984) (high, en) → AND-CONSOLE-11, PRIV-04
- **2026-02 · location app** — "Issue found: Inadequate Prominent Disclosure — The in-app Prominent Disclosure does not disclose the usage of accessed or collected Location data." (five developers with the same question) — the disclosure must state *how the location is used*, not just that it is accessed. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/407575005) (high, en) → PRIV-04
- **2025-04 · app reading contacts** — PE restated the six prominent-disclosure requirements (in the app itself, in normal usage, describes the data, explains use/sharing, not only in a privacy policy/ToS, not bundled with unrelated disclosures). [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/340118209) (medium, en) → PRIV-04
- **2026 · Focus Messenger (contacts)** — "Prominent Disclosure for Contacts" rejection loop on versions 149–151 although a standalone AlertDialog precedes READ_CONTACTS; the appeal revealed the reviewer could not log in (expired demo credentials), and the identical build was rejected again after fixing them. Lesson: a login failure is reported under whatever policy the static scan flags, not as a credentials problem. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/434648529) (medium, en) → AND-CONSOLE-03, PRIV-04, AND-PERM-13
- **2025-01 · app that removed `queryIntentActivities` + `<package android:name="com.facebook.orca"/>`** — "Your app is uploading users' Installed Application information without a prominent disclosure." cleared only after the approved build reached 100 % on all tracks; generic `<queries><intent>` filters are fine. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/317529241) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-02, PRIV-04, AND-CONSOLE-11
- **2026-05 · Simple SMS Forwarder (RECEIVE_SMS + SEND_SMS, declared "Cross-device synchronization or transfer of SMS")** — rejected twice: "Store listing information does not match the core functionality for the declared use case; In-app experience does not match the core functionality for the declared use case". PE: forwarding to another number is not *synchronisation*; sync requires a backend that mirrors messages. The developer later confirmed Google's reply matched that reading. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/436772560) (high, en) → AND-PERM-01
- **2024-08 · SMS-to-e-mail forwarding app, >10 rejections** — "Issue found: Unable to verify core functionality of app — Your declared that your permission use case is the core functionality of your app. However, after review, we found that your app does not match the declared use case(s)." under the same sync category. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/288938705) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-01
- **2026-05 · Cyber Phone (default Phone + SMS handler)** — several cycles of "unable to verify" under SMS/Call Log and foreground-service policies although default-handler prompts precede every restricted feature; no screen named. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/429998983) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-01, AND-PERM-05
- **2026-07 · Connected (open-source local-first phone↔desktop sync incl. SMS/calls)** — approved once with a demo video, then every minor update auto-rejected with "couldn't find evidence that the app offers the declared core functionality" despite five new videos. Lesson: SMS/Call-log declarations are re-verified on *every* update; keep the demo path reachable in seconds. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/455323271) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-01
- **2025-04 · missed-call verification app (Call Log shared with Exotel)** — live a month, then suspended under the Spyware policy: "Your app currently contains malicious code or behavior that can be considered as spying on the user or exfiltrates data that is not related to provide policy-compliant functionality." Appeal returned the same text; no pre-launch report access. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/340445859) (medium, en) → AND-PERM-01, AND-QUALITY-02
- **2024-09 · v1.30 update rejected for "Broken Functionality"** — no screenshot or trace; installs and runs on 4 physical + 10 virtual devices and in the pre-launch report; appeal returned the same canned text; PE asks for the screenshot — without one nothing can be diagnosed. [source](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/thread/296060165) (medium, en) → AND-REVIEW-01, CONTENT-01

## Korean and Japanese sources — 3rd pass (2026-08-28: GitHub issues, OKKY, brunch, damoang, teratail, hatena, Toss mini-app)

Collected 2026-08-28. Sources: GitHub issues/PRs in Korean and Japanese (via `gh api search/issues`, ~120 threads read), OKKY, brunch, damoang, teratail, Naver blog (mobile endpoint), a tistory post found via a GitHub cross-reference. All URLs were checked against `known_urls_kj.txt` (773 URLs) — every entry below is new. `high` = full thread/page read with reviewer wording; `medium` = summary-level thread, pre-emptive audit, or unresolved question. Pre-emptive audits (no rejection yet) are labelled `(pre-emptive)` and kept only when they document a concrete reviewer-facing rule.

---

### Apple App Store

### 1.2 User-Generated Content
- **2026-08 · reading-community app, RN/Expo, Windows dev env (ko)** — 1.2 + 5.1.1(v). "A mechanism for users to block abusive users (blocking should also notify the developer of the inappropriate content and should remove it from the user's feed instantly)". Cause: terms only as a My-page web link (no pre-signup EULA), report UI not wired to server, no block; account deletion existed but was a grey `#888` text at the very bottom of My-page (`paddingTop: 100`) and reviewer on iPad Air 11" (M3) never found it. Fix: EULA consent screen before signup with zero-tolerance clause, report API, block that filters feed/comments/records server-side, deletion entry promoted, 3 physical-device recordings (consent/report/block+delete) + demo account in Review Notes. Team notes: "회신만으로는 통과할 수 없습니다", simulator recordings are refused. 1 rejection, in progress. [source](https://github.com/THIP-TextHip/THIP-Mobile/issues/172) / [remaining work](https://github.com/THIP-TextHip/THIP-Mobile/issues/177) (high, ko) → UGC-01, UGC-03, UGC-04, UGC-06, ACC-02, ACC-03, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-03 · barter/second-hand app, Flutter v1.9.71 (ko)** — 5.1.5 + 4.0 + 1.2 in one round on iPad Air 11" (M3)/iPadOS 26.3.1. "The app is not functional when Location Services are disabled." / "Specifically, the menu bar at the bottom was cut off." / "Make sure to provide a demo account with pre-populated content that will allow us to verify all of the precautions." Cause: app blocked without location; `flutter_screenutil` `.h` units scaled a 70.h bottom bar to ~194pt on iPad; UGC safeguards mostly existed but no demo account / review notes so reviewer could not verify. Fix: fully functional without location, fixed-pixel bottom bar + iOS bottom SafeArea, demo account with pre-populated content + notes. Full rejection email preserved in the thread. [source](https://github.com/TEAM-ROMROM/RomRom-FE/issues/675) / [iPad bar](https://github.com/TEAM-ROMROM/RomRom-FE/issues/677) / [UGC](https://github.com/TEAM-ROMROM/RomRom-FE/issues/678) (high, ko) → PERM-02, IOS-IPAD-01, UGC-01~06, ACC-03, META-05
- **2026-06 · KBO baseball community app, Capacitor/Next.js (ko)** — 2.1(a) crash + 1.2. Cause: `Info.plist` had no `NSCameraUsageDescription` although profile photo used `<input type="file" accept="image/*">` (camera via WebView → immediate crash); report/block buttons rendered only in DM, not on the community board, and no feed filter for blocked users. Fix: 3 usage strings; EULA checkbox gate in login sheet with zero-tolerance + 24h wording; report/block on every post/comment/chat message; block = `user_blocks` + auto-report to ops + instant removal; content filter applied on create **and edit** paths (internal reviewer insisted edits are a UGC write path too). Then re-record EULA/report/block/filter on device. [source](https://github.com/hwinside/kbo-everyday/pull/417) (high, ko) → PERM-01, UGC-01~04, UGC-06, CONTENT-12
- **2024-11 · lyrics/notes app, Swift (ko)** — invalid binary, then 1.2 + 2.1, then 1.2 again. "Please provide a pre-populated demo account for Kakao. Please make sure that we'll bypass any verification step." Round 1: wrong value in privacy manifest ("잘못된 바이너리"). Round 2: full 1.2 list + Kakao demo account. Round 3: still "A mechanism for users to block abusive users" → added block/unblock. Approved on 4th. [source](https://github.com/project-lyrics/app-iOS/pull/88) (high, ko) → IOS-PRIV-03, UGC-01~05, UGC-04, ACC-05, ACC-03
- **2024-07 · movie-review social app (ko)** — 1.2 + 5.1.1. "Update the app to not require users to provide the following personal information: - Gender - Birth year". Cause: UGC precautions incomplete; signup required gender/birth year. Fix: precautions + optional fields. [source](https://github.com/imad-project/imad-ios/issues/118) (high, ko) → UGC-01~05, ACC-01, PRIV-04
- **2024-05 · second-hand/sharing community app (ko)** — 1.2 "악의적인 사용자를 차단하는 메커니즘 또는 신고에 대한 24시간 이내의 콘텐츠 조치". Fix: report-sheet placeholder states 3 accumulated reports → suspension; post-report toast promises 24h review; report flow video submitted. [source](https://github.com/APP-iOS4/ANBD/pull/352) (medium, ko) → UGC-04, UGC-05, UGC-06
- **2022-12 · Flutter community app, corporate first launch (ko)** — 1.2 "차단 기능" missing while Google approved the same build a month earlier. Also warns: features described in privacy policy but absent (background location, push) are a rejection reason; "test/테스트" strings in seeded data get rejected; social-login-only apps need a shared Google test account. iOS first review ~3 weeks, 1 rejection, second review 1 week; Google re-review 2 hours. [source](https://brunch.co.kr/@@d4Cs/17) (high, ko) → UGC-04, PRIV-02, CONTENT-02, ACC-03
- **2026-08 · goshuin (temple-stamp) map app, Expo, round 4 (ja)** — 4.8 + 1.2 after rounds 1–3 (5.1.1(iv)/(v), 2.1(a), 5.1.1 placeholder string) had cleared. Cause: Sign in with Apple existed on `LoginScreen`, but the fix for 5.1.1(iv) made onboarding exit with a single "続ける", so the reviewer landed on the map unauthenticated → FAB → `LoginPromptModal`, which only offered Google ("実装漏れではなく導線漏れ"). Gallery/bottom-sheet showed other users' photos/notes/display names → 1.2. Fix: Apple button above Google in the modal (order pinned by test); public UGC feature removed from v1.0 (v1.1 will add EULA/report/block/filter/24h). Internal reviewer found write-side still public (`default_stamp_public`). [source](https://github.com/Kaiwa-Jun/goshuin-app/pull/150) (high, ja) → IOS-LOGIN-01, UGC-01~05, CONTENT-10
- **2021-05 · photo-sharing pilot app, Google/Apple sign-in only, submitted from California (ja)** — 7 rejections (incl. metadata rejections) over 12 days on a *first* submission. (1) camera permission text had to state what the photo is used for; (2) UGC raised as three separate findings — terms must define objectionable content and the operator's response with a path to read them, a report function plus an explanation of how the operator acts ("24 時間以内に確認し… 削除や BAN"), and user/comment blocking (mailto-based reporting was accepted); (3) demo account impossible with social login only → walkthrough video in App Review Information (Apple did not accept `.mov`); (4) 4.3 Spam because the icon was identical to the company's other app; (5) "this button doesn't work" — it was the logo, explained in the Appeal field; (6) a reviewer change re-raised the already-answered terms finding. [source](https://tomoima525.hatenablog.com/entry/2021/05/16/161955) (high, ja) → PERM-01, UGC-01, UGC-03, UGC-04, UGC-05, ACC-03, META-05, CONTENT-07, META-07
- **2026-08 · team chat / workspace app, Web + Expo (ja, pre-emptive)** — 1.2 build-out before first iOS submission: report with 6 reasons + snapshot, block with DM/mention/push suppression, moderator allow-list, deterministic server filter, links from signup/settings. Documented as Apple-1.2 acceptance criteria. [source](https://github.com/keishingu/Cairn/issues/471) (medium, ja) → UGC-02~05

### 2.1 App Completeness — crashes, hangs, "unable to proceed", demo accounts
- **2026-08 · meetup/group app, Next.js in Expo WebView (ko)** — 2.1(a) "The app loaded indefinitely after we logged in through the 'Sign in with Apple' option." (iPad Air 11" M3 / iPadOS 26.6) + 2.1(a) Information Needed for a demo-account auth code. Cause: axios instance had no `timeout` (infinite pending), OAuth callback screen rendered a splash with no escape path, no `error.tsx`. Fix: 15s API timeout, 20s watchdog → `/login?error=timed_out`, error boundary with retry; web-only so deployed via Vercel without a new binary. Same app, 2 days earlier: 2.5.1 + 5.1.1 for unused purpose strings (see 2.5 section). [source](https://github.com/Central-MakeUs/moyeo-client/issues/244) (high, ko) → CONTENT-01, ACC-03, CONTENT-12
- **2026-08 · photo/AI service app, Kakao + Apple login only (ko)** — 2.1 "We were unable to sign in with the following demo account credentials". Cause: Kakao accounts need a Korean phone number so reviewers cannot create one and no demo Kakao existed; Sign in with Apple always lands as a new user → SMS verification the reviewer cannot pass. Fix: backend `POST /auth/review/login` issuing a USER token; hidden entry = long-press the logo 2s on the login screen; fixed bypass SMS code `123456` for `01012345678`; review-notes text drafted in the PR. [source](https://github.com/Finders-Official/FE/pull/374) (high, ko) → ACC-03, ACC-05, IOS-IPAD-02, META-05
- **2026-03 · multiplayer location game, Flutter v1.3.4 (ko)** — 5.1.1(iv) + 2.1(a). Reviewed on iPhone 17 Pro Max and iPad Air 11" (M3). Cause: after "허용 안 함" on location, app auto-called `Geolocator.openAppSettings()`/`openLocationSettings()`; no demo account for a multiplayer game. Fix: `ensurePermission()` only returns false, caller shows a dialog with a user-initiated "설정으로 이동"; demo account with pre-created sessions/chats + single-player demo mode considered. [source](https://github.com/cops-and-robbers/cops-and-robbers-FE/issues/173) (high, ko) → PERM-04, PERM-02, ACC-03, META-05
- **2026-02 · spec-management app "스펙토" (ko)** — five findings in one table: 1.5 (support URL was a blog → built a simple site), 2.1 ("앱 실행 시 표시되는 시작 화면 이후에는 아무런 동작도 나타나지 않습니다" → splash needed a scroll cue "Scroll down!" + demo video), 2.3.3 (iPad Pro screenshots showed an iPhone frame → iPad mock-ups), 4.8 (add Sign in with Apple), 4.0 ("여러 화면이 복잡하거나 레이아웃이 불편" → My-page button only tappable in simulator, verify on device). [source](https://github.com/salmonco/tip-archive/issues/84) (high, ko) → CONTENT-05, CONTENT-01, IOS-META-01, IOS-LOGIN-01, IOS-UI-01
- **2026-07 · re-planning/schedule app, Capacitor (ko)** — 2.1 crash on "사진 찍기" (build 6). Crash log: "attempted to access privacy-sensitive data without a usage description. The app's Info.plist must contain an NSCameraUsageDescription key". Fix: camera + photo-library strings, `ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption=false`, build 7. [source](https://github.com/PlanA-RePLAN/RePlan_FE/pull/83) (high, ko) → PERM-01, IOS-PLIST-01, IOS-META-03
- **2026-05 · aikido training-log app, Expo WebView wrapper (ja)** — 2.1(a) crash on "写真を撮る" (Build 25). Cause: camera launched via `<input type="file" capture>` inside WebView still triggers `UIImagePickerController`; `app.json` `ios.infoPlist` had only `ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption` → TCC SIGABRT. Fix: `NSCameraUsageDescription`, `NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription`, `NSMicrophoneUsageDescription` in Japanese. [source](https://github.com/Tomoya-Sonok/aikinote-native-app/pull/3) (high, ja) → PERM-01, IOS-PLIST-01, CONTENT-03
- **2026-05 · household-ledger app, SwiftUI + CloudKit (ja)** — 2.1(a) white screen at launch on iPad Air 11" (M3)/iPadOS 26.5. Cause: loading overlay dismissed unconditionally after 0.6s while CloudKit restore ran; iCloud-not-signed-in path waited up to 30s; on iPad a sheet did not inherit `EnvironmentObject` → blank. Fix: dismiss after restore task with 5s timeout, personal-mode fallback, account-status pre-check, EnvironmentObject injected into sheets, `guard let` instead of `!`. [source](https://github.com/irochi1184/KaKeBo/pull/186) (high, ja) → CONTENT-01, IOS-IPAD-01, CONTENT-12
- **2026-07 · chemistry quiz app v3.0.0 (ja)** — 2.1(a) "Bug description: Unable to proceed" on iPad Air 11" (M3)/iPadOS 26.5.2. Cause analysis: app had been switched to iPhone-only (`TARGETED_DEVICE_FAMILY` "1") purely to avoid iPad screenshots, but review ran on iPad in iPadOS 26 compatibility mode where window size differs; a `PageSwipeLock` helper disabled the first ancestor `UIScrollView` — the workbook list itself — so items below the fold were unreachable and the onboarding could not advance. Fix options: restore iPad support with 13" screenshots or test the compat mode; fix scroll-view targeting. [source](https://github.com/takoikatakotako/rikako/issues/250) (high, ja) → IOS-IPAD-01, CONTENT-01, IOS-META-01
- **2026-05 · fitness-video overlay tool, macOS (ja)** — 2.1(a) Information Needed: reviewer needed sample files to exercise the app. Fix: Garmin `.fit` + GoPro `.mp4` hosted permanently on GitHub Releases, step-by-step test instructions in the Resolution Center reply; on resubmission also hit build-number not bumped, hardened-runtime/team missing, "Sign to Run Locally" identity, expired Program License Agreement, and added `ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption=false`. [source](https://github.com/bokuno-studio/activity-video-studio/issues/40) (high, ja) → META-05, IOS-META-05, IOS-META-03, META-07
- **2026-02 · medicine barcode scanner (ja)** — 2.1 (no way to test scanning) + 1.5 (support URL) + 2.1 (IAP referenced but not submitted). Fix: 5 demo drug barcodes built into the DB plus a GitHub Pages page with EAN-13 images for the reviewer; support page with FAQ; create/submit the 3 IAP products in ASC. [source](https://github.com/bigmakers/konakannsa/pull/1) (medium, ja) → META-05, CONTENT-05, PAY-04, IOS-IAP-01
- **2026-04 · care-note app, Firebase (ja)** — 4.8 (no Sign in with Apple) + 2.1(a) (no demo account). Fix: SIWA + email/password auth, capability enabled in the portal and Firebase (Dev+Prod), `demo-reviewer@carenote.jp`, Review info, iPhone+iPad screenshots replaced, Build 22. [source](https://github.com/system-279/carenote-ios/issues/67) (medium, ja) → IOS-LOGIN-01, ACC-04, ACC-03
- **2026-05 · Mastodon-style client, Flutter, macOS App Store (ja)** — 2.1(a) "We received the attached action wheel indefinitely after logging in." Cause: with Distribution signing + App Sandbox, `flutter_secure_storage` without an explicit Keychain access-group `groupId` failed silently with `errSecMissingEntitlement (-34018)` so the OAuth token never persisted. Fix: `MacOsOptions(groupId:)`, hide push registration UI on macOS. [source](https://github.com/pooza/capsicum/pull/478) (high, ja) → IOS-ENT-01, CONTENT-12
- **2025-11 · office-search app (ja)** — 2.1 "Bug description: 'サテライトオフィスを探す' leads to an invalid page." on iPhone 13 mini and iPad Air (5th gen), iOS/iPadOS 26.0.1. Unanswered. [source](https://teratail.com/questions/pt2y3f78d2it4s) (medium, ja) → CONTENT-01, META-04
- **2021-05 · Unity quiz game (ja)** — 2.1 "we were not able to fully load your app content after launch" repeatedly; removing AdMob did not help; Apple sent no screenshot/log even when asked. Advice in thread: ask for device/OS/screenshot; suspect newest OS. Unresolved. [source](https://teratail.com/questions/338028) (medium, ja) → CONTENT-01, CONTENT-09
- **2024-10 · fashion-fit/review scraper app (ko)** — review rejected because scraper failure left the review screen in infinite loading. Fix: error status + concrete error messages when scraping fails. [source](https://github.com/swm-muinma/okku-server/pull/119) (medium, ko) → CONTENT-01
- **2026-08 · Flutter widget app built only on Codemagic, no Mac (ko)** — approved on the 3rd submission; "위젯을 설정하지 않은 상태에서 빈 화면이 나오면 리젝됩니다. placeholder를 반드시 넣어야 합니다. 인앱결제가 있으면 '구매 복원' 버튼도 필수입니다." No login/token (parses public GitHub contributions) so no account deletion / demo account / privacy items were needed. [source](https://okky.kr/articles/1561990) (high, ko) → IOS-BG-02, PAY-03, CONTENT-01
- **2021-12 · first-time submitter (ja)** — 2.1 Information Needed: "we need a video that demonstrates the current version, 1.0, in use on a physical iOS device" — "Only use footage of your app running on a physical iOS device, not on a simulator." Replied in Japanese; status stayed "却下済み" while waiting. [source](https://teratail.com/questions/375580) (medium, ja) → META-05, UGC-06, META-07
- **2022-04 · Flutter app (ja)** — "Rejected" with reason "Other": "We need additional time to evaluate your submission and Apple Developer Program account… we do not require a revised binary" — account review, wait 7 business days before Contact Us. [source](https://teratail.com/questions/ooxqkxi6yiokcr) (medium, ja) → META-07, AND-ACCOUNT-01
- **2026-04 · webview-based service (ko)** — Toss Payments SDK script cannot load from the reviewer's network, so the payment page errors only for App Review; Toss support said it is Apple-side. Unresolved. [source](https://okky.kr/articles/1555256) (medium, ko) → CONTENT-09, CONTENT-13, PAY-04

### 2.1 "unable to locate the ATT permission request" / 2.1(b) IAP not submitted
- **2026-08 · baby-log app, Expo (ja)** — 2.1 Information Needed: ATT prompt not shown on iPadOS 26.6 (1.7.5 (29)). Cause: `requestTrackingPermissionsAsync` called in the first commit of `App`, before the window was active, so iOS resolved without showing; AdMob banner mounted concurrently. Fix: wait for `AppState === 'active'`, gate `AdBanner` behind a `TrackingReadyContext`. Previous round (1.7.4): 5.1.1 "placeholder or otherwise insufficient purpose strings" — `expo-image-picker` injected default "Allow $(PRODUCT_NAME) to access your camera" for unused camera/mic, and `NSPhotoLibraryAddUsageDescription` was missing → `cameraPermission:false`, `microphonePermission:false`, add `expo-media-library` plugin with Japanese text. [ATT](https://github.com/tsuku723/little-baby-log/pull/237) / [strings](https://github.com/tsuku723/little-baby-log/pull/236) (high, ja) → PRIV-05, IOS-PRIV-02, ADS-01, IOS-PLIST-01, PERM-03
- **2026-08 · baseball-stats app "BUZZ BASE", Expo + RevenueCat 2.0.0 (43) (ja)** — three findings on iPad Air 11" (M3)/iPadOS 26.6: 2.3.10 "Revise the app's binary to remove Google Play references." (cancel guidance said "App Store / Google Play" unconditionally), 2.1 ATT not located (shown-flag stored in `expo-secure-store`/Keychain survives reinstall — flag set on the 8/20 review so never shown on 8/25; flag written before the request; request fired at login before `AppState` active), 2.1(b) IAP unloadable (Sentry: "None of the products registered in the RevenueCat dashboard could be fetched from App Store Connect", locale `en_SG`). Fix: platform-specific store label; drop the app-side flag and use `status === 'undetermined'`, wait for active, move to root layout; check Paid Apps agreement/bank/tax, IAP "Ready to Submit", Offering current, subscription attached to the version; also re-check screenshots for Google Play badges. [PR](https://github.com/ippei-shimizu/buzzbase_mobile/pull/186) / [issue](https://github.com/ippei-shimizu/buzzbase/issues/555) (high, ja) → CONTENT-04, PRIV-05, IOS-PRIV-02, PAY-05, IOS-IAP-01, META-01
- **2026-08 · casual game collection "あそびば" v1.1.0 (ja)** — 5.2.1 + 2.1. "Ravensburger objects to apps available in Germany that use the mark, Reversi, in the app name, subtitle, icon, screenshots, or previews." — the only occurrence was the ASC **keyword** field ("リバーシ"). 2.1: "we are unable to locate the App Tracking Transparency permission request when reviewed on iPadOS 26.6" — ATT was triggered only after finishing the first game and returning to the hub, which the reviewer never did. Fix: keyword removed (オセロ kept, different mark), custom pre-explanation modal deleted and system ATT shown 600ms after first hub render, 20-second screen recording attached to the reply. Earlier the same app sat 8 days with no status change because "Submit for Review" had apparently never been pressed. [5.2.1](https://github.com/hiroky1983/game_collection/pull/300) / [ATT](https://github.com/hiroky1983/game_collection/pull/301) / [resubmit tracking](https://github.com/hiroky1983/game_collection/issues/306) / [8-day stall](https://github.com/hiroky1983/game_collection/issues/166) (high, ja) → META-02, PRIV-05, IOS-PRIV-02, META-07, IOS-META-05
- **2026-07 · memo/subscription app, Expo (ja)** — 2.1(b) "IAP商品が審査に提出されていない" + 2.1 ATT dialog not visible (iPad Air 11" M3 / iPadOS 26.5.2, 1.1.3 (55)). Cause: `requestTrackingPermissionsAsync()` fired in the startup `useEffect` together with DB/AdMob init before the window was displayed. Fix: `InteractionManager.runAfterInteractions()`, device screen recording, submit the Standard-plan IAP with screenshot and a new binary. [source](https://github.com/satouhiroyuki/Morincum-specs/issues/44) (high, ja) → IOS-IAP-01, PAY-04, PRIV-05, IOS-PRIV-02
- **2025-05 · audio-guide app 1.1.0 (ja)** — 2.1 Information Needed: ATT request not found on iPadOS 18.4.1. Developer replied "Our app is intended for iPhone only… please review on an iPhone" → Apple: "The issues we previously identified still need your attention." (iPhone-only is not accepted as an excuse; the prompt must appear on the iPad review device.) [source](https://github.com/Ayato-kosaka/nanicore-audio-guide/issues/106) (high, ja) → PRIV-05, IOS-IPAD-01, META-07
- **2021-05 · app with IAP (ja)** — after a code-only rejection the IAP stayed "デベロッパの対応が必要" and could not be returned to "審査待ち"; comment claims first-submission IAP rejections require re-creating the product. Resolved by following that advice. [source](https://teratail.com/questions/339572) (medium, ja) → IOS-IAP-01, PAY-05

### 2.3 Metadata — screenshots, other platforms, keywords
- **2026-08 · voice-chat AI app, Flutter, 1.0.0+22 (ja)** — 2.3.10 "App Store 説明文に Google Play への言及がある" — the only finding, zero binary issues; cancellation instructions in the description named Google Play. Fix: neutral wording, metadata-only resubmission; a "Play Store" string inside `revenuecat_service.dart` error text was logged as latent risk. Round-1 rejection (recorded from memory in the team's playbook): Terms of Use link missing from the App Description. [source](https://github.com/shibuyer-jp/voikerchat/pull/113) / [playbook](https://github.com/shibuyer-jp/voikerchat/pull/43) (high, ja) → CONTENT-04, META-02, PAY-02, META-07
- **2026-07 · camera app "カメコロ" (ja)** — 2.3.10 re-rejection because the privacy-policy page linked from the app said "Apple/Google の決済プラットフォーム". Fix: "お使いのアプリストア（Apple App Store）"; the sister app's terms and landing page (Android store card, "Google Play 版を準備しています", tester recruitment) were scrubbed pre-emptively; Firebase/Google Sign-In mentions left as they are service providers, not platforms. [source](https://github.com/sakanafuto/7ofu-site/pull/14) (high, ja) → CONTENT-04, META-04
- **2025-09→11 · label timer app, iOS (ko)** — 2.5.4 + 2.3.3. `Info.plist` declared background `audio` but the app never used it; iPad was a supported destination but only iPhone screenshots were uploaded. Fix: uncheck "Audio, AirPlay, and Picture in Picture", `TARGETED_DEVICE_FAMILY` "1,2,7" → "1,7" (iPhone-only) so no iPad screenshots are needed. [source](https://github.com/data-sy/quick-label-timer/pull/21) (high, ko) → IOS-PLIST-02, BG-01, IOS-META-01
- **2023-09 · couple widget app (ko)** — rejected for iPad screenshots/previews and for asking the user's name again after Sign in with Apple. Fix: iPad screenshots re-shot; name-entry step removed. [source](https://github.com/Dr-Yomang/Yomang/issues/111) (medium, ko) → IOS-META-01, IOS-LOGIN-04
- **2022-01 · Unity game with AdMob (ja)** — 2.5.10 "We continue to notice that your app or its screenshots include test advertisements." Real ad-unit IDs were in use but the banner showed a "Test mode" label because the app was not yet linked in AdMob (which requires being on the store). [source](https://teratail.com/questions/eddnqt0u5hltbr) (high, ja) → ADS-03, META-01

### 2.5 Software requirements — purpose strings, unused permissions, private API
- **2026-08 · meetup app, Expo SDK 54 CNG (ko)** — 2.5.1 + 5.1.1: Info.plist contained purpose strings unrelated to app features. Cause: unused `expo-location` dependency auto-injected 3 `NSLocation*` strings (and Android `ACCESS_*_LOCATION`), `expo-secure-store` plugin injected `NSFaceIDUsageDescription` although `requireAuthentication` was never used; all in default English "Allow $(PRODUCT_NAME) to…". Fix: remove `expo-location`, `faceIDPermission:false`, Korean photo-library text; verified with `npx expo config --type introspect`; needs a new binary (not OTA). [source](https://github.com/Central-MakeUs/moyeo-client/issues/231) (high, ko) → PERM-03, IOS-PLIST-01, AND-PERM-13
- **2026-08 · goshuin map app v1.0.0, Expo (ja)** — 5.1.1 "An automated analysis of the submission indicates the following purpose strings… include placeholder text or are otherwise insufficient: • NSMicrophoneUsageDescription: 'Allow app to access your microphone'". Cause: `expo-camera`/`expo-image-picker` plugins add a default mic string for video even when only still photos are used. Fix: `microphonePermission:false`, `recordAudioAndroid:false`, drop `RECORD_AUDIO`; verified via `npx expo config --type prebuild`. [source](https://github.com/Kaiwa-Jun/goshuin-app/pull/113) (high, ja) → IOS-PLIST-01, PERM-03, AND-PERM-13
- **2026-04 · metronome sync app (ja, pre-emptive)** — `MultipeerConnectivity.framework` linked (confirmed by `otool -L`) without `NSBluetoothAlwaysUsageDescription`; added to `project.yml` to avoid the 5.1.1 automated rejection even though the default transport is Wi-Fi. [source](https://github.com/hakaru/PeerClock/issues/16) (medium, ja) → IOS-PLIST-01, IOS-ENT-04
- **2026-08 · retro camera app v0.1.2 (ja, pre-emptive)** — requests `PHPhotoLibrary` read/write authorization every shot but never writes to Photos (Documents only) while the store text and `NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription` promise album saving → flagged as 5.1.1 data-minimization and 2.3.1 risk. Fix plan: actually save with `.addOnly`, switch the key to `NSPhotoLibraryAddUsageDescription`, request on first capture. [source](https://github.com/entaku0818/RetroSnap/issues/5) (medium, ja) → PERM-03, PERM-04, META-02
- **2025-06 · ACNH wiki app (ko, pre-emptive)** — removed use of private `UINavigationItem.__largeTitleTwoLineMode` selector to avoid a 2.5.1 rejection. [source](https://github.com/leeari95/ACNH-wiki/pull/92) (medium, ko) → IOS-UI-02
- **2026-07 · photo keyboard app with AdMob (ja, pre-emptive)** — no `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy`, no ATT/`NSUserTrackingUsageDescription`, UMP linked but unused, `sel_registerName("openURL:")` responder-chain hack in the keyboard extension flagged as private-API risk. Fixed manifest + UMP→ATT→`GADMobileAds.start()` order; AdMob GDPR message still had to be created in the console ("Failed to read publisher's account configuration"). [source](https://github.com/gucchi43/syame/issues/32) (medium, ja) → IOS-PRIV-03, IOS-PRIV-02, ADS-01, IOS-UI-02

### 3.1 Payments / subscriptions
- **2026-08 · coin-laundry app with auto-renewing subscription, Expo 1.0.0 (17) (ja)** — 3.1.2(c): no functional Terms of Use (EULA) link in app or metadata. Fix: settings link to Apple's **standard EULA** rather than the web `/terms`, because the web terms/`/tokushoho`/help pages contain "Proプラン ¥780/月" pricing and would trigger 3.1.3(a) if shown in-app; standard-EULA URL must also go in the App Description; new build + screen recording in the reply. [source](https://github.com/Tiffany-sho/coinlaundy_app_iOS/pull/1) (high, ja) → PAY-02, IOS-UI-03, JP-01
- **2026-06 · AI story-book app, Flutter (ko, pre-emptive)** — `restorePurchases()` existed but was called from no screen; wired a "구매 복원" AppBar action with a regression test, citing "Apple 3.1.1 자동 리젝". [source](https://github.com/sterlingstarai-ai/ai-story-book/pull/35) (medium, ko) → PAY-03
- **2026-03 · prediction game with banana coins (ko, pre-emptive)** — to stay clear of 5.3 gambling, coins split into FREE (earned, prediction-only) and PAID (IAP, cosmetics-only) with no cash-out path, documented as an App Review talking point. [source](https://github.com/gguloadoong/chimp-pick/pull/83) (medium, ko) → PAY-01, LAW-02
- **2026-08 · gym-management app, Capacitor (ja, pre-emptive)** — native app now opens Stripe Checkout in the system browser via a `/billing/return` relay; the author flags Apple review risk, hides it behind a `NATIVE_DIRECT_CHECKOUT` flag "リジェクトされたらフラグを倒して再提出" and recommends shipping Android first. [source](https://github.com/kmunemoto/gymboard/pull/274) (medium, ja) → PAY-01, IOS-UI-03, IOS-IAP-02

### 4.0 Design / iPad / 4.3 Spam / 4.8 Login services
- **2026-08 · dating/meetup app, Expo (ko)** — Guideline 4 (iPad). Fix: `supportsTablet:false` → iPhone-only build. [source](https://github.com/Yarr-mio/mio_app/pull/68) (medium, ko) → IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-07 · interest-exchange app (ko)** — App Store rejection addressed by forbidding landscape and removing iPad rotation settings; login failures now sent to Sentry. [source](https://github.com/swyp-app-team6/swyp-app-team6-app/pull/21) (medium, ko) → IOS-IPAD-01, IOS-UI-01
- **2024-09 · Electron desktop app with Firebase Auth, Mac App Store (ja)** — 4.0 Design, three times. "The user is taken to the default web browser to sign in or register for an account, which provides a poor user experience." Round 1: login opened the default browser with an Express callback server. Round 2: same after switching to a bundled `ASWebAuthenticationSession` helper binary (the reply text's suggested route). Round 3: App Review Board appeal — "The App Review Board determined that the original rejection feedback was valid." Fix that passed: open the web login page in an Electron `BrowserWindow` and capture the credential via `will-navigate`. Reply also reminded 5.1.1(v) account deletion. [source](https://blog.covelline.com/entry/2024/09/16/111028) (high, ja) → IOS-UI-03, ACC-04, META-07, ACC-02
- **2025-06 · paid variant of a free app (ja)** — 4.3(a) "We noticed your app shares a similar binary, metadata, and/or concept as other apps you already submitted to the App Store, with only minor differences." — a separate paid SKU (ads removed + a few features) of an existing free app. Thread advises IAP unlock inside one app instead. [source](https://teratail.com/questions/ob6vgvirv5ts0g) (high, ja) → CONTENT-07, PAY-01
- **2025-09 · walking-route app, Google Sign-In only (ja)** — 4.8 + 5.1.2 + 5.1.1(v) in one review (2025-09-30). 4.8: only Google login → add Sign in with Apple as an equal option (relay e-mail handling, no notification e-mails to hidden addresses). 5.1.2: ASC privacy labels declared "tracking" for fitness/location data but no ATT → audit proved no tracking (Analytics/Ads disabled) → fix the labels instead of adding ATT. 5.1.1(v): privacy policy promised account deletion but Settings only had logout → full Firebase Auth + Firestore/Storage deletion with `withThrowingTaskGroup`. [4.8](https://github.com/RRRRRRR-777/TokoToko/issues/136) / [5.1.2](https://github.com/RRRRRRR-777/TokoToko/issues/138) / [5.1.1(v)](https://github.com/RRRRRRR-777/TokoToko/issues/137) (high, ja) → IOS-LOGIN-01, IOS-LOGIN-04, IOS-PRIV-01, PRIV-02, ACC-02
- **2023-11 · social quiz app (ko)** — 5.1.2 (photo purpose string lacked reason/usage), 5.1.1 (gender required at signup — "구체적으로, 다음의 필드… 성별"), 4.8 (third-party login without Apple). Fix: purpose strings, explain/optional gender, add Sign in with Apple. [source](https://github.com/APP-iOS2/ZENO-iOS/issues/376) (high, ko) → PERM-01, PRIV-04, IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2026-08 · project-management SaaS shell app (ko, pre-emptive)** — adding Sign in with Apple for 4.8 before first iOS submission; design gate required the HIG **white** Apple button variant in dark theme because the black button vanished against a `#0b0c0d` card ("심사(4.8)서 Apple은 버튼 시각을 봄"). Notes the ES256 client-secret JWT, `form_post` callback needing `SameSite=None` cookies, and platform-gated exposure (iOS/macOS shells only). [source](https://github.com/moonklabs/sprintable/pull/3526) (medium, ko) → IOS-LOGIN-01, ACC-04
- **2026-07 · dating app for 50+, Expo (ko, pre-emptive)** — store-blocker sweep before first submission: signup must proceed when camera/mic/notification permissions are denied (5.1.1), Apple button replaced with the official `AppleAuthenticationButton` (4.8), terms/privacy links in My-page, fake profile placeholders removed, test/payment/mock routes deleted (~1,900 lines), unused Android `SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW`/`FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MEDIA_PLAYBACK` removed, iPad disabled. [source](https://github.com/UMC-Eum/Frontend/pull/195) (medium, ko) → PERM-02, IOS-LOGIN-01, CONTENT-02, AND-PERM-13, IOS-IPAD-01

### 5.1.1 Privacy — login walls, required fields, permission UX (iv), account deletion (v), organization (ix)
- **2026-08 · running-report app, HealthKit (ko)** — 5.1.1(iv) (1.0(5), 2026-08-15). "A custom message appears before the permission request, and to proceed users press a '건강 데이터 연결하기' button. Use words like 'Continue' or 'Next' on the button instead." Fix: CTA → "다음"; the *reason* moved into a caption under the button ("리포트를 만들려면 러닝 기록이 필요해서… 허용 여부는 직접 정하시면 됩니다"), behaviour unchanged. [source](https://github.com/JKPark83/RunWrap/pull/6) (high, ko) → PERM-04, PERM-01
- **2026-06 · barter app v1.10.5(161) (ko)** — 5.1.1(iv) + 1.5 (iPad Air 11" M3, 2026-05-31; Korean-language rejection preserved). "권한 요청을 표시하기 전에 사용자를 설정 앱으로 리디렉션하여 접근 권한을 허용하도록 합니다." Support URL `https://team-romrom.github.io/` returned an error. Fix: show the system camera prompt first; only after denial show an alert with a Settings link; restore the support page. [source](https://github.com/TEAM-ROMROM/RomRom-FE/issues/906) (high, ko) → PERM-04, PERM-02, CONTENT-05, META-04
- **2026-08 · voice-chat AI app, Flutter Build 15 (ja)** — 5.1.1(iv) + 5.1.1(i)/5.1.2(i) on iPad Air 11" (M3). Custom mic/speech pre-prompt had a "キャンセル" button letting users skip the system request; third-party AI data sharing (Anthropic; OpenAI for TTS) was only in the privacy policy. Fix: single "続ける" button, `barrierDismissible:false` + `PopScope(canPop:false)`, always proceed to the OS prompt; new `AiDataConsentScreen` listing data sent and recipients, blocking until "同意して続ける", shown to existing users on next launch; `OpenAI, L.L.C.` row added to the privacy policy. [source](https://github.com/shibuyer-jp/voikerchat/pull/36) (high, ja) → PERM-04, AI-01, PRIV-04, PRIV-01
- **2026-03 · study-timer app with "딥타임" lock (ko)** — rejected because on iPad Air 11" (M2) tapping the lock icon → "설정하러 가기" did not present any system permission popup. [source](https://github.com/bookstarV2/clientv/issues/238) (medium, ko) → IOS-ENT-02, PERM-04, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2025-03 · photo-studio booking app (ko)** — 5.1.1 "The app requires users to register or log in to access features that are not account based. Specifically, the app requires users to register before accessing a list of photo studios." Fix: studio list viewable without login. [source](https://github.com/Toucheese-MVP/Touchesse-iOS/issues/72) (high, ko) → ACC-01
- **2025-09 · campus map / cafeteria app (ko)** — 5.1.1 login wall on map, search, meal menu, shuttle. Fix: backend dropped the auth token requirement on those endpoints; favourites/My-page/inquiry stay member-only. [source](https://github.com/YOGIITSU-App/YOGIITSU_back/issues/140) (high, ko) → ACC-01
- **2024-08 / 2024-10 · youth-policy app and zero-sugar product app (ko)** — 5.1.1 login wall; fixed with a "둘러보기" (browse) mode / a browse account. [with-peace](https://github.com/WithPeace/with-peace-iOS/issues/149) / [zerosome](https://github.com/zerosome-dev/zerosome-ios/issues/101) (medium, ko) → ACC-01, ACC-03
- **2025-06 · wishlist/image-analysis app (ko)** — 5.1.1 for collecting gender and birth date. Fix: on-screen reason "성별과 생년월일 정보는 이미지 분석 시 보다 정확한 키워드 추천을 위하여 수집함". [source](https://github.com/back-young-company/project-bestwish/issues/93) (medium, ko) → PRIV-04, ACC-01
- **2026-03 · university-club app (ko)** — 5.1.1(v) "The app supports account creation but does not include an option to initiate account deletion." Cause: deletion existed only in My-page, unreachable while the account sits in the "승인 대기" (pending approval) state that the reviewer's fresh account lands in. Fix: delete button on the pending-approval view; verify a deletion path from every post-signup state. [source](https://github.com/UMC-PRODUCT/Big-Dipper-iOS/issues/365) (high, ko) → ACC-02, ACC-03
- **2026-08 · deposit/booking marketplace, web-app (ko)** — 5.1.1(v): only a web request page (`legal/account_deletion.html`), no in-app entry. Fix: My-page → 설정 → 계정 삭제 (KO/EN/JA) with balance/booking warning, typed "계정 삭제" confirmation, soft-delete + PII masking, billing-key/push cleanup, transaction records kept under e-commerce law with notice. [source](https://github.com/dnfmeod1139/taam-app/pull/148) (high, ko) → ACC-02, KR-02
- **2026-08 · goshuin app v1.0 (12) (ja)** — 5.1.1(v) "The app supports account creation but does not include an option to initiate account deletion." Terms already promised deletion. Fix: Settings entry (logged-in only), two-step confirmation listing what disappears, Edge Function deleting Storage → DB → auth user, back to guest map. Apple SIWA token revoke deliberately deferred ("should", not the rejection reason; `authorizationCode` not stored so cannot apply retroactively). [source](https://github.com/Kaiwa-Jun/goshuin-app/issues/134) (high, ja) → ACC-02, IOS-LOGIN-02
- **2026-08 · leash-remover photo app (ja, pre-emptive)** — once Apple identity + credits were persisted in Supabase, the 5.1.1(v) TODO became a blocker: no settings screen, `signOut` unreachable from UI, deletion needs an Edge Function with `service_role` `auth.admin.deleteUser()`; author budgets "리젝 2回目はさらに延びる… 1往復まるごと持っていかれる". [source](https://github.com/yamatoa0606ezwebnejp-max/leash-remover-app/issues/5) (medium, ja) → ACC-02
- **2026-07 / 2026-08 · Korean backends adding Apple token revoke on withdrawal (ko, pre-emptive ×3)** — three separate teams treat "탈퇴 시 Apple 연동 해제" as a 5.1.1(v) rejection risk: exchange `authorizationCode` (single-use, 5 min) at login for a refresh token, store it with the client ID (Bundle ID vs Services ID), call `/auth/revoke` last and never fail the withdrawal on Apple errors (TN3194 fallback). One team found native `expo-apple-authentication` tokens have `aud` = App ID, so the web Services-ID verifier and revoke path had to be duplicated or "네이티브 가입 사용자는 탈퇴가 503으로 실패". [offway](https://github.com/team-offway/core/pull/293) / [ddara](https://github.com/ddara-app/ddara-server/issues/104) / [moyeo native SIWA](https://github.com/Central-MakeUs/moyeo-client/issues/98) / [bookiibookii](https://github.com/bookiibookii/bookiibookii-ios/pull/127) (medium, ko) → IOS-LOGIN-02, ACC-02, ACC-04
- **2021-09 · student news app carrying COVID-19 articles (ko)** — 5.1.1(ix), twice. "the seller and company names associated with your app are not from a recognized institution… apps that provide services or collect sensitive user information in highly-regulated fields, such as healthcare, should be submitted by a legal entity". Even a COVID subsidy article and a "펜데믹" keyword in a film piece were flagged. Fix path: remove/blank "코로나" search results until launch, or publish under the institution's organization account. [source](https://github.com/innovationacademy-kr/slabs-saver/issues/536) (high, ko) → ACC-06, META-02
- **≤2023-05 · habit-tracking app (ko)** — 5.1.1 (camera purpose string "사진을 선택하여 첨부할 수 있습니다" was the album text pasted into the camera key, no reason) + 5.1.2 (privacy answers said "product interaction used for tracking" but no ATT). Fix: "프로필 사진 설정 및 변경, 습관 인증을 위해 카메라 권한을 허락해 주세요."; changed App Privacy to "no tracking" instead of adding ATT; replaced the build and replied in the Resolution Center. [source](https://gyuios.tistory.com/174) (high, ko) → PERM-01, IOS-PRIV-01, PRIV-02, META-07

### 1.5 Developer information / support URL
- **2026-08 · friend-memo app (ja)** — 1.5 re-rejection: "does not direct to a website with information users can use to ask questions and request support" although the URL returned 200 — the page was still a pre-launch teaser ("準備中", "公開前に"). Fix: support/contact section right under the hero with clickable mail/tel and address, placeholder screenshot section removed; caveat that `contact@begrad.jp` had no MX record. [source](https://github.com/Begrad/Begrad.jp/pull/60) (high, ja) → CONTENT-05, META-04
- **2026-08 · policy placeholders served by API (ko, pre-emptive)** — `GET /api/policies/PRIVACY` still returned `[문의 이메일]`, `[담당자 이름 또는 부서]` placeholders days before submission; replaced with real contact, DPO name, effective date; push-token clause deleted because push is not implemented. [source](https://github.com/Nexters/pallang-server/issues/79) (medium, ko) → PRIV-01, CONTENT-02, KR-02

### Webview / no-code app agencies (aggregated real cases)
- **2026-07 · 스윙투앱 (webview/push app builder) — "앱스토어 심사 거절 대표 사례 15가지" (ko)** — from thousands of client submissions: 4.2 marketing-only sites ("푸시를 넣는다고 해서 문제가 해결되는 것이 아닙니다"), demo account missing ("한글이 기재된 계정은 제출 불가"), social login without Apple ("100% 심사 거절"), counterfeit luxury goods, unrelated screenshots, no account deletion, "테스트/점검중/준비중/데모/베타" strings, UGC without report/block, internal/beta-only apps, Play Store content in the app, developer info missing on the website, sexual/adult-venue content, digital goods without IAP, site blocked for overseas IPs so the app cannot load, unnecessary signup fields. [source](https://m.blog.naver.com/PostView.naver?blogId=swing2app&logNo=224110778425) (high, ko) → CONTENT-03, ACC-03, IOS-LOGIN-01, META-01, ACC-02, CONTENT-02, UGC-03, CONTENT-04, CONTENT-05, PAY-01, CONTENT-13, PRIV-04
- **2025 · 스윙투앱 "2025년 심사 통과 팁" top-5 (ko)** — 1) no account deletion ("외부 페이지 링크만 제공하면 거절"), 2) UI not optimised for tablet ("iPad에서 일부 영역이 잘리는 경우"), 3) description ≠ features, 4) privacy-policy URL missing, 5) no test account; plus "심사 중에는 웹뷰 앱이라도 화면 변경 금지". [source](https://m.blog.naver.com/PostView.naver?blogId=swing2app&logNo=223866779544) (medium, ko) → ACC-02, IOS-IPAD-01, META-02, PRIV-01, ACC-03, CONTENT-12
- **2026-02 · PM's first vibe-coded iOS app (ko)** — several rejections; learned the app needs a hosted privacy policy and a Q&A/support page; approval then a stuck auto-release. [source](https://brunch.co.kr/@@HxJ/579) (medium, ko) → PRIV-01, CONTENT-05, META-07
- **2026-07 · memo app built with AI over 3 months (ko)** — 6 rejections before approval; 3 quick bug-fix updates approved fast afterwards ("초기 등록 심사는 빡빡한데, 업데이트 심사는 퀵하게"). [source](https://damoang.net/ai/4642) (medium, ko) → META-07

### Google Play

### Policy — misleading claims, privacy policy, ads/age, abuse, target audience
- **2026-08 · waste-sorting guide app "여기버려" 1.0.0 (ko)** — Misleading Claims policy on the production release after closed test + production access. "제공된 출처가 불충분함 — 앱 설명에 기재된 내용은 두 개 이상의 정보 출처가 사용된 것처럼 보이나, 모든 출처가 나열되어 있지 않습니다." Google requires, regardless of affiliation: official sources of the government information, working URLs to the originals, and a non-affiliation disclaimer — location flagged: Play "자세한 설명". Fix: re-audit → 8 government sources (기후에너지환경부 ×5, 행정안전부 ×3) + 2 public-agency references listed in the description and `DATA_SOURCES.md`. [source](https://github.com/AndroidStudy-Sesac/Yeogi-Beoryeo/issues/543) (high, ko) → AND-META-04, AND-CONSOLE-12, AND-META-01
- **2026-03 · routine app reading Health Connect / HealthKit (ko)** — Play Console: "사용자 데이터 정책의 개인정보처리방침 섹션: 잘못된 개인정보처리방침 — Health Data의 액세스 또는 수집과 사용을 포괄적으로 공개하세요". Cause: policy (loaded from a Supabase table) never mentioned health data, `READ_HEALTH_DATA_IN_BACKGROUND`, `ACTIVITY_RECOGNITION`. Fix: add a Health Data section (items, purposes, local+sync processing, no third-party sharing, background reads, deletion on account deletion) in KR and EN. [source](https://github.com/minor-laboratory/minorlab_home/issues/1) (high, ko) → AND-CONSOLE-01, PRIV-01, AND-CONSOLE-06
- **2026-04 · beef-bowl radar app "牛めしレーダー" (ja)** — privacy-policy rejection: "プライバシー ポリシーに、アプリの名称または Google Play ストアの掲載情報に記載されている法人（デベロッパーや会社など）が含まれている必要があります". The title had the app name but the body never named the app or the registered developer "ギュメシー". Fix: state both at the top and in the contact section. [source](https://github.com/yamada-gyumesy/gyumeshi-rader-data/issues/1) (high, ja) → AND-CONSOLE-01, AND-META-03
- **2026-03 · utility app rated 3+ with AdMob (ko)** — Play rejection because almost all AdMob ad categories (incl. alcohol) were enabled for a 3+ app. Author's takeaway: enable sensitive categories in AdMob and restrict in code (`tagForChildDirectedTreatment` docs) instead of relying on console settings; ONE store did not check this. [source](https://okky.kr/articles/1553496) (high, ko) → ADS-02, AND-CONSOLE-05, AND-CONSOLE-07
- **2026-03 · webview browser app that blocks YouTube ads (ko)** — "기기 및 네트워크 악용 정책 및 개발자 배포 계약 4.9항 위반" on a bug-fix update; Google sent 4 screenshots of YouTube ads not rendering ("앱이 광고를 표시하는…"). Appeal argued mainstream browsers (Brave etc.) behave the same and a Korean reviewer accepted it; author notes reviewer inconsistency ("승인해주는 검토자도 있고, 그렇지 않은 검토자도 있어서"). [rejection](https://okky.kr/articles/1554421) / [appeal outcome](https://okky.kr/articles/1554596) (high, ko) → AND-QUALITY-02, AND-ENFORCE-01, CONTENT-14
- **2022-03 · Twitter-client engine app (ja)** — app removed: "Although you haven't included children under 13 in your target age groups, we determined that some elements of your store listing may appeal to children under 13… Animated characters in the app icon. Young characters." Twitter API forbids under-13 so the icon lost its character; the actual fix was answering **yes** to "Could your store listing unintentionally appeal to children?" in Target audience and content — approved. [source](https://github.com/nishikawa-shi/NyanNyanEngine_Android/pull/107) (high, ja) → AND-CONSOLE-07, AND-META-02
- **2021-08 · character app published on a client's Play account (ja)** — update rejected for intellectual-property infringement: the title and icon used company A's character while the uploading developer (invited into company A's developer account) was treated as a non-rights-holder. Fix: appeal to support with only the stamped page of the licence contract attached → accepted in ~3 hours, "1時間くらいしたら再度アプリを申請してね". [source](https://blog.covelline.com/entry/2021/08/12/112847) (high, ja) → AND-META-01, AND-ENFORCE-01, AND-ACCOUNT-01
- **2025-05 · 스윙투앱 "구글 플레이스토어 잘못된 오해" (ko)** — agency FAQ from client submissions: re-submitting a rejected app unchanged or evading the cited policy is treated as spam → app removal / account termination; same app under a different package name = clone; human review is triggered for new developer accounts, prior rejections, news/politics/religion/medical/finance content, plain webview apps, description≠function, gambling/adult/supplement/crypto keywords or external-payment steering, and every re-review; "단순 웹뷰는 거절됩니다". [source](https://www.swing2app.co.kr/blog/809780/) (medium, ko) → AND-ENFORCE-01, AND-CONSOLE-11, CONTENT-03, AND-META-01, AND-PAY-01
- **2021-06 · hobby app (ja)** — Metadata policy: "최근 송신된 앱의 타이틀과 설명이 같습니다" (title and description identical — the description was just the app name). Fix: real description + new version code re-upload. [source](https://teratail.com/questions/344487) (medium, ja) → AND-META-01
- **2025-08 · elderly-care companion app (ko)** — Play review response: temporarily disabled call/SMS/contacts collection (upload GPS only) and added a mandatory terms screen (service, privacy, 위치정보 이용약관) before the intro. [source](https://github.com/phenokio/phenokio-android/pull/46) (medium, ko) → AND-PERM-01, KR-02, PRIV-04
- **2026-07 · dictionary app with AdMob banners (ja, pre-emptive)** — Data-safety/privacy-policy declared "no ads, no SDK" while AdMob was shipped — called out as "Google Play の虚偽申告（リジェクト＋アカウント措置級）". Fix: `AD_ID` permission, Data safety = advertising ID collected/shared, policy AdMob section; UMP not implemented → launch Japan-only, adults-only, no child-directed tag. [source](https://github.com/lanlangh/moffy/pull/27) (medium, ja) → AND-CONSOLE-02, AND-CONSOLE-04, ADS-01, PRIV-02
- **2026-03 · on-device AI agent for Android (ja, pre-emptive)** — pre-submission audit: `ExecTool` arbitrary shell execution, `QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES` (lint-suppressed), `WRITE_SETTINGS`, no privacy policy → "確実にリジェクト"; accessibility service (`canTakeScreenshot`/`canPerformGestures`), `SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW`, `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_SPECIAL_USE` need declaration forms; plan = separate `googleplay` product flavour. [source](https://github.com/KarakuriAgent/clawdroid/issues/53) (medium, ja) → AND-PERM-02, AND-PERM-06, AND-PERM-09, AND-PERM-05, AND-CONSOLE-06, AND-CONSOLE-01
- **2026-08 · Flutter PDF reader (ja, pre-emptive)** — `MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` + `requestLegacyExternalStorage` flagged: "ファイルマネージャ等の限られた用途以外は原則却下"; only the "Add Directory" tab needs it; plan to move to SAF tree URIs. [source](https://github.com/ShinoharaTa/flutter_pdf_reader/issues/25) (medium, ja) → AND-PERM-03

### Technical — 16 KB, Wear OS, background location
- **2025-11 · life-story app, React Native + RN Skia 1.12.4 (ko)** — Play upload refused for missing 16 KB page-size support in Skia's native libs (RN 0.77 core was fine; Skia 2.x needs React 19). Fix: `patch-package` adding `-Wl,-z,max-page-size=16384` to Skia's `CMakeLists.txt`. [source](https://github.com/itmca/lifepuzzle-rn/pull/79) (high, ko) → AND-SDK-02
- **2025-02 · train-position app, Wear OS build (ja)** — Google Play rejected the Wear OS version as non-functional ("そもそもこのアプリ動いてねーぞ"); developer confirmed and fixed. [source](https://github.com/TrainLCD/MobileApp/issues/4051) (medium, ja) → AND-QUALITY-01
- **2024-12 · same train app (ja)** — "Google Playからすでにリジェクトを食らってる" for the background-location permission flow; rewrote the prompt/rationale flow and warnings on the privacy screen. [source](https://github.com/TrainLCD/MobileApp/pull/3952) (medium, ja) → AND-PERM-04, AND-CONSOLE-06

### Closed testing (12 testers / 14 days)
- **2026-08 · solo dev planning a Play launch (ko)** — research write-up: the requirement is two-layered — 12 opt-ins for 14 consecutive days (uninstall lowers the count and resets the clock; the 12 must overlap the same window, so recruit 15–16) plus a substantive review comparing the promotion questionnaire with real Console metrics; most common refusal is lack of engagement ("옵트인 12명 + 세션 0"은 확정 반려); paid tester farms risk permanent bans (emulator detection). [source](https://github.com/m2222n/AI_agent/pull/124) (medium, ko) → AND-TEST-01, AND-ENFORCE-01
- **2025-01 · QA-vendor write-up "クローズドテストが却下される理由" (ja)** — five refusal triggers seen in production-access applications: tester count dropped below 12 during the 14 days (opt-out then re-opt-in does not count; recruit 15–25), low tester usage ("テストの継続を求められる"), no feedback summary, no changes made based on the test ("クローズドテストで学んだことに基づき、どのような変更を行いましたか？"), crashes surfaced by the pre-launch report. [source](https://primetestlab.com/ja/blog/why-google-play-closed-testing-gets-rejected) (medium, ja) → AND-TEST-01, AND-QUALITY-01
- **2026-04 / 2026-08 · Japanese solo devs (ja)** — tracking issues to satisfy 12 testers × 14 days before production access; a Qiita write-up of a junior-high developer (14 testers, one initial rejection) is referenced. [LaKiite](https://github.com/Inoworl/LaKiite-flutter-app/issues/117) / [AndroidDagashi digest](https://github.com/AndroidDagashi/AndroidDagashi/issues/3575) (medium, ja) → AND-TEST-01

---

### Toss mini-app store (Korea) — 앱인토스 심사
- **2026-08 · reading-timer mini-app (ko)** — Toss review refusal: "미니앱 접속 직후 바텀시트가 바로 노출돼요. 진입 시 즉시 나타나지 않도록 수정해 주세요." (a coach-mark rendered as a dimmed bottom-anchored panel). Earlier refusal 2026-08-12: "서비스 설명 없이 즉시 토스 로그인 유도". Fix: one-time in-page banner "👋 처음이신가요? 앱 사용법 보기 ›" starting the tour on tap; release note must say "사용법 보기를 누르면 안내", not "진입 시 자동 표시". [source](https://github.com/Goospel/booktimer/pull/841) (high, ko) → KR-04, PERM-04, ACC-01
- **2026-08 · gym-rest-timer mini-app (ko)** — refused 18 minutes after submission for one reason: "탭바를 사용하는 경우, 토스 미니앱 브랜딩 가이드에 적합한 플로팅 형태를 사용해야 해요." Standard bottom-anchored tab bar collides with Toss's own tabs. Fix: floating capsule (20px side inset, pill radius, shadow, no top border), body bottom padding from the same CSS variable, 3 of 4 store screenshots re-shot because they showed the old bar; note: `review_get` had an empty `rejectMessages` — reason was only in `review_get_feedback.comments`; a new bundle is required (`review_cancel` is not re-requestable). [source](https://github.com/Goospel/restfit/pull/20) (high, ko) → KR-04, META-01, META-06
- **2026-07 · apartment price simulator (ko)** — Toss mini-app integration rejected because the real-estate category is unsupported; shipped as Capacitor + responsive web on App Store/Play instead. [source](https://okky.kr/articles/1560870) (medium, ko) → KR-04

## Apple — 3rd pass (2026-08-28): thin areas — IPv6, 5.2.3 media rights, 5.2.5 Apple trademarks, Mac entitlement scans, widgets/watchOS, age assurance, AI disclosure, reader apps, frameworks

Sources: GitHub Search API (issue + PR bodies, authenticated `gh`), Stack Exchange API (titles only — throttled before bodies could be read), HN Algolia, dev.to API, Zenn API + r.jina.ai, Qiita API, Ionic forum (Discourse JSON), RevenueCat community (r.jina.ai). 195 unique URLs, all checked against `rejections/community-cases.md` (773 known URLs) — none repeated; 190 case bullets. Fields as in community-cases.md. Confidence: **high** = full rejection text/body read; **medium** = title/excerpt only or secondary account.

### Area 1 — 2.5.5 IPv6 / 2.5.6 browser engine / 2.5.13 Face ID / 2.5.15 Files / 2.5.16 extensions / 2.5.18 ads

### 2.5.5 / 2.1 IPv6-only (NAT64/DNS64) network failures
- **2016-10 · Meteor 1.4.2 Cordova mobile app** — 2.1 (IPv6). "we discovered one or more bugs in your app when reviewed on iPad and iPhone running iOS 10.1 on Wi-Fi connected to an IPv6 network. Specifically, we were unable to login to your app due to the 'No Internet Connection' error message. … Please run your app on a device while connected to an IPv6 network (all apps must support IPv6)". Reproducible on a local NAT64 network (`Meteor.status().status` stays `waiting`); maintainer pointed at the Cordova HTTP plugin / Meteor 1.4.2 Cordova updates. [source](https://github.com/meteor/meteor/issues/7969) (high, en) → CONTENT-09
- **2016-12 · angular-meteor app** — 2.1 (IPv6) "well-known ipv6-only network issue". Reproduced on a NAT64/DNS64 network built per Apple's guide with an IPv6-literal ROOT_URL: websocket connects but `Meteor.loginWithPassword` never returns. [source](https://github.com/meteor/meteor/issues/8192) (high, en) → CONTENT-09
- **2016-09 · app using Reachability.swift** — 2.1 (IPv6). Developer: "rejected on the App Store because this library is not supported 'NAT64' environment"; maintainer disputed the cause (dup of #119). Listed only as a symptom report. [source](https://github.com/ashleymills/Reachability.swift/issues/137) (medium, en) → CONTENT-09
- **2016-10 · SAP Sail InSight 1.0.17 (sailing tracker)** — internal bug tracker entry "rejected due to IPv6 error", P1; no further detail. [source](https://github.com/eclipse-sailing-analytics/sailing-analytics/issues/3965) (medium, en) → CONTENT-09
- **2017-09 · "Ask HN: How do you handle Appstore IPv6 rejection?"** — links Apple forum thread 88384 (2.1 IPv6 rejection); discussion thread only. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=15355879) (medium, en) → CONTENT-09
- **2016-06→2017-03 · Stack Overflow cluster (titles verified via the SE search API; bodies not readable this pass — SE API throttled)**: "required iPv6 compatibility - iOS app rejected by apple" (score 30) [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/37693256/required-ipv6-compatibility-ios-app-rejected-by-apple); "IPv6 - Apple rejects iOS app because of not Supporting IPv6 DNS64 / NAT64 Networks" (14) [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/39222694/ipv6-apple-rejects-ios-app-because-of-not-supporting-ipv6-dns64-nat64-networ); "React Native: App Store Rejection, Support for IPv6" [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/38783443/react-native-app-store-rejection-support-for-ipv6); "Apple rejected our phonegap app for not having ipv6 support" [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/38367265/apple-rejected-our-phonegap-app-for-not-having-ipv6-support); "Local IPv6 DNS64/NAT64 configuration for ipv6 compatibility testing always offline. iOS app rejected" [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/39836392/local-ipv6-dns64-nat64-configuration-for-ipv6-compatibility-testing-always-offli); "Configure server for IPv6 compatibility (for API used by rejected iOS app)" [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/41281211/configure-server-for-ipv6-compatibility-for-api-used-by-rejected-ios-app); "App store Rejects App for IPV6 network support on iOs 10" [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/39872072/app-store-rejects-app-for-ipv6-network-support-on-ios-10); "Application rejected with the reason of lack of IPv6 support" [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/42554285/application-rejected-with-the-reason-of-lack-of-ipv6-support). All are the 2016 iOS 9/10 wave; the common fix in this cluster is hostnames instead of IPv4 literals and testing on a macOS Internet-Sharing NAT64 network. (medium, en) → CONTENT-09

### 2.5.6 WebKit / alternative browser engine
- **2024-09 · .NET MAUI 8 apps with WebView (Xcode 16 / .NET 8.0.402)** — ITMS-91017 "Invalid SDK usage - The following frameworks aren't permitted for apps using an alternative web browser engine: [BrowserEngineKit]". Cause: MAUI toolchain emitted `-weak_framework BrowserEngineKit` when built with Xcode 16. Fix: pin workload `maui-mobile 8.0.402` + Xcode 15.4 (later fixed upstream). Two apps blocked at upload. [source](https://github.com/dotnet/maui/issues/24980) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04, IOS-META-04

### 2.5.13 Face ID / LocalAuthentication
- **2020-01 · app using FirebaseUI Sign in with Apple** — 2.5.13. "The Apple Sign-in authentication method does not use LocalAuthentication as the App's primary authentication mechanism. LocalAuthentication must be your app's primary biometric authentication method, and any other custom facial recognition mechanism should be optional for login and cannot replace LocalAuthentication." Cause: reviewer mistook `FUIOAuth.appleAuthProvider()` for custom facial recognition (misapplied rule). Fix: appeal/explain SIWA is not biometric; some re-implemented SIWA natively. [source](https://github.com/firebase/FirebaseUI-iOS/issues/823) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-01
- **2026-06 · finance app (piggy-pulse) with Keychain DEK protected by `.biometryCurrentSet`** — 2.1 crash/blocked flow, "repeatedly" rejected. Reviewer devices (iPads without Face ID/Touch ID) got `Keychain error: -25293 (errSecAuthFailed)` at registration and every login, because `SecItemAdd` with `.biometryCurrentSet` fails when no biometry is enrolled. Fix: check `LAContext.canEvaluatePolicy(.deviceOwnerAuthenticationWithBiometrics)` and fall back to `kSecAttrAccessibleWhenUnlockedThisDeviceOnly`. [source](https://github.com/leocalm/piggy-pulse-ios/pull/93) (high, en) → PERM-02, IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-06 · live AI stylist app streaming face crops to Gemini** — 2.1 Information Needed. "We need additional information about how the app uses face data. Face data is sensitive user information that must be handled properly to protect the user's security and privacy." Six standard questions (what is collected, uses, storage, retention, sharing, privacy-policy location). Fix: add a quotable "Face Data" section to the privacy policy and answer in Resolution Center. [source](https://github.com/STGime/live-stylist-ai/issues/54) (high, en) → PRIV-04, AI-01

### 2.5.15 Files app / document picker
- **2020-08 · app using YPImagePicker for file selection** — 2.5.15. "We noticed that your app enables users to view and select files, but it does not include functionality to allow users to view or select items from the Files app and the user's iCloud documents". Fix: add `UIDocumentPickerViewController`. [source](https://github.com/Yummypets/YPImagePicker/issues/540) (high, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2018-07 · app with in-app file selection** — 2.5.15 "We noticed that your app enable[s] users to view and select files…" (search excerpt; full body not readable this pass). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/51130398/app-rejected-due-to-file-selection) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
