### Area 2 — 5.3 gambling / 5.4 VPN / 5.5 MDM / 5.1.3 HealthKit / 5.2.3 downloading / 5.2.4–5.2.5 Apple product names

### 5.4 VPN / NEVPNManager
- **2022-04 · Moonlight game-streaming client (open source)** — 5.4 VPN. "App rejected on VPN Services issue. Apple requires the use of the NEVPNManager API"; the app has no VPN — static scan flagged a networking dependency. Fix: identify and remove the flagged symbol / explain. [source](https://github.com/moonlight-stream/moonlight-ios/issues/500) (medium, en) → IOS-ENT-04, IOS-UI-02
- **2026-02 · Flutter app using `vpn_connection_detector` v2** — 2.5.1 (NEVPNManager). "The app uses public APIs in an unapproved manner … Specifically, NEVPNManager APIs. … It would be appropriate to remove these APIs from the app if you have no approved use case." Cause: plugin v2 switched to NEVPNManager to detect VPN state; non-VPN apps may not link it. Fix: revert to v1 / use `getifaddrs` utun-interface check. [source](https://github.com/bazl-E/vpn_connection_detector/issues/9) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02, IOS-ENT-04

### 5.2.3 Audio/video streaming & downloading
- **2026-07 · music-discovery app (Fluncle) with own rendered videos containing excerpts of commercial recordings** — 5.2.3. "potentially unauthorized access to third-party audio or video streaming, catalogs, and discovery services." Fix: feed videos play muted with the 30-s official store preview as the audio bed; type system forbids `hasAudio: true`. [source](https://github.com/mauricekleine/fluncle/pull/805) (high, en) → LAW-02, CONTENT-01
- **2026-06→07 · self-hosted Jellyfin client (Cinemax) with offline downloads** — 5.2.3 ×3 + 1.5. Round 1: description "Téléchargez films, épisodes, saisons…" read as a generic media downloader, and GitHub *issues* tab rejected as Support URL. Round 2/3: still rejected under 5.2.3 despite a per-user server-side download gate; product removed the download feature entirely (−3,794 lines) and scrubbed all "offline" claims from metadata. tvOS build had been approved. [source](https://github.com/b-raillard/Cinemax/pull/55) / [round 1](https://github.com/b-raillard/Cinemax/pull/43) (high, en) → META-02, CONTENT-05, LAW-02
- **2026-06 · TV/movie tracker (Couch) using TMDB** — 5.2.3. Reviewer requested "documentary evidence" of rights to third-party catalog/discovery data. Fix: exact TMDB notice ("uses TMDB and the TMDB APIs but is not endorsed, certified, or otherwise approved by TMDB") + logo on 6 surfaces, evidence PDF in App Review Information. [source](https://github.com/Nahder-Bot/Couch/pull/10) (high, en) → LAW-02, META-05
- **2026-08 · BYO-server music player (Aria) that also fronted YouTube** — 5.2.3 risk mitigation after rejection pattern: removed "YouTube" from the search-scope segmented control and stopped Share emitting youtube.com links. [source](https://github.com/evince55/Aria/pull/68) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2025-04 · PeerTube clients (OwnTube: XR Tube, Basspistol, Privacy Tube, Blender Tube) — 4 iOS+tvOS apps** — 5.2.3 for all four the morning after submission: "your app provides potentially unauthorized access to third-party audio or video streaming, catalogs, and discovery services." Fix: documentary evidence that the instances are operated by/for the submitter. [source](https://github.com/Chocobozzz/PeerTube/issues/6966) (high, en) → LAW-02
- **2024-07 · app embedding react-native-youtube-iframe** — 5.2.3. Same quote; developer had already attached the YouTube API ToS as evidence and was still rejected. Answer: reviewers want proof of rights to the *content*, not the API. [source](https://github.com/LonelyCpp/react-native-youtube-iframe/issues/342) (high, en) → LAW-02
- **2021-01 · Swift-Radio-Pro based radio app** — 5.2.3. "attach documentary evidence … evidencing that you have all necessary rights or permissions to the third-party audio or video streaming, catalogs, and discovery services". [source](https://github.com/analogcode/Swift-Radio-Pro/issues/154) (high, en) → LAW-02
- **2021-01 · radio app** — 5.2.3 "documentary evidence" question ("fix issue documentary evidence for radio app in appstore", score 5; body not readable this pass). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/65591763/fix-issue-documentary-evidence-for-radio-app-in-appstore) (medium, en) → LAW-02
- **2022-05 · video-downloader app** — 5.2.3 ("How to get permission of video downloader from appstore?", score 4; title-level only). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/72428033/how-to-get-permission-of-video-downloader-from-appstore) (medium, en) → LAW-02

### 5.2.5 Apple product names / trademarks / WeatherKit attribution
- **2026-08 · Mac utility (MacHeadroom)** — 5.2.5 for the product name containing "Mac". Fix: rename. [source](https://github.com/vscarpenter/MacHeadroom/pull/1) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-08 · macOS companion app (Perch Companion 1.0)** — 5.2.5: subtitle "Send it to your Mac's shelf" flagged. Fix: "Send it to your desktop shelf"; metadata-only resubmission of the same build. [source](https://github.com/hausfold/perch/pull/79) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-06 · display utility "OpenSidecar"** — 5.2.5: "Sidecar" in the app name confusingly similar to Apple's feature. Fix: renamed OpenDisplay (bundle IDs unchanged, new build required since on-device name changed). [source](https://github.com/peetzweg/opendisplay/pull/79) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-06 · "RunseMac" / "润色 Mac 版"** — 5.2.5 "the app name used Apple's 'Mac' trademark, both in App Store metadata and in the name displayed on the device" (+2.1(a) crash on macOS 26.5.1). Fix: `CFBundleName` → Runse, store names in all locales stripped of "Mac"; descriptive "Mac app" text in description left. [source](https://github.com/madeye/runse-mac/pull/16) (high, en) → META-02, CONTENT-01
- **2026-06 · weather comparison app** — 5.2.5 ×2: missing WeatherKit attribution (Apple Weather mark + legal link) and app icon confusingly similar to Apple Weather. Fix: attribution footer on every WeatherKit surface, new icon, screen recording on physical device in review notes. [source](https://github.com/kush2shah/WeatherApp/pull/3) (high, en) → META-02, META-05
- **2026-07 · Capacitor travel planner (SafarMate) using WeatherKit** — 5.2.5: "did not clearly display the official Apple Weather mark and legal source link". Fix: attribution card after forecast loads; new build number 159. [source](https://github.com/planetearthkh-arch/muslim-travel-planner/pull/78) (high, en) → META-02
- **2026-08 · Mac icon tool (IconCraft)** — 5.2.5 (copy used "iOS"), 2.4.5(i) entitlements without matching functionality (Downloads read-write, incoming network, USB), 5.1.1(ii) purpose strings missing (blank alert when choosing Home folder). Fix: user-selected read-write + security-scoped bookmarks only; purpose strings; "iOS" → "app icon". [source](https://github.com/SongJiaqiang/IconCraft/pull/2) (high, en) → META-02, IOS-ENT-04, IOS-PLIST-01
- **2026-08 · macOS meeting agent (Deep State 0.8)** — 5 issues: 2.4.5(i) unused entitlements (`network.server`, `files.downloads.read-write`, `device.camera` injected by `ENABLE_*` build settings, not the entitlements file), 5.1.1(iv) priming screen button must say "Continue"/"Next" and must not be dismissible before the system prompt, 5.2.5 "macOS" in the app name (Release bundle-ID drift uploaded to a duplicate ASC record), plus support/privacy pages. [source](https://github.com/jessiegibson/deep-state/pull/15) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04, PERM-04, META-02, CONTENT-05
- **2026-07 · handwriting app "Inkwell"** — 5.2.5: "Inkwell" is on Apple's trademark list (Mac OS X handwriting feature) even though the USPTO registration is dead; + 1.5 support URL. Fix: rename to Inkstone; support page. [source](https://github.com/tsainez/Inkwell/pull/35) (high, en) → META-02, CONTENT-05
- **2026-07 · Remote Shutter 6.0.10 (camera remote for Apple Watch)** — 5.1.1(iv) priming screens ("Continue" button, no "Not Now" exit), 5.1.2(i) tracking without ATT (stale `NSUserTrackingUsageDescription`/`SKAdNetworkItems` left after removing AdMob → privacy label said tracking), 5.2.5 nine localized subtitles led with "Apple Watch". Fix: remove leftovers, update privacy label, rewrite subtitles. [source](https://github.com/security-union/remote-shutter/pull/130) (high, en) → PERM-04, IOS-PRIV-02, META-02
- **2026-08 · UniFi SFP Wizard 1.2.2 (Mac App Store)** — 5.2.5 "Terms for Mac in the app subtitle in an inappropriate manner" + 2.1 "We need a demo video that shows a physical Apple device and the designated hardware pairing together and interacting during the use of the app." (2nd round). [source](https://github.com/Gamer08YT/UniFi-SFP-Wizard/issues/5) (high, en) → META-02, META-05
- **2026-08 · fitness app (fitkiku)** — 5.2.5 metadata-only correction of subtitle/keywords; resubmitted same build. [source](https://github.com/kiku-jw/fitkiku/pull/11) (medium, en) → META-02
- **2020-03 / 2018-11 · apps with Apple product imagery/names** — 5.2.5 (SO titles: "Apple reject my app with Guideline 5.2.5 - Legal - Intellectual Property", "App got rejected due to Guideline 5.2.5"; bodies not readable this pass). [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/60501794/apple-reject-my-app-with-guideline-5-2-5-legal-intellectual-property-whats) / [source](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/53095960/app-got-rejected-due-to-guideline-5-2-5-legal-intellectual-property) (medium, en) → META-02, META-01

### Area 3 — Live Activities / widgets / App Clips / watchOS / visionOS / Mac Catalyst / Mac App Store entitlements

### Widgets & Live Activities (2.1(a) "widget not found", Watch→iPhone parity)
- **2026-04 · walking-meditation app (Pilgrim 1.2.0 b45), SwiftUI** — 2.1(a). "We were unable to install the widget of the app using an iPhone. … Tapped the 'Add Widget' button — We couldn't find the app name among the possible selections." Cause: `WidgetBundle` contained only an `ActivityConfiguration` (Live Activity), which never appears in the Home-Screen widget gallery, yet the listing/notes mentioned a widget. Fix: added a minimal `StaticConfiguration` home-screen widget (no App Group). [source](https://github.com/walktalkmeditate/pilgrim-ios/pull/30) (high, en) → IOS-BG-02, META-06
- **2026-07 · freediving tracker (DiveFree 1.0.33), iPhone + Apple Watch** — 2.1(a) "Session started on Apple Watch but nothing appear on iPhone" + 2.1 demo video on physical iPhone + Watch. Cause: WatchConnectivity only shipped completed sessions. Fix: live snapshot over `updateApplicationContext` (~2 s), in-app banner + Live Activity/Dynamic Island, `NSSupportsLiveActivities`. [source](https://github.com/yurqo/dive-free/pull/189) (high, en) → IOS-BG-02, META-05
- **2026-08 · Mac-companion remote app (AgentDeck 1.0.2), iPad** — 2.1(a): on an iPad Air 11" (M3) with no companion Mac on the review network, the connection screen "kept animating after discovery had already finished" (indicator bound to the long-lived Bonjour browser). Fix: bounded foreground search, explicit "No AgentDeck found on this network" + Search Again, no-Mac path. [source](https://github.com/puritysb/AgentDeck/pull/114) (high, en) → IOS-IPAD-01, CONTENT-01
- **2026-07 · OBD2/BLE trip recorder (6.0.0) with background location + Live Activity** — 2.1 Information Needed: demo video on a physical device showing the background-mode features and all permission prompts; reviewer note "updated demo videos will need to be provided for every app submission". Fix: recording + link in Review Notes wired into fastlane. [source](https://github.com/fdittgen-png/tankstellen/issues/3537) (high, en) → META-05, IOS-BG-01
- **2026-08 · tennis scorer, iPhone + watchOS (DeuceMate)** — 2.1 Information Needed (no bug cited): the standard seven-item new-app request — physical-device screen recording on the latest OS + six written answers (accounts/IAP/UGC to film, permission prompts, external services, AI hand-offs, regional differences, third-party marks). [source](https://github.com/EMRahman/DeuceMate/pull/89) (high, en) → META-05, IOS-META-02
- **2022-12 · watchOS-first app with $19.99 IAP** — repeated rejections because "the reviewers who review watchOS app don't even look at the watchOS app": content unlocked on the Watch, reviewer only opened the iOS companion and reported missing content. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=33898266) (medium, en) → META-05
- **2024-05 · visionOS game with realistic guns** — rejected for objectionable content, then "APPLE APPROVED THE GAME WITHOUT ANY CHANGE! They told me that upon further review, they have determined that my app is compliant". [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=40276883) (medium, en) → META-07
- **2026-07 · tide app 1.0.0 for visionOS (Swift, multiplatform)** — 3.1.1: a GitHub Sponsors link ("donations for the development of the app must use In-App Purchase"). Fix: removed the link; also fixed `requestWhenInUseAuthorization` never called and missing macOS sandbox location entitlement. [source](https://github.com/ngs/tides-swift/pull/10) (high, en) → PAY-01, PERM-02
- **2026-08 · task-lock app with Screen Time (FamilyControls) + widget extension** — "An automated analysis indicates the app uses one or more Screen Time APIs but the app has not been submitted with the Family Controls entitlement." Cause: a shared Swift file importing `FamilyControls`/`ManagedSettings` under `#if canImport` was compiled into the **widget** target, which had no entitlement; scan is per binary. Fix: split the file; CI cross-checks sources vs entitlements per target. [source](https://github.com/celikj/psychic-giggle/pull/18) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-02, IOS-BG-02

### Mac App Store / Catalyst — 2.4.5 entitlement ↔ functionality (automated scan)
- **2026-08 · Capacitor app FlatFold 1.0 for macOS (Catalyst)** — two rejections: bundle named `App.app` (`PRODUCT_NAME = $(TARGET_NAME)` — `CFBundleDisplayName` does not rename the macOS bundle) vs listing "FlatFold"; 2.4.5(i) `com.apple.security.device.camera` "had no matching functionality" (Mac WKWebView has no `mediaDevices`). Also found Catalyst shipping `aps-environment: development`. [source](https://github.com/AnmolS1/FlatFold/pull/17) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04, META-02
- **2026-08 · VPN client (BirdoVPN macOS b10420)** — 2.4.5 by "App Review's automated scan … no human ever tested the app": "The app includes the `com.apple.security.network.server` entitlement but does not appear to have matching functionality." + the standard VPN data-collection questionnaire. Fix: drop the entitlement (WireGuard inbound UDP doesn't need it); new `CFBundleVersion`. [source](https://github.com/BirdoVPN/Mobile-Client/pull/302) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04, META-05
- **2026-08 · Tauri GIS desktop app (GeoLibre 2.4.0, MAS + iOS)** — 2.4.5: `network.server` "with no functionality the automated analysis could match". It was real (Google OAuth loopback redirect on 127.0.0.1:5173). Apple offered "justify in App Review Information or remove"; they compiled the feature out for MAS/iOS builds. [source](https://github.com/opengeos/GeoLibre/pull/1651) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04
- **2026-08 · clipboard tool (geda-clipboard 0.7.0)** — 2.4.5: the app asked for Accessibility to send Cmd+V to another app; "Accessibility may not be used for automation". Fix: App Store build without auto-paste; Developer ID build keeps it. [source](https://github.com/thienanblog/geda-clipboard/pull/20) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04
- **2026-06 · clipboard manager (Clipnyx, Mac App Store)** — 2.4.5, App Review Board appeal upheld ("却下は妥当"). Root cause found afterwards: `CGRequestPostEventAccess()` fired unconditionally in `applicationDidFinishLaunching`, so the reviewer saw the "control this computer using accessibility features" dialog with no context; approved competitors (Maccy, PasteBar…) only request on first actual paste. Fix: defer to first paste with `CGPreflightPostEventAccess()`. [source](https://github.com/sawasige/clipnyx/issues/48) (high, ja) → PERM-04, IOS-ENT-04
- **2026-08 · file-transfer app (sandboxed macOS)** — 2.4.5(i) ×2: "data stored inside the app container" (the UI displayed the container path string although `Downloads` is a symlink to `~/Downloads`) and `files.downloads.read-write` "appears to have no matching functionality". Fix: user-selectable receive folder via open panel + security-scoped bookmark. [source](https://github.com/zhoulf1006/transfer/pull/52) (high, zh) → IOS-ENT-04
- (See also IconCraft / Deep State in Area 2 — `ENABLE_*` build settings inject entitlements independently of the `.entitlements` file; verify the signed binary.)

### 4.5.3 Live Activities spam (2026-06 rule)
- (No real case found in any source this pass; only pre-submission audits reference the June 2026 4.3/4.5.3 text.)

### Area 4 — Age assurance 2025–2026 (age-rating tiers, parental controls, Kids)

### 2.3.6 new age-rating questionnaire mismatches (2025-09 → 2026)
- **2026-07 · Bluesky client (bluepicca)** — 2.3.6 + 1.2. 2.3.6: the questionnaire said the app has "Parental Controls / Age Assurance" but nothing implemented → set both to **None** in ASC (no code change). 1.2 (NSFW UGC): added standard-EULA checkbox on login, per-post report via `com.atproto.moderation.createReport`, removed the "show" option for sensitive content (only hide/warn). [source](https://github.com/akagisrapid/bluepicca/pull/126) (high, ja) → META-03, IOS-META-06, UGC-01, UGC-03
- **2026-03 · kids story app "Storytime4Kids" (Expo)** — three rejections: 2.3.8 kids/children/parent references in name and copy (renamed "Storytime"), 5.1.1(v) subscriptions only after account creation (added unauthenticated paywall via `expo-iap`), 2.3.6 parental-controls answer set to None. Round 2 (2026-05): added math parental gate on IAP/external links/camera/account linking, hid coupon redemption on iOS (1.3). [source](https://github.com/Bolt-Silverfox/storytime-mobile/pull/208) / [round 2](https://github.com/Bolt-Silverfox/storytime-mobile/pull/235) (high, en) → META-03, ACC-01, UGC-07
- **2026-08 · Capacitor AI chat app (navbharatai 1.0 b46), iPad Air 11" / iPadOS 26.5.2** — four items: 2.1(a) "The microphone button is unresponsive" (Web Speech API absent in WKWebView → hide the button where unsupported), 2.3.8 placeholder iOS icon, 2.3.6 Age Rating "In-App Controls" → None, 2.1 third-party-AI questions. [source](https://github.com/docasheesh-png/navbharatai/pull/2025) (high, en) → CONTENT-01, META-03, AI-01
- **2026-08 · Mandarin-tone trainer (PinYintone 1.0 b15)** — three items: 5.1.1(iv) priming button "Allow Microphone" judged as inducing consent → "Continue"; 2.3.7 the word "free" ("Free Text Practice") "is considered a price reference"; 2.3.6 Parental Controls & Age Assurance → None. Build 16. [source](https://github.com/wjiezhu/PinYintone/pull/45) (high, zh) → PERM-04, META-01, META-03
- **2026-07 · memory/photo app (explorer b58, Expo)** — 2.1 account deletion could not re-authenticate Sign in with Apple/Google users (password-only flow), 2.1(a) "Unresponsive when we tapped save memory" (awaited 12-s upload), 2.3.6 set "User-Generated Content: Yes", Contacts question (on-device only). [source](https://github.com/simondeans-rgb/explorer/pull/379) (high, en) → ACC-02, CONTENT-01, META-03
- **2026-04 · minesweeper game (Flutter)** — 4.8 (Facebook login without Sign in with Apple), 2.3.6 advertising answer vs. actual ads mismatch (set Advertising → Yes, Parental Controls → No), 5.1.1(v) mandatory country field → optional. [source](https://github.com/manduulya/minesweeper/pull/18) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-01, META-03, ACC-01
- **2026-03 · social gaming app (lagalaga)** — process note: the new iOS 26 age-rating questionnaire had a **January 31, 2026** deadline; uncompleted apps risk removal. [source](https://github.com/ilpeppino/lagalaga/issues/82) (medium, en) → IOS-META-06

### 1.3 Kids Category — parental gate & third-party identifiers
- **2026-07 · kids math app (mathpad V1, Expo)** — 1.3: "a Kids-category app must place a parental gate in front of any link that leaves the app or any commerce, and the gate must not be disable-able." Fix: read-spelled-out-numbers gate in front of support/privacy links and purchase/restore. [source](https://github.com/yamireh/mathpad/pull/1) (high, en) → UGC-07
- **2026-01 · kids puzzle app (max-puzzles)** — 1.3 parental gate missing before the share sheet; 2.2 "Coming soon!" toggles and a disabled privacy button counted as beta/incomplete features. [source](https://github.com/DJBarker87/max-puzzles/pull/26) (high, en) → UGC-07, CONTENT-01
- **2026-02 · Flutter kids app using flutter_branch_sdk** — 1.3: "We found that your app references the ASIdentifierManager API … Frameworks/flutter_branch_sdk.framework … It would be appropriate to remove all instances of 'ASIdentifierManager' from your app, even if they are not utilized in your app's functionality." Cause: BranchSDK reads `advertisingIdentifier` via selector. Fix: remove Branch from the Kids build. [source](https://github.com/RodrigoSMarques/flutter_branch_sdk/issues/489) (high, en) → UGC-07, IOS-ENT-04
- **2024-10 · React Native 0.75 children's app** — 1.3: "Your app implements the App Tracking Transparency framework, which is used when apps collect data about users and share it with third-parties for tracking purposes." (RN core references ATT). [source](https://github.com/react/react-native/issues/47200) (high, en) → UGC-07
- **2020-11 · RN Firebase Analytics kids app** — 1.3: "Third-party analytics or third-party advertising with the ability to collect, transmit or share identifiable information, including, for example, IDFA." Apple by phone: disabling IDFA collection is not enough — the code (GoogleAppMeasurement) must be removed. [source](https://github.com/invertase/react-native-firebase/issues/4494) (high, en) → UGC-07

### Texas SB 2420 / Declared Age Range (implementation stories, no rejections found)
- **2025-12 · app team implementing state age-verification APIs** — "I spend well over a month now on the topic to implement the different half cooked APIs into our apps … Our first implementation simply failed open for all kinds of errors"; Play Age Signals pushes error handling onto apps. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=46373229) (medium, en) → LAW-01
- **2026-06 · dating-adjacent app (Tatum)** — moved the 18+ self-attestation before OAuth for SB 2420; OS age signal via `expo-age-range` (Declared Age Range / Play Age Signals) kept non-blocking when "unavailable". [source](https://github.com/tatumappofficial-afk/Tatum-App-Launch/pull/29) (medium, en) → LAW-01
- **2026-06 · friendship app (buddylist, Capacitor) resubmission audit** — raised rating 17 → 18 under 2.3.6/1.2.1(a) for unrestricted UGC + unfiltered media; removed unbacked "Pro" badges (2.3.1/3.1.1); posture "Apple: no changes to the App Review process" for SB 2420. [source](https://github.com/Pakiboy23/buddylist-app/pull/57) (medium, en) → LAW-01, META-03
- **2026-06 · expo-age-range module (alpha)** wraps Declared Age Range + Play Age Signals for RN. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48717518) (medium, en) → LAW-01

### Area 5 — 1.2 chat safety, 5.1.1(i)/5.1.2(i) third-party AI disclosure, 4.7 mini-app hosts

### 5.1.1(i) / 5.1.2(i) third-party AI — "name the destination, disclose the data, consent before sending"
- **2026-08 · companion-chat app (leisurecho, Swift)** — 5.1.2(i)/5.1.1(i): sends data to a third-party AI "without clearly identifying the destination before sending". Cause: privacy policy said "OpenAI, Anthropic or xAI" — listing options is not identification. Fix: name OpenAI, L.L.C. only, state exactly what is sent and how obtained, purpose, protections, and re-consent if provider changes; consent key includes provider ID. [source](https://github.com/jamjam315/leisurecho-site/pull/2) (high, ja) → AI-01, PRIV-04
- **2026-08 · impulse-control coach app (0.4 b95)** — 5.1.1(i)/5.1.2(i): "sends personal data to a third-party AI service without saying what is sent, naming who it goes to, or asking permission first. All three are required before any data leaves." Fix: versioned consent constant shared by consent screen, privacy policy and server gate. [source](https://github.com/impulse-training/impulse-shared/pull/64) (high, en) → AI-01
- **2026-08 · medical-tourism inquiry app (HEALO, RN)** — one of three 2026-08-05 rejections: 5.1.1(i)+5.1.2(i) "Specify who the data is sent to" — the consent copy said only "AI processing". Fix: name Google Gemini in 6 languages. Resubmission also blocked until Individual → Organization account conversion. [source](https://github.com/bonroi2296-tech/HEALO_KHIDI/pull/1352) (high, ko) → AI-01, ACC-06
- **2026-08 · voice-chat language app (voikerchat b15, Flutter), iPad Air M3** — 5.1.1(iv): the custom mic/speech pre-prompt had a "Cancel" button that let users bypass the system request; 5.1.1(i)/5.1.2(i): third-party AI (Anthropic; OpenAI for TTS) — privacy-policy mention alone insufficient. Fix: single "Continue" non-dismissible dialog straight into the OS prompt; blocking AI consent screen naming both providers. Later (2026-08-19) 2.3.10 for "Google Play" in the description (metadata-only). [source](https://github.com/shibuyer-jp/voikerchat/pull/36) / [2.3.10](https://github.com/shibuyer-jp/voikerchat/pull/113) (high, ja) → PERM-04, AI-01, CONTENT-04
- **2026-08 · AI keyboard (Sumibi, BYOK)** — pre-submission prep: 5.1.2(i) consent + Privacy Manifest/App Privacy aligned ("Other user content", linked, not tracking); 4.4.1 keyboard must work without Full Access; review-only OpenAI API key with budget cap in Review Notes. [source](https://zenn.dev/kiyoka/articles/japanese-input-method-sumibi-21) (medium, ja) → AI-01, META-05

### 1.2 UGC / chat
- **2026-08 · labor-law community app (Jurio, Expo)** — 1.2 rejection; while fixing, found account deletion only deleted rows and left the auth user (re-login resurrected the profile) → Edge Function deleting auth too; posts failed to insert (RLS). [source](https://github.com/alexjlee23/Jurio/pull/1) (high, ko) → UGC-03, ACC-02
- (Random/anonymous chat removals under the 2026-02 rule: nothing found beyond pre-emptive tickets, e.g. one-to-one peer chat adding block + attributed reports [source](https://github.com/xolace-official/xolace_app/issues/125).)

### 4.7 / 2.3.1 mini-app hosts and gated capabilities
- **2026-07 · university super-app (Mini-HBUT 1.4.3, Vue + Capacitor)** — rejected 3.1.1 for a WeChat tip-QR "sponsor" screen; the TestFlight/App Store build uses a compile-time flag that locks high-risk modules (remote config cannot re-enable them) for **all** users so the reviewer sees the same feature tree; sponsor entry hidden for guest/demo sessions only. Guidelines tracked: 2.1/2.3/4.2/4.7/4.8/5.1/5.1.5/5.2. [source](https://github.com/superdaobo/mini-hbut/issues/431) / [epic](https://github.com/superdaobo/mini-hbut/issues/379) (high, zh) → PAY-01, CONTENT-10, CONTENT-14

### Area 6 — 3.1.1 / 3.1.3(a) reader & login-only shells / 3.1.2 / 3.1.5 / 5.1.1(ix)

### 3.1.3(a) reader apps & "login-only shell" web-wrapped SaaS
- **2026-06 · e-book reader (InkColors 1.0(1))** — 3.1.3(a): reader apps may sign in existing customers but "must not include registration" — the Login screen's "New to InkColors? Create an account" link opened web sign-up. Fix: remove the link; build 1.0.0(2). [source](https://github.com/morrigangirl/inkyomi-ios/pull/30) (high, en) → IOS-UI-03, PAY-01
- **2026-08 · Capacitor web-shell (PitchShuffle)** — 3.1.1/3.1.3(a): hiding sign-up with client JS was not enough — routes `/register`, Google/Apple auto-create were blocked server-side when the request carries the native user-agent token. [source](https://github.com/worthamjh/PitchShuffle/pull/1) (high, en) → IOS-UI-03
- **2026-08 · invoicing app (facteo b39)** — 3.1.1: "the iOS app still allowed creating a business/organization account". Fix: iOS sign-in only, `/register` deep links redirect to login. [source](https://github.com/AizenPYTH/facteo/pull/10) (high, en) → IOS-UI-03
- **2026-07 · hospitality SaaS (Wingman b3, Capacitor)** — 3.1.1 "again": the webview opened the marketing site with signup/pricing. Fix: middleware blocks marketing/registration/pricing routes for the native UA; keeps `/login`, `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/delete-account`. [source](https://github.com/TheMaverick1982/Wingman-hospitality-os/pull/435) (high, en) → IOS-UI-03
- **2026-08 · Arabic Q&A SaaS (Jawab24 2.0.33)** — 3.1.1 caused "by our own paperwork": Review Notes cited 3.1.3(b) Multiplatform Services as the reason for web-only billing; the reviewer quoted that sentence back (3.1.3(b) *requires* IAP). Correct clause is 3.1.3(f) Free Stand-alone Apps. Lesson: post the Resolution Center reply **before** cancelling a rejected submission (cancelling loses the thread); never call the app "multiplatform" to App Review. [source](https://github.com/aliahdab2/jawab24/pull/739) (high, en) → META-05, META-07, IOS-UI-03
- **2026-07 · fleet SaaS mobile (store-ops 0.1.0)** — 3.1.1: "The app accesses digital content purchased outside the app, such as plans, but that content isn't available to purchase using In-App Purchase." (Stripe checkout via `openBrowserAsync`). Fix chosen: sell nothing (3.1.3(b) access-only), 402 state says "Plans are managed at shipaso.com" with no CTA; repo guard rejects `ExternalPurchaseLink`/`openBrowserAsync(url)`. Same round: 2.3.7 price language in screenshots. [source](https://github.com/airowe/store-ops/pull/423) (high, en) → PAY-01, IOS-UI-03, META-01
- **2026-08 · flight tracker (InFlight 1.0(199)), iPad** — Guideline 4 (account creation handed off to Safari) + 3.1.1 (Pro tier not sold via IAP) + 2.1 wrong demo email. Fix: in-app Supabase sign-up; StoreKit 2 subscription with restore/EULA links; server mirror of entitlement. [source](https://github.com/zcxr-gif/Inflight-IOS/pull/80) (high, en) → IOS-UI-03, PAY-01, ACC-03
- **2026-08 · transcription app (Whisper Polish 1.0 b2)** — 2.3.7 "App Store screenshots included the price of the service" ($4.99 slide) + 3.1.1: paid polish unlockable with a user-supplied OpenRouter API key. Fix: cloud/IAP-only UI, no BYO-key field. [source](https://github.com/jonclegg/whisper-polish/pull/6) (high, en) → META-01, PAY-01
- **2026-08 · comedy audio app (bleepkit)** — 3.1.1: a hardcoded local promo code unlocked Pro outside IAP (and its UserDefaults flag outranked StoreKit forever). Fix: delete; "Redeem a Code" now opens `AppStore.presentOfferCodeRedeemSheet`. [source](https://github.com/taylordrew4u2/bleepkit/pull/1) (high, en) → PAY-01
- **2026-08 · rugby training app (tryline)** — 3.1.1 because the app had **no** purchase path at all while plans are sold on the web; decision reversed to add RevenueCat IAP rather than wait for Japan's smartphone act. [source](https://github.com/Gosshi/tryline/pull/681) (high, ja) → PAY-01
- **2026-08 · coin-laundry app (1.0.0 b17, Expo)** — 3.1.2(c): auto-renewable subscription "without a functioning link to the Terms of Use (EULA)" in app/metadata. Fix: Apple standard EULA link in Settings (web `/terms` avoided because it shows web prices → 3.1.3(a) risk) + EULA URL in the App Description. [source](https://github.com/Tiffany-sho/coinlaundy_app_iOS/pull/1) (high, ja) → PAY-02
- **2026-04 · relish (RN/Expo, RevenueCat)** — 2.1(b) "tapping the upgrade button did nothing in sandbox" (offerings `null`, no restore) then 2.1(b) IAP display name mismatch ("Premium" vs submitted "RELISH PEAK") + 3.1.2(c) missing Terms/Privacy links. [source](https://github.com/ohadren-source/relish/pull/3) / [round 2](https://github.com/ohadren-source/relish/pull/6) (high, en) → PAY-03, PAY-04, PAY-02
- **2026-08 · exam-prep app (StoreKit 2)** — 3.1.1: "tapped Restore Purchases but no feedback that restore completed" — `AppStore.sync()` is instant in Sandbox but slow in production. Fix: syncing/success/none/failed states. [source](https://qiita.com/Okojyo-Soft/items/fae218490660eb5b5236) (high, ja) → PAY-03
- **2026-02 · subscription app running RevenueCat pricing experiment** — 2.1 "In-app purchase products … could not be found in the submitted binary" + 3.1.2: "the different subscription products of your auto-renewable subscriptions were created as separate in-app purchase products, rather than as different subscription products within the same subscription group." (separate groups per price test). [source](https://community.revenuecat.com/general-questions-7/apple-rejects-distinct-ios-subscription-groups-for-experiment-7387) (high, en) → PAY-05, IOS-IAP-01
- **2025-11 · subscription app (RevenueCat), iPad Air M2 / iPadOS 26.1** — 2.1 "We were still unable to purchase the subscription successfully" ×N + 2.3.2 "You submitted duplicate or identical promotional images for different promoted in-app purchase products" → products in Developer Action Needed. [source](https://community.revenuecat.com/general-questions-7/multiple-ios-app-store-rejection-due-to-revenue-cat-purchase-failure-there-was-a-problem-with-the-apple-store-7175) (high, en) → PAY-04, IOS-META-07
- **2023-10 · RevenueCat sample paywall** — 3.1.1 "The subscriptions can be purchased in the app using payment mechanisms other than in-app purchase" (screenshot showed the loader before the StoreKit sheet). Resolved by resubmitting the same binary with an explanation; "after a couple of resubmits … approved". [source](https://community.revenuecat.com/general-questions-7/app-store-review-rejected-due-to-other-payment-mechanisms-3406) (medium, en) → META-07
- **2022-10 · IAP app** — 1.1.6 "your app's in-app purchase products are mislabeled as Apple Pay" (reviewer saw the StoreKit sheet); resubmitted unchanged. [source](https://community.revenuecat.com/general-questions-7/how-to-resolve-a-rejected-app-due-to-mislabeled-as-apple-pay-2158) (medium, en) → IOS-IAP-03
- **2026-08 · finance app** — 2.1 "Error 23" with IAPs in Ready to Submit: the subscriptions must be attached to the same review submission. [source](https://community.revenuecat.com/general-questions-7/app-gets-rejected-due-to-error-23-6883) (medium, en) → IOS-IAP-01
- **2026-08 · credits app (ugc-copy 0.0.4)** — 2.1(b) "the app displayed an error message, even after successfully purchasing the credits": server dropped receipts with `is_sandbox: true`, and App Review buys in the store sandbox against production. [source](https://github.com/112kratoss/ugc-copy/pull/43) (high, en) → PAY-04
- **2026-08 · health app (Vitta 1.0(11)), iPad Air 11" M3** — first submission 2.1(b): "an error message displayed when we tried to subscribe"; subscription `vitta_premium_monthly` returned with the version. [source](https://github.com/rodrigobastosv/vitta/issues/294) (high, en) → PAY-04
- **2026-08 · dev.to: RN app with subscriptions** — 3.1.2: EULA link must be in the **App Description** when using the standard EULA (ASC has no URL field for it); metadata-only fix. Same author's ATT case below. [source](https://dev.to/ninomaedev/why-your-app-tracking-transparency-prompt-doesnt-show-up-and-how-it-got-my-app-rejected-oob) (high, en) → PAY-02

### 3.1.5 crypto / 5.1.1(ix) payments
- **2026-07 · Bitcoin Lightning wallet (Glow)** — App Review asked for cryptocurrency-exchange licensing evidence (3.1.5(iii)) because of a "Buy Bitcoin" button that only links to licensed third-party on-ramps. Fix: build-time flag hides Buy in App Store submission builds. [source](https://github.com/breez/glow-web/pull/281) (high, en) → LAW-02
- **2026-07 · crypto wallet (Veyrnox)** — 3.1.5(b) requires Organization enrollment; Individual → Organization conversion is not self-serve (Developer Program Support; D-U-N-S, Companies House, address-mismatch disclosure). [source](https://github.com/VEYRNOX/veyrnox/pull/1263) (medium, en) → ACC-06, LAW-02
- **2026-07 · grocery e-commerce app (Flutter)** — 5.1.1(ix): "Add payment card" screen collected raw PAN + CVV in plain text fields (never tokenized). Fix: remove the custom form; cards saved only through the Stripe checkout sheet. [source](https://github.com/mnastrojarna-hub/Vel-n/pull/1829) (high, en) → ACC-06, PRIV-04

### Area 7 — 2.3.1 hidden features / review-mode detection, 2.5.2 code push, 4.2.6/4.3 templates, 5.6.3

### 2.3.1 hidden features, geo/review detection, code push
- **2024-03 · RN 0.70 app with react-native-code-push 7.1.0** — 2.3.1 "The app contains hidden features." No clue given; "I removed all CodePush package and re-submit again, and it is passed." [source](https://github.com/microsoft/react-native-code-push/issues/2680) (high, en) → CONTENT-10, IOS-UI-02
- **2024-03 · Flutter app (get/encrypt/http)** — 2.3.1 on resubmission: "The app may contain hidden features, functionality, or content. Specifically, the app may still contain financial transactions without the necessary authorization … including in third-party code or SDKs." [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/144684) (high, en) → CONTENT-10, LAW-02
- **2024-08 · apps with IP geoblocking** — "we've had apps rejected because they hit our IP geoblock, and the logs would always indicate they were testing via an Irish mobile network. We specifically allow-listed Apple's /8 range, but they don't always use the corporate VPN." [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=41152587) (medium, en) → CONTENT-13, META-05
- **2025-08 · geolocked app (splash outside jurisdiction)** — repeated rejections; reviewers "aren't reading the instructions"; resubmitting until a reviewer follows the notes/video. [source](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=45061154) (medium, en) → CONTENT-13, META-05
- **2026-08 · German party app (Grünerator, Expo)** — beta review stopped under 2.1(a): every visible login led to federated party directories Apple can't get an account for. Fix: a username/password Keycloak provider gated behind a 5-tap logo gesture, **documented in Review Notes** because "a function only we know about violates 2.3.1". [source](https://github.com/netzbegruenung/Gruenerator/pull/2994) (high, de) → ACC-03, CONTENT-10
- **2026-06 · Kotlin Multiplatform permission library (Grant) consumers** — Apple's static scan demanded `NSBluetoothAlwaysUsageDescription` / `NSLocationAlwaysAndWhenInUseUsageDescription` because the selectors were compiled into `grant-core`; an obfuscation workaround (`performSelector(NSSelectorFromString("request"+"AlwaysAuthorization"))`) was rejected as 2.3.1 circumvention; fix is module isolation. [source](https://github.com/brewkits/Grant/pull/46) / [report](https://github.com/brewkits/Grant/issues/45) (medium, en/es) → IOS-PLIST-01, IOS-ENT-04
- (Shorebird / expo-updates 2025–2026 rejection stories: none found; only an explainer of why EAS OTA is allowed [source](https://zenn.dev/shomtsm/articles/4bef79a964cda1).)

### 4.2.6 / 4.3(a) templates, spam, escalation
- **2026-01 · Microsoft Dynamics 365 Store Commerce iOS sample (9.55) repackaged by a partner** — 4.3(a): "your app shares a similar binary, metadata, and/or concept as apps submitted to the App Store by other developers, with only minor differences." [source](https://github.com/microsoft/Dynamics365Commerce.Solutions/issues/392) (high, en) → CONTENT-07
- **2026-05→06 · grocery-list app (forager v2.0 b140, native Swift)** — 4.3(a) three times (verbatim boilerplate on re-review); App Review Board appeal (ticket APL466617) **upheld** on 2026-06-23 naming no specific app; next step Meet with Apple. [source](https://github.com/rfhayn/forager/pull/154) / [Board denial](https://github.com/rfhayn/forager/pull/156) (high, en) → CONTENT-07, META-07
- **2026-08 · Suika-style merge game (cosmic-merge), 3rd rejection, iPhone 17 Pro Max / iOS 26** — 4.3(a) + 5.1.2(i). Fix: signature mechanic (Constellation Missions), dropped "suika/watermelon/merge/2048" keywords, AdMob non-personalized only, removed ATT, privacy label update. [source](https://github.com/Jeffrey5304Lab/cosmic-merge/pull/1) (high, en) → CONTENT-07, IOS-PRIV-02
- **2025-12 · derivative app "iko for friends" (same developer as "iko")** — 4.3(a): "your app shares a similar binary, metadata, and/or concept as other apps you already submitted to the App Store, with only minor differences." A reply explaining different audience (couples/solo vs 3+ groups), roadmap and why merging would harm UX got it approved "within hours, no further review". [source](https://zenn.dev/tatukane/articles/8c893a78126d92) (high, ja) → CONTENT-07, META-07
- **2025-12 · escape-room game** — 4.3(a) repeatedly despite original story/UI/assets; learned: resubmitting before the reviewer replies kills the thread; ask "I'd like to talk" for a call; Meet with Apple (Japanese-language) → appeal approved in 7 days and the 4.3(a) flag withdrawn; "once flagged, 4.3(a) tends to reappear on each resubmission". [source](https://qiita.com/bdog_games/items/9444f0edab995c7fbc38) (high, ja) → CONTENT-07, META-07
- **2026-08 · indie dev with 13 apps** — 4.3 patterns: reviewers compare against your **own** account's apps and ask "explain the difference from app X"; write the differentiation in Review Notes up front; 2026 submission volume +84% lengthens waits to 7–30 days. [source](https://zenn.dev/artemis_rh/articles/5323c0b9ef0a6c) (medium, ja) → CONTENT-07, META-05
- **2019-05 · NativeScript agency apps** / **2018-01 · Siberian app-builder clients** — 4.2.6 "created from an app generation service or a commercialized template, but it was not directly submitted by the provider of the app's content" → submit under the client's own account or build a single "picker" app. [source](https://github.com/NativeScript/NativeScript/issues/7191) / [source](https://github.com/Xtraball/Siberian/issues/512) (high, en) → CONTENT-07

### 5.6.3 rating prompts (no fake-review / 5.6.2 identity cases found)
- **2026-07 · Omi wearable app (Flutter)** — 5.6.3: "The app requests users to rate the app on first launch or during onboarding, before they've had enough time to gain a clear understanding of the app's value." Fix: removed the "Loving Omi?" onboarding step; post-engagement prompt kept. [source](https://github.com/BasedHardware/omi/pull/10498) (high, en) → CONTENT-06
- **2026-08 · food-scanner app (nutriapp/Sage)** — 5.6.3: onboarding "Loved by clean eaters · 4.9 ★" screen with invented testimonials whose Continue fired `SKStoreReviewController.requestReview`. Fix: delete; prompt only after ≥3 scans, >1 launch, ≥24 h, once per version. [source](https://github.com/matheusfunabashi/nutriapp/pull/57) (high, en) → CONTENT-06
- **2026-06 · Rainbow crypto wallet** — 5.6.3: native rating prompt on first wallet-screen appearance (right after onboarding). Fix: disable all automatic triggers; Settings "Rate App" only. [source](https://github.com/rainbow-me/rainbow/pull/7633) (high, en) → CONTENT-06

### Area 8 — Framework-specific 2025–2026

### Flutter
- **2025-12 · Flutter 3.35.x apps with flutter_inappwebview** — 2.5.1: "non-public or deprecated APIs: Frameworks/flutter_inappwebview_ios.framework — Classes: PGHostedWindow; Runner — Classes: __SwiftValue". Same app approved on Flutter 3.27.3. [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/180188) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02, IOS-META-04
- **2025-04 · Flutter camera app** — rejected for deprecated API `AVCapturePhotoSettings.uniqueID` in `camera_avfoundation`. [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/166396) (medium, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2025-01 · add-to-app Flutter module with webview_flutter** — "Invalid binary" during review: `webview_flutter_wkwebview` framework missing code signature. [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/162406) (medium, en) → IOS-META-03
- **2025-02 · Flutter app on iOS 18** — App Store rejection because the app "becomes unresponsive to taps and gestures" (iOS 18 only, intermittent). [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/164305) (medium, en) → CONTENT-01
- **2026-08 · Flutter release IPA** — ITMS-90111 "Unsupported SDK or Xcode version" on three consecutive submissions; `App.framework` carries a fixed iOS SDK 13.0 `LC_BUILD_VERSION` stamp; host was also on macOS 27 beta (see beta-host cases below). [source](https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/190970) (medium, en) → IOS-META-04
- **2026-08 · Flutter fintech (konto), iPad Air 11"** — 2.1(a): "no keyboard appeared from the second OTP digit" — one `TextFormField` per digit with `requestFocus()` hops tears down the text-input connection on iPad. Fix: single hidden field behind six boxes. [source](https://github.com/adolfokrah/konto/pull/78) (high, en) → IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-07 · Flutter AR app (meetro.lx)** — 2.5.1: "the binary includes TrueDepth APIs" — `arkit_plugin` links `ARFaceTrackingConfiguration` even though the AR feature was compiled out. Fix: drop the plugin (+ remove `NSCameraUsageDescription`). [source](https://github.com/JaimeFerreira2002/meetro.lx/pull/47) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04, IOS-UI-02
- **2026-07 · Flutter media app (daccord 0.2.6 b141, media_kit)** — 2.5.1 (automated, no API named). `Mpv.xcframework` from `media_kit_libs_ios_video` imports `_fork/_execve/_setsid/_waitpid/_kill`, tty functions and deprecated OpenGL ES. [source](https://github.com/DaccordProject/daccord/pull/228) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2026-07 · LiveKit Flutter/Swift SDK consumers** — ITMS-90338 / 2.5.1: the literal `buttonPressed:` selector (used to auto-open `RPSystemBroadcastPickerView`) now blocks any app linking LiveKit, even without screen share. Fix in SDK: `sendActions(for: .touchUpInside)`. [source](https://github.com/livekit/client-sdk-swift/pull/1065) (high, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2026-08 · Flutter iPad build (rikako 3.0.0 b31)** — 2.1(a) "Unable to proceed" on iPad Air 11" / iPadOS 26.5.2 after switching `TARGETED_DEVICE_FAMILY` to iPhone-only to avoid iPad screenshots — reviewers still run it in compatibility mode. [source](https://github.com/takoikatakotako/rikako/issues/250) (high, ja) → IOS-IPAD-01
- **2026-08 · Flutter game (ATLASARROWS 1.0(41)), iPad Air M3** — 5.1.2(i): privacy label says IDFA used for tracking but the ATT prompt never appeared: `requestTrackingAuthorization()` resolves silently `notDetermined` when a Game Center sign-in sheet is on top. Fix: retry while `resumed`, defer Game Center until consent settled. [source](https://github.com/zeros1205/ATLASARROWS/pull/242) (high, ko) → PRIV-05
- **2025-12 · Flutter AdMob app (ojeomneo)** — 2.1 Information Needed: AdMob without any ATT request (only the plist string existed). [source](https://github.com/ggorockee/ojeomneo/pull/128) (high, ko) → PRIV-05
- **2025-08 · Flutter app with old SDKs** — ITMS-91061 missing privacy manifest; wrote a hand-made `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` for the flagged frameworks. [source](https://qiita.com/TakanobuSano/items/fed7d1aa5b4af41c9842) (medium, ja) → IOS-PRIV-03
- **2026-08 · dev.to: Flutter App Review guide** — recurring rejections: 3.1.1 web checkout reuse and "manage your plan on our website" text, 5.1.1(v), 4.3 for multi-app codebases, 3.1.2 paywall disclosures, 2.1 demo credentials, generic purpose strings. [source](https://dev.to/shahryarahmad/getting-a-flutter-app-through-app-review-3did) (medium, en) → PAY-01, ACC-02

### React Native / Expo
- **2025-09 · Expo app using expo-location geofencing** — 2.5.4: "The app declares support for location in the UIBackgroundModes key … but we are unable to locate any features that require persistent location." `expo-location` required the background mode to start geofencing; PR removes that requirement. [source](https://github.com/expo/expo/pull/39419) (high, en) → IOS-PLIST-02
- **2026-08 · Expo app with expo-audio (rougether 1.0 b35)** — 2.5.4: "The app declares support for audio in the UIBackgroundModes key … but we are unable to play any audible content when the app is running in the background." Cause: `expo-audio` config plugin defaults `enableBackgroundPlayback: true`. Fix: set false → `UIBackgroundModes` removed. [source](https://github.com/TripleS-soma/rougether-mobile/issues/719) (high, ko) → IOS-PLIST-02

[source](https://github.com/shogo-labs/shogo-ai/issues/562) / [568](https://github.com/shogo-labs/shogo-ai/issues/568) / [crash](https://github.com/shogo-labs/shogo-ai/issues/693) (high, en) → IOS-LOGIN-01, IOS-IPAD-01, PAY-05, CONTENT-01
- **2026-08 · RN baby-log app (1.7.5 b29), iPadOS 26.6** — 2.1 Information Needed: ATT dialog not shown — requested before the window was active and AdMob banner mounted concurrently. Fix: wait for `AppState === 'active'`, gate ads behind ATT completion. [source](https://github.com/tsuku723/little-baby-log/pull/237) (high, ja) → PRIV-05
- **2026-08 · dev.to: RN app** — 2.1 "unable to locate the App Tracking Transparency permission request": ATT called before the scene was active on the slower review device. [source](https://dev.to/ninomaedev/why-your-app-tracking-transparency-prompt-doesnt-show-up-and-how-it-got-my-app-rejected-oob) (high, en) → PRIV-05
- **2026-07 · Expo social app (blackhawk-runner)** — 2.1(a) "An error appeared during login." The Supabase **free-plan project had auto-paused** after 7 idle days; keep-alive GitHub Action added; `getSession()` failure no longer hangs on Loading. [source](https://github.com/avwyaan/blackhawk-runner/pull/3) (high, en) → ACC-03, CONTENT-01
- **2026-06 · RN Firebase app (imuatrak)** — round 1: 5.1.1(v) no deletion, 2.5.1 "HealthKit not identified in the UI"; round 2 (iPhone 17 Pro Max / iOS 26.5.1): 2.1(b) dead Subscribe button when RevenueCat offerings empty (Paid Apps Agreement), 2.5.1 HealthKit again because `requestAuthorization()` was never called. [source](https://github.com/kedhead/imuatrak/pull/42) / [round 2](https://github.com/kedhead/imuatrak/pull/43) (high, en) → ACC-02, IOS-ENT-04, PAY-04
- **2026-06 · RN fitness app (ryzrapp 1.0.9 b22)** — 2.5.1: HealthKit linked (entitlement + strings) but the UI showed a "SOON" placeholder; Apple: "remove references to the HealthKit framework from the binary". [source](https://github.com/joshbradik-blip/ryzrapp/pull/4) (high, en) → IOS-ENT-04
- **2026-08 · Expo app with custom progressive-blur module** — pre-emptive removal of reflectively constructed private `CAFilter`/`variableBlur` (class names base64-obfuscated) as a 2.5.1 risk. [source](https://github.com/SchroederNathan/amber/pull/10) (medium, en) → IOS-UI-02
- **2025-08 · apps upgrading AppAuth-iOS 1.7.6 → 2.0.0** — rejected: "the user is taken to the default web browser to sign in or register for an account, which provides a poor user experience"; reverting to 1.7.6 was approved. Fix: native `ASAuthorizationController` via `@capacitor-community/apple-sign-in` + `signInWithIdToken`; team shipped without Apple sign-in meanwhile.
