## Cases from Russian, Spanish, Portuguese, Vietnamese, Indonesian, Turkish, German and French sources (3rd pass, 2026-08-28)

Collected 2026-08-28. Only URLs actually fetched or seen in search results. Confidence: high = full text read; medium = partial/unreadable or second-hand.
Format: `- **YYYY-MM · <app type> (<lang>)** — <guideline/policy>. "<quote>" Cause: … Fix: … <rounds/time>. [source](URL) (confidence, lang) → <checklist IDs>`

---

### iOS / App Store

### 1.x Safety (prohibited app classes, Kids, developer info)

- **2026-07 · photo-rating social app "Rate Me" (ru)** — Guideline 1.2 (Safety – User-Generated Content; appearance-rating / "hot-or-not" mechanics). "Первый отказ был техническим (не показывался один системный запрос) — починил за день. А вот второй оказался приговором: Guideline 1.2. У Apple прямо запрещены приложения, чья основная механика — оценка внешности людей." Cause: core mechanic (rate strangers' photos 1–10) is a banned class on iOS even though the same app lived for years on Google Play. A re-skin as "fashion advice" was also rejected. Fix: gave up on App Store; shipped in the EU via AltStore PAL (Alternative Terms Addendum, notarization passed in <2 days) + PWA for the rest of the world. 2 rejections, ~1 week of AltStore setup. [source](https://vc.ru/life/3043397-kak-oboyti-otkaz-apple-v-app-store) (high, ru) → UGC-01, UGC-07, CONTENT-14, PERM-02

- **2026-08 · macOS/iOS playlist-URL utility (tr)** — Guideline 1.5 Safety – Developer Information. "The Support or Privacy URL provided in App Store Connect, in one or more localization, is currently not functional and/or displays an error." Cause: privacy-policy URL in both `tr` and `en-US` locales pointed to a GitHub file on a branch named `main` while the repo default branch was `master` AND the file had never been pushed (404); the support URL (repo issues page) returned 200 but was empty. Fix: moved privacy + support pages to the developer's own domain, one page per store locale (tr/en), support page with real e-mail, usage notes and known limitations; updated ASC URLs without a new build and resubmitted; added a fastlane `verify_urls` lane that reads the **live** ASC URLs and HTTP-checks each before `upload_to_app_store`. 1 round (metadata-only). [source](https://github.com/salihgencer/playlist-url-extractor/issues/1) (high, tr) → META-04, PRIV-01, CONTENT-05, IOS-META-02

### 2.x Performance (completeness, demo account, hardware)

- **2026-07 · shared-expense splitter "WeCount" (ru)** — Guideline 2.1 App Completeness / 5.1.1 Data Collection (registration & login). "Push‑уведомления были намеренно отключены. Ревьюеры просто отклоняли системный запрос на их получение… в качестве адреса электронной почты использовался test@test.com." Cause: sign-up verified the phone number via a push code, then via e-mail — neither actually proves ownership of the phone number, and reviewers deliberately deny the push permission and use throwaway e-mails; when the author switched to SMS/voice-call OTP, reviewers' test numbers cannot receive SMS or calls. Fix: rewrote registration to SMS + toll-free call verification and added a reviewer-only demo mode that bypasses OTP (documented in review notes); two phone calls with the App Review lead. >2 months, "несколько отказов подряд". [source](https://habr.com/ru/articles/1063510/) (high, ru) → ACC-03, PERM-02, PUSH-01, META-05, CONTENT-13

- **2024-02 · EMS muscle-stimulator companion app "PowerDot" (ru, agency КОД9)** — Guideline 2.1 (hardware apps must be testable). "Перед первым релизом модераторы попросили нас отправить девайсы к ним в Калифорнию… Посылка шла около месяца… Apple так и не вернули стимуляторы обратно. А во время релиза одного из обновлений модераторы потребовали у нас видео." Cause: reviewer could not test the Bluetooth device flow. Fix: shipped physical units to Cupertino (first release, ~1 month wait); for later updates attached a demo video of pairing and a stimulation program. [source](https://vc.ru/life/1030818-kak-proiti-moderaciyu-app-store-v-2024-m-i-chto-uchest-chtoby-reliz-proshel-v-srok) (high, ru) → META-05, UGC-06, IOS-META-02

- **2024-02 · e-commerce MVP labelled "интернет-магазин" (ru, agency КОД9)** — Guideline 2.1 / 2.3 Accurate Metadata (category Shopping but no purchase flow). "В названии было слово «интернет-магазин», в описании мы обещали функции интернет-магазина, поставили категорию Shopping, но купить там товары было нельзя. В релизе нам отказали." Cause: MVP only let users paste product links into a support chat; name/description/category promised a store. Fix: align category, name and description with what the build can actually do; never write "Beta"/"MVP" in metadata; ship MVPs via TestFlight. [source](https://vc.ru/life/1030818-kak-proiti-moderaciyu-app-store-v-2024-m-i-chto-uchest-chtoby-reliz-proshel-v-srok) (high, ru) → META-02, META-06, CONTENT-01, META-03

### 3.x Business (IAP)

- **2024-02 · subscription app that sold plans on its website (ru, agency КОД9)** — Guideline 3.1.1 In-App Purchase. "Однажды мы отправили на проверку приложение, в котором были платные подписки, но для их покупки нужно было уйти на сайт. Модераторы это заметили и не дали нам запуститься." Cause: subscriptions were only purchasable on the web; app had no IAP and no in-app registration. Fix: implemented StoreKit subscriptions + in-app sign-up/login, configured IAP products (prices, screenshots, Terms of Use / privacy links) in ASC and submitted them with the binary. Release delayed by the IAP build-out. [source](https://vc.ru/life/1030818-kak-proiti-moderaciyu-app-store-v-2024-m-i-chto-uchest-chtoby-reliz-proshel-v-srok) (high, ru) → PAY-01, PAY-02, PAY-05, IOS-IAP-01

### 4.x Design (copycat / minimum functionality)

- **2024-10 · price-comparison metasearch "YoloPrice" (ru)** — Guideline 4.1 Copycats → then 4.2.2 Minimum Functionality ("not different from a web browser"). "Приложение или его метаданные содержат вводящие в заблуждение ссылки на сторонний контент. В частности, приложение включает контент, напоминающий Wildberries, Яндекс, Aliexpress… / Ваше приложение содержит только ссылки, изображения или контент, собранный из Интернета." Cause: app already live with 1000+ installs; an update was flagged for showing marketplace logos, then repeatedly for being a "web wrapper". Fix: removed/explained logos and listed searched stores in description; wrote a Resolution Center letter explaining metasearch across 136 stores, ML ranking, NN grouping, patented SDK; the letter that worked compared native-iOS usage (Siri & Search, push) with Skyscanner/Kayak/Aviasales in a table, demanded specifics, offered a call, and noted Android users already had the update. 4 rejections over several weeks; approved hours after the last letter. [source](https://habr.com/ru/articles/853698/) (high, ru) → CONTENT-03, META-02, META-05, META-07

- **2026-05 · aggregators / price-comparison / travel apps (ru, consultant write-up)** — Guideline 4.1 Copycats and 4.2.2 Minimum Functionality on **updates** of already-published apps. "приложение не отличается от стандартного веб-браузера… Потому что каждое обновление проходит ревью заново. История приложения учитывается не всегда как защитный фактор." Cause: reviewers judge visual/functional similarity to marketplaces and "could this be Safari tabs?", not architecture. Fix: in the reply describe number of sources, aggregation/ranking/dedup steps, native iOS features used (Siri, Search, Live Activities), and benchmark against an already-accepted category (Skyscanner, Kayak, HotelsCombined, Aviasales). [source](https://vc.ru/id5982369/2951534-prichiny-otkloneniya-obnovleniy-v-app-store-i-guideline-4-2-2) (medium, ru) → CONTENT-03, META-05, META-07

### 5.x Legal / Discovery fraud

- **2026-06 · subscription apps prompting for a rating during onboarding (ru coverage of RevenueCat report)** — Guideline 5.6.3 Discovery Fraud (enforced against `SKStoreReviewController` in onboarding). "Apple начала отклонять приложения, которые просят пользователей поставить оценку ещё во время онбординга… Apple не вводила новых правил, а начала более внимательно следить за соблюдением пункта 5.6.3." Cause: rating prompt shown on welcome/first-launch screens before any meaningful use. Fix: move the prompt after a completed action (saved project, finished workout); respect the 3-per-365-days system limit. [source](https://habr.com/ru/articles/1049768/) (medium, ru) → CONTENT-06

### Account / enrollment (Apple Developer Program)

- **2023-10 · Chinese client's org enrollment handled by a Russian agency (ru)** — Apple Developer Program enrollment: D-U-N-S mismatch + payment rejected. "Apple не приняла номер, поскольку адрес компании… должны совпадать полностью, а также название компании… подписку в Apple Developer Program можно оплачивать только картой, привязанной к указанной организации… выпущена именно в городе, где компания зарегистрирована." Cause: company address/name differed between D&B and Apple; repeated $99 payments silently declined because the card was not the organization's own card. Fix: sync D&B record, call Apple support (callbacks faster than e-mail), pay with the org's corporate card; identity confirmed by phone with the owner. ~3 months instead of 2 weeks. [source](https://dtf.ru/flood/2223521-registraciya-v-kachestve-razrabotchika-apple-istoriya-neozhidannyh-slozhnostei) (high, ru) → ACC-06, IOS-META-02

### 2.5.x Software requirements (downloaded code)

- **2026-04 · vibe-coding app "Anything" (ru coverage of MacRumors/9to5Mac)** — Guideline 2.5.2 (apps must be self-contained; no downloading/executing code that changes functionality). "Компания сослалась на нарушение пункта 2.5.2 правил App Store: «приложения должны быть самодостаточными и не могут загружать или исполнять код, изменяющий их функциональность»." Cause: generated user apps ran inside the host app; Replit and Vibecode updates were blocked under the same clause in March 2026. Fix: a "preview in browser" update was rejected; team moved app generation into iMessage; Apple restored the app on 3 April with changes. Removed ~1 week. [source](https://vc.ru/dev/2852315-vozvrashchenie-prilozheniya-anything-v-apple-app-store) (medium, ru) → IOS-UI-02, CONTENT-10, CONTENT-14

### 2.3 Accurate metadata (screenshots)

- **2026-03 · emergency-medicine toolkit "EmerKit" (es)** — Guideline 2.3.10 Accurate Metadata (non-iOS status bar in screenshots). "Revise the app's screenshots to remove non-iOS status bar images. Revised screenshots should accurately reflect the app in use on the supported devices." Cause: App Store Connect screenshots were Android captures; reviewed on iPad Air 11" (M2). Fix: regenerate all required iPhone (6.9"/6.5"/5.5") and iPad (13"/12.9") screenshots from iOS simulators. 1 round. [source](https://github.com/GlobalEmergency/EmerKit/issues/25) (high, es) → META-01, IOS-META-01, META-06

- **2026-08 · work-reflection journaling app (vi)** — Guideline 2.1 Information Needed (7 items incl. physical-device demo video). "Apple từ chối bản 1.0 build 2 với 2.1 Information Needed. Không phải lỗi app: reviewer chưa chạy app, họ dừng vì ô App Review Information → Notes không đủ thông tin." Cause: review notes too thin; the old demo-video script skipped sign-up, account deletion and permission prompts — exactly what Apple asked to see. Fix: 3,996-char Notes + Resolution Center reply, 13-scene demo video shot on the same build (sign-up, full account-deletion flow, permission dialogs); Google sign-in button removed so 4.8 would not be triggered. [source](https://github.com/vietdang7/workreflection_mobile_app/pull/12) (high, vi) → META-05, UGC-06, ACC-02, IOS-LOGIN-01

### 4.8 Login services / iPad

- **2026-03 · Turkish recipe app "Tariften" (tr)** — Guideline 4.8 Login Services. "Apple rejected the app under Guideline 4.8 — offering Google Sign-In without an equivalent privacy-preserving login option. Sign in with Apple is required." Fix: `expo-apple-authentication`, `usesAppleSignIn: true`, backend `/auth/apple`; Apple button active on iOS only; handle `fullName` only on first sign-in. [source](https://github.com/selim-create/tariften-mobile/pull/28) (medium — PR text in English, tr project) → IOS-LOGIN-01, IOS-ENT-03, ACC-04

- **2026-03 · optical measurement app "MedidorOtica" (pt-BR)** — App Store upload validation: incomplete iPad multitasking orientation support. "O upload anterior foi rejeitado pela validacao da Apple por suporte incompleto a multitarefa no iPad." Fix: add all orientations to `UISupportedInterfaceOrientations~ipad`, keep portrait-only on iPhone. [source](https://github.com/minega/MedidorOtica/pull/128) (medium, pt) → IOS-IPAD-01

---

### Android / Google Play

### Metadata / impersonation / misleading claims

- **2022-01 · loyalty-card wallet "Catima" (ru translation of the maintainer's post)** — Metadata policy ("title contains promotional/price information") in nl_NL/no_NO and 9 more locales, then a bogus "login credentials required" rejection. "«в текущей версии в локалях nl_NL и no_NO поле с названием содержит рекламную информацию и сведения о стоимости»… шестнадцатого января приложение отклонили из-за того, что в нем нет данных для входа в аккаунт. Несмотря на то, что Catima работает на сто процентов в оффлайне." Cause: Google machine-translates localized titles — Dutch *vrij* ("libre") became "free"; a Console bug re-validated every locale on each edit while the new 30-char title limit blocked saving. Fix: rewrote titles in ~11 languages under 30 chars; replied that the app has no accounts; approved 18 Jan after ~3 months (Oct 2021–Jan 2022) of "Action Required" mails. [source](https://habr.com/ru/articles/646999/) (high, ru) → AND-META-01, META-02, AND-CONSOLE-03

- **2026-08 · Bundeswehr fitness-test calculator "SGT Rechner" (de)** — Impersonation policy ("Richtlinie zu Identitätsdiebstahl"), then Misleading claims ("Fehlender Quell-Link für Behördeninformationen"). "Der Store-Titel („SGT Soldaten-Grundfitness-Tool") und der Schriftzug im App-Symbol trugen den amtlichen Programmnamen ohne erkennbare Distanzierung." Cause: official government program name in title and icon; description cited the official manual without linking it — Google "prüft ausdrücklich auch die App selbst". Fix: retitle "SGT Rechner (inoffiziell)", new logo/feature graphic, short description starts "Inoffizieller …"; second round added clickable links to the three official bundeswehr.de sources inside the app (Settings → Quellen), in the listing and in the privacy page plus a "vertritt keine Behörde" disclaimer; new build each time because the icon lives in the bundle. 2 rounds in 4 days. [source](https://github.com/MarqEwi/sgt-rechner/pull/13) · [round 2](https://github.com/MarqEwi/sgt-rechner/pull/16) (high, de) → AND-META-01, AND-META-04, AND-CONSOLE-12, AND-META-02

### Permissions (library-injected, foreground service, media, Health Connect, hardware)

- **2023-05 · React Native app using `react-native-multiple-image-picker` (vi)** — All files access (MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE) policy. "ứng dụng của em đang bị google từ chối vì chứa quyền này, em không làm cách nào xoá nó đi được" Cause: library v1.0.0 declared `MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` in its own manifest; merged manifest carried it into the app. Fix: delete the `<uses-permission>` line in the library's AndroidManifest and persist with `npx patch-package` (equivalently `tools:node="remove"`). [source](https://github.com/NitrogenZLab/react-native-multiple-image-picker/issues/105) (high, vi) → AND-PERM-03, AND-PERM-13

- **2025-03 · telehealth app embedding the MediQuo SDK (es, Ecuador)** — Foreground-service permission declaration ("insuficiencia en la justificación del uso del permiso FOREGROUND_SERVICE"). "la ultima versión de nuestra aplicación que utiliza el SDK de Mediquo en Google Play fue rechazada debido a una insuficiencia en la justificación del uso del permiso FOREGROUND_SERVICE… en el formulario de declaración de uso no existe un apartado específico para justificar FOREGROUND_SERVICE de forma general." Cause: SDK required `FOREGROUND_SERVICE`, `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CAMERA`, `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MICROPHONE` for video calls; no matching declaration. Fix: SDK 3.3.10 dropped foreground services; remove the three permissions; vendor warns "tendréis que hacer una Release al 100%" or the old build keeps tripping the check. [source](https://github.com/mediquo/mediquo-android-sdk/issues/4) (high, es) → AND-PERM-05, AND-CONSOLE-06, AND-PERM-13, AND-CONSOLE-11

- **2026-08 · calisthenics training app (es)** — Health Connect permissions policy ("acceso excesivo a datos de la función declarada"). "Google marcó 7 permisos. La auditoría del código encontró que la app pedía 12 tipos de datos y 3 no tenían ninguna función detrás." Cause: `READ_DISTANCE`, `READ_EXERCISE` requested with no feature; `READ_TOTAL_CALORIES_BURNED` read and cached but never shown; HRV/VO2max shown as raw numbers. Fix: cut to 7 record types, each with a visible screen; removed from `app.json`, `READ_RECORD_TYPES` and dead code so the permission cannot creep back. [source](https://github.com/guillermoscript/calistenia-app/pull/593) (high, es) → AND-CONSOLE-06, PERM-03, AND-PERM-13

- **2026-08 · multi-tenant education app template "Averon" (pt-BR)** — Photo and Video Permissions policy ("Usar seletores de sistema alternativos para fotos / vídeos"). "O Google Play recusou a publicacao com 'Usar seletores de sistema alternativos para fotos / videos'. Apps que miram API 33+ so podem pedir READ_MEDIA_IMAGES / READ_MEDIA_VIDEO se o seletor do sistema for tecnicamente insuficiente." Cause: `expo-image-picker` already uses the Android Photo Picker, but leftover `requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync()` calls plus the `expo-media-library` plugin kept injecting `READ_MEDIA_*`; every tenant built from the template failed. Fix: `android.blockedPermissions` (→ `tools:node="remove"`), `usePermissions({ writeOnly: true })` for the one save-to-gallery feature; verified with `expo prebuild`. [source](https://github.com/averonapp-design/template-averon/pull/3) (high, pt) → AND-PERM-08, AND-PERM-13

- **2026-06 · video app, Android TV build "onevid" (es)** — "Usos de hardware no admitidos" (android.hardware.faketouch). "Google Play rechazó el AAB de Android TV por 'Usos de hardware no admitidos': android.hardware.faketouch (las TVs no tienen táctil)." Fix: TV plugin declares `android.hardware.touchscreen` and `faketouch` with `required="false"`, `leanback` `required="true"`; phone AAB unchanged. [source](https://github.com/j1zuz/onevid/pull/25) (high, es) → AND-BUILD-02

### User data: prominent disclosure, location SDKs, Play Protect

- **2026-03 · SMS/phone utility "Luzzy Messages" (es)** — User Data policy: "APK REQUIRES PROMINENT DISCLOSURE". "Rechazo de Google Play por 'APK REQUIRES PROMINENT DISCLOSURE' — la app transmitía número de teléfono sin mostrar un aviso explícito con consentimiento afirmativo." Fix: non-dismissable first-launch privacy dialog (Accept → stores consent; Decline → app closes) shown in the normal flow before any phone-number/SMS/contacts access; version bump 8.3.0. [source](https://github.com/cesarve77/Messages-Luzzy/pull/13) (high, es) → PRIV-04, AND-PERM-01, AND-CONSOLE-02

- **2024-07 · Xamarin B2C app, 5 years on Play (ru, agency DD Planet)** — Location Permissions policy triggered by an old Yandex AppMetrica SDK; then repeated rejections because the **production** bundle still contained it. "Before resubmitting your app for further review, please make sure to deactivate the non‑compliant App Bundle Version: 649, Track: Production and increment the version code of app bundle in the Production / Open test / Closed test / Internal test track." Cause: AppMetrica Xamarin wrapper 2.3.0 collected background location by default; updates with 5.0.0 and even with the SDK fully removed (verified by `apktool` on `assemblies.manifest`) were still rejected with the same template; 14-day removal banner appeared on the live app. Fix: unpublish the app themselves (Release → Setup → Advanced settings → App availability), upload the clean build to every track, review passed in <24 h, republish; the 5.0.0 build then passed too. ~1 month, 5+ appeals. [source](https://habr.com/ru/articles/827088/) (high, ru) → AND-CONSOLE-11, AND-PERM-04, AND-PERM-13, AND-CONSOLE-02

- **2024-01 · maps & navigation "2ГИС", off-Play APK (ru)** — Google Play Protect classified the app as harmful ("spyware"). "2ГИС нет в Google Play, и мы не можем получать и оспаривать перед релизом решения алгоритмов проверки… можно предположить только одну причину — чтение контактов и фото." Cause: sideloaded APK (removed from Play by sanctions) requesting READ_CONTACTS, CALL_PHONE, SMS Retriever API and optional background location for "Friends on the map"; appeal stuck "In progress". Fix: shipped 6.31.0.535 without READ_CONTACTS, CALL_PHONE and SMS Retriever → warning gone; users on old builds still saw "malware trying to install another app" on update. [source](https://vc.ru/services/986803-vsya-istoriya-s-google-play-i-2gis-nash-post-mortem) (high, ru) → AND-QUALITY-02, AND-PERM-13, PERM-03, AND-PERM-04

### Console: privacy policy / account-deletion URLs

- **2026-08 · crypto wallet "Workizen" (vi)** — Privacy policy URL and Account-deletion link (Data safety) returned 404 → rejected twice. "Play từ chối `ai.workizen.wallet` hai lần cùng một nguyên nhân: `https://workizen.net/privacy` trả 404 — một lần cho Privacy policy, một lần cho Account Deletion Link trên Data safety form." Cause: the legal pages existed only on an unmerged branch; a `rsync --delete` deploy from `main` wiped them. Fix: restore `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/about`, `/contact` (the layout links to them — otherwise the reviewer hits new 404s) and add a dedicated account-deletion section with real steps (in-app delete wipes local data; e-mail for server records — kept separate to stay truthful). [source](https://github.com/poupou97/workizen-3d/pull/6) (high, vi) → AND-CONSOLE-01, AND-CONSOLE-08, META-04, ACC-02

- **2026-07 · ride-hailing driver app "Higo" (es, Venezuela)** — Data safety: "Enlace de eliminación de cuenta no válido en su formulario de seguridad de datos". "la URL declarada `https://higodriver.com/privacys` devuelve 404 (tenía una 's' de más y apuntaba a privacidad, no a borrado de cuenta)." Fix: publish `/eliminar-cuenta/` with how to request deletion (e-mail/WhatsApp with identity check, no reinstall needed), what is deleted vs. retained (transactions ~5 years) and the 30-day SLA; link from privacy policy; update the URL in Play Console → Data safety → Account deletion and resubmit. [source](https://github.com/manuelgonzalez18-ui/Higo/pull/63) (high, es) → AND-CONSOLE-08, ACC-02, META-04

### Enforcement: account termination / association

- **2022-02 · indie Android developer couple (ru translation)** — Developer account termination, "prior violations… on this or associated accounts". "Ваш аккаунт разработчика на Google Play был удален. Причина удаления: предшествующие нарушения правил программы для разработчиков… на данном аккаунте или на аккаунтах, связанных с ним… Не пытайтесь создавать новые аккаунты." Cause: never stated; the spouse's separate account received an identical termination four days later (account association). Fix: appeal form promised 7 days, answered after >1 month with a refusal; apps with ~1M installs lost; advised to "use other distribution methods". [source](https://habr.com/ru/articles/652317/) (high, ru) → AND-ENFORCE-01

### Enforcement / termination (qna.habr.com, ru)

- **2023-06 · indie game studio "Woger Games", 5.5M installs (ru)** — Developer account terminated: "Prior violations… by this or associated, previously-terminated Google Play Developer accounts". "14-го июня мне пришло очередное письмо с предупреждением, что одна из игр нарушает политику и у меня есть 30 дней… 21 июня, то есть спустя 7 дней… я получаю письмо об удалении моего аккаунта… we can't share the reasons we've concluded that one account is related to another." Cause: years of ad-mediation policy warnings + one old game ban ("Carnage") + alleged association with a terminated account; the 30-day fix window was not honored. Fix: none found; answers warn that even contacting support about an app block led to a full account ban and card blacklisting for another developer. [source](https://qna.habr.com/q/1291118) (high, ru) → AND-ENFORCE-01

- **2024-06 · kids-math game + "Skibidi Nextbot Toilet" game (ru)** — Permanent termination for repeated violations: misleading screenshot on "Математика для детей" (rejected 3–4 times, "оформление фотки… вводит в заблуждение") plus copyrighted content in the Skibidi game. "мне дали перманент бан за множественное нарушение правил" Cause: strikes from repeated metadata resubmissions + IP infringement counted together. Fix: none (no answers). [source](https://qna.habr.com/q/1356152) (medium, ru) → AND-ENFORCE-01, AND-META-02, AND-META-01

- **2022-02 · Android app republished to Samsung Galaxy Store (ru)** — Galaxy Store rejected the APK because Play Protect showed "Заблокировано Play Защитой" on install. "в Galaxy Store отклонили из-за того, что при установке apk файла выходит сообщение «Заблокировано Play Защитой»" Cause: sideloaded signed APK of an app whose Play listing (1k installs) used the AAB signing key; Play Protect flags unknown-signature APKs. Fix: author gave up on Galaxy Store. [source](https://qna.habr.com/q/1112416) (medium, ru) → AND-QUALITY-02, AND-BUILD-01

### 5.1.1(ix) organization requirement (ru)

- **2022-08 · beauty-salon client app "Центр красоты «Эстетик»" published by a sole proprietor (ru)** — Guideline 5.1.1 Legal – Data Collection and Storage (ix): "The app must be published under a seller and company name that is associated with the organization or company providing the services. In this case, your app must be published under a seller name and company name that reflects the Эстетик name… you cannot resolve this issue with documentation showing permission to publish this app on behalf of the content owner." Cause: earlier update was blocked until the individual account was converted to an organization (ИП Володин…), but the seller name still did not match the salon brand; Apple Developer Support then e-mailed separately. Fix path: rename the company on the account via the Team Agent or resubmit from the client's own account. [source](https://qna.habr.com/q/1194796) (high, ru) → ACC-06

### Vietnam / Indonesia (blogs)

- **2025-11 · Vietnamese studio app on a personal Play account (vi, Haposoft)** — Closed-testing / production-access requirement (12 testers × 14 days). "4 lần Closed Testing – 3 lần thất bại, 1 lần thành công… Thiếu log, thiếu báo cáo, không có video minh chứng — Google đánh giá là 'thiếu tính xác thực' và từ chối lần nữa." Cause: round 1 only one tester; round 2 twelve testers but not 14 continuous days; round 3 no evidence of daily use. Fix: 18 testers for 17 days, daily checklist, device tracking/logs, testers record videos, small feature updates during the test, honest and consistent production-access form. 4 rounds. [source](https://blog.haposoft.com/closed-testing-giai-doan-kho-nhan-nhat-trong-qua-trinh-phat-hanh-app-len-google-play/) (high, vi) → AND-TEST-01

- **2026 (undated post) · pawn-shop / lending app for a Vietnamese client (vi, BigDataTech)** — Guideline 3.2 Business / 5.1.1(ix) (regulated financial services). "Trong một dự án gần đây, app bị yêu cầu: Chỉ phát hành tại Việt Nam; Cung cấp giấy chứng nhận liên quan tới hoạt động cầm đồ." Cause: fintech/pawn category triggers licensing checks. Fix: set Availability to Vietnam only, attach the business licence, explain the operating model in Review Notes; review continued. Also lists 2.1 (crash, test account, internal API) and 5.1 (missing purpose strings) as their most frequent rejections. [source](https://bigdatatech.vn/case-study/kinh-nghiem-submit-app-len-apple-store-va-nhung-loi-de-bi-reject) (medium, vi) → LAW-02, ACC-06, META-05, IOS-PLIST-01

- **2024-12 (KB updated) · white-label e-commerce apps built with bukaOlshop selling game top-ups (id)** — Play "Issue: Violation of Payment Policy"; also "Issue: Need login credentials for app review" and "Issue: Invalid Data Safety section". "kita tidak diperbolehkan untuk mengupload aplikasi yang menawarkan pembelian item game menggunakan pembayaran selain dari google play billing. Hal ini membuat kebanyakan aplikasi yang terdapat pembelian item game didalamnya langsung ditolak oleh playstore dengan alasan 'Issue: Violation of Payment Policy'." Cause: store apps listing Mobile Legends diamonds etc. paid by bank transfer = digital goods outside Play Billing. Vendor "fix": rename/strip game titles and images from products, disable "Transfer", keep only wallet balance during review, re-add products after approval (a workaround, not compliance); for login: register an account and add it under App content → App access; for Data safety: fill the form per their screenshots. [source](https://help.bukaolshop.com/tips-tips-mengatasi-upload-ke-playstore-ditolak-reject/) (medium, id) → PAY-01, AND-PAY-01, AND-CONSOLE-03, AND-CONSOLE-02, CONTENT-10

### Brazil — Fabapp no-code community (comunidade.fabapp.com, pt-BR)

- **2022-01 · white-label local app with login (pt-BR)** — App access / "login credentials needed for review". "o seu app pede um log in para entrar e o Google Play está pedindo que você forneça um para eles entrarem e poderem verificar o aplicativo… A Google está exigindo o fornecimento de dados de acesso para aprovar aplicativos com login ativado." Fix: add a working username/password under App content → App access. Resolved in a week. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/app-rejeitado-na-google-play-store/4019) (high, pt) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03

- **2024-08 · no-code app with restricted areas (pt-BR)** — Same "login credentials" rejection although the credentials were valid. "estamos com problemas de recusa do aplicativo por motivo de erro de credenciais, porem revisamos mais de 3 vezes e os acessos estao OK." Fix (moderator): give a valid e-mail/password *and* say in the instructions which parts of the app are restricted — "Essas instruções precisam ser em inglês". [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/estou-com-problemas-para-submeter-um-aplicativo-no-google-console-play-por-recusa-de-erro-de-login-no-aplicativo/7036) (medium, pt) → AND-CONSOLE-03, META-05

- **2022-02 · no-code business app (pt-BR)** — App Store: 4.2 Design – Minimum Functionality **and** 5.2.1 Legal – Intellectual Property (app must be published from the owner's account). "Funcionalidade mínima… o app não agrega benefício exclusivo para o usuário a ponto de se diferenciar da experiência de um site no navegador… Quanto à propriedade intelectual, a Apple exige que o app seja publicado na conta de seu proprietário." Fix: send the reviewer a *contrato social* / CNPJ card proving the account owner owns the app; add native value beyond the website. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/app-rejeitado-na-app/4102) (medium, pt) → CONTENT-03, ACC-06, META-05

- **2022-07 · city-hall citizen app "Ita na Mão" (pt-BR)** — User Data policy: "seção de segurança de dados inválida" (status "Aprovado com problemas – Ação adicional necessária"). "Detectamos dados do usuário transmitidos do dispositivo que você não divulgou na seção Segurança de dados." Cause: the no-code runtime (Firebase) sends device identifiers not declared. Fix: in Data safety mark "Identificadores de dispositivo" as collected per the platform's screenshots. Same template hit dozens of Fabapp apps through 2022–2023 with the verbatim "SPLIT_BUNDLE 90000/10000: Policy Declaration – Data Safety Section: Device Or Other IDs". [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/resolvido-recusa-do-meu-app-na-google-play/4693) · [4930](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/resolvido-politica-de-privacidade-do-google-play-bloqueando-app/4930) · [5107](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/app-rejeitado-invalid-data-safety-form-na-google/5107) · [5473](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/status-do-aplicativo-rejeitado/5473) · [5799](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/rejeitado-atualizacao-no-google/5799) · [6395](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/resolvido-aplicativo-nao-esta-em-conformidade-com-as-politicas-do-google-play-horabus/6395) (high, pt) → AND-CONSOLE-02, PRIV-02, AND-CONSOLE-04

- **2023-05 · no-code app, Data safety resubmitted several times (pt-BR)** — "Formulário de Segurança dos dados inválido… Identificamos que o app transmite dados para fora do dispositivo que não foram informados no formulário." Tip from a moderator: "após envio das informações vc ainda receberá informações de que seu app não está de acordo, pois a Google demora até 20 dias úteis para avaliar as informações." [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/rejeitado-atualizacao-no-google/5799) (medium, pt) → AND-CONSOLE-02, META-07

- **2023-11 · church app "Ministério de Primeira" (pt-BR)** — Advertising ID declaration blocked the update. "sobre o erro é só marcar que o app possui ID de publicidade e sobre os avisos, eles não impede a publicação ou atualização" Cause: AdMob present but "uses advertising ID" not declared in Play Console. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/socorro-gp-nao-esta-aceitando-atualizar-meu-app/6383) (medium, pt) → AND-CONSOLE-04, AND-CONSOLE-09

- **2023-06 · no-code app (pt-BR)** — "Issue found: Violation of Webviews and Affiliate Spam policy". "Google Play não aceita aplicativo que seja apenas um simples webview" — resolved after the builder replaced the pure web wrapper with native tabs/content. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/resolvido-publicacao-rejeitada-google/5975) (medium, pt) → CONTENT-03

- **2023-09 · bus-schedule / public-information app (pt-BR)** — Government-services / misleading-claims policy on an update ("adequar meu app"). Moderator's fix, quoting Google: "Identify a clear source of government information. Include in the app an easy-to-see disclaimer stating that the app doesn't represent a government entity. Check your app's store listing page for compliance and add the same disclaimer there too." [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/problemas-com-atualizacao-do-app-na-play-store/6196) (medium, pt) → AND-CONSOLE-12, AND-META-04

- **2023-08 · no-code app whose name resembled an existing app (pt-BR)** — App suspended for a name similar to an already-published app; cannot push a new version while suspended. "Meu aplicativo foi suspenso da Play store pois o nome dele era parecido com um já criado!! Eu não consigo nem lançar uma nova versão." Fix: rename the app (package name cannot change), appeal. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/suspensao-do-aplicativo/6130) (medium, pt) → AND-META-01, AND-ENFORCE-01

- **2024-05 · developer whose account was terminated, re-publishing under a new account (pt-BR)** — Upload blocked: "Remova os conflitos do manifesto antes de fazer upload. As seguintes autoridades do provedor de conteúdo estão sendo usadas por outros desenvolvedores: …FacebookInitProvider, …fileprovider, …firebaseinitprovider… Use um nome de pacote diferente. '…' já existe no Google Play." Cause: package name (and ContentProvider authorities) stay reserved by the terminated account forever. Fix: rebuild with a new package name ("terá que criar do zero"). [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/dificuldades-para-publicar-um-app-que-foi-excluido/6842) (high, pt) → AND-ENFORCE-01, AND-BUILD-02

- **2024-05 · first upload of an app with login (pt-BR)** — Data safety → Account deletion: "Adicione um link que os usuários possam usar para solicitar a exclusão da conta e dos dados… mencionar o nome do app…; destacar as etapas…; especificar os tipos de dados que são excluídos ou mantidos". Fix: separate web page (not the privacy policy) with deletion steps + a request form, and an in-app delete option. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/msg-google-play-link-que-os-usuarios-possam-usar-para-solicitar-a-exclusao-da-conta-e-dos-dados/6816) (medium, pt) → AND-CONSOLE-08, ACC-02

- **2024-08 / 2024-12 · two no-code apps rejected for privacy-policy URL (pt-BR)** — "a página da política de privacidade não está sendo encontrada, mas quando eu testo, funciona normalmente" / "a URL da política de privacidade do meu app não é válida". Fix that worked: the privacy-policy link must also be reachable **inside the app**, not only in the Console. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/meu-app-foi-rejeitado-por-causa-da-politica-de-privacidade-mas-a-politica-esta-100-correta/7006) · [7198](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/ajuda-com-rejeicao-de-url-de-politica-de-privacidade-no-google-play/7198) (medium, pt) → AND-CONSOLE-01, PRIV-01, META-04

- **2025-03 · business-directory app on a new personal account (pt-BR)** — Closed testing refused. "pedi a amigos e familiares mas como apenas baixaram o app e não usaram a Play Story recusou. Pelo oq entendi os testadores devem usar o app com frequência durante esses 14 dias." [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/teste-fechado-play-story/7358) (medium, pt) → AND-TEST-01

- **2025-05 · no-code app on iPad (pt-BR)** — App Store 2.1 Information Needed + 4.2 Minimum Functionality + 5.1.1: "O aplicativo usa a estrutura AppTrackingTransparency, mas não conseguimos localizar a solicitação de permissão App Tracking Transparency quando analisado no iPadOS 18.4.1… A solicitação deve aparecer antes da coleta de quaisquer dados que possam ser usados para rastrear o usuário." Cause: ATT framework linked (ads SDK) but the prompt never shows on a fresh iPad. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/app-rejeitado-apple-motivos/7417) (high, pt) → PRIV-05, IOS-PRIV-02, CONTENT-03, IOS-IPAD-01

- **2023-08 · no-code app flagged by App Review as a game (pt-BR)** — "a Appstore esta falando q meu app é um jogo?" — Fabapp staff: open the app in App Store Connect, tick/untick "Game Center", resubmit. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/rejeitado-appstore/6137) (medium, pt) → IOS-ENT-01, META-03

### Turkey (r10.net / technopat / donanimhaber, tr)

- **2024-01 · dormant hobby account with 4–5 old apps (tr)** — Account terminated after old apps accumulated policy-violation notices the owner could not fix or delete. "hesabıma girdiğimde eski yüklediğim 4-5 uygulamanın politika ihlali yaptığını ve güncellememi istediğini gördüm bende uygulamaları yayından kaldırıldığı için silmek istedim ama silemedim." Forum "fix": open a new account on a relative's identity, new device/IP (VDS), never reuse old APKs — i.e. the association-evasion playbook Google explicitly bans. [source](https://www.r10.net/android-ios-uygulama-gelistirme/3965861-kapatilan-gelistirici-hesabi-nasil-tekrar-acilir.html) (medium, tr) → AND-ENFORCE-01

- **2022-11 · first Play account publishing a purchased, bot-driven dating reskin (tr)** — Termination for association ("Uygulama botlu dating uygulamasıydı"): "Fesih nedeni: Geliştirici Programı Politikaları ve Geliştirici Dağıtım Sözleşmesi'nin bu veya ilişkili, önceden feshedilmiş Google Play Geliştirici hesapları tarafından önceden ihlal edilmiş olması… hesabınızın… feshedilen diğer Geliştirici hesaplarıyla bilgi paylaştığına… dair güçlü göstergelerimiz…" Cause: the source code was bought on a forum — the same code/signature had been published by a previously-terminated account. [source](https://www.r10.net/sorum-var/3440380-play-console-hesabim-kapatildi-ne-yapmam-gerek.html) (medium, tr) → AND-ENFORCE-01, AND-QUALITY-02

- **2024-12 · app stuck "in review" >2 months after a description-mismatch rejection (tr)** — "Uygulama, açıklama kısmındaki uyuşmazlıktan dolayı reddetmişti, bunun düzenlemesini 2 hafta önce gerçekleştirip tekrar incelemeye gönderdim… 3. hafta bitmek üzere halen incelemede." Support template after a month: "our review process may take up to or even more than 7 days depending on the review volume and complexity of the checking." Forum advice: edit the listing to re-trigger review, reply on the original violation ticket. [source](https://www.r10.net/android-ios-uygulama-gelistirme/4293256-google-play-console-2-aydan-fazladir-incelemede-olan-uygulama-hakkinda.html) (medium, tr) → AND-META-01, META-07

### Germany (android-hilfe.de, de)

- **2024-09 · open-source 2FA app "Authenticator Pro" (de forum coverage of the maintainer's notice)** — Developer-verification / public contact-details policy (answer 14177239): "Am 3. September 2024 wird mein Google Play Entwickler Konto von Google gelöscht… kann ich keine Apps mehr veröffentlichen, es sei denn ich willige ein meine Privatadresse anzeigen zu lassen." Cause: account had once sold a paid app, so the "monetised" flag forces a public address/phone even after unpublishing; organisation route needs a PO box + support phone. Fix: none on Play — moved to F-Droid/GitHub. [source](https://www.android-hilfe.de/forum/tools.441/authenticator-pro-aus-dem-play-store-verbannt.1100961.html) (high, de) → AND-META-03, AND-ACCOUNT-01, AND-ACCOUNT-02

- **2024-05 · new personal Play account that created its own tester accounts (tr)** — Account closed as "related to bot accounts". "Google, yeni geliştiriciler için 20 tane test hesabı istiyor. Ben de mecburen yeni hesaplar açmak zorunda kaldım… Bu açtığım test hesaplarından birini bot sanmışlar." Cause: self-created Google accounts for the closed-testing quota were flagged as bots → developer account terminated; only template replies. Forum advice: use an organisation account with tax ID, run many internal/closed/open releases, Firebase Test Lab; a deleted payments profile cannot be recreated; Google's Turkish entity (Google Reklamcılık ve Pazarlama Ltd. Şti.) can be sued locally. [source](https://forum.donanimhaber.com/play-console-hesabim-haksiz-yere-kapatildi-ne-yapabilirim--158802406) (medium, tr) → AND-TEST-01, AND-ENFORCE-01, AND-ACCOUNT-01

- **2024-10 · B2B app with QR-code login, already live on iOS (tr)** — 25–30 consecutive Play rejections: first description/category mismatch, then "we could not log in to test". "aynı uygulama iOS da tek seferde yayimlanmasina rağmen Google Play Store'dan 25 30 kere ret yedik… şimdi ise uygulamamıza test için giriş yapamadıklarını söylüyorlar. Uygulamamıza QR code ile giriş yapılıyor, QR kodu notlar da açıklama olarak yazıyorum." Cause: QR-only login cannot be exercised by the (largely automated) Play review; the QR pasted as text in App access notes did not work. Fix implied: provide a username/password or deep-link demo path. [source](https://www.technopat.net/sosyal/konu/google-play-console-surekli-olarak-ret-yeme.3471346/) (medium, tr) → AND-CONSOLE-03, ACC-03, AND-META-01

- **2026-07 · medical-education app "Tusoskop" (tr)** — App Store Connect upload validation: "Invalid bundle. The 'UIInterfaceOrientationPortrait,UIInterfaceOrientationPortraitUpsideDown' orientations were provided for the UISupportedInterfaceOrientations Info.plist key … but you need to include all of the … orientations to support iPad multitasking." Cause: previous PR locked iPad to portrait without `UIRequiresFullScreen`. Fix: restore all four orientations in `UISupportedInterfaceOrientations~ipad` (or set `UIRequiresFullScreen = true`). [source](https://github.com/Zahmett1227/tusoskop/pull/40) (high, tr) → IOS-IPAD-01

### Brazil — more Fabapp cases

- **2022-05 · municipal council app "Câmara Municipal Guarará" (pt-BR)** — Families policy violation → app removed. "Meu aplicativo de Câmara Municipal foi removido da Google Play por motivo de violação a política de família… Ele não tem nada haver com esse tema." Cause: target-audience questionnaire included children (<13) for a civic app, plus no reviewer login. Fix: "Você deve fornecer os dados de login… e não marque que seu aplicativo para menores de 13 anos… o ideal que seja para maiores de 16 anos"; Google reinstated the next day but required the Data safety section (device identifiers) to be completed. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/resolvido-app-suspenso-pela-google-play-motivo-violacao-a-politica-de-familia/4448) (high, pt) → AND-CONSOLE-07, AND-CONSOLE-03, AND-CONSOLE-02

- **2022-03 · no-code app whose hi-res icon showed rating imagery (pt-BR)** — "política de classificações, comentários e instalações do usuário" (User Ratings, Reviews and Installs). "Encontramos um problema nas seguintes áreas: Ícone de alta resolução (pt_BR)… Os desenvolvedores não devem tentar manipular o posicionamento de nenhum aplicativo no Google Play." Cause: icon/screenshots suggested ratings or install counts; rejected again after icon swap. [source](https://comunidade.fabapp.com/t/ola-queria-acompanhamento-de-um-profissional-meu-app-foi-rejeitado-de-novo/4263) (medium, pt) → AND-META-02, CONTENT-06

### Spain / Latin America — GitHub-documented cases (es)

- **2026-08 · race-tracking app "camberas-track" (es)** — Guideline 2.1(a) App Completeness: reviewer needs a demo QR to evaluate the app. "Apple rechazó la 1.0 (20) de camberas-track con la Guideline 2.1(a): necesitan un QR de demostración para evaluar las funciones de la app." Fix: seeded a permanent demo GPS token (bib 999, no real event), hosted a `demo-review.html` page with the QR + English instructions, linked from ASC Notes. [source](https://github.com/Unocrono/camberas/pull/4) (high, es) → ACC-03, META-05, UGC-06

- **2026-07 · expense-splitting app "Settly" (es)** — Guideline 3.1.1: unlocking Pro via a redeem code / external Stripe checkout. "Corrige el rechazo de Apple guideline 3.1.1 (desbloquear Pro con código de acceso no está permitido)." Fix: switched to the multiplatform model — native app never sells or unlocks Pro and shows no external purchase links; Pro gated only by account entitlement synced from the web; demo account set Pro in the DB for review. [source](https://github.com/patricbarca/settly/pull/219) (high, es) → PAY-01, IOS-UI-03, ACC-03

- **2026-07/08 · budgeting app "Presu" (es, Chile)** — Guideline 3.1.1 twice, both times based on the **website and store description**, not the binary. "Apple nos rechazó dos veces leyendo la web, no el código: 31-jul (3.1.1): citó «Plan Completo», término que nunca estuvo en el código del móvil — salió de la portada. 6-ago: sostuvo el rechazo citando la descripción de la ficha, donde el plan pago decía incluir «la sincronización entre tu celular y tu computador»." Fix: made phone sync free, rewrote pricing page/FAQ/terms/privacy and the store description so nothing ties the mobile app to a paid plan; added a build-time test that fails if the built HTML re-couples the phone to a price. [source](https://github.com/esteban86/presu/pull/19) (high, es) → PAY-01, META-02, IOS-UI-03, META-04

- **2026-04 · gym app "Mega Vital" (es)** — Guideline 2.3.8 (app name on device must match App Store name). "Cambia CFBundleName de 'gym_app' a 'Mega Vital' para que el nombre mostrado en el dispositivo coincida con el nombre en el App Store, resolviendo el rechazo de Apple por guideline 2.3.8." [source](https://github.com/vallejoj203k/Mega_vital/pull/42) (medium, es) → META-02, META-06

- **2026-08 · "Nebu" mobile app, closed-testing Alpha (es, Peru)** — Data safety vs. manifest mismatch: Firebase Analytics adds `com.google.android.gms.permission.AD_ID` transitively while the form says no advertising ID. "La publicación del build 1069 en la prueba cerrada Alpha fue rechazada por Google Play: Firebase Analytics declara AD_ID de forma transitiva, pero la declaración de seguridad de datos… dice que Nebu no recolecta advertising IDs." Fix: `tools:node="remove"` on the permission (collection already disabled via `google_analytics_adid_collection_enabled=false`). [source](https://github.com/Duvet05/nebu-mobile/pull/75) (high, es) → AND-CONSOLE-04, AND-PERM-13, AND-CONSOLE-02

- **2026-08 · coaching app "GC Fitness" (es, Argentina)** — Health Connect policy: privacy policy did not mention Health Connect. "la app de Android fue rechazada de Google Play el 2026-08-21 por información insuficiente sobre Health Connect… La política hablaba de Apple Health y no mencionaba Health Connect en ninguna de las dos versiones." Fix: dedicated bilingual section listing the two data types (steps, heart rate), purpose, read-only, sharing (coach + Firebase as processor), retention/deletion; published before resubmitting because "el revisor abre la URL". [source](https://github.com/mdb1/golden-crow-website/pull/349) (high, es) → PRIV-01, AND-CONSOLE-06, PRIV-02

- **2023-05 · Android app using OneSignal (es, es.stackoverflow)** — "Google Play, problemas Políticas Privacidad con One Signal – App retirada" (app removed for a User Data / privacy-policy discrepancy attributed to the OneSignal SDK). Body unreadable (Cloudflare + API quota exhausted); title only. [source](https://es.stackoverflow.com/questions/594829/google-play-problemas-pol%c3%adticas-privacidad-con-one-signal-app-retirada) (medium — title only, es) → AND-CONSOLE-02, PRIV-01, AND-PERM-13

- **2025-04 · indie game (es, es.stackoverflow)** — "Mi juego ha sido rechazado en Google Play por 'metadata misleading', pero no sé qué está mal" (Metadata policy). Title only. [source](https://es.stackoverflow.com/questions/631052/mi-juego-ha-sido-rechazado-en-google-play-por-metadata-misleading-pero-no-s%c3%a9) (medium — title only, es) → AND-META-01, AND-META-02

### France — GitHub-documented cases (fr)

- **2026-06 · news app "Facteur", Play flavor (fr)** — Sensitive permission REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES refused. "L'AAB du flavor playstore contient la permission REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES, refusée par Google Play (permission sensible sans cas d'usage valide pour une app d'actualité)… injectée par la dépendance open_filex (utilisée pour l'auto-update APK side-load)." Fix: `tools:node="remove"` in the playstore flavor manifest; keep it only in the side-load beta flavor. [source](https://github.com/boujonlaurin-dotcom/facteur/pull/867) (high, fr) → AND-PERM-15, AND-PERM-13

- **2026-07 · health-tracking app "Piloo" 1.0 build 51 (fr)** — Guideline 2.1(a) App Completeness (dead buttons) + 5.1.1(v) account deletion missing. "Bouton photo du profil : `onChangePhoto: () {/* TODO image_picker */}` ne fait rien. Ligne Aide & FAQ : aucun onTap… L'app permet la création de compte mais n'offre aucun flux de suppression." Fix: wire Help/FAQ and disclaimer rows, remove the unfinished photo button, add delete-account UI calling the existing `POST /v1/me/delete` (J+7), resubmit build 52 with a video of the deletion flow. [source](https://github.com/my-monkeys/piloo/issues/380) (high, fr) → CONTENT-01, ACC-02, UGC-06

### Russia-based indie in Serbia — both stores (habr, ru)

- **2025-10 · AI vocabulary app "VibeLing" (ru)** — App Store rejection because the Pro subscription purchase failed in review (Guideline 2.1 / IAP not functional). "В итоге покупка в проде не сработала. Через пару дней мне пришёл отказ от Apple, потому что покупка не срабатывала." Cause: RevenueCat set up with a single project; sandbox vs. production not separated, never tested through TestFlight. Fix: two RevenueCat projects (sandbox + prod), test success/failure paths in a TestFlight build; 1.0.1 approved. [source](https://habr.com/ru/articles/1015122/) (high, ru) → PAY-04, PAY-05, IOS-IAP-01

- **2025-11 · same developer on Google Play (ru)** — Six-year-old Play Console account found terminated for inactivity; re-paying the $25 fee was refunded; a new developer account could not be registered with Russian profile data. "Оказалось, что Google проводил массовую блокировку аккаунтов, в которых в течение года не появлялось приложений… Google давал оплатить 25 долларов, но через пару дней возвращал… пришлось поменять ей данные с РФ на Сербию, потому что на РФ не получалось зарегистрировать аккаунт разработчика." Fix path: new account on spouse's Google identity with Serbian residence card + Serbian phone, physical Android device login required for verification, 12+ closed testers (recruited ~20 via Telegram), ~5-day review; payouts only accepted a real-bank business account (Serbian), Turkish Ziraat card and virtual cards rejected by Apple. [source](https://habr.com/ru/articles/1015122/) (high, ru) → AND-ACCOUNT-01, AND-ENFORCE-01, AND-TEST-01


---

---

## Patterns (both stores)

1. **The reviewer can't reach the feature** — broken/expired/2FA demo accounts, forced-update gates, ATT or age gates shown too late, deletion buried. Fixes: demo mode, version-check logic, screenshots/videos in review notes. → ACC-03, CONTENT-11, META-05
2. **Library-injected permissions and capabilities** — `READ_MEDIA_*`, FGS types, `QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES`, `USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT`, `AD_ID`; HealthKit/FamilyControls/ATT references left in the binary or on the App ID. Fixes: `tools:node="remove"`, merged-manifest audit, capability removal. → AND-PERM-13, IOS-ENT-04
3. **Stale artifacts in other tracks / App ID** — rejections cite old version codes; capability still enabled in the portal. → AND-CONSOLE-11, IOS-ENT-04
4. **Category minimums missed** — UGC (terms + filter + report + block + contact), subscriptions (name/period/price + EULA & privacy links **in app and in metadata**), account deletion in-app + web. → UGC-*, PAY-02, ACC-02
5. **Sign-in asymmetry** — social login without Sign in with Apple; asking name/email again after SIWA; rejecting relay emails; wrong button wording. → IOS-LOGIN-01, IOS-LOGIN-04
6. **Purpose strings and disclosures in the wrong place or too vague** — "what" without "why + example"; the post-deny message; not localized; Play disclosures in the listing instead of in-app. → PERM-01, IOS-PLIST-01, PRIV-04
7. **Declared capability with no visible feature** — background audio for timers/alarms/recording; FGS type declared but not started; Data safety missing SDK collection. → IOS-BG-01, AND-PERM-05, PRIV-02
8. **Store listing must explain sensitive features** — Health Connect, FGS, VPN, background location, financial declarations are judged from the **full description** and demo **video**. → AND-CONSOLE-06, AND-CONSOLE-09
9. **Hosting defects** — privacy policy / deletion URL on GitHub Markdown, Blogspot, PDF, editable posts, JS-only pages, servers that fail non-browser TLS. → PRIV-01, ACC-02
10. **Automated heuristics** — 4.3 similarity/terminated-account matches, "free"/"libre", "Tutorial & Rules", WakeLock as "changing device settings", threat-feed false positives. What worked: substantial icon/UI/feature change, evidence-heavy appeals, reviewer notes on every later update. → CONTENT-07, META-02, AND-QUALITY-02

## Reviewer phrasings index (for parsing rejection emails)

| Wording (verbatim fragments) | Store | → IDs |
|---|---|---|
| "We were unable to sign in / log in with the provided credentials" · "Invalid login credentials" · "provided login credentials did not allow access" | both | ACC-03, AND-CONSOLE-03 |
| "does not appear to offer an equivalent login option" · "must offer Sign in with Apple as an equivalent option" | Apple 4.8 | IOS-LOGIN-01 |
| "This information is already provided by the Authentication Services framework" | Apple 4.0 | IOS-LOGIN-04 |
| "supports account creation but does not include an option to initiate account deletion" · "Invalid account / data deletion link" | both | ACC-02, AND-CONSOLE-08 |
| "requires users to register or log in to access features that are not account-based" | Apple 5.1.1 | ACC-01 |
| "purpose strings… do not sufficiently explain… provide a specific example" · ITMS-90683 | Apple 5.1.1 | PERM-01, IOS-PLIST-01 |
| "declares support for audio in the UIBackgroundModes key… unable to play any audible content" · "can be replicated with Local Notifications" | Apple 2.5.4 | IOS-BG-01 |
| "non-public or deprecated APIs" · "still includes ScreenTime API without ScreenTime features" · "binary includes references to HealthKit" · "binary contains references to App Tracking Transparency" | Apple 2.5.1 | IOS-UI-02, IOS-ENT-04 |
| "did not include all the required information for apps offering auto-renewable subscriptions" · "billed amount… not clearly and conspicuously displayed" · "only appropriate for services that provide dynamic, ongoing value" · "Details about the subscription offer are missing" | both | PAY-02 |
| "mechanisms other than in-app purchase" · "promo codes" · "does not include a 'Restore Purchases' feature" | Apple 3.1.1 | PAY-01, PAY-03 |
| "shares a similar binary, metadata, and/or concept" · "terminated Apple Developer Program account" · "We cannot provide individual feedback" | Apple 4.3 | CONTENT-07 |
| "information about third-party platforms that may not be relevant for App Store users" | Apple 2.3.10 | CONTENT-04 |
| "The app contains hidden features" · "behave differently during the review process" | Apple 2.3.1 | CONTENT-10 |
| "only includes links, images, or content aggregated from the Internet" · "intended for a small, or niche, set of users" | Apple 4.2 | CONTENT-03 |
| "not functional when Location Services are disabled" | Apple 5.1.5 | PERM-02 |
| "unable to access content without updating the app" | Apple 2.1 | CONTENT-11 |
| "Missing API declaration" ITMS-91053 · "Missing privacy manifest" ITMS-91061 | Apple | IOS-PRIV-03 |
| "Permission use is not directly related to your app's core purpose" · "Remove the use of … permission from all version codes within the submission" · "consider using the Android photo picker" | Play | AND-PERM-08, AND-CONSOLE-11 |
| "You must let us know whether your app uses any Foreground Service permissions" · "permissions that require a declaration but were not declared in Play Console" · "does not sufficiently demonstrate the use of permission" | Play | AND-PERM-05, AND-CONSOLE-06, AND-PERM-13 |
| "cannot use BOOT_COMPLETED broadcast receivers to launch certain foreground service types" | Play | AND-PERM-05 |
| "APK REQUIRES PROMINENT DISCLOSURE" · "without a prominent disclosure" · "could not locate prominent disclosure of the AccessibilityService API" | Play | PRIV-04, AND-PERM-06 |
| "Invalid Data safety form. We detected user data transmitted off device" · "Your app's privacy policy does not meet necessary policy requirements" · "プライバシーポリシーへのリンクが無効です" | Play | PRIV-02, PRIV-01 |
| "The app title or description does not accurately describe the app's functionality" · "誤解を招く記載" | Play | META-02 |
| "App installs, but doesn't load" · "crash, force close, freeze" · "button that is not responsive or leads to a blank page" | Play | CONTENT-01 |
| "unable to confirm your app's declared use of VpnService" | Play | AND-PERM-14 |
| "Excessive data access for declared feature" · "Insufficient justification for requested permission(s)" · "Inappropriate Health Connect Access Requested" | Play | AND-CONSOLE-09 |
| "Financial features declaration… is inaccurate" | Play | AND-CONSOLE-09 |
| "MALWARE, BEHAVIOR TRANSPARENCY AND MOBILE UNWANTED SOFTWARE" · "changing device settings without user consent" | Play | AND-QUALITY-02 |
| "schedules exact alarms without checking whether the SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM permission has been granted" · "USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT… no longer be eligible to have this permission pre-granted" | Play | AND-PERM-10 |

| "사용자에 대한 신고도 추가" (add user reporting too) | Play | UGC-03 |

| "「照片和视频权限」政策：对该权限的使用与您应用的核心用途没有直接关系" · "您的应用只需访问设备上的媒体文件一次，或者很少访问此类文件" · "Change your app's target API level to at least 31" | Play | AND-PERM-08, AND-SDK-01 |

### Google Play — 3rd pass wording (verbatim; thread ids in parentheses)

- "The published standards are invalid" (Child Safety Standards; standards must load, mention CSAE and name the app/developer) — 386705063

- "Make changes to your store listing by removing any excessive elements that may appeal to children" — 235692157
- "Apps that declare themselves or are listed as 'News' in the Google Play Console must meet the News policy requirements. Specifically, it appears that your app: Contains only static content—for example, content that's more than three months old" — 343077446 / 300747191
- "You told us that your app is not a news app" — 313918927
- "Play Console Terms Policy: Violation of Play Console Terms — Some app types can only be distributed by organizations. You've selected an app category or stated that your app offers specific features that require you to submit your app using an organization account." — 404291888
- "Violation of Play Console Requirements – Some apps (VPN) must be published using an organization account." — 401621866
- "Health Connect Minimum Scope" / "Your application does not require the following permissions: BodyFat, BloodPressure. Please ensure you are only requesting the minimum necessary scopes." — 401465466 / 381235420
- "Excessive data access for declared feature" — 391538459
- "Inappropriate Health Connect Access Requested — StepsCadence/Steps" — 384553939
- "Health features in your app don't match the information you provided in your health declaration. Check you have properly declared the following category or categories: Others" — 459205932
- "The partner bank(s) or NBFC(s) listed in your app's description does not match with the name and/or URLs of bank(s) or NBFC(s) provided in your Financial features declaration." — 297079562
- "Violation of Personal Loan policy… Your app requests access to one or more of the following permissions… 'App Split Bundle [11]: READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE, WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE'" — 367661031
- "We are unable to approve your app's use of FOREGROUND_SERVICE permission because we found the information provided to be insufficient… The video link and/or the app description did not clearly demonstrate the need for this permission." — 429187736
- "Auto App Quality Guidelines: the content of your application is not able to load" / "App crashes when we attempted to test functionality." / "Issue found: Parking and Charging Functionality — The app didn't provide meaningful functionality in its category relevant to driving." — Speed-Limit #1, armstrong-and-getty #1, track-history #137
- "Latest app update not accepted into Android (Auto)… Issue: Invalid or incomplete login credentials… (for example, a QR code, instructions, etc.)" — 383218015
- "Missing ongoing activity: When a user has an ongoing activity, your app did not do one or more of the following…" — 395667997
- "Wear app functionality not working as described… Your app indicates it is a non-standalone but the companion app or browser (mobile or desktop) doesn't connect with the Wear app" — 358380132
- "Requests for in-app user consent and runtime permission are not immediately preceded by an in-app disclosure." — openremote #67
- "Issue found: Inadequate Prominent Disclosure — The in-app Prominent Disclosure does not disclose the usage of accessed or collected Location data." — 407575005
- "Issue found: Prominent disclosure Non compliant design… A toggle button will not meet the threshold for explicit consent… Apps must have two buttons clearly displaying consent options for users." — 457911309
- "Braintree Payments SDK com.braintreepayments.api:data-collector: Consider upgrading to version 4.31.0 of the SDK." — braintree-android-drop-in #505
- "We detected user data transmitted off devices that you have not disclosed in your app's data safety form as user data collected… Precise Location" — RealUnit #463
- "Invalid Encryption Declaration" (Data safety) — 352952614
- "You have declared that your app does not allow users to create an account in your app's Data safety form and we've detected that there is account creation available." — 293361577 / 292353185
- "Paywall restriction. The in-app experience is behind a paywall or a subscription is required. Update your app login credentials in the app access declaration." — 346992873
- "Insufficient user engagement" (closed-testing production-access denial) — HN 46938101
- "Your app was suspended for repeated non-compliance with Developer Program Policies, demonstrated by multiple previous rejections… Review other apps within your developer account…" — 454167198
- "Billing Fraud — code that automatically charges the user in an intentionally deceptive way" (Malware policy, appeal reply) — 460254661
- "Your app currently contains malicious code or behavior that can be considered as spying on the user or exfiltrates data that is not related to provide policy-compliant functionality." (Spyware) — 340445859
- "ELEVATED PRIVILEGE ABUSE" (Malware policy) — 313897268
- "the 'isMonitoringTool' flag is missing from the manifest file" — 347248114
- "Store listing information does not match the core functionality for the declared use case; In-app experience does not match the core functionality for the declared use case" (SMS) — 436772560
- "Issue found: Unable to verify core functionality of app — Your declared that your permission use case is the core functionality of your app…" — 288938705
- "couldn't find evidence that the app offers the declared core functionality" — 455323271
- "Issue found: Intent Redirection… Code Analysis: 'zc.b$b.onReceive'" — plus_plugins #3255

- "Update your target API level by August 31, 2026." — 453449487

(305447204); "Pausing is not enough, you will have to either fully replace or deactivate the track." (398834984); "changing anything on the console will reset the review timer" (448776654); "Your test login credentials must be accessible at all times, reusable, and valid regardless of user location." (320923249)

- Community shorthand: "리젝 대응", "심사 반려", "심사 거절", "리젝 먹다", "둘러보기 계정/모드" (guest browse mode), "무관용 조항", "구매 복원 버튼"

### Apple — 3rd pass wording (2025–2026)

- "We were unable to install the widget of the app using an iPhone. … We couldn't find the app name among the possible selections." (2.1(a))
- "Session started on Apple Watch but nothing appear on iPhone." (2.1(a))
- "An automated analysis indicates the app uses one or more Screen Time APIs but the app has not been submitted with the Family Controls entitlement."
- "The app includes the com.apple.security.network.server entitlement but does not appear to have matching functionality." (2.4.5(i))
- "We need a demo video that shows a physical Apple device and the designated hardware pairing together and interacting during the use of the app." (2.1)
- "updated demo videos will need to be provided for every app submission" (2.1)
- "We need additional information about how the app uses face data. Face data is sensitive user information that must be handled properly to protect the user's security and privacy." (2.1)
- "The app uses public APIs in an unapproved manner, which does not comply with guideline 2.5.1. Specifically, NEVPNManager APIs." (2.5.1)
- "The app uses or references the following non-public or deprecated APIs: Frameworks/flutter_inappwebview_ios.framework … Classes: PGHostedWindow / Runner Classes: __SwiftValue" (2.5.1)
- "the binary includes TrueDepth APIs" / "it would be appropriate to remove references to the HealthKit framework from the binary" (2.5.1)
- "The app declares support for audio in the UIBackgroundModes key in the Info.plist, but we are unable to play any audible content when the app is running in the background." (2.5.4)
- "The app declares support for location in the UIBackgroundModes key … but we are unable to locate any features that require persistent location." (2.5.4)
- "The app's metadata includes content that is similar to designs or terms used for Apple products and services and may cause confusion for users. Specifically, your metadata includes: – Terms for Mac in the app subtitle in an inappropriate manner." (5.2.5)
- "the app name used Apple's 'Mac' trademark, both in App Store metadata and in the name displayed on the device" (5.2.5)
- "Note that references to free or discounted services are considered a price reference and are not appropriate for app metadata." (2.3.7)
- "App Store screenshots included the price of the service." (2.3.7)
- "Revise the app's screenshots to remove non-iOS status bar images." (2.3.10)
- "The app accesses digital content purchased outside the app, such as plans, but that content isn't available to purchase using In-App Purchase." (3.1.1)
- "donations for the development of the app must use In-App Purchase" (3.1.1, visionOS)
- "the iOS app still allowed creating a business/organization account" (3.1.1 / 3.1.3(a))
- "We noticed that the different subscription products of your auto-renewable subscriptions were created as separate in-app purchase products, rather than as different subscription products within the same subscription group." (3.1.2)
- "In-app purchase products associated with the app version submitted for review … could not be found in the submitted binary." (2.1)
- "the app displayed an error message, even after successfully purchasing the credits" (2.1(b))
- "We were still unable to purchase the subscription successfully. … your server needs to be able to handle a production-signed app getting its receipts from Apple's test environment." (2.1)
- "You submitted duplicate or identical promotional images for different promoted in-app purchase products and/or win back offers." (2.3.2)
- "sends personal data to a third-party AI service without saying what is sent, naming who it goes to, or asking permission first" / "Specify who the data is sent to" (5.1.1(i)/5.1.2(i))
- "The app requests users to rate the app on first launch or during onboarding, before they've had enough time to gain a clear understanding of the app's value." (5.6.3)
- "We noticed your app shares a similar binary, metadata, and/or concept as other apps you already submitted to the App Store, with only minor differences." (4.3(a), same account)
- "The app contains hidden features." / "the app may still contain financial transactions without the necessary authorization" (2.3.1)
- "We found that your app references the ASIdentifierManager API … It would be appropriate to remove all instances of 'ASIdentifierManager' from your app, even if they are not utilized in your app's functionality." (1.3)
- "Your app implements the App Tracking Transparency framework, which is used when apps collect data about users and share it with third-parties for tracking purposes." (1.3)
- "the user is taken to the default web browser to sign in or register for an account, which provides a poor user experience" (Guideline 4)
- "Parts of the app's user interface were crowded, laid out, or displayed in a way that made it difficult to use the app when reviewed on iPad Air 11-inch (M3) running iPadOS 26.1. Specifically, buttons were not fully visible when reviewed on iPad." (4.0)
- "Bug description: Unable to proceed" / "The microphone button is unresponsive." / "An error appeared during login." / "Unable to create an account, and it always gives us an error message." (2.1(a), iPad Air 11-inch (M3), iPadOS 26.x)
- "Accessibility may not be used for automation" (2.4.5, macOS)
- ITMS-91017: "Invalid SDK usage - The following frameworks aren't permitted for apps using an alternative web browser engine: [BrowserEngineKit]"
- ITMS-90111: "Unsupported SDK or Xcode version - App submissions must use the latest Xcode and SDK Release Candidates (RC)." (also emitted for beta `BuildMachineOSBuild`)
- ITMS-91065: "Missing signature - Your app includes 'Frameworks/liqpay_ios.framework/liqpay_ios', which includes IQKeyboardManagerSwift, an SDK that was identified in the documentation as a commonly used third-party SDK … must include a signature file."
- (2016, IPv6) "we discovered one or more bugs in your app when reviewed on iPad and iPhone running iOS 10.1 on Wi-Fi connected to an IPv6 network. Specifically, we were unable to login"
- (2020) "The Apple Sign-in authentication method does not use LocalAuthentication as the App's primary authentication mechanism." (2.5.13, misapplied)
- (2020) "your app enables users to view and select files, but it does not include functionality to allow users to view or select items from the Files app and the user's iCloud documents" (2.5.15)

### Russian / Spanish / Portuguese / Turkish / German / French wording (3rd pass)

**Apple (English text quoted inside ru/vi/pt/es/tr posts)**
- "The Support or Privacy URL provided in App Store Connect, in one or more localization, is currently not functional and/or displays an error." (1.5)
- "We need a video that demonstrates the current version in use on a physical iOS device, pairing and interacting with the designated hardware." (2.1 hardware; ru)
- "Your app provides a limited user experience as it is not sufficiently different from a mobile browsing experience… Including iOS features such as push notifications, Core Location, and sharing do not provide a robust enough experience" (4.2.2)
- "Ваше приложение содержит только ссылки, изображения или контент, собранный из Интернета. Поскольку ваш контент недостаточно отличается от обычного веб-браузера…" (4.2.2, Russian-localized Resolution Center text)
- "Приложение или его метаданные содержат вводящие в заблуждение ссылки на сторонний контент. В частности, приложение включает контент, напоминающий Wildberries, Яндекс, Aliexpress" (4.1, Russian)
- "Обозначенные ранее проблемы всё ещё требуют вашего внимания" (follow-up rejection, Russian)
- "The app must be published under a seller and company name that is associated with the organization or company providing the services… you cannot resolve this issue with documentation showing permission to publish this app on behalf of the content owner" (5.1.1(ix))
- "Revise the app's screenshots to remove non-iOS status bar images." (2.3.10)
- "O aplicativo usa a estrutura AppTrackingTransparency, mas não conseguimos localizar a solicitação de permissão App Tracking Transparency quando analisado no iPadOS 18.4.1." (2.1 / 5.1.1, Portuguese-localized)
- "Invalid bundle. The 'UIInterfaceOrientationPortrait,UIInterfaceOrientationPortraitUpsideDown' orientations were provided for the UISupportedInterfaceOrientations Info.plist key … but you need to include all of the … orientations to support iPad multitasking." (upload validation)
- "Хэлоу, спасибо за предоставленную информацию, мы продолжим ревью вашего приложения" / "Thank you for the information, we will continue the review" (the reply that precedes approval)
**Google Play (English/localized)**
- "Action Required: Your app is not compliant with Google Play Policies" / «Требуется действие: ваше приложение нарушает правила Google Play»
- "Issue found: Invalid Data safety form … We detected user data transmitted off device that you have not disclosed … SPLIT_BUNDLE 90000: Policy Declaration - Data Safety Section: Device Or Other IDs Data Type" (pt-BR threads 2022–2023; Portuguese: "Problema encontrado: formulário de segurança de dados inválido … Detectamos dados do usuário transmitidos pelo dispositivo que você não divulgou")
- "Status: Aprovado com problemas - Ação adicional necessária"
- "Issue: Need login credentials for app review" (id) / "recusa devido a login" (pt)
- "Issue: Violation of Payment Policy" (id, game top-ups)
- "Issue found: Violation of Webviews and Affiliate Spam policy"
- "Issue found: Violation of Misleading Claims policy … apps that provide government information, whether or not they are government affiliated, must strictly adhere" + fix steps "Identify a clear source of government information. Include in the app an easy-to-see disclaimer stating that the app doesn't represent a government entity."
- "Richtlinie zu Identitätsdiebstahl" / "Richtlinie zu irreführenden Behauptungen: Fehlender Quell-Link für Behördeninformationen" (de)
- "Permissão 'solicitar pacotes de instalação': O uso da permissão não tem relação direta com a finalidade principal do app." (pt) / "permission sensible sans cas d'usage valide" (fr)
- "Usar seletores de sistema alternativos para fotos / vídeos" (pt, READ_MEDIA_*) / "acceso excesivo a datos de la función declarada" (es, Health Connect) / "Usos de hardware no admitidos: android.hardware.faketouch" (es)
- "APK REQUIRES PROMINENT DISCLOSURE" (es post)
- "Enlace de eliminación de cuenta no válido en su formulario de seguridad de datos" (es) / "Adicione um link que os usuários possam usar para solicitar a exclusão da conta e dos dados" (pt)
- "Before resubmitting your app for further review, please make sure to deactivate the non-compliant App Bundle Version: NNN, Track: Production and increment the version code of app bundle in the Production / Open test / Closed test / Internal test track" (ru)
- Termination template, seen in ru/pt/tr/fr: "Reason for termination: Prior violations of the Developer Program Policies and Developer Distribution Agreement by this or associated, previously-terminated Google Play Developer accounts… we can't share the reasons we've concluded that one account is related to another." / «Не пытайтесь создавать новые аккаунты. Все новые аккаунты будут удаляться без возмещения взноса.» / "Fesih nedeni: … ilişkili, önceden feshedilmiş Google Play Geliştirici hesapları" / "Please do not attempt to register a new developer account. Any new accounts will be closed, and your developer registration fee will not be refunded."
- "More testing required — Aplikasi Anda belum siap dipublikasikan" (id closed testing)
- Support stall: "our review process may take up to or even more than 7 days depending on the review volume and complexity of the checking" (tr)
- Play Protect on sideloaded builds: «Заблокировано Play Защитой» / harmful-app category "spyware" (ru)
